def cap_reply_target_to_config(config: object, adjusted_target: int) -> int:
    """Keep dynamic goal pressure from exceeding the configured reply target."""
    try:
        target = int(adjusted_target)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0
    try:
        limits = config.get("session_limits", {}) if hasattr(config, "get") else {}
        configured = limits.get("comments_per_session") if hasattr(limits, "get") else None
        if configured is None or isinstance(configured, bool):
            return max(0, target)
        return max(0, min(target, int(configured)))
    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        return max(0, target)

        # Risiko: Pool=1 Quality (Gatekeeper-Vorfilter bleibt aktiv, F27 CJK-Block aktiv).

    def get_variable_interval(self, base_interval, variation_percent=50):
        """
        Generiert variable Intervalle fuer menschlicheres Timing.
        
        Args:
            base_interval: Basis-Intervall in Minuten
            variation_percent: Prozentuale Variation (Standard: 50%)
        
        Returns:
            Zufaelliges Intervall in Minuten
        """
        if not self._enhanced_flag("smart_intervals", True):
            return base_interval
        from core.timing import variable_interval_minutes

        variable_interval = variable_interval_minutes(
            float(base_interval),
            float(variation_percent),
            natural_timing_distribution=self._anti_detection_flag("natural_timing_distribution", True),
            activity_clustering=self._anti_detection_flag("activity_clustering", False),
        )

        self.safe_log(f"[UHR] Variable Timing: {base_interval}min +/-+/-{variation_percent}% -> {variable_interval:.1f}min", "TIMING", "DEBUG")
        
        return variable_interval

    def load_config(self, config_file):
        """Laedt erweiterte Konfiguration."""
        from core.config_adapter import load_config_dict

        try:
            return load_config_dict(config_file)
        except Exception as e:
            try:
                self.safe_log(f"Konfig-Laden fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "CONFIG", "WARNING")
            except Exception as log_exc:
                print(f"Logging failed in load_config: {log_exc}")
            return {}
    def safe_log(self, message, category="GENERAL", level="INFO"):
        """Erweiterte sichere Logging-Funktion mit Kategorien."""
        try:
            # Optional: DEBUG nur bei detailed_logging
            try:
                lvl = str(level or "INFO").upper()
            except Exception:
                lvl = "INFO"
            if lvl == "DEBUG":
                try:
                    ef = self.config.get("enhanced_features", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                    if not bool(ef.get("detailed_logging", True)):
                        return
                except Exception:
                    pass
            # Erstelle LogRecord mit Kategorie
            record = logging.LogRecord(
                name=logger.name,
                level=getattr(logging, level),
                pathname="",
                lineno=0,
                msg=message,
                args=(),
                exc_info=None
            )
            record.category = category
            
            # Log mit entsprechendem Level
            if level == "DEBUG":
                logger.debug(message, extra={'category': category})
            elif level == "WARNING":
                logger.warning(message, extra={'category': category})
            elif level == "ERROR":
                logger.error(message, extra={'category': category})
            else:
                logger.info(message, extra={'category': category})
            append_control_event(
                "log",
                component=category,
                status="recorded",
                level=level,
                message=message,
            )
                
        except UnicodeEncodeError:
            safe_message = message.encode('ascii', 'ignore').decode('ascii')
            logger.info(f"[SAFE] {safe_message}", extra={'category': category})
            append_control_event(
                "log",
                component=category,
                status="recorded",
                level=level,
                message=safe_message,
                context={"sanitized": True},
            )

    def _flag(self, name: str, default: bool = True) -> bool:
        try:
            if isinstance(self.config, dict) and name in self.config:
                return bool(self.config.get(name))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'An unexpected error occurred: {e}')
        return bool(default)

    def _enhanced_flag(self, name: str, default: bool = True) -> bool:
        try:
            ef = self.config.get("enhanced_features", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if isinstance(ef, dict) and name in ef:
                return bool(ef.get(name))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'An unexpected error occurred: {e}')
        return bool(default)

    def _intelligence_flag(self, name: str, default: bool = True) -> bool:
        try:
            it = self.config.get("intelligence_settings", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if isinstance(it, dict) and name in it:
                return bool(it.get(name))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'Error in _intelligence_flag: {e}')
        return bool(default)

    def _anti_detection_flag(self, name: str, default: bool = True) -> bool:
        try:
            ad = self.config.get("anti_detection_features", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if isinstance(ad, dict) and name in ad:
                return bool(ad.get(name))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f'Error in _anti_detection_flag: {e}')
        return bool(default)

    def safe_event(self, category: str, event: str, data: dict | None = None, level: str = "INFO", trace_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        """Schreibt ein strukturiertes Event in das JSONL-Log und die Konsole.

        - category: log category (e.g., TWEETS, MEME)
        - event: short machine-friendly event name
        - data: optional dictionary with details (selectors, counts, timings)
        - level: INFO|DEBUG|WARNING|ERROR
        - trace_id: optional correlation id for multi-step flows
        """
        try:
            extra = {'category': category, 'event': event}
            if trace_id:
                extra['trace_id'] = trace_id
            if data is not None:
                extra['data'] = data
            lvl = getattr(logging, level.upper(), logging.INFO)
            logger.log(lvl, f"{event}", extra=extra)
            append_control_event(
                "event",
                component=category,
                status="recorded",
                level=level,
                message=event,
                metrics=data or {},
                context={"trace_id": trace_id or ""},
            )
        except Exception:
            # Fallback to plain log
            self.safe_log(f"{event} {data if data is not None else ''}", category, level)

    # Menschliche Interaktion: kleine Hilfsfunktionen
    def _slow_factor(self) -> float:
        try:
            cfg = self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(cfg, dict):
                return 1.0
            factor = float(cfg.get('slow_factor', 1.0))
            profile = str(cfg.get("speed_profile", "safe")).strip().lower()

            if cfg.get('slow_mode'):
                # Wenn slow_mode aktiv ist, min. 1.6x
                factor = max(factor, 1.6)

            # Optional: speed profiles allow safe tuning without code edits.
            # safe: keep behavior as configured
            # balanced: ~10% faster overall, still human
            # fast: ~20% faster, still respects min 1.0
            if profile == "balanced":
                factor *= 0.9
            elif profile == "fast":
                factor *= 0.8
            return max(1.0, min(factor, 4.0))
        except Exception:
            return 1.0

    def human_pause(self, a=0.8, b=1.6):
        try:
            f = self._slow_factor()
            if not self._anti_detection_flag("random_delays", True):
                t = ((a + b) / 2.0) * f
            elif self._anti_detection_flag("natural_timing_distribution", True):
                t = random.triangular(a * f, b * f, ((a + b) / 2.0) * f)
            else:
                t = random.uniform(a * f, b * f)

            # Micro-Pause: 22% Chance auf zusaetzliche kurze Pause (simuliert Nachdenken)
            if self._anti_detection_flag("micro_pauses_enabled", True):
                chance = float(self.config.get("anti_detection_features", {}).get("micro_pause_chance", 0.22))
                if random.random() < chance:
                    micro = random.triangular(0.3, 2.5, 0.8)
                    t += micro

            # Think-Time: Bei laengeren Aktionen (a > 3s) simuliere menschliches Zoegern
            if a >= 3.0 and random.random() < 0.15:
                think = random.triangular(1.0, 4.0, 2.0)
                t += think

            time.sleep(t)
        except Exception:
            time.sleep(random.uniform(0.6, 1.2))

    def human_scroll_jitter(self):
        try:
            if self._anti_detection_flag("varied_interaction_patterns", True) and random.random() < 0.08:
                dy = -random.randint(30, 80)
            else:
                dy = random.randint(80, 250)
            self.driver.execute_script(
                f"window.scrollBy({{top: {dy}, behavior: 'smooth'}});"
            )
            self.human_pause(1.5, 3.5)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Scroll-Fehler: {e}")

    def _base_interval_minutes(self, key: str, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
        """Liest ein Basisintervall aus activity_intervals und bildet ggf. den Mittelwert."""
        try:
            ai = self.config.get('activity_intervals', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            val = ai.get(key)
            if isinstance(val, (int, float)):
                base = float(val)
            elif isinstance(val, list) and val:
                nums = [float(x) for x in val if isinstance(x, (int, float))]
                base = (sum(nums) / len(nums)) if nums else float(default)
            else:
                base = float(default)

            safety_cfg = self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(safety_cfg, dict):
                safety_cfg = {}
            mins = safety_cfg.get("min_activity_intervals_minutes", {}) if isinstance(safety_cfg, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(mins, dict):
                mins = {}
            fallback_mins = {
                "like_burst_minutes": 12.0,
                "comment_interval_minutes": 40.0,
                "tweet_interval_minutes": 60.0,
                "retweet_interval_minutes": 60.0,
            }
            min_val = mins.get(key, fallback_mins.get(key, 0.0))
            try:
                min_val = float(min_val)
            except Exception:
                min_val = float(fallback_mins.get(key, 0.0))
            if min_val > 0:
                base = max(base, min_val)
            return float(base)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return float(default)

    def _record_night_idle(self) -> None:
        """Signal a bounded intentional pause without authorizing any action."""
        try:
            from core.control_events import append_control_event
            append_control_event(
                "night_idle", component="session", status="paused",
                context={"watchdog_session_id": os.environ.get("BOT_WATCHDOG_SESSION_ID", "")},
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _mark_activity(self, category: str = "SESSION") -> None:
        """Update last_action_time when a real action succeeds (no-op if it fails)."""
        try:
            self.last_action_time = datetime.now()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _refresh_brain_quality_brief(self) -> None:
        """Replace, never retain, a prior hint when evidence disappears."""
        self._brain_topic_hint = None
        self._brain_topic_evidence = None
        self._brain_avoid_reasons = []
        self._brain_preferred_mode = "balanced"
        try:
            brain = getattr(self, "session_brain", None)
            if brain is None:
                return
            brief = brain.get_tweet_quality_brief()
            hint = brief.get("best_topic")
            if isinstance(hint, str) and hint.strip():
                self._brain_topic_hint = hint
                evidence = brief.get("reach_evidence")
                if isinstance(evidence, dict):
                    self._brain_topic_evidence = {**evidence, "recommended_topic": hint}
            self._brain_avoid_reasons = brief.get("avoid_reasons", [])
            self._brain_preferred_mode = brief.get("preferred_mode", "balanced")
            planner = getattr(self, "session_planner", None)
            if planner is not None:
                self.session_plan = planner.revise_reach_plan(
                    getattr(self, "session_plan", None), self._brain_topic_hint,
                    brief.get("reach_evidence"),
                )
        except Exception:
            self._brain_topic_hint = None
            self._brain_topic_evidence = None

    def _record_reach_topic_choice(self, source: str, requested: str, selected, *, evidence=None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Trace actual topic selection, not successful generation/publication."""
        if evidence is None:
            evidence = getattr(self, "_brain_topic_evidence", None)
        self._brain_topic_evidence = None
        if not isinstance(evidence, dict) or evidence.get("recommended_topic") != requested:
            return
        try:
            from uuid import uuid4
            choice_id = "reach:" + uuid4().hex
            append_control_event(
                "reach_topic_choice", component="LEARNING",
                status="applied" if selected == requested else "replaced",
                context={
                    **evidence, "source": source, "selected_topic": selected,
                    "reach_choice_id": choice_id,
                    "plan_id": (getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("plan_id"),
                },
            )
            return choice_id
        except Exception:
            # Telemetry failures do not alter the already chosen content topic.
            pass

    def _record_execution_signal(
        self,
        action: str,
        success: bool,
        *,
        reason: str = "",
        context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        outcome_status: Optional[str] = None,
        confirmed_delta: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Persist live execution signals for governance and review."""
        normalized = str(action or "").strip().lower()
        if not normalized:
            return
        now = datetime.now()
        try:
            append_control_event(
                "execution",
                component="EXECUTION",
                status=("success" if outcome_status == "confirmed" else "failure" if outcome_status == "failed" else outcome_status) if outcome_status else ("success" if success else "failure"),
                level="INFO" if success else "WARNING",
                message=normalized,
                metrics={
                    "action": normalized,
                    "success": (bool(success) if outcome_status in (None, "confirmed", "failed") else None),
                    "outcome_status": outcome_status or "reported_unverified",
                    **({"confirmed_delta": confirmed_delta} if confirmed_delta is not None else {}),
                    "tweets_posted": int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0),
                    "likes_given": int(getattr(self, "likes_given", 0) or 0),
                    "comments_posted": int(getattr(self, "comments_posted", 0) or 0),
                    "retweets_posted": int(getattr(self, "retweets_posted", 0) or 0),
                },
                context={
                    "reason": str(reason or ""),
                    "mission_objective": str(((getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {}).get("objective", "")),
                    **dict(context or {}),
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            governor = getattr(self, "execution_governor", None)
            if governor is not None:
                governor.record(normalized, bool(success), when=now, reason=reason)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _record_scheduler_action_result(
        self, action: str, success: bool, *, reason: str = "",
        producer: str = "", confirmed_delta: Optional[int] = None,
        learning_action: str = "", duration: float = 0, stuck_detector: Any = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Retain uncertain/abstained attempts without teaching fake failures."""
        if action == "like" and getattr(self, "_last_like_attempt_uncertain", False):
            status = "unknown"
        elif action == "like" and success:
            status = "confirmed"
        elif action == "like" and (getattr(self, "_last_like_attempt_stopped", False) or reason == "no_likes_found"):
            status = "skipped"
        elif action == "reply":
            field = "_last_quote_outcome_status" if producer == "quote" else "_last_reply_outcome_status"
            status = getattr(self, field, "failed")
        else:
            status = "confirmed" if success else "failed"
        if status not in {"confirmed", "unknown", "rejected", "skipped", "safety_hold", "failed"}:
            status = "unknown"
        if status == "skipped" and not account_actions_allowed(self):
            status = "safety_hold"
        known = status in {"confirmed", "failed"}
        known_success = status == "confirmed"
        typed_reason = reason if known else status
        if known:
            self._record_health_action(action, known_success)
            if stuck_detector:
                try:
                    if known_success:
                        stuck_detector.record_success(action)
                    else:
                        stuck_detector.record_failure(action)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if learning_action:
                try:
                    self.learning_system.record_action_result(learning_action, learning_action, known_success, duration, {})
                except Exception:
                    pass
        brain = getattr(self, "session_brain", None)
        if brain:
            try:
                brain.record_action(action, known_success, reason=typed_reason, outcome_status=status)
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._record_execution_signal(
            action, known_success, reason=typed_reason, outcome_status=status,
            confirmed_delta=confirmed_delta,
        )
        return status

    def _record_health_action(self, action: str, success: bool) -> None:
        """Feed action outcomes into the rolling self-healing monitor."""
        monitor = getattr(self, "health_monitor", None)
        if monitor is None:
            return
        try:
            if success:
                monitor.on_post_success(action)
            else:
                monitor.on_post_failure(action)
        except Exception:
            # Health telemetry must never affect the guarded action loop.
            pass

    def _record_action_timestamp(self, action_type: str, when: Optional[datetime] = None) -> None:
        try:
            t = (action_type or "").strip().lower()
            if not t:
                return
            now = when or datetime.now()
            window = now - timedelta(hours=1)
            kept = [x for x in self._action_timestamps.get(t, []) if x >= window]
            kept.append(now)
            self._action_timestamps[t] = kept
        except Exception:
            return

    def _in_active_hours(self, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> bool:
        """F84 (2026-05-28): True wenn innerhalb menschlicher Aktivzeiten.

        Default 07:00-23:00 mit tages-stabilem Jitter (+/- jitter_minutes), damit
        die Fenster-Grenzen nicht jeden Tag exakt gleich sind (Bot-Tell). Ein Mensch
        postet nicht gleichmaessig um 2-6 Uhr frueh — genau das vermeidet diese Gate.
        Config: config['human_hours'] = {enabled, start_hour, end_hour, jitter_minutes}.
        """
        try:
            cfg = self.config.get("human_hours", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(cfg, dict):
                cfg = {}
            if not cfg.get("enabled", True):
                return True
            start_h = int(cfg.get("start_hour", 7))
            end_h = int(cfg.get("end_hour", 23))
            jitter_min = int(cfg.get("jitter_minutes", 40))
            now = now or datetime.now()
            import random as _r
            rng = _r.Random(int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d")))  # tages-stabiler Seed
            start_min = start_h * 60 + rng.randint(-jitter_min, jitter_min)
            end_min = end_h * 60 + rng.randint(-jitter_min, jitter_min)
            cur_min = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
            return start_min <= cur_min < end_min
        except Exception:
            return True

    def _action_allowed(self, action_type: str) -> bool:
        """Basic per-hour limiter (configurable via safety_limits.per_hour)."""
        try:
            t = (action_type or "").strip().lower()
            if not t:
                return True
            if t in ("tweet", "comment", "retweet", "like", "follow") and not account_actions_allowed(self):
                return False
            # F84: Ausserhalb menschlicher Aktivzeiten kein Posting/Engagement (Anti-Bot-Tell)
            if t in ("tweet", "comment", "retweet", "like", "follow") and not self._in_active_hours():
                return False
            if t == "tweet" and self._quality_first_enabled():
                if not self._tweet_action_attempt_store().is_allowed(
                    "tweet", self._tweet_action_limit(),
                    history=self._tweet_action_history(),
                    **self._tweet_editorial_limits(),
                ):
                    return False
            safety_cfg = self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(safety_cfg, dict):
                safety_cfg = {}
            per_hour = safety_cfg.get("per_hour", {}) if isinstance(safety_cfg, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(per_hour, dict):
                per_hour = {}
            default_limits = {"tweet": 2, "comment": 12, "retweet": 2, "like": 10, "follow": 10}
            limit = per_hour.get(t, default_limits.get(t))
            if limit is None:
                return True
            try:
                limit_i = int(limit)
            except Exception:
                limit_i = int(default_limits.get(t, 999999))
            if limit_i <= 0:
                return False
            now = datetime.now()
            window = now - timedelta(hours=1)
            if t == "tweet" and not getattr(self, "_tweet_limit_restored", False):
                try:
                    # Restore before the first publishing decision, not from
                    # outcome eligibility: pending posts already consumed budget.
                    history = self.tweet_learner._load(strict=True)["tweets"]
                    restored = []
                    for entry in history:
                        if entry.get("source") in ("reply", "quote_tweet", "quote", "retweet"):
                            continue
                        stamp = float(entry["timestamp"])
                        if not math.isfinite(stamp):
                            raise ValueError("invalid publication timestamp")
                        published = datetime.fromtimestamp(stamp)
                        if published >= window:
                            restored.append(published)
                    self._action_timestamps[t] = sorted(set(
                        self._action_timestamps.get(t, []) + restored
                    ))
                    self._tweet_limit_restored = True
                except Exception as exc:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"Tweet geblockt: Stundenlimit-Historie nicht lesbar ({type(exc).__name__})",
                        "SAFETY", "WARNING",
                    )
                    return False
            recent = [x for x in self._action_timestamps.get(t, []) if x >= window]
            self._action_timestamps[t] = recent
            return len(recent) < limit_i
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Aktion geblockt: Sicherheitsprüfung fehlgeschlagen ({type(exc).__name__})",
                    "SAFETY", "WARNING",
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            return False

    def _apply_safe_session_limits(self, likes: int, comments: int, tweets: int, retweets: int):
        """Clamp session targets to safer maxima unless explicitly disabled."""
        safety_cfg = self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
        if not isinstance(safety_cfg, dict):
            safety_cfg = {}
        if bool(safety_cfg.get("disable_session_clamps", False)):
            return likes, comments, tweets, retweets

        caps = safety_cfg.get("session_caps", {})
        if not isinstance(caps, dict):
            caps = {}
        hard_defaults = {"likes": 60, "comments": 6, "tweets": 4, "retweets": 6}

        def _cap(name: str, val: int) -> int:
            limit = caps.get(name, hard_defaults.get(name))
            try:
                limit = int(limit)
            except Exception:
                limit = int(hard_defaults.get(name, 0))
            if limit <= 0:
                return 0
            return int(min(int(val), limit))

        likes2 = _cap("likes", likes)
        comments2 = _cap("comments", comments)
        tweets2 = _cap("tweets", tweets)
        retweets2 = _cap("retweets", retweets)
        if (likes2, comments2, tweets2, retweets2) != (likes, comments, tweets, retweets):
            self.safe_log(
                f"Safety clamps aktiv: Ziele reduziert auf {tweets2} Tweets, {likes2} Likes, {comments2} Kommentare, {retweets2} Retweets",
                "SAFETY",
                "WARNING",
            )
        return likes2, comments2, tweets2, retweets2

    def _reach_goal_missed(self, elapsed_fraction, tweets_target, tweet_timer_ready, threshold: float = 0.60) -> bool:
        """[REACH] Standalone-Tweet-Budget droht zu verfallen?

        Reichweite kommt fast nur ueber Standalone-Posts (Replies sind vom X-Algo
        gedeckelt). Der Standalone-Cap ist 3 (session_caps.tweets), aber gemessen
        wird nur ~1 Tweet/Session gepostet (tweet_share 0.07-0.17, brain_memory
        reach 2026-06/07), weil action_order=["reply","tweet",...] plus
        single_action_per_tick + Reply-First-Lock jeden Tick mit einem Reply
        abbricht, bevor "tweet" erreicht wird. Die bestehende Tweet-Timer-Garantie
        (bot.py:6929) ist wirkungslos, weil min_tweets_guarantee in der Config auf 0
        steht (_tweets_behind wird nie True). Dieser Helper knuepft daher an das
        echte tweets_target an: Ist die Session schon >= threshold fortgeschritten,
        das Tweet-Budget noch offen und der Tweet-Timer bereit, gibt er True zurueck
        -> der Aufrufer zieht "tweet" vor "reply", damit das Budget nicht verfaellt.
        """
        if self._quality_first_enabled():
            return False
        try:
            if not tweet_timer_ready:
                return False
            target = int(tweets_target or 0)
            if target <= 0:
                return False
            if int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0) >= target:
                return False
            return float(elapsed_fraction) >= float(threshold)
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _is_recent_duplicate_post(self, text: str) -> bool:
        try:
            return bool(self._recent_posts.is_duplicate(text))
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _remember_posted_text(self, text: str) -> None:
        try:
            self._recent_posts.remember(text)
            self._recent_posts.save(self._posted_text_cache_path)
        except Exception:
            return

    def human_hover(self, element):
        try:
            ActionChains(self.driver).move_to_element(element).pause(random.uniform(0.2, 0.6)).perform()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def type_text_human(self, element, text):
        self._typing_active = True
        try:
            try:
                td = float(self.config.get('typing_delay', 0.09)) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else 0.09
            except Exception:
                td = 0.09

            # Playwright fast-path: focus once, then type via keyboard with delay.
            # Avoids the adapter's per-character click which garbles text on X.
            page = getattr(self, 'page', None)
            if page:
                try:
                    # Focus element once
                    if hasattr(element, '_locator') and element._locator:
                        element._locator.click(timeout=3000)
                    elif hasattr(element, '_handle') and element._handle:
                        element._handle.click()
                    else:
                        element.send_keys("")  # fallback focus

                    # Type with built-in per-character delay (ms)
                    delay_ms = int(td * 1000)
                    if self._anti_detection_flag("varied_interaction_patterns", True):
                        delay_ms = int(td * 1000 * random.uniform(0.75, 1.35))
                    page.keyboard.type(text, delay=max(20, delay_ms))
                    return
                except Exception:
                    pass  # fall through to character-by-character

            # Fallback: character-by-character (legacy/non-Playwright)
            for ch in text:
                if self._anti_detection_flag("varied_interaction_patterns", True):
                    td_eff = max(0.02, td * random.uniform(0.75, 1.35))
                else:
                    td_eff = td
                try:
                    element.send_keys(ch)
                except Exception:
                    self.human_pause(0.05, 0.12)
                    try:
                        element.send_keys(ch)
                    except Exception:
                        break
                self.human_pause(max(0.02, td_eff * 0.5), td_eff * 1.6)
        finally:
            self._typing_active = False

    # normalize_search_keyword: via BotUtils (bot_modules/utils.py) in MRO


    def get_next_manual_tweet(self):
        """Gibt den naechsten manuellen Tweet zurueck (erweitert mit Video- und Bild-Support)."""
        if not self.manual_tweets:
            return None
            
        if self.manual_tweet_index >= len(self.manual_tweets):
            self.manual_tweet_index = 0
            self.safe_log("Alle manuellen Tweets verwendet - starte von vorne", "TWEETS", "INFO")
        
        tweet_data = self.manual_tweets[self.manual_tweet_index]
        self.manual_tweet_index += 1
        return tweet_data


    # Browser-Methoden (check_browser_connection, _check_browser_connection_safe,
    # reconnect_browser, _recover_invalid_session, ensure_browser_connection):
    # via SessionManager (bot_modules/session.py) in MRO - Playwright-native.

    def _page_render_diagnostics(self) -> tuple:
        """Liefert (testid_count, title) der aktuellen Seite robust.

        Wiederverwendet die F49-Diagnose-Signale. Gibt (-1, "") zurueck
        wenn die Werte nicht ermittelbar sind (z.B. Browser tot) — ein
        Wert von -1 wird vom Blank-Check NICHT als 'leer' gewertet, damit
        Verbindungsfehler nicht mit einer leeren Seite verwechselt werden.
        """
        testid_count = -1
        title = ""
        try:
            testid_count = int(self.driver.execute_script(
                "return document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid]').length;"
            ) or 0)
        except Exception:
            testid_count = -1
        try:
            title = (self.driver.execute_script("return document.title || '';") or "")
        except Exception:
            title = ""
        return testid_count, title

    def _page_is_blank_or_unrendered(self, testid_count: int = None, title: str = None) -> bool:
        """Erkennt eine KOMPLETT leere / nicht-gerenderte Seite.

        Belegt durch F49-Diagnose: bei ALLEN 116 Composer-Fehlern war
        testid_count=0 UND title leer — also eine leere X-Fehler-/Rate-Limit-
        Seite, KEIN Selektor-Drift. Genau dieser Zustand wird hier hart von
        'Element nicht gefunden' (Seite gerendert, aber Composer-Selektor
        passt nicht) unterschieden.

        Heuristik: nur als 'leer' werten, wenn die Werte tatsaechlich gemessen
        wurden (>= 0) und praktisch nichts gerendert ist. testid_count == -1
        (Messung fehlgeschlagen) zaehlt NICHT als leer.
        """
        if testid_count is None or title is None:
            testid_count, title = self._page_render_diagnostics()
        # X.com rendert im Normalbetrieb dutzende data-testid-Knoten + Titel.
        # 'Leer' = quasi nichts gerendert. Schwelle bewusst niedrig (<=2),
        # damit nur echte Leerseiten getroffen werden.
        very_few_testids = (0 <= testid_count <= 2)
        empty_title = (len(title.strip()) == 0)
        return very_few_testids and empty_title

    def _recover_blank_page(self, max_retries: int = 3) -> bool:
        """Reload + eskalierendes Backoff solange die Seite leer/nicht gerendert ist.

        Vorgehen je Versuch: page.reload(networkidle) -> kurze Wartezeit ->
        erneute Render-Diagnose. Backoff eskaliert (5s, 10s, 20s, ...), damit
        wir X bei einer Rate-Limit-/Fehlerseite NICHT haemmern. Gibt True
        zurueck, sobald die Seite gerendert ist, sonst False nach max_retries.
        """
        for attempt in range(1, int(max_retries) + 1):
            testid_count, title = self._page_render_diagnostics()
            if not self._page_is_blank_or_unrendered(testid_count, title):
                if attempt > 1:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[RENDER] Seite wieder gerendert (testid_count={testid_count}) "
                        f"nach {attempt - 1} Reload(s)",
                        "RENDER", "INFO",
                    )
                return True
            backoff = min(60, 5 * (2 ** (attempt - 1)))
            self.safe_log(
                f"[RENDER] Leere/nicht-gerenderte Seite erkannt "
                f"(testid_count={testid_count} title={title!r}) — Reload {attempt}/{max_retries}, "
                f"danach {backoff}s Backoff",
                "RENDER", "WARNING",
            )
            try:
                # networkidle: warte bis X die App tatsaechlich gerendert hat,
                # nicht nur bis DOMContentLoaded der leeren Shell.
                self.driver.refresh(wait_until="networkidle")
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"[RENDER] Reload-Fehler: {e}", "RENDER", "DEBUG")
                try:
                    self.driver.refresh()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            time.sleep(backoff)
        # Letzte Messung nach dem finalen Backoff
        if not self._page_is_blank_or_unrendered():
            return True
        self.safe_log(
            f"[RENDER] Seite blieb nach {max_retries} Reloads leer — Composer-Suche uebersprungen",
            "RENDER", "ERROR",
        )
        return False

    def dynamic_find_tweet_input_field(self):
        """OPTIMIERTE Tweet-Eingabefeld-Suche (erweitert mit Metriken)."""
        start_time = time.time()

        # RENDER-HANG-FIX (2026-06-07): Bevor wir nach dem Composer suchen,
        # hart unterscheiden zwischen "Seite leer/nicht gerendert" (X-Rate-Limit-/
        # Fehlerseite) und "Element nicht gefunden". Belegt durch F49: alle 116
        # Composer-Fehler hatten testid_count=0 — keine Selektor-Drift, sondern
        # eine leere Seite. Bei leerer Seite erst reload+Backoff, dann erst suchen.
        try:
            if self._page_is_blank_or_unrendered():
                self._recover_blank_page(max_retries=3)
        except Exception as _render_err:
            self.safe_log(f"[RENDER] Blank-Check Fehler: {_render_err}", "RENDER", "DEBUG")

        
        tweet_selectors = [
            "//div[@data-testid='tweetTextarea_0']",
            "//div[@data-testid='tweetText']",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][@data-testid='tweetTextarea_0']",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Post text')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Post-Text')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Post-Text')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'What is happening')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Was ist los')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Post-Text')]",  # Abdeckung neuer deutscher Labels
            "//div[@role='textbox'][@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Post')]",
            "//div[@role='textbox'][@contenteditable='true']",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@class, 'DraftEditor-content')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@class, 'public-DraftEditor-content')]",
            "//div[@contenteditable='true'][contains(@aria-label, 'Tweet')]",
            "//textarea[contains(@placeholder, 'What is happening')]",
            "//textarea[contains(@placeholder, 'Was ist los')]"
        ]
        
        for i, selector in enumerate(tweet_selectors):
            try:
                self.safe_log(f"Teste Tweet-Selektor {i+1}/{len(tweet_selectors)}: {selector}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                
                elements = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                if elements:
                    # Warte kurz auf Clickability, um Interception zu reduzieren
                    try:
                        element = WebDriverWait(self.driver, 3).until(
                            EC.element_to_be_clickable((By.XPATH, selector))
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        element = elements[0]
                    if element and element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled():
                        try:
                            self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({block:'center'});", element)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        # optionaler JS-Fokus um Overlays zu umgehen
                        try:
                            self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].focus();", element)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        duration = time.time() - start_time
                        self.metrics.record_action("find_tweet_field", True, duration)
                        self.safe_log(f"Tweet-Eingabefeld gefunden mit Selektor {i+1}!", "ELEMENTS", "INFO")
                        return element
                    self.safe_log(f"Element gefunden aber nicht interaktiv", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                else:
                    self.safe_log(f"Keine Elemente gefunden", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Selektor {i+1}: {e}", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
                continue
        
        duration = time.time() - start_time
        self.metrics.record_action("find_tweet_field", False, duration)
        self.safe_log("KEIN TWEET-EINGABEFELD GEFUNDEN!", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
        # F49 (2026-05-19): Diagnostik-Snapshot bei Selektor-Versagen.
        # H99: X.com-DOM-Drift vermutet, aber unbekannt was statt Composer angezeigt wird.
        try:
            diag_url = ""
            diag_title = ""
            diag_ce_count = -1
            diag_testid_count = -1
            diag_body_snippet = ""
            diag_login_indicator = False
            try:
                diag_url = self.driver.current_url or ""
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                diag_title = self.driver.execute_script("return document.title || '';") or ""
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                diag_ce_count = int(self.driver.execute_script(
                    "return document.querySelectorAll(\"[contenteditable='true']\").length;"
                ) or 0)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                diag_testid_count = int(self.driver.execute_script(
                    "return document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid]').length;"
                ) or 0)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                diag_body_snippet = (self.driver.execute_script(
                    "var b=document.body; return b? (b.innerText||'').slice(0,400):'';"
                ) or "").replace("\n", " | ")
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                diag_login_indicator = bool(self.driver.execute_script(
                    "var t=(document.body&&document.body.innerText||'').toLowerCase();"
                    "return t.indexOf('sign in')>=0 || t.indexOf('anmelden')>=0 "
                    "|| t.indexOf('log in')>=0 || t.indexOf('einloggen')>=0;"
                ))
            except Exception:
                pass
            # Harte Klassifikation: leere Seite vs. echtes Selektor-Versagen.
            page_blank = self._page_is_blank_or_unrendered(diag_testid_count, diag_title)
            classification = "PAGE_BLANK" if page_blank else "ELEMENT_NOT_FOUND"
            self.safe_log(
                f"[F49 DIAG] composer_missing class={classification} url={diag_url!r} title={diag_title!r} "
                f"ce_count={diag_ce_count} testid_count={diag_testid_count} "
                f"login_indicator={diag_login_indicator} body400={diag_body_snippet!r}",
                "ELEMENTS",
                "WARNING",
            )
        except Exception as diag_err:
            try:
                self.safe_log(f"[F49 DIAG] snapshot failed: {diag_err}", "ELEMENTS", "WARNING")
            except Exception:
                pass
        return None

    def ensure_composer_ready(self, navigate_to_home: bool = True):
        """Stellt sicher, dass der Composer offen und fokussierbar ist."""
        try:
            # Sicherstellen, dass wir auf einer Composer-Seite sind
            cur = ""
            try:
                cur = self.driver.current_url or ""
            except Exception:
                pass
            if navigate_to_home and ("/compose" not in cur and "/home" not in cur):
                try:
                    self.driver.get("https://x.com/compose/post")
                    time.sleep(2.5)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            selectors = [
                "//div[@data-testid='tweetTextarea_0']",
                "//div[@data-testid='tweetText']",
                "//div[@role='textbox'][@contenteditable='true']",
            ]
            combined = f"({selectors[0]}|{selectors[1]}|{selectors[2]})"
            target = None
            try:
                target = WebDriverWait(self.driver, 6).until(
                    EC.element_to_be_clickable((By.XPATH, combined))
                )
            except Exception:
                # Fallback: versuche erneut per normaler Suche
                for xp in selectors:
                    try:
                        els = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, xp)
                        target = next((el for el in els if el.is_displayed() and el.is_enabled()), None)
                        if target:
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue

            if not target:
                self.safe_log("Composer nicht bereit (Textbox fehlt)", "ELEMENTS", "WARNING")
                return None

            try:
                self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({block:'center'});", target)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                target.click()
            except Exception:
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].focus(); arguments[0].click();", target)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            return target
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"ensure_composer_ready Fehler: {e}", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
            return None
    
    def dynamic_find_reply_button(self, max_wait_seconds=15):
        """OPTIMIERTE Reply-Button-Suche (erweitert mit Metriken)."""
        try:
            rbs = self.config.get("reply_button_settings", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if isinstance(rbs, dict):
                if isinstance(rbs.get("max_wait_seconds"), (int, float)):
                    max_wait_seconds = float(rbs.get("max_wait_seconds"))
                dynamic_detection = bool(rbs.get("dynamic_detection", True))
            else:
                dynamic_detection = True
        except Exception:
            dynamic_detection = True

        if not self._flag("reply_button_optimization", True):
            try:
                elements = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//button[@data-testid='tweetButtonInline']")
                for element in elements or []:
                    if element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled():
                        return element
            except Exception:
                return None

        start_time = time.time()
        self.safe_log("STARTE OPTIMIERTE REPLY-BUTTON-SUCHE...", "ELEMENTS", "INFO")

        if not dynamic_detection:
            try:
                for selector in self.reply_button_selectors:
                    els = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                    for element in els or []:
                        if element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled():
                            return element
            except Exception:
                return None
            return None

        while (time.time() - start_time) < max_wait_seconds:
            for i, selector in enumerate(self.reply_button_selectors):
                try:
                    self.safe_log(f"Teste Reply-Selektor {i+1}/{len(self.reply_button_selectors)}: {selector}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                    
                    elements = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                    if elements:
                        for element in elements:
                            try:
                                if element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled():
                                    aria_label = element.get_attribute("aria-label") or ""
                                    text_content = element.text or ""
                                    
                                    is_reply_button = (
                                        "reply" in aria_label.lower() or
                                        "antwort" in aria_label.lower() or
                                        "post" in aria_label.lower() or
                                        "tweet" in aria_label.lower() or
                                        "reply" in text_content.lower() or
                                        "post" in text_content.lower() or
                                        "antwort" in text_content.lower()
                                    )
                                    
                                    if is_reply_button:
                                        duration = time.time() - start_time
                                        self.metrics.record_action("find_reply_button", True, duration)
                                        self.safe_log(f"REPLY-BUTTON GEFUNDEN mit Selektor {i+1}!", "ELEMENTS", "INFO")
                                        self.safe_log(f"Aria-Label: {aria_label}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                                        self.safe_log(f"Text: {text_content}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                                        return element
                                    else:
                                        self.safe_log(f"Element gefunden aber kein Reply-Button", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                            except Exception:
                                continue
                    else:
                        self.safe_log(f"Keine Elemente gefunden", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Reply-Selektor {i+1}: {e}", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
                    continue
            
            self.safe_log(f"Warte 2s und versuche erneut... ({int(time.time() - start_time)}s/{max_wait_seconds}s)", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
            self.human_pause(1.2, 2.0)
        
        duration = time.time() - start_time
        self.metrics.record_action("find_reply_button", False, duration)
        self.safe_log("REPLY-BUTTON NICHT GEFUNDEN nach allen Versuchen!", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
        return None
    
    def stale_element_safe_click(self, element_finder_func, description="Element", max_retries=2):
        """Stale-Element-sichere Klick-Funktion (erweitert mit Metriken)."""
        if not self._flag("stale_element_recovery", True) or not self._enhanced_flag("error_recovery", True):
            try:
                element = element_finder_func()
                if not element:
                    return False
                ok = self.robust_click_element(element, description)
                try:
                    self.metrics.record_action("stale_safe_click", bool(ok), 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return bool(ok)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    self.metrics.record_action("stale_safe_click", False, 0.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return False

        start_time = time.time()
        
        for retry in range(max_retries):
            try:
                self.safe_log(f"Versuche {description} zu klicken (Versuch {retry+1}/{max_retries})...", "ACTIONS", "INFO")
                
                element = element_finder_func()
                if not element:
                    self.safe_log(f"{description} nicht gefunden", "ACTIONS", "WARNING")
                    return False
                
                success = self.robust_click_element(element, description)
                if success:
                    duration = time.time() - start_time
                    self.metrics.record_action("stale_safe_click", True, duration)
                    return True
                
            except StaleElementReferenceException:
                self.safe_log(f"Stale Element bei {description} - Wiederhole...", "ACTIONS", "WARNING")
                self.metrics.record_stale_recovery()
                self.human_pause(2.5, 4.5)
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei {description}: {e}", "ACTIONS", "ERROR")
                if retry < max_retries - 1:
                    self.human_pause(1.2, 2.0)
                    continue
                
                duration = time.time() - start_time
                self.metrics.record_action("stale_safe_click", False, duration)
                return False
        
        duration = time.time() - start_time
        self.metrics.record_action("stale_safe_click", False, duration)
        self.safe_log(f"{description} konnte nach {max_retries} Versuchen nicht geklickt werden", "ACTIONS", "ERROR")
        return False
    
    def robust_click_element(self, element, description="Element"):
        """Robuste Element-Klick-Funktion mit verbesserter Click-Interception-Behandlung."""
        try:
            self.safe_log(f"Versuche {description} zu klicken...", "ACTIONS", "DEBUG")
            if self._anti_detection_flag("human_like_pauses", True):
                self.human_pause(0.15, 0.55)
            
            # Strategie 1: Normaler Klick
            try:
                try:
                    WebDriverWait(self.driver, 2).until(lambda d: element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled())
                except Exception:
                    pass
                element.click()
                if self._anti_detection_flag("human_like_pauses", True):
                    self.human_pause(0.12, 0.45)
                self.safe_log(f"{description} erfolgreich geklickt (normaler Klick)", "ACTIONS", "INFO")
                return True
            except StaleElementReferenceException:
                raise  # outer stale_element_safe_click retry-loop übernimmt
            except Exception as e:
                # F62 Pre-AW (2026-05-22): Catch broadened. Playwright's Locator.click
                # wirft TimeoutError (nicht ClickInterceptedException) wenn X.com DOM-Overlay
                # den Click blockiert. Strategien 2 (Scroll) + 3 (JS-Click) müssen
                # auch bei Timeout greifen, sonst fällt der ganze Composer-Pfad durch.
                self.safe_log(f"Normaler Klick blockiert: {str(e)[:80]}", "ACTIONS", "DEBUG")

                # Schliesse stoerende Overlays (Suchfeld, etc.)
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("document.activeElement && document.activeElement.blur();")
                    self.driver.execute_script("document.body.click();")
                    time.sleep(0.3)
                except Exception:
                    pass

                # Strategie 2: Scroll + Klick
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({block: 'center'});", element)
                    try:
                        self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollBy(0, -150);")
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    time.sleep(0.5)
                    element.click()
                    self.safe_log(f"{description} erfolgreich geklickt (nach Scroll)", "ACTIONS", "INFO")
                    return True
                except StaleElementReferenceException:
                    raise
                except Exception:
                    # Strategie 3: JavaScript-Klick (letzter Versuch)
                    try:
                        self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].click();", element)
                        self.safe_log(f"{description} erfolgreich geklickt (JavaScript)", "ACTIONS", "INFO")
                        return True
                    except Exception:
                        self.safe_log(f"Alle Klick-Strategien fuer {description} fehlgeschlagen", "ACTIONS", "WARNING")
                        return False
                        
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler beim Klicken auf {description}: {e}", "ACTIONS", "ERROR")
            return False


    
    def dynamic_find_like_buttons(self, do_scroll: bool = True):
        """Dynamische Like-Button-Suche mit optionalem Scroll fuer stabilere Klicks."""
        start_time = time.time()
        if not self._check_browser_connection_safe():
            self.safe_log("Browser-Session nicht verfuegbar ? versuche Wiederherstellung", "ELEMENTS", "WARNING")
            if not self.reconnect_browser():
                self.safe_log("Keine aktive Session fuer Like-Suche", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
                return []

        # Scroll nur beim initialen Scan, um Stale-Refs bei Refreshes zu vermeiden
        if do_scroll:
            self.safe_log("FEINTUNING: Erweiterte Content-Ladung gestartet", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
            prev_count = 0
            for scroll_round in range(6):
                try:
                    # Scroll zum absoluten Ende um Lazy-Loading zu triggern
                    self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                    self.human_pause(2.5, 4.0)
                    # Prüfe ob neue Tweets geladen wurden
                    cur_tweets = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
                    cur_count = len(cur_tweets)
                    if cur_count >= 8:
                        self.safe_log(f"Genug Tweets geladen: {cur_count}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                        break
                    if cur_count == prev_count and scroll_round >= 2:
                        self.safe_log(f"Keine neuen Tweets nach Scroll ({cur_count} total)", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                        break
                    prev_count = cur_count
                except Exception:
                    pass
            # Scroll zurueck zum Start damit Buttons sichtbar und klickbar sind
            try:
                self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
            except Exception:
                pass

        like_selectors = [
            # Primaer: data-testid ist zuverlaessigster Selektor
            "//button[@data-testid='like']",
            "//div[@data-testid='like']",
            # Deutsche UI: aria-label enthaelt 'Gef\u00e4llt mir'
            "//button[contains(@aria-label, 'Gef\u00e4llt mir')]",
            "//div[contains(@aria-label, 'Gef\u00e4llt mir')]",
            # Englische UI Fallback
            "//button[contains(@aria-label, 'Like')]",
            "//div[contains(@aria-label, 'Like')]",
            # Breitere Suche innerhalb von Artikeln
            "//article[@data-testid='tweet']//button[@data-testid='like']",
        ]

        all_like_buttons = []
        for i, selector in enumerate(like_selectors):
            try:
                self.safe_log(f"Teste erweiterten Like-Selektor {i+1}/{len(like_selectors)}", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                elements = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                if elements:
                    self.safe_log(f"{len(elements)} Elemente gefunden", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
                    for element in elements:
                        try:
                            if element.is_displayed() and element.is_enabled():
                                aria_label = element.get_attribute("aria-label") or ""
                                data_testid = element.get_attribute("data-testid") or ""
                                pressed = element.get_attribute("aria-pressed") or "false"
                                if ("Unlike" in aria_label) or ("Liked" in aria_label) or (data_testid == 'unlike') or (pressed == 'true'):
                                    continue
                                all_like_buttons.append(element)
                        except Exception:
                            continue
                else:
                    self.safe_log("Keine Elemente gefunden", "ELEMENTS", "DEBUG")
            except InvalidSessionIdException:
                self.safe_log("KRITISCHER FEHLER: Browser-Session verloren! Breche Suche ab.", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Like-Selektor {i+1}: {e}", "ELEMENTS", "ERROR")
                continue

        unique_buttons = []
        seen_locations = set()
        for button in all_like_buttons:
            try:
                location = button.location
                location_key = (round(location['x']), round(location['y']))
                if location_key not in seen_locations:
                    seen_locations.add(location_key)
                    unique_buttons.append(button)
            except Exception:
                unique_buttons.append(button)

        duration = time.time() - start_time
        self.metrics.record_action("find_like_buttons", len(unique_buttons) > 0, duration)
        self.safe_log(f"RESULT: {len(unique_buttons)} OPTIMIERTE LIKE-BUTTONS GEFUNDEN!", "ELEMENTS", "INFO")
        return unique_buttons

    def _detect_rate_limit_or_block(self, *, fail_closed=False) -> bool:
        """Erkennt einfache Rate-Limit-/Block-Signale in der UI.

        GAP-004: Now increments counter and logs detection for telemetry.
        """
        try:
            current_url = getattr(self.driver, "current_url", None)
            if not isinstance(current_url, str) or not current_url:
                return bool(fail_closed)
            parsed_url = urlsplit(current_url)
            platform_hosts = {"x.com", "www.x.com", "twitter.com", "www.twitter.com"}
            if parsed_url.scheme != "https" or parsed_url.hostname not in platform_hosts:
                return bool(fail_closed)
            url_reason = account_signal_from_url(current_url)
            if url_reason:
                if not record_account_hold(self, url_reason):
                    return True
                self._rate_limit_detections += 1
                self._rate_limit_timestamps.append(time.time())
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Account-Sperrsignal erkannt: {url_reason}",
                    "SAFETY", "WARNING",
                )
                return True
            transient = re.compile(
                r"\b(something\s+went\s+wrong|etwas\s+ist\s+schiefgelaufen|"
                r"try\s+again|erneut\s+versuchen)\b",
                re.IGNORECASE,
            )
            account_nodes = []
            for sel in ("//div[@role='alert']", "//div[contains(@class,'toast')]"):
                account_nodes.extend(self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel) or [])
            for node in account_nodes:
                try:
                    if not node.is_displayed():
                        continue
                    if node.find_elements(
                        By.XPATH,
                        "ancestor::article | self::article | ancestor::*[@data-testid='quoteTweet'] | self::*[@data-testid='quoteTweet']",
                    ):
                        continue
                except Exception:
                    # A node whose visibility/ownership cannot be established
                    # is not evidence for a durable account hold.
                    continue
                text = str(getattr(node, "text", "") or "")
                if not text and hasattr(node, "get_attribute"):
                    text = str(node.get_attribute("aria-label") or "")
                if is_rate_limit_message(text):
                    if not record_account_hold(self, "rate_limit"):
                        return True
                    self._rate_limit_detections += 1
                    self._rate_limit_timestamps.append(time.time())
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GAP-004] Rate-limit detected (total: {self._rate_limit_detections})",
                        "SAFETY", "WARNING",
                    )
                    return True
                if transient.search(text):
                    return True
            # Twitter sometimes renders the transient error as a plain text
            # node without role=alert/toast.  Query direct text nodes only:
            # matching an ancestor's full string would mistake tweet content
            # (especially the body) for an error.  Account-owned articles and
            # quote containers are never transient account evidence.
            transient_text_selectors = (
                "//*[contains(translate(normalize-space(text()), 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ', 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'), 'something went wrong')]",
                "//*[contains(translate(normalize-space(text()), 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ', 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'), 'try again')]",
                "//*[contains(translate(normalize-space(text()), 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ', 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'), 'etwas ist schiefgelaufen')]",
                "//*[contains(translate(normalize-space(text()), 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ', 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'), 'erneut versuchen')]",
            )
            seen_transient_nodes = set()
            for sel in transient_text_selectors:
                for node in self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel) or []:
                    marker = id(node)
                    if marker in seen_transient_nodes:
                        continue
                    seen_transient_nodes.add(marker)
                    try:
                        if not node.is_displayed():
                            continue
                        if node.find_elements(
                            By.XPATH,
                            "ancestor::article | self::article | ancestor::*[@data-testid='quoteTweet'] | self::*[@data-testid='quoteTweet']",
                        ):
                            continue
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    text = str(getattr(node, "text", "") or "")
                    if not text and hasattr(node, "get_attribute"):
                        text = str(node.get_attribute("aria-label") or "")
                    if transient.search(text):
                        return True
        except Exception as e:
            if fail_closed:
                return True
            # GAP-002: Log exception instead of silent pass
            self.safe_log(f"[GAP-002] Rate-limit detection error: {e}", "SAFETY", "DEBUG")
        return False

    def _try_recover_error_page(self) -> bool:
        """Versucht eine Twitter-Fehlerseite zu recovern (Erneut versuchen / Reload)."""
        try:
            # Klick auf "Erneut versuchen" Button
            retry_selectors = [
                "//*[text()='Erneut versuchen']",
                "//*[text()='Try again']",
                "//*[text()='Retry']",
            ]
            for sel in retry_selectors:
                btns = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel)
                if btns:
                    self.safe_log("Twitter-Fehlerseite erkannt — klicke 'Erneut versuchen'", "RECOVERY", "WARNING")
                    try:
                        btns[0].click()
                        self.human_pause(4.0, 7.0)
                        # Prüfe ob Tweets jetzt laden
                        tweets = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
                        if tweets:
                            self.safe_log(f"Recovery erfolgreich: {len(tweets)} Tweets geladen", "RECOVERY", "INFO")
                            return True
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            # Fallback: Page reload
            self.safe_log("Retry-Button nicht gefunden — lade Seite neu", "RECOVERY", "WARNING")
            self.driver.execute_script("location.reload();")
            self.human_pause(5.0, 8.0)
            tweets = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
            if tweets:
                self.safe_log(f"Reload erfolgreich: {len(tweets)} Tweets geladen", "RECOVERY", "INFO")
                return True
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Error-Page Recovery fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "RECOVERY", "ERROR")
            return False

    def _rate_limit_cooldown_seconds(self) -> int:
        """Return escalating cooldown based on recent rate-limit detections.

        1st detection: 60s, 2nd within 30min: 300s, 3rd+: 900s.
        Returns 0 when no recent detection.
        """
        now = time.time()
        window = now - 1800  # 30 minutes
        recent = [t for t in self._rate_limit_timestamps if t >= window]
        self._rate_limit_timestamps = recent  # prune old entries
        count = len(recent)
        if count <= 0:
            return 0
        if count == 1:
            return 60
        if count == 2:
            return 300
        return 900

    # _try_smart_follow: geerbt von FollowManager (follow.py:401)

    def _like_action_store(self):
        existing = getattr(self, "_like_store", None)
        if existing is not None:
            return existing
        config = getattr(self, "config", {})
        path = config.get("action_attempts_state_file") if isinstance(config, dict) else None
        if not path:
            path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "state", "action_attempts.json")
        self._like_store = ActionAttemptStore(path)
        return self._like_store

    def _like_target_status_id(self, tweet_element):
        try:
            # The Playwright adapter returns raw (often relative) hrefs.
            # Reuse strict X-permalink validation before parsing identity.
            href = self._quote_target_url(tweet_element) or ""
            _author, status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(href)
            return status_id if re.fullmatch(r"[1-9][0-9]{0,19}", status_id) else ""
        except Exception:
            return ""

    def _like_action_ready(self) -> bool:
        self._last_like_attempt_stopped = True
        try:
            if not self._action_allowed("like") or self._detect_rate_limit_or_block(fail_closed=True):
                return False
            config = getattr(self, "config", {})
            limits = config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(config, dict) else {}
            per_hour = limits.get("per_hour", {}) if isinstance(limits, dict) else {}
            limit = int(per_hour.get("like", 10))
            if limit <= 0:
                return False
            ready = bool(self._like_action_store().is_allowed("like", limit))
            self._last_like_attempt_stopped = not ready
            return ready
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _send_reserved_like(self, button, tweet_element=None) -> bool:
        self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = False
        if not self._like_action_ready():
            return False
        try:
            if not button.is_displayed() or not button.is_enabled():
                return False
            before_pressed = str(button.get_attribute("aria-pressed") or "").lower()
            before_testid = str(button.get_attribute("data-testid") or "").lower()
            before_aria = str(button.get_attribute("aria-label") or "").lower()
            if before_pressed == "true" or before_testid == "unlike" or "unlike" in before_aria:
                return False
        except Exception:
            return False
        status_id = self._like_target_status_id(tweet_element) if tweet_element is not None else ""
        if not status_id:
            self.safe_log("Like geblockt: keine kanonische Zielidentitaet", "LIKES", "WARNING")
            return False
        config = getattr(self, "config", {})
        limits = config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(config, dict) else {}
        per_hour = limits.get("per_hour", {}) if isinstance(limits, dict) else {}
        try:
            limit = int(per_hour.get("like", 10))
            if limit <= 0:
                return False
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            limit = 10
        store = self._like_action_store()
        try:
            reserved = store.reserve("like", status_id, limit, initial_quiet_period=3600)
        except Exception:
            reserved = False
        if not reserved:
            self.safe_log("Like geblockt: Ziel bereits reserviert oder Cap erreicht", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
            return False
        try:
            self._record_action_timestamp("like")
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
                self.safe_log("Like finaler Versand durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                return False
            button.click()
        except Exception as exc:
            self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
            try:
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.safe_log(f"Like-Klick fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "LIKES", "WARNING")
            return False
        try:
            pressed = str(button.get_attribute("aria-pressed") or "").lower()
            testid = str(button.get_attribute("data-testid") or "").lower()
            confirmed = pressed == "true" or testid == "unlike"
        except Exception:
            confirmed = False
        if not confirmed:
            self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
            try:
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return False
        try:
            if self._detect_rate_limit_or_block(fail_closed=True):
                self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
                return False
        except Exception:
            self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
            try:
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return False
        try:
            durable_confirmation = bool(store.mark_confirmed("like", status_id))
        except Exception:
            durable_confirmation = False
        if not durable_confirmation:
            self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = True
            try:
                store.mark_uncertain("like", status_id)
            except Exception:
                pass  # The prior durable reservation still blocks another send.
            self.safe_log("Like-Bestaetigung nicht persistiert; kein Erfolgs-Commit", "LIKES", "WARNING")
            return False
        self.likes_given += 1
        self._mark_activity("LIKE")
        return True

    def like_top_successful_tweets(self, keyword, target_likes):
        """Likt nur sehr erfolgreiche Tweets (Top/Score-basiert)."""
        try:
            if target_likes <= 0:
                return 0
            if not self._like_action_ready():
                return 0
            search_url = f"https://x.com/search?q={self.normalize_search_keyword(keyword).replace(' ', '%20')}&src=typed_query&f=top"
            self.driver.get(search_url)
            self.human_pause(5.0, 9.0)
            tweets = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
            if not tweets:
                return 0
            sf = self.config.get('success_filter', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            min_likes = int(sf.get('min_likes', 250))
            min_views = int(sf.get('min_views', 20000))
            top = self.filter_high_engagement_tweets(tweets[:25], min_likes=min_likes, min_views=min_views)
            if not top:
                return 0
            def _score(t):
                m = self.extract_tweet_engagement(t) or {}
                return m.get('likes', 0) + 2*m.get('retweets', 0) + 3*m.get('comments', 0)
            top.sort(key=_score, reverse=True)
            liked = 0
            for tw in top:
                if liked >= target_likes:
                    break
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center'});", tw)
                    self.human_pause(4.0, 7.0)
                    btn = None
                    for sel in [
                        ".//div[@data-testid='like']",
                        ".//button[@data-testid='like']",
                        ".//div[contains(@aria-label,'Like')]",
                        ".//button[contains(@aria-label,'Like')]",
                    ]:
                        cand = tw.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel)
                        if cand:
                            for b in cand:
                                if b.is_displayed() and b.is_enabled():
                                    aria = b.get_attribute('aria-label') or ''
                                    pressed = b.get_attribute('aria-pressed')
                                    if (not pressed or pressed == 'false') and ('Unlike' not in aria and 'Liked' not in aria):
                                        btn = b
                                        break
                        if btn:
                            break
                    if not btn:
                        continue
                    if self._send_reserved_like(btn, tw):
                        liked += 1
                        self.safe_log(f"Like (Top) {liked}/{target_likes} gesetzt", "LIKES", "INFO")
                        # SMART FOLLOW INTEGRATION
                        self._try_smart_follow(tw)
                    elif self._last_like_attempt_uncertain or self._last_like_attempt_stopped:
                        break
                    
                    self.human_pause(6.0, 10.0)
                except Exception:
                    continue
            return liked
        except InvalidSessionIdException as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Invalid session waehrend Like-Burst: {e}", "LIKES", "ERROR")
            if self._recover_invalid_session():
                return 0
            return 0
        except Exception as e:
