            self.safe_log(f"Fehler in like_top_successful_tweets: {e}", "LIKES", "ERROR")
            return 0

    def connection_resilient_like_burst(self, keyword, target_likes=30):
        """Verbindungs-resiliente Like-Burst-Session (erweitert mit Intelligence)."""
        try:
            self.safe_log(f"STARTE VERBINDUNGS-RESILIENTEN LIKE-BURST FUER: {keyword}", "LIKES", "INFO")
            if not self._like_action_ready():
                return 0
            
            # [BRAIN] Intelligente Timing-Anpassung
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                adaptive_interval = self.intelligence.get_adaptive_timing('like_burst', 3.0)
                self.safe_log(f"[BRAIN] Adaptive Timing: {adaptive_interval:.1f} Minuten", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
            
            if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung konnte nicht wiederhergestellt werden", "LIKES", "ERROR")
                return 0
            
            search_url = f"https://x.com/search?q={self.normalize_search_keyword(keyword).replace(' ', '%20')}&src=typed_query&f=top"
            self.driver.get(search_url)
            self.human_pause(5.0, 9.0)

            # Fehlerseite-Recovery vor Scroll
            if self._detect_rate_limit_or_block():
                self.safe_log("Search rate-limited — Like-Burst fail-closed beendet", "LIKES", "WARNING")
                return 0

            # Scrolle fuer mehr Content (konfigurierbar, mit Early-Stop)
            try:
                scroll_limit = int(self.config.get("like_burst_scrolls", 3))
            except Exception:
                scroll_limit = 3
            scroll_limit = max(0, min(scroll_limit, 8))
            for scroll in range(scroll_limit):
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Scrolle fuer mehr Content ({scroll+1}/{scroll_limit})...",
                    "LIKES",
                    "DEBUG",
                )
                self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                self.human_pause(2.5, 4.5)
                if scroll >= 1:
                    try:
                        tweets_now = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
                        if len(tweets_now) >= 20:
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            
            likes_in_burst = 0

            # Priorisiere sehr erfolgreiche Tweets zuerst (nur Top-Tweets liken)
            try:
                liked_top = self.like_top_successful_tweets(keyword, target_likes)
                likes_in_burst += liked_top
                if getattr(self, "_last_like_attempt_uncertain", False) or getattr(self, "_last_like_attempt_stopped", False):
                    return likes_in_burst
                if likes_in_burst >= target_likes:
                    self.safe_log("Ziel durch sehr erfolgreiche Tweets erreicht", "LIKES", "INFO")
                    return likes_in_burst
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Vorpriorisierung fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "LIKES", "WARNING")

            # Nach like_top_successful_tweets: Seite neu laden fuer frische Tweets
            try:
                search_url_latest = f"https://x.com/search?q={self.normalize_search_keyword(keyword).replace(' ', '%20')}&src=typed_query&f=top"
                self.driver.get(search_url_latest)
                self.human_pause(4.0, 7.0)
                # Aggressiv scrollen um mehr Tweets zu laden
                for _reload_scroll in range(4):
                    self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                    self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)
                self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Filtere Tweets nach Engagement vor dem Liken
            all_tweets = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
            if all_tweets:
                sf = self.config.get('success_filter', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                min_likes = int(sf.get('min_likes', 250))
                min_views = int(sf.get('min_views', 20000))
                high_engagement_tweets = self.filter_high_engagement_tweets(all_tweets[:20], min_likes=min_likes, min_views=min_views)
                if high_engagement_tweets:
                    self.safe_log(f"[ZIEL] {len(high_engagement_tweets)} erfolgreiche Tweets fuer Likes gefunden", "LIKES", "INFO")
                else:
                    self.safe_log("[WARN] Keine erfolgreichen Tweets gefunden - verwende alle verfuegbaren", "LIKES", "WARNING")

            # Like-Batches mit erweiterten Metriken
            for batch in range(3):
                if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                    self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung verloren - ueberspringe Batch", "LIKES", "ERROR")
                    break

                self.safe_log(f"Like-Batch {batch+1}/6...", "LIKES", "INFO")
                
                like_buttons = self.dynamic_find_like_buttons(do_scroll=True)
                
                if not like_buttons:
                    self.safe_log("Keine Like-Buttons gefunden - ueberspringe Batch", "LIKES", "WARNING")
                    continue
                
                batch_likes = 0
                consecutive_errors = 0
                for i, button in enumerate(like_buttons[:2]):  # kleinere Stapel verringern Risiko auf langen Sessions
                    try:
                        if not self._action_allowed("like"):
                            self.safe_log("Like uebersprungen (SAFETY: per-hour Limit erreicht)", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                            break
                        if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                            self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung verloren - beende Like-Burst", "LIKES", "ERROR")
                            break
                        
                        self.safe_log(f"Like {likes_in_burst + 1}...", "LIKES", "DEBUG")
                        
                        tweet_el = None
                        try:
                            tweet_el = button.find_element(By.XPATH, "./ancestor::article")
                        except Exception:
                            tweet_el = None
                        if self._send_reserved_like(button, tweet_el):
                            likes_in_burst += 1
                            batch_likes += 1
                            consecutive_errors = 0
                            
                            # SMART FOLLOW INTEGRATION
                            try:
                                if tweet_el:
                                    self._try_smart_follow(tweet_el)
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                            # Human-like Pause (etwas kuerzer)
                            pause = random.uniform(3.5, 7.0)
                            self.safe_log(f"Pause {pause:.1f}s...", "LIKES", "DEBUG")
                            time.sleep(pause)
                            # Rate-Limit-Check nach Like
                            try:
                                if self._detect_rate_limit_or_block():
                                    return likes_in_burst
                            except Exception as e:
                                self.safe_log(f"Rate-limit check error: {e}", "LIKES", "DEBUG")
                                return likes_in_burst
                        elif self._last_like_attempt_uncertain or self._last_like_attempt_stopped:
                            return likes_in_burst
                        
                        if likes_in_burst >= target_likes:
                            break
                            
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Like {likes_in_burst + 1}: {e}", "LIKES", "ERROR")
                        consecutive_errors += 1
                        if consecutive_errors >= 3:
                            cool = random.uniform(60, 120)
                            self.safe_log(f"Viele Fehler in Folge - kuehle {cool:.0f}s ab", "LIKES", "WARNING")
                            time.sleep(cool)
                            break
                        else:
                            time.sleep(random.uniform(3, 6))
                            continue
                
                self.safe_log(f"Batch {batch+1} abgeschlossen: {batch_likes} Likes", "LIKES", "INFO")
                
                if likes_in_burst >= target_likes:
                    break
                
                time.sleep(random.uniform(20, 40))
            
            self.safe_log(f"VERBINDUNGS-RESILIENTER LIKE-BURST ABGESCHLOSSEN: {likes_in_burst} Likes vergeben!", "LIKES", "INFO")
            return likes_in_burst
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei verbindungs-resilientem Like-Burst: {e}", "LIKES", "ERROR")
            return 0
    

    # Erste extract_tweet_engagement-Definition entfernt (Duplikat, zweite Definition weiter unten ist aktiv)

    def filter_high_engagement_tweets(self, tweet_containers, min_likes=50, min_views=1000):
        """
        Delegiert auf die erweiterte Implementierung aus EngagementManager,
        damit zentrale Schwellenwerte und Fallbacks verwendet werden.
        """
        try:
            try:
                return EngagementManager.filter_high_engagement_tweets(
                    self,
                    tweet_containers,
                    min_likes=min_likes,
                    min_views=min_views,
                )
            except TypeError as te:
                # Backward compatible call if EngagementManager signature changed
                if "unexpected keyword argument" in str(te):
                    return EngagementManager.filter_high_engagement_tweets(self, tweet_containers)
                raise
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei filter_high_engagement_tweets: {e}", "ENGAGEMENT", "ERROR")
            return []

    def generate_intelligent_comment(self, tweet_text: str) -> str:
        """Generate a comment based on tweet sentiment and context (Enhanced with LLM)."""
        # 1 LLM Response (Primary)
        try:
            if hasattr(self, 'llm_responder'):
                llm_response = self.llm_responder.generate_response(tweet_text)
                if not llm_response or not str(llm_response).strip():
                    # Respect responder abstention/rejection; generic templates
                    # must not turn it into another paid regeneration pipeline.
                    return ""
                if llm_response:
                    self.safe_log(f"LLM-Antwort generiert: {llm_response}", "LLM", "DEBUG")
                    return llm_response
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"LLM-Fehler, nutze Fallback: {e}", "LLM", "WARNING")

        # 2 Fallback: reply_guy Templates (43 quality templates from config)
        import random
        try:
            rg_templates = getattr(self, 'comment_templates_reply_guy', None)
            if rg_templates and isinstance(rg_templates, list) and len(rg_templates) > 0:
                return random.choice(rg_templates)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 3 Fallback: Rule-based Logic (Sentiment + Context)
        # Sentiment analysis
        sentiment_analyser = SentimentAnalyzer()
        sentiment, confidence = sentiment_analyser.analyze_sentiment(tweet_text or "")

        # Base comment from sentiment pool
        base_pool = sentiment_analyser.comments.get(sentiment, [])
        if not base_pool:
            base_pool = sentiment_analyser.comments.get('neutral', [])
        base_comment = random.choice(base_pool)

        if not self._intelligence_flag("contextual_comments", True):
            return base_comment

        # Context detection
        try:
            contexts = self.intelligence.analyze_context(tweet_text or "")
        except Exception:
            contexts = ['general']

        # Enrich with context-specific suggestions
        enriched_comments = [base_comment]
        try:
            contextual = self.intelligence.get_contextual_comment(contexts)
            if isinstance(contextual, list):
                enriched_comments.extend(contextual[:2])
            else:
                enriched_comments.append(contextual)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return random.choice(enriched_comments)

    def generate_quote_comment(self, tweet_text: str) -> str:
        """Erzeugt eine praegnante Quote-Formulierung (LLM-Prioritaet, sonst Fallback)."""
        from core.llm_task_budget import BudgetExceeded

        import random as _rnd
        _quote_voices = [
            "Du schreibst Quote-Tweets. Du bist eine Finance-Führungskraft, die auch KI-Tools baut.\nSTIMME: Pragmatisch, zahlengetrieben.\n",
            "Du schreibst Quote-Tweets. Du bist ein Builder, der KI-Automatisierungen ausliefert.\nSTIMME: Praxisnah, auf Auslieferung fokussiert.\n",
            "Du schreibst Quote-Tweets. Du hast echte Praxiserfahrung mit KI und Business.\nSTIMME: Direkter Beobachter, keine Selbstvorstellung.\n",
        ]
        system_prompt = (
            f"{_rnd.choice(_quote_voices)}"
            "TON: Direkt, leicht kontrarisch, null Füllmaterial.\n"
            "REGELN:\n"
            "- Eine scharfe Beobachtung zum zitierten Tweet (5-12 Wörter)\n"
            "- Optional: Datenhoheit, Europa-Tech oder EU-Infrastruktur einordnen, wenn es zum Tweet passt\n"
            "- Keine erfundenen Zahlen, keine Ich-Erlebnisse, keine Case-Studies hinzufügen\n"
            "- Keine Slogans, keine Mission-Statements\n"
            "- NIE mit 'Als CFO' oder 'Als jemand, der' beginnen\n"
            "VERBOTEN (sofortige Ablehnung):\n"
            "- 'wo X auf Y trifft'\n"
            "- 'schärfe deine Fähigkeiten/Edge'\n"
            "- 'hebe dein/e'\n"
            "- 'entfessle dein Potenzial'\n"
            "- 'strategischer Vorteil'\n"
            "- 'tief eintauchen'\n"
            "- 'cutting-edge'\n"
            "- 'next-level'\n"
            "Maximal 20 Wörter. Keine Hashtags. Keine Emojis.\n"
            "SPRACHE: Schreibe IMMER nur auf Deutsch. Antworte nie in anderen Sprachen."
        )
        user_prompt = f"Schreibe einen prägnanten Quote-Tweet als Reaktion auf: '{tweet_text}'."

        try:
            from intelligent_features.llm_responder import check_banned_phrases
            from content.authenticity_gate import authenticity_gate
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quote required check unavailable - fail-closed", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return ""

        def _sanitize_quote(text):
            """Sanitize quote: remove emojis/hashtags, enforce word budget."""
            import re
            text = text.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
            emoji_pattern = re.compile(
                "["
                "\U0001F600-\U0001F64F"
                "\U0001F300-\U0001F5FF"
                "\U0001F680-\U0001F6FF"
                "\U0001F1E0-\U0001F1FF"
                "\U00002700-\U000027BF"
                "\U0001F900-\U0001F9FF"
                "\U00002600-\U000026FF"
                "]",
                flags=re.UNICODE,
            )
            text = emoji_pattern.sub("", text)
            text = re.sub(r"#\S+", "", text)
            words = text.split()
            if len(words) > 20:
                text = " ".join(words[:20])
            return " ".join(text.split())

        try:
            if hasattr(self, 'llm_responder'):
                # Try up to 2 times (single retry on banned phrase)
                for attempt in range(2):
                    llm_response = self.llm_responder.generate_response(user_prompt, context={'system': system_prompt})
                    if not llm_response:
                        continue

                    llm_response = _sanitize_quote(llm_response)

                    # Check banned phrases
                    banned = check_banned_phrases(llm_response)
                    if banned:
                        excerpt = llm_response[:80] + "..." if len(llm_response) > 80 else llm_response
                        self.safe_log(
                            f'[AUDIT] WARNING: BANNED_PHRASE DROP: phrase="{banned}", source="QUOTE_LLM"',
                            "AUDIT",
                            "WARNING",
                        )
                        self.safe_log(f"[AUDIT] DEBUG: Excerpt: [{excerpt}]", "AUDIT", "DEBUG")
                        continue

                    ok_auth, auth_reason = authenticity_gate(
                        llm_response, source="quote", original_text=tweet_text or ""
                    )
                    if not ok_auth:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[AUDIT] Quote DROP: authenticity={auth_reason}, source=quote",
                            "AUDIT",
                            "WARNING",
                        )
                        continue

                    self.safe_log(f"Quote-LLM generiert: {llm_response}", "LLM", "DEBUG")
                    return llm_response

                # Both attempts failed - silent drop (fail-closed)
                self.safe_log("Quote-LLM failed after retry (banned phrase) - fail-closed", "LLM", "WARNING")
                return ""
        except BudgetExceeded:
            self.safe_log("Quote-LLM budget exhausted - fail-closed", "LLM", "WARNING")
            return ""
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Quote-LLM Fehler, nutze Fallback: {e}", "LLM", "WARNING")

        # A reply fallback is not automatically a publishable quote. Reject
        # overlong prose instead of silently amputating its argument.
        try:
            fallback = self.generate_intelligent_comment(tweet_text)
            if not isinstance(fallback, str) or len(fallback.split()) > 20:
                return ""
            fallback = _sanitize_quote(fallback)
            if not fallback:
                return ""
            from intelligent_features.llm_responder import check_banned_phrases
            from content.authenticity_gate import authenticity_gate

            if check_banned_phrases(fallback):
                return ""
            ok_auth, _ = authenticity_gate(
                fallback, source="quote", original_text=tweet_text or ""
            )
            return fallback if ok_auth else ""
        except Exception:
            self.safe_log("Quote fallback unavailable or rejected - fail-closed", "LLM", "WARNING")
            return ""

    def extract_tweet_engagement(self, tweet_element) -> dict:
        """Extrahiert Engagement-Metriken (Likes, Retweets, Replies, Views) aus einem Tweet-Element.

        HINWEIS: Diese Methode ueberlagert _extract_engagement_playwright aus bot_modules/engagement.py
        per MRO. Sie ist der Primary Path fuer Selenium-basierte DOM-Extraktion.
        """
        metrics = {'likes': 0, 'retweets': 0, 'comments': 0, 'views': 0}
        evidence_source = 'keine'

        try:
            # Sanity-Caps: alle Metriken
            _caps = {'likes': 5_000_000, 'retweets': 2_000_000,
                     'comments': 1_000_000, 'views': 100_000_000}

            # Patterns fuer aria-label Keywords (deutsch & englisch)
            # Wortstaemme, nicht Vollformen: 'reply' matcht NICHT 'replies', und
            # 'gefaell'/'gefall' matchen NICHT 'gefaellt' mit Umlaut (gefaellt). Beides
            # empirisch belegt: das deutsche X schreibt "18 Gefaellt mir" mit ae-Umlaut,
            # das englische "2 replies". Reihenfolge ist relevant: laengere/spezifischere
            # Staemme zuerst, damit 'repost' nicht von 'repl' verdeckt wird.
            patterns = {
                'repost': 'retweets', 'retweet': 'retweets',
                'antwort': 'comments', 'kommentar': 'comments', 'repl': 'comments',
                'gefäll': 'likes', 'gefaell': 'likes', 'gefall': 'likes', 'like': 'likes',
                'aufruf': 'views', 'angezeigt': 'views', 'anzeige': 'views', 'view': 'views',
            }

            def parse_count(text: str) -> int:
                """Parst Zahlen mit deutsch & englischen Trennern und Suffixen (K/M/Tsd./Mio./Mrd.)."""
                if not text:
                    return 0
                try:
                    low = text.lower()
                    m = re.search(r'(\d[\d.,]*)', low)
                    if not m:
                        return 0

                    raw = m.group(1)
                    tail = low[m.end():m.end() + 12]

                    # Multiplikator bestimmen
                    if re.match(r'\s*(mrd|milliard|billion|b\b)', tail):
                        mult = 1_000_000_000
                    elif re.match(r'\s*(mio|million|m\b)', tail):
                        mult = 1_000_000
                    elif re.match(r'\s*(tsd|thousand|k\b)', tail):
                        mult = 1_000
                    else:
                        mult = 1

                    if mult > 1:
                        # Mit Suffix: letzter Trenner = Dezimalzeichen, anderer = Gruppierung
                        if "." in raw and "," in raw:
                            decimal_sep = "." if raw.rfind(".") > raw.rfind(",") else ","
                            grouping_sep = "," if decimal_sep == "." else "."
                            normalized = raw.replace(grouping_sep, "")
                            if normalized.count(decimal_sep) != 1:
                                return 0
                            normalized = normalized.replace(decimal_sep, ".")
                        elif "." in raw or "," in raw:
                            decimal_sep = "." if "." in raw else ","
                            if raw.count(decimal_sep) != 1:
                                return 0
                            normalized = raw.replace(decimal_sep, ".")
                        else:
                            normalized = raw
                        num = float(normalized)
                    else:
                        # Ohne Suffix: Punkt = Tausendertrenner (deutsch), Komma entfernen
                        num = float(re.sub(r'[.,]', '', raw))

                    result = int(num * mult)
                    return 0 if result < 0 else result
                except Exception:
                    return 0

            # === PRIMARY PATH: role="group" mit kombinierter aria-label ===
            try:
                groups = tweet_element.find_elements(By.XPATH, ".//div[@role='group'][@aria-label]")
                if groups:
                    for group_el in groups:
                        glabel = (group_el.get_attribute('aria-label') or "").lower()
                        # NICHT an [,;.] zerlegen: das zerschneidet jede Zahl mit
                        # Trennzeichen. Empirisch belegt: "1.234 Aufrufe" wurde zu 234,
                        # und "1,2 Mio. Aufrufe" zu 0, weil Zahl und Schluesselwort in
                        # verschiedene Segmente fielen. Statt zu trennen werden
                        # Zahl-Einheit-Wort-Paare direkt gesucht.
                        for m_pair in re.finditer(
                            r'(\d[\d.,]*)\s*'
                            r'(mrd\.?|mio\.?|tsd\.?|milliarden?|millionen?|tausend|[kmb])?\s*'
                            r'([a-zäöüß]+)',
                            glabel,
                        ):
                            zahl_teil = m_pair.group(0)
                            wort = m_pair.group(3) or ""
                            for pattern, metric_key in patterns.items():
                                if pattern in wort:
                                    count = parse_count(zahl_teil)
                                    if count > 0 and count <= _caps[metric_key]:
                                        metrics[metric_key] = max(metrics[metric_key], count)
                                    break
                    # Wenn mindestens ein Wert > 0, erfolgreicher Primärpfad
                    if any(v > 0 for v in metrics.values()):
                        evidence_source = 'group'
            except Exception:
                pass

            # === FALLBACK: einzelne Buttons (data-testid), button UND div probieren ===
            if evidence_source == 'keine':
                test_ids = [
                    ('reply', 'comments'),
                    ('retweet', 'retweets'),
                    ('like', 'likes'),
                ]
                for test_id, metric_key in test_ids:
                    if metrics[metric_key] > 0:
                        continue  # Bereits aus Primärpfad gefunden

                    for selector_type in ['button', 'div']:
                        try:
                            xpath = f".//{selector_type}[@data-testid='{test_id}']"
                            optional_elements = tweet_element.find_elements(By.XPATH, xpath)
                            if not optional_elements:
                                continue
                            el = optional_elements[0]
                            aria = el.get_attribute('aria-label') or ""
                            if aria:
                                count = parse_count(aria)
                                if count > 0 and count <= _caps[metric_key]:
                                    metrics[metric_key] = count
                                    if evidence_source == 'keine':
                                        evidence_source = 'fallback'
                                    break
                        except Exception:
                            pass

            # === FALLBACK: Views via Analytics-Link (nur wenn nicht bereits gefunden) ===
            if metrics['views'] == 0:
                try:
                    views_elements = tweet_element.find_elements(By.XPATH, ".//a[contains(@href, '/analytics')]")
                    aria = (views_elements[0].get_attribute('aria-label') or "") if views_elements else ""
                    if aria:
                        count = parse_count(aria)
                        if count > 0 and count <= _caps['views']:
                            metrics['views'] = count
                            if evidence_source == 'keine':
                                evidence_source = 'analytics'
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # === DIAGNOSTISCHE LOGZEILE ===
            try:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[ENGAGEMENT-DIAG] quelle={evidence_source} views={metrics['views']} likes={metrics['likes']}",
                    "ENGAGEMENT", "DEBUG"
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

            return metrics
        except Exception:
            return metrics

    # _extract_tweet_text: geerbt von EngagementManager (engagement.py:264)

    def _log_reply_confirm(self, zweig: str, **felder) -> None:
        """Protokolliert, WELCHER Zweig ueber die Reply-Bestaetigung entschieden hat.

        Am 2026-08-18 standen 26 Zeilen "Reply-Klick ohne bestaetigtes Posting-Signal"
        im Log, bei 0 bestaetigten Postings. Aus dem Log war NICHT zu erkennen, ob
        die Antworten draussen waren und nur die Bestaetigung fehlte, oder ob der
        Klick nichts ausgeloest hat — beide Faelle enden im selben `return False`.
        Diese Zeile trennt sie:
          composer_voll     -> Text steht noch im Feld, also wurde nichts gesendet
          ausnahme          -> gar nicht geprueft, das Urteil ist wertlos
          erfolg_toast      -> gesendet und bestaetigt
          composer_entfernt/unsichtbar/leer -> gesendet, ueber den Feldzustand erkannt
        Rein diagnostisch, ohne Einfluss auf den Rueckgabewert.
        """
        try:
            rest = " ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in felder.items() if v not in (None, ""))
            self.safe_log(
                f"[REPLY-CONFIRM] zweig={zweig} {rest}".strip(),
                "REPLIES",
                "INFO",
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _confirm_reply_posted(
        self,
        reply_box,
        final_reply_text: str,
        *,
        known_own_urls: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Bestaetigt den Reply ueber Toast oder geschlossenen/geleerten Composer."""
        # ``False`` is normally definitive. A stale/ambiguous DOM is the
        # exception: remember that distinction so the caller may re-read the
        # page once without ever clicking the post button a second time.
        self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = False
        try:
            notices = self.driver.find_elements(
                By.XPATH,
                "//*[@data-testid='toast' or @role='alert']",
            )
        except Exception:
            notices = []

        notice_texts = []
        for notice in notices or []:
            try:
                if hasattr(notice, "is_displayed") and not notice.is_displayed():
                    continue
                candidates = [
                    getattr(notice, "text", ""),
                    notice.get_attribute("textContent"),
                    notice.get_attribute("aria-label"),
                ]
                notice_texts.extend(
                    value.strip().lower()
                    for value in candidates
                    if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip()
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

        combined_notice = " ".join(notice_texts)
        error_markers = (
            "rate limit",
            "try again",
            "something went wrong",
            "failed",
            "versuche es erneut",
            "etwas ist schief",
            "konnte nicht",
        )
        if any(marker in combined_notice for marker in error_markers):
            self._log_reply_confirm("fehler_toast", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return False

        success_markers = (
            "your post was sent",
            "post sent",
            "reply sent",
            "dein post wurde gesendet",
            "antwort wurde gesendet",
        )
        if any(marker in combined_notice for marker in success_markers):
            self._log_reply_confirm("erfolg_toast", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return True

        try:
            evaluator = getattr(reply_box, "_locator", None)
            if evaluator is None:
                evaluator = getattr(reply_box, "_handle", None)
            if evaluator is None:
                self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = True
                self._log_reply_confirm("kein_evaluator", toast=combined_notice[:120])
                return False
            # Direkt ueber Playwright evaluieren. Der Selenium-Kompatibilitaets-
            # Wrapper fuer execute_script() verschachtelt Arrow-Functions und
            # wuerde deren Rueckgabewert verlieren.
            composer_state = evaluator.evaluate(
                """
                el => {
                    if (!el || !el.isConnected) {
                        return {connected: false, visible: false, text: ""};
                    }
                    const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
                    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                    const visible = style.display !== "none"
                        && style.visibility !== "hidden"
                        && Number(style.opacity || "1") > 0
                        && rect.width > 0
                        && rect.height > 0;
                    return {
                        connected: true,
                        visible: visible,
                        text: String(el.textContent || "")
                    };
                }
                """,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            # WICHTIG: hier lag die Ursache dafuer, dass 26 Reply-Versuche als
            # "nicht bestaetigt" galten, ohne dass erkennbar war warum. Ein
            # stilles `return False` gibt Nichtwissen als Negativbefund aus.
            fallback = self._confirm_reply_posted_fresh_dom(
                final_reply_text,
                known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
            )
            if fallback is not None:
                return fallback
            self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = True
            self._log_reply_confirm(
                "ausnahme", toast=combined_notice[:120], fehler=type(e).__name__
            )
            return False

        if not isinstance(composer_state, dict):
            self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = True
            self._log_reply_confirm("composer_unlesbar", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return False
        if composer_state.get("connected") is False:
            self._log_reply_confirm("composer_entfernt", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return True
        if composer_state.get("visible") is False:
            self._log_reply_confirm("composer_unsichtbar", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return True
        text = composer_state.get("text")
        if isinstance(text, str) and not text.strip():
            self._log_reply_confirm("composer_leer", toast=combined_notice[:120])
            return True
        # Composer steht noch da UND enthaelt noch Text -> spricht dafuer, dass
        # nichts abgeschickt wurde. Textlaenge mitloggen, damit sich das von den
        # anderen Zweigen unterscheiden laesst.
        self._log_reply_confirm(
            "composer_voll",
            toast=combined_notice[:120],
            textlen=len(text) if isinstance(text, str) else -1,
        )
        return False

    def _retry_uncertain_reply_confirmation(
        self,
        final_reply_text: str,
        *,
        known_own_urls: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Re-read an inconclusive reply DOM without repeating the click.

        X can replace the composer asynchronously after a successful post. Two
        short, bounded reads recover that race while preserving strict
        fail-closed behavior: continued uncertainty never becomes success.
        """
        if not bool(getattr(self, "_reply_confirmation_uncertain", False)):
            return False
        for attempt in range(1, 3):
            try:
                pause = getattr(self, "human_pause", None)
                if callable(pause):
                    pause(0.8, 1.4)
                result = self._confirm_reply_posted_fresh_dom(
                    final_reply_text,
                    known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                self._log_reply_confirm(
                    "retry_ausnahme", versuch=attempt, fehler=type(exc).__name__
                )
                continue
            if result is True:
                self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = False
                self._log_reply_confirm("retry_erfolg", versuch=attempt)
                return True
            if result is False:
                self._reply_confirmation_uncertain = False
                self._log_reply_confirm("retry_nicht_gesendet", versuch=attempt)
                return False
        return False

    def _confirm_reply_posted_fresh_dom(
        self,
        final_reply_text: str,
        *,
        known_own_urls: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ):
        """Prueft den aktuellen Composer nach einem stale/timeout Locator.

        X ersetzt den Composer beim Absenden haeufig im DOM. Der urspruengliche
        Locator kann dann timeouten, obwohl ein neuer, leerer Composer bereits
        existiert. Dieser Fallback liest deshalb nur die aktuelle Seite und ist
        bewusst fail-closed: ohne aktuelle DOM-Daten wird ``None`` geliefert,
        sodass der aufrufende Pfad weiterhin ``False`` zurueckgibt.
        """
        page = getattr(self, "page", None)
        if page is None:
            page = getattr(getattr(self, "driver", None), "_page", None)
        evaluate = getattr(page, "evaluate", None)
        if not callable(evaluate):
            return None

        try:
            states = evaluate(
                """
                () => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(
                    "[data-testid='tweetTextarea_0'], "
                    + "[role='textbox'][contenteditable='true'], "
                    + "textarea[placeholder*='What is happening'], "
                    + "textarea[placeholder*='Was ist los']"
                )).map(el => {
                    const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
                    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                    const visible = style.display !== "none"
                        && style.visibility !== "hidden"
                        && Number(style.opacity || "1") > 0
                        && rect.width > 0
                        && rect.height > 0;
                    return {
                        visible,
                        text: String(el.value || el.innerText || el.textContent || "")
                    };
                })
                """
            )
        except Exception as error:
            self._log_reply_confirm(
                "fallback_ausnahme", fehler=type(error).__name__
            )
            return None

        # If X replaced the composer entirely, use a second independent proof:
        # exactly one newly visible own-authored status with the exact final
        # text and a canonical status URL.  This avoids treating a timeline
        # tweet, an old own reply, or an ambiguous composer as success.
        if not isinstance(states, list) or not states:
            status_result = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
                final_reply_text,
                known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
            )
            if status_result is not None:
                return status_result
            self._log_reply_confirm("fallback_keine_daten")
            return None

        visible = [item for item in states if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("visible")]
        nonempty = [
            item for item in states
            if item in visible and str(item.get("text", "")).strip()
        ]
        if nonempty:
            self._log_reply_confirm(
                "fallback_composer_voll",
                textlen=len(str(nonempty[0].get("text", ""))),
            )
            return False
        if len(visible) == 1:
            self._log_reply_confirm("fallback_composer_leer")
            return True
        if len(visible) > 1:
            status_result = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
                final_reply_text,
                known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
            )
            if status_result is not None:
                return status_result
            self._log_reply_confirm("fallback_mehrdeutig", kandidaten=len(visible))
            return None

        # Nur unsichtbare Restknoten gefunden: kein belastbarer Erfolgsbeweis.
        status_result = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
            final_reply_text,
            known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
        )
        if status_result is not None:
            return status_result
        self._log_reply_confirm("fallback_composer_unsichtbar")
        return None

    def _confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
        self,
        final_reply_text: str,
        *,
        known_own_urls: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        return_url: bool = False,
    ):
        """Return a result only for one exact, new own-authored status article.

        This is deliberately independent from the composer selectors.  It is
        used only when the composer locator is stale/ambiguous, and remains
        fail-closed when author, text, or canonical status identity is unclear.
        """
        page = getattr(self, "page", None)
        if page is None:
            page = getattr(getattr(self, "driver", None), "_page", None)
        evaluate = getattr(page, "evaluate", None)
        if not callable(evaluate):
            return None

        try:
            import json as _json

            own_username = str(
                (getattr(self, "config", None) or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
                if isinstance(getattr(self, "config", None), dict)
                else "ai_studioxyz"
            ).strip().lstrip("@").casefold()
            needle = " ".join(str(final_reply_text or "").split())
            known = set()
            for raw_url in known_own_urls or []:
                match = re.fullmatch(
                    r"https://(?:x|twitter)\.com/([A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15})/status/(\d+)",
                    str(raw_url).strip(),
                    flags=re.IGNORECASE,
                )
                if match:
                    known.add(
                        f"https://x.com/{match.group(1).casefold()}/status/{match.group(2)}"
                    )
            script = f"""
                () => {{
                    const expectedAuthor = {_json.dumps(own_username, ensure_ascii=False)};
                    const needle = {_json.dumps(needle, ensure_ascii=False)};
                    const known = new Set({_json.dumps(sorted(known), ensure_ascii=False)});
                    const normalize = value => String(value || '').replace(/\\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                    const matches = [];
                    const matchedUrls = new Set();
                    for (const article of Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("article[data-testid='tweet']"))) {{
                        if (article.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') || article.parentElement?.closest('article')) continue;
                        const direct = node => node.closest('article') === article && !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]');
                        const textNodes = Array.from(article.querySelectorAll("[data-testid='tweetText']")).filter(direct);
                        if (textNodes.length !== 1) continue;
                        const textNode = textNodes[0];
                        const text = normalize(textNode.innerText || textNode.textContent);
                        if (!needle || text !== needle) continue;
                        const own = Array.from(article.querySelectorAll('a[href]')).filter(direct).some(anchor => {{
                            try {{
                                const url = new URL(anchor.href, location.origin);
                                return url.hostname.toLowerCase() === 'x.com'
                                    && url.pathname.toLowerCase() === '/' + expectedAuthor;
                            }} catch (_) {{ return false; }}
                        }});
                        if (!own) continue;
                        const primary = Array.from(article.querySelectorAll('a[href]')).filter(direct)
                            .filter(anchor => Array.from(anchor.querySelectorAll('time')).some(direct));
                        const urls = primary.map(anchor => {{
                            try {{
                                const url = new URL(anchor.href, location.origin);
                                if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.hostname.toLowerCase() !== 'x.com' || url.search || url.hash) return '';
                                const path = url.pathname;
                                if (!new RegExp('^/[A-Za-z0-9_]{{1,15}}/status/[0-9]+$', 'i').test(path)) return '';
                                return url.origin + path;
                            }} catch (_) {{ return ''; }}
                        }});
                        if (!urls.length || urls.some(url => !url) || new Set(urls.map(url => url.toLowerCase())).size !== 1) continue;
                        if (!new URL(urls[0]).pathname.toLowerCase().startsWith('/' + expectedAuthor + '/status/')) continue;
                        for (const url of urls) {{
                            const identity = url.toLowerCase();
                            if (!known.has(identity) && !matchedUrls.has(identity)) {{
                                matchedUrls.add(identity);
                                matches.push({{url, text}});
                            }}
                        }}
                    }}
                    return matches;
                }}
            """
            matches = evaluate(script)
        except Exception as error:
            self._log_reply_confirm(
                "status_dom_ausnahme",
                fehler=type(error).__name__,
            )
            return None

        if not isinstance(matches, list) or not matches:
            return None
        if len(matches) != 1:
            return None
        match = matches[0] if isinstance(matches[0], dict) else {}
        canonical = str(match.get("url", "")).strip()
        if not canonical:
            return None
        self._log_reply_confirm("status_dom", url=canonical)
        return canonical if return_url else True

    def _confirm_standalone_post(
        self, content: str, known_urls: list[str], sent_after: float
    ) -> str:
        """Bounded, exact confirmation for one standalone tweet send."""
        from learning.tweet_learner import _decode_snowflake_timestamp

        own = str((getattr(self, "config", {}) or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")).lstrip("@").casefold()
        for _ in range(3):
            candidate = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
                content, known_own_urls=known_urls, return_url=True
            )
            if not candidate:
                self.human_pause(0.5, 1.0)
                continue
            author, status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(candidate)
            if author.casefold() != own or not re.fullmatch(r"[1-9][0-9]{0,19}", status_id):
                continue
            published_at = _decode_snowflake_timestamp(status_id)
            now = time.time()
            if published_at is None or published_at < sent_after - 2.0 or published_at > now + 2.0:
                self.human_pause(0.5, 1.0)
                continue
            return f"https://x.com/{author.casefold()}/status/{status_id}"
        return ""

    def _canonical_owned_reply_receipt(self, url, known_urls=None, *, sent_after=None) -> str:
        """Validate a captured own-status identity, independent of UI notices."""
        if not isinstance(url, str):
            return ""
        match = re.fullmatch(r"https://(?:www\.)?(?:x\.com|twitter\.com)/([A-Za-z0-9_]+)/status/([1-9][0-9]{0,19})", url.strip())
        own = str((getattr(self, "config", {}) or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")).lstrip("@").casefold()
        if not match or match.group(1).casefold() != own:
            return ""
        if sent_after is not None:
            from learning.tweet_learner import _decode_snowflake_timestamp
            if (isinstance(sent_after, bool) or not isinstance(sent_after, (int, float))
                    or not math.isfinite(sent_after) or sent_after < 0):
                return ""
            published_at = _decode_snowflake_timestamp(match.group(2))
            # The same two-second clock tolerance as standalone confirmation.
            if (published_at is None or not math.isfinite(published_at)
                    or published_at < sent_after - 2.0 or published_at > time.time() + 2.0):
                return ""
        canonical = f"https://x.com/{own}/status/{match.group(2)}"
        for baseline in known_urls or []:
            if self._canonical_owned_reply_receipt(baseline) == canonical:
                return ""
        return canonical

    def _record_confirmed_reply_learning(
        self,
        *,
        original_text: str,
        final_reply_text: str,
        topic: str,
        target_tweet_url: str,
        own_reply_url: str = "",
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """Verknuepft den bestaetigt geposteten Finaltext mit beiden Learnern."""
        tweet_learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        if not tweet_learner:
            return None
        own_reply_url = self._canonical_owned_reply_receipt(own_reply_url)
        if not own_reply_url:
            self.safe_log("[REPLY-ID] Kein eigener Status-Beleg; kein Lern-Commit", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
            return None

        reply_learner_id = None
        responder = getattr(self, "llm_responder", None)
        try:
            from contextlib import nullcontext

            mutation_factory = getattr(tweet_learner, "_mutation", None)
            transaction = (
                mutation_factory()
                if callable(mutation_factory)
                else nullcontext()
            )
            # Aufnahme + ID-Link bleiben unter demselben Prozesslock. Dadurch
            # kann kein paralleler Writer zwischen record_tweet() und _save()
            # verloren gehen.
            with transaction:
                # Identitaet aus eigener Reply-URL ableiten
                url_author = ""
                status_id = ""
                canonical = ""
                content_meta = {}
                if own_reply_url:
                    try:
                        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
                        own = str(
                            (config or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
                            if isinstance(config, dict)
                            else "ai_studioxyz"
                        ).lstrip("@")
                        url_author, status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(own_reply_url)
                        if status_id and url_author.lower() == own.lower():
                            canonical = own_reply_url
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # Falls Capture fehlgeschlagen: parent_url dokumentieren
                if not canonical:
                    content_meta = {"parent_url": target_tweet_url}
                    if not own_reply_url:
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[REPLY-ID] eigene Reply-URL nicht erfasst",
                            "REPLIES",
                            "WARNING",
                        )

                # Ueber die von record_tweet() zurueckgegebene stabile ID
                # verknuepfen, nicht ueber [-1]: ein paralleler Append darf
                # niemals den falschen Text mit diesem Outcome verbinden.
                tweet_record_id = tweet_learner.record_tweet(
                    text=final_reply_text,
                    topic=topic,
                    source="reply",
                    tweet_url=own_reply_url if canonical else "",
                    status_id=status_id if canonical else "",
                    own_username=url_author if canonical else "",
                    canonical_url=canonical,
                    content_metadata=content_meta,
                )
                # Eintrag in _pending_tweet_checks, falls canonical vorhanden
                if canonical and hasattr(self, "_pending_tweet_checks"):
                    try:
                        self._pending_tweet_checks.append({
                            "id": tweet_record_id,
                            "tweet_id": tweet_record_id,
                            "url": canonical,
                            "tweet_url": canonical,
                            "canonical_url": canonical,
                            "status_id": status_id,
                            "own_username": url_author,
                            "ts": time.time(),
                            "topic": topic,
                            "source": "reply",
                            "darwin_strategy": None,
                            "content_metadata": content_meta,
                            "checked": False,
                        })
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                if responder and hasattr(responder, "record_posted_reply"):
                    reply_learner_id = responder.record_posted_reply(
                        original_text,
                        final_reply_text,
                    )
                if reply_learner_id:
                    linked = False
                    for entry in tweet_learner._data.get("tweets", []):
                        if entry.get("id") == tweet_record_id:
                            entry["reply_learner_id"] = reply_learner_id
                            tweet_learner._save()
                            linked = True
                            break
                    if not linked:
                        raise RuntimeError("tweet learner record disappeared before link")
                    if not hasattr(responder, "confirm_posted_reply"):
                        raise RuntimeError("reply learner cannot finalize durable link")
                    if not responder.confirm_posted_reply(reply_learner_id):
                        # Der durable Tweet-Link bleibt als Reconciliation-Key
                        # bestehen. Der provisional Reply beeinflusst weder
                        # Prompt noch Stats und wird beim Engagement erneut
                        # finalisiert.
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[REPLY_LEARNING] Link durable, Finalisierung auf Engagement verschoben",
                            "REPLY_LEARNING",
                            "WARNING",
                        )
        except Exception as exc:
            if (
                reply_learner_id
                and responder
                and hasattr(responder, "discard_posted_reply")
            ):
                try:
                    responder.discard_posted_reply(reply_learner_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            self.safe_log(
                f"[REPLY_LEARNING] Tweet-Verknuepfung fehlgeschlagen: {type(exc).__name__}",
                "REPLY_LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None
        return reply_learner_id

    def _guard_reply_output_for_post(self, comment: object) -> Optional[str]:
        """Letzte deterministische Grenze fuer extern beeinflusste Replies."""
        guarded = guard_external_response_output(
            comment,
            max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS,
            max_mentions=1,
        )
        if guarded:
            return guarded
        self.safe_log(
            "[AUDIT] Reply DROP: external_output_guard, source=reply",
            "AUDIT",
            "WARNING",
        )
        return None

    def _is_reply_candidate_blocked(self, tweet_el) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Prüft ob ein Kandidat von der Krypto-Sperrliste blockiert wird.

        Gibt (blocked: bool, reason: str) zurück.
        """
        blocked_handles = self.config.get('reply_blocked_handles', []) or []
        blocked_terms = self.config.get('reply_blocked_terms', []) or []

        # Alle auf Kleinschreibung normalisieren fuer Vergleich
        blocked_handles_lower = [h.lower() for h in blocked_handles]
        blocked_terms_lower = [t.lower() for t in blocked_terms]

        try:
            # Handle des Tweet-Autors extrahieren
            handle_el = tweet_el.find_element(By.XPATH, ".//a[contains(@href,'/')]/div/span/span/text()")
            author_handle = handle_el.text.strip().lower() if handle_el else ""

            if author_handle and author_handle in blocked_handles_lower:
                return True, author_handle
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            # Tweet-Text extrahieren
            tw_text = self._extract_tweet_text(tweet_el) or ""
            tw_text_lower = tw_text.lower()

            # Auf gesperrte Begriffe prüfen (Wort-Grenzen wo sinnvoll)
            for term in blocked_terms_lower:
                # "bitcoin", "btc", etc. als ganze Wörter
                if term in ['bitcoin', 'btc', 'ethereum', 'eth', 'blockchain', 'crypto', 'krypto', 'token', 'wallet', 'hodl', 'moon', 'pump']:
                    if re.search(r'\b' + re.escape(term) + r'\b', tw_text_lower):
                        return True, term
                else:
                    # Andere Begriffe substring-match (z.B. "coinbase", "solana", "dogecoin")
                    if term in tw_text_lower:
                        return True, term
        except Exception:
            pass

        return False, ""

    # Mindestlaenge des Ziel-Tweet-Textes, damit ein Reply ueberhaupt sinnvoll ist.
    _MIN_TARGET_TEXT_CHARS = 15

    def _extract_target_tweet_text(self, target_tweet) -> str:
        """Liest den Text des Ziel-Tweets, robust gegen stille Adapter-Fehler.

        Warum eigene Methode: `PlaywrightElement.text` und `is_displayed()` fangen
        JEDE Ausnahme und geben "" bzw. False zurueck (selenium_adapter.py:256-262
        und 302-308). Ein veraltetes oder mehrdeutiges Element wird damit lautlos
        zu leerem Text — ohne Log, ohne Fehler.

        Befund 2026-08-18, der das aufdeckte: 27 Ziel-Profile korrekt geladen und
        ueberall Tweets gefunden, aber das Modell bekam leeren Text und verweigerte
        ("Ich kann hier nicht antworten, weil der zitierte Tweet leer ist").
        43 von 52 Entwuerfen wurden von der Reply-Grenze verworfen, es entstand
        1 Reply in 30 Stunden.
        """
        selektoren = (
            ".//div[@data-testid='tweetText']",
            ".//div[@lang]",
            ".//span[@lang]",
        )
        gefunden = {}
        for sel in selektoren:
            try:
                parts = target_tweet.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel)
            except Exception:
                parts = []
            gefunden[sel.split("@")[-1].rstrip("]")] = len(parts)
            if not parts:
                continue
            # Bewusst KEIN is_displayed()-Filter: inner_text() liefert schon den
            # gerenderten Text, und is_displayed() faellt bei jedem Fehler auf
            # False zurueck — es wuerde brauchbaren Text stillschweigend wegwerfen.
            stuecke = []
            for p in parts:
                try:
                    t = (p.text or "").strip()
                except Exception:
                    t = ""
                if t:
                    stuecke.append(t)
            text = " ".join(stuecke).strip()
            if len(text) >= self._MIN_TARGET_TEXT_CHARS:
                return text

        # Letzter Versuch direkt im DOM — umgeht den Adapter vollstaendig.
        try:
            js = (
                "var el = arguments[0];"
                "var n = el.querySelector('[data-testid=tweetText]');"
                "return ((n || el).innerText || '').trim();"
            )
            text = (self.driver.execute_script(js, target_tweet) or "").strip()
            if len(text) >= self._MIN_TARGET_TEXT_CHARS:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[REPLY-EXTRACT] Ziel-Text erst per DOM-Fallback gelesen",
                    "REPLIES", "INFO",
                )
                return text
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[REPLY-EXTRACT] DOM-Fallback fehlgeschlagen: {e}",
                "REPLIES", "DEBUG",
            )

        self.safe_log(
            f"[REPLY-EXTRACT] Ziel-Text nicht lesbar; Elemente je Selektor={gefunden}",
            "REPLIES", "WARNING",
        )
        return ""

    def _get_reply_attempt_store(self):
        """Lazily load the durable reply-target reservation store."""
        existing = getattr(self, "_reply_attempts", None)
        if existing is not None:
            return existing
        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
        configured_path = (
            config.get("reply_attempts_state_file", DEFAULT_REPLY_ATTEMPTS_PATH)
            if isinstance(config, dict)
            else DEFAULT_REPLY_ATTEMPTS_PATH
        )
        store = ReplyAttemptStore(configured_path)
        self._reply_attempts = store
        return store

    def _reply_target_blocked(self, target_id: str) -> bool:
        """Return True on reservation or any persistence/read failure."""
        try:
            return bool(self._get_reply_attempt_store().is_blocked(target_id))
        except Exception:
            return True

    def _reserve_reply_attempt(self, target_id: str, final_text: str) -> bool:
        try:
            return self._reserve_comment_action("reply", target_id, final_text, self._get_reply_attempt_store())
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _mark_reply_attempt_confirmed(self, target_id: str) -> bool:
        try:
            return bool(self._get_reply_attempt_store().mark_confirmed(target_id))
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(self, target_id: str) -> bool:
        try:
            return bool(self._get_reply_attempt_store().mark_uncertain(target_id))
        except Exception:
            return False

    @bounded_llm_task(label="reply", max_calls=24, failure_value=False)
    def reply_to_tweet_intelligent(self):
        """Antwortet mit intelligentem Kommentar auf einen hochwertigen Tweet."""
        reserved_target_id = ""
        self._last_reply_outcome_status = "skipped"
        try:
            if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                return False
            if not self._comment_action_ready():
                return False
            if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung konnte nicht wiederhergestellt werden", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                return False

            _niche_kw = self.config.get('search_keywords', []) or []
            _reach_kw = self.config.get('reply_reach_keywords', []) or []
            _target_accounts = self.config.get('reply_target_accounts', []) or []

            # Gross-Account-Hebel (15.06): zu ~30% direkt auf einen kuratierten,
            # reichweitenstarken DACH/Business-Account gehen und dort auf den
            # FRISCHESTEN Tweet antworten — borgt echte Reichweite beim richtigen
            # deutschsprachigen Publikum (Keyword-Suche surft oft nur winzige Tweets).
            # Die restliche Pipeline (Frische-/Reichweiten-Sortierung, Semantik-Filter,
            # Reply-Posting) bleibt identisch. Falscher Handle -> Profil leer ->
            # sauberer Fallback auf Home-Timeline weiter unten.
            _acct_prob = float(self.config.get('reply_account_mode_prob', 0.65) or 0.0)
            _reach_prob = float(self.config.get('reply_reach_keyword_prob', 0.80) or 0.0)
            _account_mode = bool(_target_accounts) and random.random() < _acct_prob
            if _account_mode:
                handle = random.choice(_target_accounts).lstrip('@').strip()
                keyword = f"@{handle}"
                search_filter = "account"
                search_url = f"https://x.com/{handle}"
                self.safe_log(f"[REACH] Gross-Account-Reply: Ziel @{handle}", "REPLIES", "INFO")
            else:
                # Reichweiten-Mix (15.06): Replies fischen zu 50% in GROESSEREN deutschen
                # KI/Business-Konversationen (reply_reach_keywords), da die nischigen
                # search_keywords (GoBD/Datev/...) nur winzige Tweets hochsurfen. Niche
                # bleibt fuer Eigentweet/Follow unberuehrt; nur der Reply-Pfad wird breiter.
                if _reach_kw and (not _niche_kw or random.random() < _reach_prob):
                    keyword = random.choice(_reach_kw)
                else:
                    keyword = random.choice(_niche_kw)
                search_filter = "top"
                # 50/50 Mix aus "Top" (hohes Engagement) und "Live" (frischere Tweets)
                if random.random() < 0.50:
                    search_filter = "live"
                search_url = f"https://x.com/search?q={self.normalize_search_keyword(keyword).replace(' ', '%20')}&src=typed_query&f={search_filter}"
            self.driver.get(search_url)
            self.human_pause(6.0, 10.0)

            # Scroll um mehr Tweets zu laden (Twitter laedt oft nur 1-2 initial)
            for _rs in range(4):
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                    self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)
                    _tw_count = len(self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']"))
                    if _tw_count >= 6:
                        break
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # [TARGET-DIAG] (2026-08-17): Tote Ziel-Handles sichtbar machen.
            # Ein falscher oder inaktiver Handle liefert ein leeres Profil, und der Code
            # faellt weiter unten still auf die Home-Timeline zurueck. Genau so blieben
            # 7 von 14 Handles monatelang unbemerkt wirkungslos (null Treffer in 286
            # Replies), zwei davon nur wegen eines Tippfehlers im Handle — und ueber
            # den Home-Fallback kamen 78 Krypto-Replies herein. Rein diagnostisch,
            # aendert den Kontrollfluss nicht.
            if _account_mode:
                try:
                    _found = len(
                        self.driver.find_elements(
                            By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']"
                        )
                    )
                    if _found == 0:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[TARGET-DIAG] handle=@{handle} tweets=0 "
                            f"-> Profil leer, Home-Fallback greift. Handle pruefen.",
                            "REPLIES", "WARNING",
                        )
                    else:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[TARGET-DIAG] handle=@{handle} tweets={_found}",
                            "REPLIES", "INFO",
                        )
                except Exception as _e:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[TARGET-DIAG] Zaehlung fehlgeschlagen: {_e}",
                        "REPLIES", "DEBUG",
                    )

            try:
                self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
            except Exception:
                pass

            tweet_containers = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
            if not tweet_containers:
                # Fehlerseite-Recovery: "Etwas ist schiefgelaufen"
                if self._detect_rate_limit_or_block():
                    # P3 (2026-06-07): Bei wiederholtem Rate-Limit NICHT weiter haemmern
                    # (das vertieft die Drosselung von X). Eskalierenden Cooldown nutzen;
                    # ab dem 3. Treffer (cool>=900s) den Reply-Zyklus sauber abbrechen.
                    cool = self._rate_limit_cooldown_seconds()
                    if cool >= 900:
                        self.safe_log(
                            "Wiederholtes Rate-Limit — Reply-Zyklus abgebrochen (Backoff statt Haemmern)",
                            "REPLIES", "WARNING",
                        )
                        return False
                    self.safe_log(f"Search rate-limited — Cooldown {cool}s, dann Recovery", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
                    if cool > 0:
                        time.sleep(min(cool, 300))
                    self._try_recover_error_page()
                    tweet_containers = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
                # Fallback: Home-Timeline statt Search nutzen
                if not tweet_containers:
                    self.safe_log("Search leer — wechsle auf Home-Timeline als Fallback", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                    try:
                        self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
                        self.human_pause(5.0, 8.0)
                        for _hs in range(4):
                            self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: document.body.scrollHeight, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                            self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)
                            _htw = len(self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']"))
                            if _htw >= 5:
                                break
                        self.driver.execute_script("window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'});")
                        self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
                        tweet_containers = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
                        if tweet_containers:
                            self.safe_log(f"Home-Fallback: {len(tweet_containers)} Tweets gefunden", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.safe_log(f"Home-Fallback fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
                if not tweet_containers:
                    self.safe_log("Keine Tweets fuer Reply gefunden (Search + Home)", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
                    return False

            sf = self.config.get('success_filter', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            min_likes = int(sf.get('min_likes', 50))
            min_views = int(sf.get('min_views', 1000))
            high_engagement = self.filter_high_engagement_tweets(tweet_containers[:15], min_likes=min_likes, min_views=min_views)

            # In-session Dedupe, damit wir nicht mehrfach denselben Tweet beantworten
            if not hasattr(self, "_replied_tweet_ids"):
                self._replied_tweet_ids = set()

            def _tweet_id(tweet_el) -> str:
                try:
                    a = tweet_el.find_element(By.XPATH, ".//a[contains(@href,'/status/')]")
                    href = (a.get_attribute("href") or "").strip()
                    m = re.search(r"/status/(\d+)", href)
                    return m.group(1) if m else href
                except Exception:
                    return ""

            # NICHT hier auf 6 kappen. Vorher wurde in reiner DOM-Reihenfolge gekappt
            # und ERST DANACH nach Reichweite sortiert — 9 von 15 gescannten Kandidaten
            # wurden also nie reichweiten-verglichen. Gekappt wird jetzt nach dem
            # Sortieren, weiter unten.
            candidates = list(high_engagement[:15] if high_engagement else tweet_containers[:15])
            _dom_reihenfolge = list(candidates)  # Baseline fuer die [REACH-SORT]-Zeile
            if high_engagement:
                # Diese Schwelle (likes>=15..50 UND views>=300..1000, Alter<=15min) war
                # wirkungslos, solange extract_tweet_engagement Nullen lieferte, und
                # greift seit der Messreparatur erstmals wirklich. Sichtbar machen,
                # damit man erkennt, ob sie gute Ziele auswaehlt oder die Pipeline
                # aushungert.
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[REACH-FILTER] {len(high_engagement)} von {len(tweet_containers[:15])} "
                    f"ueber der Engagement-Schwelle",
                    "REPLIES", "INFO",
                )

            # Freshness-Filter: Bevorzuge Tweets < 2h — frische Replies = mehr Sichtbarkeit
            def _tweet_age_minutes(tweet_el) -> float:
                """Gibt das Alter eines Tweets in Minuten zurueck (999999 bei Fehler)."""
                try:
                    time_el = tweet_el.find_element(By.XPATH, ".//time")
                    dt_str = time_el.get_attribute("datetime")
                    if dt_str:
                        tweet_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(dt_str.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                        age = (datetime.now(tweet_dt.tzinfo) - tweet_dt).total_seconds() / 60
                        return max(0.0, age)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return 999999.0

            # Alter je Kandidat bestimmen
            candidates_with_age = [(tw, _tweet_age_minutes(tw)) for tw in candidates]
            # P4 (2026-06-07): Zweistufige Frische. Erste ~90min = maximaler Algo-Push
            # (Reply landet, solange der Tweet noch im Feed pusht); 90-120min als zweite
            # Stufe; aelteres nur als Backup. "Erste Minuten" schlaegt Reichweite.
            very_fresh = [tw for tw, age in candidates_with_age if age < 90]
            semi_fresh = [tw for tw, age in candidates_with_age if 90 <= age < 120]
            older_candidates = [tw for tw, age in candidates_with_age if age >= 120]

            # P2 (2026-06-07): GEBORGTE REICHWEITE. Innerhalb jeder Frische-Stufe die
            # Tweets mit der groessten Reichweite zuerst beantworten (Views+Likes als
            # Reach-Proxy fuer grosse Accounts), statt random.shuffle. Frische bleibt
            # primaeres Kriterium, Reichweite das Tiebreak — so landen Replies frueh
            # unter SICHTBAREN Tweets grosser Accounts statt unter toten. Variety kommt
            # aus Keyword-Rotation pro Session + In-Session-Dedupe (_replied_tweet_ids).
            # Messung je Tweet nur EINMAL. filter_high_engagement_tweets hat dieselben
            # Container oben schon gemessen; ohne Cache wuerde jeder sort-Aufruf erneut
            # ins DOM greifen.
            _reach_cache = {}

            def _reach_score(tweet_el) -> float:
                schluessel = _tweet_id(tweet_el)
                if schluessel and schluessel in _reach_cache:
                    return _reach_cache[schluessel]
                try:
                    eng = self.extract_tweet_engagement(tweet_el) or {}
                    wert = (
                        float(eng.get('views', 0) or 0)
                        + float(eng.get('likes', 0) or 0) * 50.0
                    )
                except Exception:
                    wert = 0.0
                if schluessel:
                    _reach_cache[schluessel] = wert
                return wert

            very_fresh.sort(key=_reach_score, reverse=True)
            semi_fresh.sort(key=_reach_score, reverse=True)
            older_candidates.sort(key=_reach_score, reverse=True)
            # Erst JETZT kappen: die Frische-Stufen bleiben in ihrer Reihenfolge
            # (Frische schlaegt Reichweite), aber innerhalb der Stufe hat die
            # Reichweite ueber alle 15 Kandidaten entschieden, nicht nur ueber 6.
            fresh_candidates = (very_fresh + semi_fresh)[:6]
            candidates = fresh_candidates + older_candidates[:2]  # frisch+reichweitenstark zuerst, max 2 aeltere als Backup

            # [REACH-SORT]: haelt fest, ob die Umstellung ueberhaupt etwas aendert.
            # Ohne diese Zeile bleibt unbelegt, ob nach echter Reichweite sortiert wird
            # oder ob wieder alle Kandidaten denselben Wert 0 tragen (dann waere die
            # stabile Sortierung erneut ein No-op).
            try:
                if candidates:
                    _gewaehlt = _reach_score(candidates[0])
                    _baseline = _reach_score(_dom_reihenfolge[0]) if _dom_reihenfolge else 0.0
                    _werte = sorted((_reach_cache.get(_tweet_id(t), 0.0) for t in candidates), reverse=True)
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[REACH-SORT] gewaehlt={_gewaehlt:.0f} dom_baseline={_baseline:.0f} "
                        f"top3={[round(v) for v in _werte[:3]]} gemessen={len(_reach_cache)} "
                        f"kandidaten={len(candidates)}",
                        "REPLIES", "INFO",
                    )
            except Exception:
                pass

            if fresh_candidates:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[FRESHNESS+REACH] {len(very_fresh)} sehr frisch (<90min), "
                    f"{len(semi_fresh)} frisch (90-120min), reichweiten-priorisiert",
                    "REPLIES", "DEBUG",
                )

            # Krypto-Sperrliste: blockiere gesperrte Handles und Begriffe VOR Semantik-Filterung
            blocked_count = 0
            filtered_candidates = []
            for tw in candidates:
                is_blocked, reason = self._is_reply_candidate_blocked(tw)
                if is_blocked:
                    blocked_count += 1
                    self.safe_log(f"[TARGET-BLOCK] verworfen: {reason}", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                else:
                    filtered_candidates.append(tw)

            if blocked_count > 0:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Krypto-Filter: {blocked_count} Kandidaten blockiert, {len(filtered_candidates)} verbleibend",
                    "REPLIES", "DEBUG",
                )
                candidates = filtered_candidates

            # Semantische Relevanz-Filterung via TopicRelevanceScorer
            reply_candidates = []
            like_only_candidates = []
            for tw in candidates:
                try:
                    tw_text = self._extract_tweet_text(tw)
                    recommendation = self.get_engagement_recommendation(tw_text)
                    if recommendation in ("reply", "quote"):
                        reply_candidates.append(tw)
                    elif recommendation == "like_only":
                        like_only_candidates.append(tw)
                    # "skip" → nicht aufnehmen
                except Exception:
                    continue

            if reply_candidates:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Semantic filter: {len(reply_candidates)} reply-worthy, "
                    f"{len(like_only_candidates)} like-only aus {len(candidates)} candidates",
                    "REPLIES", "INFO",
                )
                candidates = reply_candidates
            else:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[REPLY-SKIP] Semantic filter: keine reply/quote-freigegebenen "
                    f"Kandidaten fuer '{keyword}' ({len(candidates)} geprueft, "
