                    f"{len(like_only_candidates)} like-only)",
                    "REPLIES", "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            target_tweet = None
            target_id = ""
            for tw in candidates:
                tid = _tweet_id(tw)
                if not tid or tid in self._replied_tweet_ids:
                    continue
                if self._reply_target_blocked(tid):
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[REPLY-RESERVATION] Ziel blockiert oder State nicht lesbar: {tid}",
                        "REPLIES",
                        "INFO",
                    )
                    continue
                target_tweet = tw
                target_id = tid
                break
            if target_tweet is None:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[REPLY-RESERVATION] Keine ungesperrte Ziel-Status-ID; kein Fallback",
                    "REPLIES",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            # URL des Original-Tweets JETZT erfassen (vor dem Posten),
            # da driver.current_url nach dem Reply auf der Search-Seite bleibt.
            target_tweet_url = ""
            try:
                _url_el = target_tweet.find_element(By.XPATH, ".//a[contains(@href,'/status/')]")
                _href = (_url_el.get_attribute("href") or "").strip()
                if "/status/" in _href:
                    # Relative URLs (z.B. /user/status/123) zu absolut machen
                    if _href.startswith("/"):
                        _href = "https://x.com" + _href
                    target_tweet_url = _href
            except Exception:
                pass

            original_text = self._extract_target_tweet_text(target_tweet)
            try:  # TypeSafe-Shadow: Reply-Ziel nur loggen, entscheidet nie
                from content.typesafe_shadow import log_reply_target_async
                log_reply_target_async(original_text, target_tweet_url)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Harte Sperre gegen Blind-Replies. Ohne sie ging der leere Text an das
            # Modell, das korrekt verweigerte; die Verweigerung wurde von der
            # Reply-Grenze verworfen, und generate_intelligent_comment fiel danach
            # auf eine GENERISCHE Vorlage zurueck — der Bot haette also einen
            # Standardsatz unter einen Tweet gesetzt, den er nicht lesen konnte.
            # Das ist zugleich Geldverbrennung (LLM-Aufruf ohne Inhalt) und genau
            # das Muster, an dem Bots erkannt werden.
            if len(original_text) < self._MIN_TARGET_TEXT_CHARS:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[REPLY-SKIP] Ziel-Tweet-Text leer oder zu kurz "
                    f"({len(original_text)} Zeichen) — kein Blind-Reply, "
                    "kein LLM-Aufruf",
                    "REPLIES", "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            comment = self.generate_intelligent_comment(original_text)
            if not comment or not comment.strip():
                self.safe_log("[REPLY-SKIP] Kein freigegebener Entwurf; keine Template-Reparatur", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                return False

            # Strip hashtags, links, emojis from reply (consistent with standalone tweet rules)
            if comment:
                comment = re.sub(r'#\S+', '', comment).strip()
                comment = re.sub(r'https?://\S+', '', comment).strip()
                comment = re.compile(
                    "[\U0001F600-\U0001F64F\U0001F300-\U0001F5FF"
                    "\U0001F680-\U0001F6FF\U0001F1E0-\U0001F1FF"
                    "\U00002700-\U000027BF\U0001F900-\U0001F9FF"
                    "\U00002600-\U000026FF]",
                    flags=re.UNICODE,
                ).sub("", comment).strip()

            # Content Gatekeeper: check reply quality — retry once with fresh generation on rejection
            gatekeeper = self._required_content_gatekeeper()
            if gatekeeper is None:
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                return False
            if gatekeeper:
                approved, improved = gatekeeper.check(comment, content_type="reply", original_tweet=original_text)
                if not approved:
                    if (getattr(gatekeeper, "_last_check_unavailable", False) is True
                            or (current_budget() is not None and current_budget().exhausted)):
                        self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                        return False
                    self.safe_log("Reply blocked by gatekeeper — retrying with fresh generation", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                    _retry = self.generate_intelligent_comment(original_text)
                    gatekeeper = self._required_content_gatekeeper()
                    if gatekeeper is None:
                        self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                        return False
                    if _retry:
                        _retry = re.sub(r'#\S+|https?://\S+', '', _retry).strip()
                        approved, improved = gatekeeper.check(_retry, content_type="reply", original_tweet=original_text)
                    if not approved:
                        self.safe_log(f"Reply blocked after retry: {comment[:50]}...", "REPLIES", "INFO")
                        self._last_reply_outcome_status = "rejected"
                        return False
                comment = improved
                # F95 (2026-06-09): Gatekeeper-Regen/Amplify kann Hashtags/Links/Emojis
                # RE-injizieren — der Strip vor check() reicht nicht (erklaert den
                # geposteten "#WidersprücheBegrüßt"). Nach improve nochmal strippen.
                if comment:
                    comment = re.sub(r'#\S+|https?://\S+', '', comment).strip()

            # F90 (2026-05-31): Harte DE-Sprach-Sperre auch fuer Antworten — der Reply-Pfad
            # laeuft NICHT durch _apply_unified_quality_gate, sonst rutscht Englisch durch.
            comment = self._guard_reply_output_for_post(comment)
            if not comment:
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "rejected"
                return False

            comment, reply_guard_reason = prepare_final_tweet_text(
                comment,
                max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS,
            )
            if not comment:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[AUDIT] Reply DROP: final_guard={reply_guard_reason}, source=reply",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            if self._is_english_drop(comment):
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "rejected"
                self.safe_log("[AUDIT] Reply DROP: Englisch erkannt, source=reply", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
                return False
            # F94 (2026-06-09): 47€/unter-50€-Kosten-Anker auch in Replies blocken. Der
            # Reply-Pfad umgeht das Gate, daher hier eigenstaendig — am 09.06 trugen 11/48
            # geposteten Tweets den Anker, alle Replies. Eigener Anker = Drop; externe
            # Dritt-Budgets (15k€/2 Mio.) loesen NICHT aus (siehe _COST_ANCHOR_PAT).
            if self._is_cost_anchor_drop(comment):
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "rejected"
                self.safe_log("[AUDIT] Reply DROP: 47€-Kosten-Anker erkannt, source=reply", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
                return False
            try:
                from content.authenticity_gate import authenticity_gate
                ok_auth, auth_reason = authenticity_gate(
                    comment, source="reply", original_text=original_text or ""
                )
                if not ok_auth:
                    self._last_reply_outcome_status = "rejected"
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[AUDIT] Reply DROP: authenticity={auth_reason}, source=reply",
                        "AUDIT",
                        "WARNING",
                    )
                    return False
            except ImportError:
                self.safe_log("[AUDIT] Reply DROP: required authenticity check unavailable", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                return False

            # Question-Hook ENTFERNT: "What's your take?" etc. → generisch und wertet Brand ab
            self.safe_log(f"Reply-Kommentar (intelligent): {comment}", "REPLIES", "DEBUG")

            # Vor Posting: Bekannte eigene Status-URLs einfangen (für Capture danach)
            try:
                own = str(
                    (getattr(self, "config", None) or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
                    if isinstance(getattr(self, "config", None), dict)
                    else "ai_studioxyz"
                ).lstrip("@")
                known_own_urls = self._collect_own_status_urls(own)
            except Exception:
                self.safe_log("Reply-Baseline unlesbar; kein Versand", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
                return False

            reply_selectors = [
                ".//div[@data-testid='reply']",
                ".//button[@data-testid='reply']",
                ".//div[contains(@aria-label,'Antwort')]",
                ".//button[contains(@aria-label,'Antwort')]",
                ".//div[contains(@aria-label,'Reply')]",
                ".//button[contains(@aria-label,'Reply')]",
            ]
            reply_button = None
            for sel in reply_selectors:
                try:
                    buttons = target_tweet.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel)
                    for b in buttons or []:
                        if b.is_displayed() and b.is_enabled():
                            reply_button = b
                            break
                    if reply_button:
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue
            if not reply_button:
                self.safe_log("Reply-Button nicht gefunden", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                return False

            try:
                self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({block: 'center'});", reply_button)
                self.human_pause(1.6, 2.8)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                reply_button.click()
            except Exception:
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].click();", reply_button)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"Reply-Button klick fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                    return False
            self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)  # Wait for reply modal to render before searching input

            box = self.dynamic_find_tweet_input_field()
            if not box:
                self.safe_log("Reply-Eingabefeld nicht gefunden", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                return False
            try:
                box.click()
                self.human_pause(0.8, 1.6)
                self.type_text_human(box, comment)
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Reply-Text Eingabe fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                return False

            post_btn = self.dynamic_find_reply_button(max_wait_seconds=10)
            if not post_btn:
                self.safe_log("Post-Button fuer Reply nicht gefunden", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
                return False
            # The durable reservation is the only gate immediately before the
            # final send.  Do not use stale_element_safe_click here: its retry
            # loop could click twice after an uncertain browser response.
            if current_budget() is not None and current_budget().exhausted:
                self.safe_log("[LLM-BUDGET] Reply verworfen; Auftragsbudget erschoepft", "REPLIES", "WARNING")
                return False
            if not self._reserve_reply_attempt(target_id, comment):
                self.safe_log(
                    "[REPLY-RESERVATION] Ziel konnte nicht atomar reserviert werden; kein Klick",
                    "REPLIES",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False
            reserved_target_id = target_id
            self._last_reply_outcome_status = "unknown"
            try:
                if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                    self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                    self.safe_log("Reply finaler Versand durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                    return False
                if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                    self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                    self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
                    return False
                reply_sent_after = time.time()
                click_result = post_btn.click()
                if click_result is False:
                    self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                    self.safe_log(
                        "Reply-Klick lieferte kein Erfolgssignal; Ziel bleibt blockiert",
                        "REPLIES",
                        "WARNING",
                    )
                    return False
            except Exception as exc:
                self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Reply-Post-Button Klick ungewiss; Ziel bleibt blockiert: {exc}",
                    "REPLIES",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            self.human_pause(3.5, 6.5)
            confirmed = self._confirm_reply_posted(
                box,
                comment,
                known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
            )
            if not confirmed and getattr(self, "_reply_confirmation_uncertain", False):
                confirmed = self._retry_uncertain_reply_confirmation(
                    comment,
                    known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
                )
            if not confirmed:
                self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                # Kein zweiter Versuch auf denselben Tweet in dieser Session:
                # Bei unbekanntem Serverzustand verhindert das Doppelposts.
                try:
                    if target_id:
                        self._replied_tweet_ids.add(target_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self.safe_log(
                    "Reply-Klick ohne bestaetigtes Posting-Signal; kein Lern-Commit",
                    "REPLIES",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False
            # UI delivery signals alone are not an own-status receipt.
            try:
                own_reply_url = self._capture_new_own_status_url(
                    known_own_urls, expected_text=comment
                )
            except Exception:
                own_reply_url = ""
            own_reply_url = self._canonical_owned_reply_receipt(
                own_reply_url, known_own_urls, sent_after=reply_sent_after,
            )
            if not own_reply_url or not self._mark_reply_attempt_confirmed(target_id):
                self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(target_id)
                return False
            reserved_target_id = ""
            self._last_reply_outcome_status = "confirmed"
            self.comments_posted += 1
            self._mark_activity("REPLY")
            # Erst nach bestaetigtem Posting wird exakt der Finaltext
            # gespeichert, der alle Gates passiert hat. Rohentwuerfe und
            # abgelehnte Retries bleiben aus der Lernbasis.
            self._record_confirmed_reply_learning(
                original_text=original_text,
                final_reply_text=comment,
                topic=keyword,
                target_tweet_url=target_tweet_url,
                own_reply_url=own_reply_url,
            )
            try:
                if target_id:
                    self._replied_tweet_ids.add(target_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Optional: Like auf den Originaltweet (mehr Sichtbarkeit/Signal)
            try:
                like_btn = None
                for sel in [".//button[@data-testid='like']", ".//div[@data-testid='like']"]:
                    try:
                        els = target_tweet.find_elements(By.XPATH, sel)
                        like_btn = next((e for e in els if e.is_displayed() and e.is_enabled()), None)
                        if like_btn:
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                if like_btn:
                    # Optional engagement must use the same durable, single-
                    # click guard; a failed like never invalidates the reply.
                    self._send_reserved_like(like_btn, target_tweet)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Optional: Smart-Follow Hook (falls implementiert)
            try:
                if hasattr(self, "_try_smart_follow"):
                    self._try_smart_follow(target_tweet)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.safe_log("Intelligente Antwort erfolgreich gepostet", "REPLIES", "INFO")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            if reserved_target_id:
                self._mark_reply_attempt_uncertain(reserved_target_id)
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "unknown"
            else:
                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "failed"
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler beim intelligenten Reply: {e}", "REPLIES", "ERROR")
            return False

    def rephrase_and_post_trending(self):
        """Retired: direct paraphrase publishing bypassed format/outcome tracking."""
        self.safe_log(
            "Direkter Rephrase-Publisher deaktiviert; nutze den gemessenen "
            "Scrape-Rewrite-Kandidatenpfad.",
            "REPHRASE",
            "INFO",
        )
        return False


    def quote_tweet_optimized(self):
        """Delegiert auf EngagementManager.quote_tweet_optimized fuer zentrale Logik."""
        try:
            return EngagementManager.quote_tweet_optimized(self)
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler in delegiertem quote_tweet_optimized: {e}", "QUOTES", "ERROR")
            return False

    # _load_follow_state, _save_follow_state: geerbt von FollowManager (bot_modules/follow.py)

    # _smart_follow_cycle: geerbt von FollowManager (bot_modules/follow.py)
    # Die geerbte Version hat korrekte Home-Navigation bei Fehlern.

    # run_growth_cycle: via FollowManager (bot_modules/follow.py) in MRO


    def _tweet_action_attempt_store(self) -> ActionAttemptStore:
        config = getattr(self, "config", {})
        path = (
            config.get("action_attempts_state_file", DEFAULT_ACTION_ATTEMPTS_PATH)
            if isinstance(config, dict) else DEFAULT_ACTION_ATTEMPTS_PATH
        )
        return ActionAttemptStore(path)

    def _tweet_action_limit(self) -> int:
        config = getattr(self, "config", {})
        safety = config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(config, dict) else {}
        hourly = safety.get("per_hour", {}) if isinstance(safety, dict) else {}
        if not isinstance(hourly, dict):
            hourly = {}
        try:
            return int(hourly.get("tweet", 2))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            return 2

    def _quality_first_enabled(self) -> bool:
        config = getattr(self, "config", {})
        policy = config.get("editorial_policy", {}) if isinstance(config, dict) else {}
        return isinstance(policy, dict) and policy.get("quality_first") is True

    def _build_execution_governor(self):
        """Build the runtime governor with the config-owned editorial flag."""
        from intelligent_features.execution_governor import SessionExecutionGovernor

        mission = ((getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {})
        mission = dict(mission) if isinstance(mission, dict) else {}
        # The safety policy is authoritative; a planner/LLM cannot override it.
        mission["quality_first"] = self._quality_first_enabled()
        return SessionExecutionGovernor(
            session_start_time=self.session_start_time,
            mission_contract=mission,
        )

    def _recover_stuck_session(self):
        """Run legacy inactivity recovery, but never manufacture activity in quality mode."""
        if self._quality_first_enabled():
            return None
        self.safe_log(
            "Inaktiv - versuche Recovery (Reconnect + Refresh + Timer-Reset)",
            "SESSION", "WARNING",
        )
        self.ensure_browser_connection()
        try:
            self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
            self.human_pause(3.0, 5.0)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(f"Recovery-Refresh fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "SESSION", "ERROR")
        now = datetime.now()
        self.last_action_time = now
        previous = now - timedelta(minutes=120)
        return (previous, previous, previous, previous)

    def _forced_action_timestamp(self, now: datetime):
        """Return the legacy force-action timestamp unless quality policy forbids it."""
        if self._quality_first_enabled():
            return None
        return now - timedelta(hours=2)

    def _apply_rebalance_boost(self, boost, now: datetime, timers):
        """Apply a legacy action boost without manufacturing urgency in quality mode."""
        timestamp = self._forced_action_timestamp(now)
        if timestamp is None:
            return timers
        values = list(timers)
        index = {"reply": 1, "tweet": 3, "like": 0, "retweet": 2}.get(boost)
        if index is not None:
            values[index] = timestamp
        return tuple(values)

    def _apply_kickoff_timers(self, timers, kickoff_minutes: float):
        """Apply optional startup acceleration only for the legacy policy."""
        if self._quality_first_enabled():
            return timers
        delta = timedelta(minutes=kickoff_minutes)
        return tuple(value - delta for value in timers)

    def _forced_readiness_allowed(self) -> bool:
        """Whether guarantee/kickoff paths may manufacture timer readiness."""
        return not self._quality_first_enabled()

    def _configured_guarantee(self, safety_limits, key: str, default: int) -> int:
        if not self._forced_readiness_allowed():
            return 0
        try:
            value = safety_limits.get(key, default)
            return max(0, int(default if value is None else value))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            return max(0, int(default))

    def _tweet_editorial_limits(self) -> dict:
        if not self._quality_first_enabled():
            return {}
        policy = self.config["editorial_policy"]
        return {
            "daily_limit": policy.get("max_posts_per_24h", 2),
            "min_interval_seconds": policy.get("min_interval_seconds", 21600),
        }

    def _tweet_action_intent_id(self, content: str) -> str:
        # Content is the only stable identity available before posting; using
        # its digest conservatively blocks duplicate sends across restarts.
        return hashlib.sha256(content.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    def _tweet_action_history(self) -> list:
        """Read actual publications, not the overlapping local success counter."""
        state = self.tweet_learner._load(strict=True)
        if not isinstance(state, dict) or not isinstance(state.get("tweets"), list):
            raise ValueError("invalid publication history")
        cutoff = time.time() - (86400 if self._quality_first_enabled() else 3600)
        observations = []
        seen = set()
        for row in state["tweets"]:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise ValueError("invalid publication entry")
            if row.get("source") in {"reply", "quote_tweet", "quote", "retweet"}:
                continue
            stamp = row.get("timestamp")
            if (isinstance(stamp, bool) or not isinstance(stamp, (int, float))
                    or not math.isfinite(stamp) or stamp < 0):
                raise ValueError("invalid publication timestamp")
            if stamp < cutoff:
                continue
            identity, text = row.get("id"), row.get("text")
            if (not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity.strip()
                    or identity in seen or not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip()):
                raise ValueError("ambiguous publication identity")
            seen.add(identity)
            final_text, _ = prepare_final_tweet_text(text, max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS)
            observations.append({
                "id": identity,
                "action_type": "tweet",
                "intent_id": self._tweet_action_intent_id(final_text or text),
                "observed_at": stamp,
            })
        return observations

    def connection_resilient_tweet(
        self,
        content: str,
        _after_reconnect: bool = False,
        _fallback_depth: int = 0,
        _budget_receipt=None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Verbindungs-resiliente Tweet-Funktion (erweitert)."""
        action_reserved = False
        intent_id = ""
        self._last_posted_tweet_url = ""
        try:
            if _budget_receipt and any(budget.exhausted for budget in _budget_receipt):
                return False
            if current_budget() is not None and current_budget().exhausted:
                self.safe_log("[LLM-BUDGET] Kein Versand aus erschoepftem Auftrag", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            content, guard_reason = prepare_final_tweet_text(
                content,
                max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS,
            )
            if not content:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Tweet geblockt (Final-Guard: {guard_reason})",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False
            if not self._action_allowed("tweet"):
                self.safe_log("Tweet uebersprungen (SAFETY: per-hour Limit erreicht)", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                return False
            if self._is_recent_duplicate_post(content):
                self.safe_log("Tweet uebersprungen (Duplicate-Gate: zu aehnlich zu recent posts)", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                return False
            self.safe_log(f"POSTE VERBINDUNGS-RESILIENTEN TWEET: {content[:50]}...", "TWEETS", "INFO")
            if _fallback_depth > 0:
                self.safe_log(f"Fallback-Post (depth={_fallback_depth})", "TWEETS", "DEBUG")

            if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung konnte nicht wiederhergestellt werden", "TWEETS", "ERROR")
                return False
            if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                return False
            
            if "/home" not in self.driver.current_url:
                self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
                self.human_pause(3.5, 6.5)
            
            def find_tweet_box():
                return self.dynamic_find_tweet_input_field()
            
            if not self.stale_element_safe_click(find_tweet_box, "Tweet-Eingabefeld"):
                self.safe_log("Tweet-Eingabefeld auf /home nicht gefunden, versuche /compose/post", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                try:
                    self.driver.get("https://x.com/compose/post")
                    self.human_pause(2.5, 4.0)
                    if not self.stale_element_safe_click(find_tweet_box, "Tweet-Eingabefeld-compose"):
                        self.safe_log("Tweet-Eingabefeld auch auf /compose/post nicht gefunden", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                        return False
                except Exception:
                    return False
            
            self.human_pause(1.2, 2.0)
            
            tweet_box = self.dynamic_find_tweet_input_field()
            if tweet_box:
                tweet_box.send_keys(Keys.CONTROL + "a")
                self.human_pause(0.6, 1.2)
                tweet_box.send_keys(Keys.DELETE)
                self.human_pause(0.6, 1.2)
                
                tweet_box.send_keys(content)
                self.human_pause(2.0, 4.0)
                
                tweet_button = self.dynamic_find_reply_button(max_wait_seconds=10)
                
                if tweet_button:
                    if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                        return False
                    # History and new reservations share one atomic budget.
                    # Readiness never reserves; only this final send boundary does.
                    action_store = self._tweet_action_attempt_store()
                    intent_id = self._tweet_action_intent_id(content)
                    own_user = str((getattr(self, "config", {}) or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")).lstrip("@")
                    try:
                        known_own_urls = list(self._collect_own_status_urls(own_user) or [])
                    except Exception:
                        known_own_urls = []
                    if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                        return False
                    if not action_store.reserve(
                        "tweet", intent_id, self._tweet_action_limit(),
                        history=self._tweet_action_history(),
                        **self._tweet_editorial_limits(),
                    ):
                        self.safe_log("Tweet geblockt (dauerhafte Versandreservierung)", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                        return False
                    action_reserved = True
                    sent_after = time.time()
                    self._record_action_timestamp("tweet")
                    self._remember_posted_text(content)
                    try:
                        if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                            action_store.mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                            self.safe_log("Tweet finaler Versand durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                            return False
                        if _budget_receipt and any(budget.exhausted for budget in _budget_receipt):
                            action_store.mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                            return False
                        if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
                            action_store.mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                            return False
                        click_result = tweet_button.click()
                    except Exception as exc:
                        action_store.mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                        self.safe_log(f"Tweet-Klick nach Reservierung fehlgeschlagen: {type(exc).__name__}", "TWEETS", "ERROR")
                        return False
                    action_store.mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                    if click_result is False:
                        return False
                    self.human_pause(3.5, 6.5)
                    confirmed_url = self._confirm_standalone_post(content, known_own_urls, sent_after)
                    if not confirmed_url:
                        self.safe_log("Tweet unbekannt: kein bestaetigter eigener Status", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                        return False
                    try:
                        if not action_store.mark_confirmed("tweet", intent_id):
                            return False
                    except Exception:
                        return False
                    self._last_posted_tweet_url = confirmed_url
                    self.tweets_posted += 1
                    self._mark_activity("TWEET")
                    self.safe_log(f"VERBINDUNGS-RESILIENTER TWEET ERFOLGREICH GEPOSTET! (#{self.tweets_posted})", "TWEETS", "INFO")
                    try:
                        base = self._base_interval_minutes('tweet_interval_minutes', 30)
                        self.learning_system.record_action_result('tweet', 'tweet', True, base, {})
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    return True
                else:
                    self.safe_log("Tweet-Button nicht gefunden", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                    try:
                        base = self._base_interval_minutes('tweet_interval_minutes', 30)
                        self.learning_system.record_action_result('tweet', 'tweet', False, base, {})
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    return False
            else:
                self.safe_log("Tweet-Eingabefeld nicht gefunden", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
        except (InvalidSessionIdException, NoSuchWindowException, WebDriverException) as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Driver session issue beim Tweet-Post: {e}", "CONNECTION", "ERROR")
            if action_reserved:
                try:
                    self._tweet_action_attempt_store().mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return False
            if _fallback_depth >= 3:
                self.safe_log("Rekursionslimit erreicht (depth>=3), breche ab", "CONNECTION", "ERROR")
                return False
            if not _after_reconnect and self.reconnect_browser():
                self.safe_log("Reconnect erfolgreich, wiederhole Tweet-Post einmal", "CONNECTION", "INFO")
                return bool(self.connection_resilient_tweet(content, _after_reconnect=True, _fallback_depth=_fallback_depth + 1, _budget_receipt=_budget_receipt))
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            if action_reserved:
                try:
                    self._tweet_action_attempt_store().mark_uncertain("tweet", intent_id)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei connection_resilient_tweet: {e}", "TWEETS", "ERROR")
            return False

    def post_resilient_tweet(self, content, _fallback_depth: int = 0, _budget_receipt=None):
        """Wrapper fuer connection_resilient_tweet."""
        try:
            return bool(self.connection_resilient_tweet(content, _fallback_depth=_fallback_depth, _budget_receipt=_budget_receipt))
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"post_resilient_tweet Fehler: {e}", "TWEETS", "ERROR")
            return False

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # THREADS (mehrteilige Posts)
    # Solange der Schalter ENABLE_THREADS aus ist, verhalten sich beide
    # oeffentlichen Methoden exakt wie die frueheren Fail-closed-Huellen.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _threads_enabled(self) -> bool:
        """Schalter-Abfrage. Config-JSON schlaegt die Default-Konstante."""
        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
        if isinstance(config, dict) and "enable_threads" in config:
            return bool(config.get("enable_threads"))
        return bool(ENABLE_THREADS)

    def _thread_part_limit(self) -> int:
        """Maximale Zeichenzahl je Thread-Teil."""
        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
        raw = None
        if isinstance(config, dict):
            raw = config.get("thread_part_max_chars")
        try:
            limit = int(raw) if raw is not None else int(THREAD_PART_MAX_CHARS)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            limit = int(THREAD_PART_MAX_CHARS)
        # Nie ueber den Final-Guard hinaus: sonst faellt jeder Teil im Gate.
        return max(40, min(limit, MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS))

    def _thread_number_style(self) -> str:
        """Nummerierungs-Stil ("none" oder "slash")."""
        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
        style = None
        if isinstance(config, dict):
            style = config.get("thread_number_style")
        style = str(style or THREAD_NUMBER_STYLE or "none").strip().lower()
        return style if style in ("none", "slash") else "none"

    def _generate_thread_raw_text(self, topic: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Erzeugt den langen Rohtext ueber den bestehenden LLM-Weg.

        Eigene Naht (Seam) fuer Tests: hier — und nur hier — haengt der
        Thread am Netzwerk. Die Zerlegung darueber ist rein lokal.
        """
        responder = getattr(self, "llm_responder", None)
        if not responder:
            self.safe_log(
                "[THREAD] Kein LLM-Responder verfuegbar",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        limit = self._thread_part_limit()
        system_prompt = (
            "##### SPRACH-LOCK — ABSOLUTE PRIORITÄT #####\n"
            "Ausgabesprache: NUR DEUTSCH. Jede englische Ausgabe ist ungültig.\n"
            "##### /SPRACH-LOCK #####\n\n"
            "Du bist Mag. Sven Steiner — österreichischer Gründer von AI Studio XYZ, "
            "CFO und Wirtschaftsprüfer, der eine Firma ohne Mitarbeiter mit AI-Agenten "
            "betreibt. Ruhig, konkret, konstruktiv, mit belegbaren Zahlen.\n\n"
            f"{STANDALONE_X_CONTENT_POLICY}"
        )
        user_prompt = (
            f"Thema: {topic}.\n"
            f"Schreibe einen zusammenhängenden deutschen Text aus {MIN_THREAD_PARTS} bis "
            f"{MAX_THREAD_PARTS} Absätzen. Jeder Absatz höchstens {limit} Zeichen und "
            "für sich allein verständlich: eine eigene Aussage, ohne Rückverweis.\n"
            "VERBOTEN: Nummerierung (kein '1/5'), Aufzählungszeichen, Zwischenüberschriften, "
            "das Wort 'Thread' und jedes Fortsetzungsversprechen ('mehr dazu gleich').\n"
            "Gib nur den Fließtext aus, Absätze durch eine Leerzeile getrennt."
        )
        try:
            response = responder.generate_freeform(
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                system_prompt=system_prompt,
                context={
                    "task_type": "tweet",
                    "temperature": 0.8,
                    "max_tokens": 900,
                },
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Rohtext-Generierung fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        text = str(response or "").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
        return text or None

    @bounded_llm_task(label="thread_generation")
    def generate_thread_content(self, topic: str) -> Optional[List[str]]:
        """Erzeugt eine vollstaendig geprueft Thread-Kette.

        Jeder einzelne Teil muss das Unified Quality Gate passieren. Faellt
        auch nur ein Teil durch, faellt der GANZE Thread durch (None) — es
        wird kein Loch in die Kette gelassen.
        """
        self._last_thread_generation_metadata = {}
        if not self._threads_enabled():
            self.safe_log(
                "[THREAD] No-Threads-Policy: Generierung uebersprungen",
                "TWEETS",
                "INFO",
            )
            return None

        limit = self._thread_part_limit()
        raw_text = self._generate_thread_raw_text(topic)
        if not raw_text:
            self.safe_log(
                "[THREAD] Generierung ohne Rohtext abgebrochen",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        raw_parts = split_into_thread(raw_text, max_len=limit)
        if len(raw_parts) > MAX_THREAD_PARTS:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] {len(raw_parts)} Teile erzeugt, gekuerzt auf {MAX_THREAD_PARTS}",
                "TWEETS",
                "INFO",
            )
            raw_parts = raw_parts[:MAX_THREAD_PARTS]
        if len(raw_parts) < MIN_THREAD_PARTS:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Nur {len(raw_parts)} Teil(e) nach Zerlegung — kein Thread",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        total = len(raw_parts)
        gated_parts: List[str] = []
        for position, part in enumerate(raw_parts, start=1):
            cleaned = self._apply_unified_quality_gate(
                part,
                source="thread",
                topic=topic,
            )
            if not cleaned:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[THREAD] Teil {position}/{total} vom Quality-Gate verworfen "
                    "— ganzer Thread faellt durch",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return None
            gated_parts.append(cleaned)

        numbered = number_thread(
            gated_parts,
            style=self._thread_number_style(),
            max_len=limit,
        )
        is_valid, reason = validate_thread(numbered, max_len=limit)
        if not is_valid:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Kette ungueltig ({reason}) — verworfen",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        self._last_thread_generation_metadata = {
            "topic": topic,
            "thread_total": len(numbered),
            "prompt_version": "unversioned:thread",
        }
        self.safe_log(
            f"[THREAD] Kette generiert: {len(numbered)} Teile, topic={topic}",
            "TWEETS",
            "INFO",
        )
        return numbered

    def _log_thread_break(
        self,
        position: int,
        total: int,
        root_id: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Protokolliert einen abgebrochenen Thread — kein stiller Teilerfolg."""
        self.safe_log(
            f"[THREAD] ABBRUCH bei Teil {position}/{total} (Grund: {reason}). "
            f"Erwartete Kettenlaenge: {total}. Wurzel-Record: {root_id or 'unbekannt'}. "
            f"Es steht ein halber Thread auf X.",
            "TWEETS",
            "WARNING",
        )

    def _collect_own_status_urls(self, own_username: str, limit: int = 25) -> List[str]:
        """Liest sichtbare eigene Status-URLs aus dem DOM (ohne Navigation)."""
        found: List[str] = []
        marker = f"/{str(own_username or '').lower()}/status/"
        try:
            articles = self.page.query_selector_all(
                'article[data-testid="tweet"]'
            )
            if not isinstance(articles, list):
                raise RuntimeError("own-status DOM result unavailable")
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] DOM-Scrape fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "WARNING",
            )
            raise RuntimeError("own-status baseline unavailable") from exc
        for article in articles[:limit]:
            try:
                link = article.query_selector('a[href*="/status/"]')
                if not link:
                    continue
                href = link.get_attribute("href") or ""
                if marker not in href.lower():
                    continue
                full = f"https://x.com{href}" if href.startswith("/") else href
                if full not in found:
                    found.append(full)
            except Exception as exc:
                raise RuntimeError("own-status baseline incomplete") from exc
        return found

    def _capture_new_own_status_url(
        self,
        known_urls,
        attempts: int = 3,
        delay: float = 2.0,
        expected_text: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Sucht die Status-URL des gerade geposteten eigenen Teils.

        Ohne eigene Status-ID je Teil waeren Teil 2..n spaeter unmessbar —
        genau die Schwaeche der alten Thread-Implementierung.
        """
        config = getattr(self, "config", None)
        own = str(
            (config or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
            if isinstance(config, dict)
            else "ai_studioxyz"
        ).lstrip("@").lower()
        seen = {str(url) for url in (known_urls or [])}
        for attempt in range(max(1, int(attempts))):
            if attempt:
                time.sleep(float(delay) * attempt)
            if expected_text is not None:
                matched_url = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
                    expected_text, known_own_urls=list(seen), return_url=True
                )
                if isinstance(matched_url, str) and matched_url:
                    return matched_url
                # Never replace an unverified reply with an unrelated own post.
                continue
            try:
                own_urls = self._collect_own_status_urls(own)
            except Exception:
                return ""
            for url in own_urls:
                if url not in seen:
                    return url
        self.safe_log(
            "[THREAD] Keine neue Status-URL im DOM gefunden",
            "FEEDBACK",
            "WARNING",
        )
        return ""

    def _record_thread_part(
        self,
        text: str,
        position: int,
        total: int,
        status_url: str,
        root_id: str,
        topic: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Schreibt einen geposteten Thread-Teil ins Lernarchiv.

        Gibt die Record-ID zurueck (leer, wenn nicht erfasst werden konnte).
        Konvention: thread_root_id == "" bedeutet "dieser Record IST die
        Wurzel" (Teil 1); Teil 2..n tragen die Record-ID von Teil 1.
        """
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        if not learner:
            return ""
        try:
            config = getattr(self, "config", None)
            own = str(
                (config or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
                if isinstance(config, dict)
                else "ai_studioxyz"
            ).lstrip("@")
            url = str(status_url or "")
            url_author, status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(url)
            canonical = url if (
                status_id and url_author.lower() == own.lower()
            ) else ""
            metadata = self._build_publish_metadata(
                {
                    "source": "thread",
                    "topic": topic,
                    "tone": "THREAD",
                    "format": "thread_part",
                    "prompt_version": "unversioned:thread",
                },
                text,
            )
            metadata["thread_position"] = int(position)
            metadata["thread_total"] = int(total)
            metadata["thread_root_id"] = str(root_id or "")
            record_id = learner.record_tweet(
                text=text,
                source="thread",
                topic=topic,
                tone="THREAD",
                tweet_url=url,
                status_id=status_id if canonical else "",
                own_username=own,
                canonical_url=canonical,
                content_metadata=metadata,
            )
            self._remember_posted_text(text)
            if canonical and hasattr(self, "_pending_tweet_checks"):
                self._pending_tweet_checks.append({
                    "id": record_id,
                    "tweet_id": record_id,
                    "url": canonical,
                    "tweet_url": canonical,
                    "canonical_url": canonical,
                    "status_id": status_id,
                    "own_username": own,
                    "ts": time.time(),
                    "topic": topic,
                    "source": "thread",
                    "darwin_strategy": None,
                    "content_metadata": metadata,
                    "checked": False,
                })
            return str(record_id or "")
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Lern-Erfassung Teil {position}/{total} fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return ""

    def _find_thread_reply_button(self, article):
        """Sucht den Antwort-Knopf innerhalb eines Tweet-Artikels."""
        selectors = [
            ".//div[@data-testid='reply']",
            ".//button[@data-testid='reply']",
            ".//div[contains(@aria-label,'Antwort')]",
            ".//button[contains(@aria-label,'Antwort')]",
            ".//div[contains(@aria-label,'Reply')]",
            ".//button[contains(@aria-label,'Reply')]",
        ]
        for selector in selectors:
            try:
                for button in article.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector) or []:
                    if button.is_displayed() and button.is_enabled():
                        return button
            except Exception:
                continue
        return None

    def _post_thread_reply(self, status_url: str, text: str) -> bool:
        """Postet einen Folgeteil als Antwort auf den eigenen letzten Teil.

        Gleiche Kette wie der bewaehrte Reply-Pfad: Antwort-Knopf, Eingabe-
        feld, menschliches Tippen, Post-Knopf und — entscheidend — der
        Posting-Beweis ueber _confirm_reply_posted().
        """
        try:
            if not self._comment_action_ready():
                return False
            target_author, target_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(status_url)
            config = getattr(self, "config", None)
            own_username = str(
                (config or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
                if isinstance(config, dict)
                else "ai_studioxyz"
            ).strip().lstrip("@").casefold()
            if not target_status_id or target_author.casefold() != own_username.casefold():
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Ziel-URL ist kein eigener Status; kein Reply-Klick",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False
            attempt_store = self._get_reply_attempt_store()
            if attempt_store.is_blocked(target_status_id):
                self.safe_log("[THREAD] Ziel bereits reserviert; kein Zweitversand", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Browser-Verbindung fehlt",
                    "CONNECTION",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            self.driver.get(status_url)
            self.human_pause(3.0, 5.5)

            articles = self.driver.find_elements(
                By.XPATH,
                "//article[@data-testid='tweet']",
            ) or []
            if not articles:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Eigener Tweet auf Status-Seite nicht gefunden",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False

            # X renders the parent tweet before the requested status.  Never
            # use articles[0]: identify the article by its own permalink and
            # exclude links belonging to nested quoted-tweet articles.
            try:
                matching_articles = []
                for article in articles:
                    direct_status_urls = self.driver.execute_script(
                        """
                        const root = arguments[0];
                        if (root.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') ||
                            root.parentElement?.closest('article')) return [];
                        return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('a[href]'))
                            // The primary tweet permalink wraps its timestamp.
                            // Text links and quoted-tweet links are not identity.
                            .filter(link => link.querySelector('time'))
                            .filter(link => !link.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]'))
                            .filter(link => link.closest('article') === root)
                            .map(link => link.href || link.getAttribute('href') || '')
                            .filter(href => /\\/(?:status|statuses)\\/\\d+(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(href));
                        """,
                        article,
                    ) or []
                    article_status_identities = {
                        (
                            author.casefold(),
                            status_id,
                        )
                        for url in direct_status_urls
                        for author, status_id in [
                            self._status_identity_from_url(str(url))
                        ]
                    }
                    # One article must expose exactly one canonical identity:
                    # the configured owner's requested status.  This rejects
                    # conflicting primary permalinks, foreign authors sharing
                    # an ID, and an unrelated second primary status.
                    if article_status_identities == {
                        (own_username, target_status_id),
                    }:
                        matching_articles.append(article)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[THREAD] Ziel-Tweet im DOM nicht sicher identifizierbar: {exc}",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False

            if len(matching_articles) != 1:
                reason = "fehlt" if not matching_articles else "mehrdeutig"
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[THREAD] Ziel-Tweet {target_status_id} im DOM {reason}; kein Reply-Klick",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            target_article = matching_articles[0]

            # Build a before-snapshot so a stale DOM cannot mistake an older
            # identical own status for the newly posted thread part.
            own_username = own_username.lower()
            known_own_urls: List[str] = []
            try:
                known_own_urls = list(self._collect_own_status_urls(own_username) or [])
            except Exception:
                self.safe_log("[THREAD] Status-Baseline unlesbar; kein Versand", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            if not known_own_urls:
                url_author, url_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(status_url)
                if url_author.casefold() == own_username.casefold() and url_status_id:
                    known_own_urls = [
                        f"https://x.com/{url_author.casefold()}/status/{url_status_id}"
                    ]
            if not known_own_urls:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Keine sichere Status-Baseline fuer Reply-Bestaetigung",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            reply_button = self._find_thread_reply_button(target_article)
            if not reply_button:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Antwort-Knopf nicht gefunden",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            try:
                self.driver.execute_script(
                    "arguments[0].scrollIntoView({block: 'center'});",
                    reply_button,
                )
                self.human_pause(1.2, 2.2)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                reply_button.click()
            except Exception:
                try:
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].click();", reply_button)
                except Exception as exc:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[THREAD] Antwort-Knopf nicht klickbar: {exc}",
                        "TWEETS",
                        "ERROR",
                    )
                    return False
            self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)

            box = self.dynamic_find_tweet_input_field()
            if not box:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Eingabefeld nicht gefunden",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            try:
                box.click()
                self.human_pause(0.8, 1.6)
                self.type_text_human(box, text)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[THREAD] Texteingabe fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False

            post_button = self.dynamic_find_reply_button(max_wait_seconds=10)
            if not post_button:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Post-Knopf nicht gefunden",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "ERROR",
                )
                return False
            if not self._reserve_reply_attempt(target_status_id, text):
                return False
            # Once reserved, an ambiguous transport failure must not trigger
            # another send. The durable reservation survives every exit path.
            try:
                if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                    attempt_store.mark_uncertain(target_status_id)
                    self.safe_log("[THREAD] Finaler Versand durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                    return False
                reply_sent_after = time.time()
                click_result = post_button.click()
            except Exception:
                attempt_store.mark_uncertain(target_status_id)
                raise
            if click_result is False:
                attempt_store.mark_uncertain(target_status_id)
                return False
            self.human_pause(3.5, 6.5)

            confirmed = self._confirm_reply_posted(
                box,
                text,
                known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
            )
            if not confirmed and getattr(self, "_reply_confirmation_uncertain", False):
                confirmed = self._retry_uncertain_reply_confirmation(
                    text,
                    known_own_urls=known_own_urls,
                )
            if not confirmed:
                attempt_store.mark_uncertain(target_status_id)
                self.safe_log(
                    "[THREAD] Kein bestaetigtes Posting-Signal",
                    "TWEETS",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            receipt = self._capture_new_own_status_url(known_own_urls, expected_text=text)
            receipt = self._canonical_owned_reply_receipt(receipt, known_own_urls, sent_after=reply_sent_after)
            if not receipt or not attempt_store.mark_confirmed(target_status_id):
                attempt_store.mark_uncertain(target_status_id)
                return False
            self._last_posted_reply_url = receipt
            self._mark_activity("THREAD")
            return True
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Folgeteil fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "TWEETS",
                "ERROR",
            )
            return False

    def post_thread(self, parts: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Postet eine Thread-Kette: Teil 1 als Tweet, Rest als Selbst-Antwort.

        Bricht die Kette bei Teil k ab, bleibt ein halber Thread auf X stehen.
        Das ist technisch unvermeidbar, wird aber IMMER als WARNING mit
        Position, erwarteter Kettenlaenge und Wurzel-Record protokolliert;
        jeder bereits geposteten Teil steht davor schon im Lernarchiv.
        """
        if not self._threads_enabled():
            self.safe_log(
                "[THREAD] No-Threads-Policy: Posting uebersprungen",
                "TWEETS",
                "INFO",
            )
            return False

        limit = self._thread_part_limit()
        is_valid, reason = validate_thread(parts, max_len=limit)
        if not is_valid:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Posting abgelehnt (Kette ungueltig: {reason})",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        total = len(parts)
        metadata = getattr(self, "_last_thread_generation_metadata", {}) or {}
        topic = str(metadata.get("topic") or "thread") if isinstance(metadata, dict) else "thread"

        if not self.connection_resilient_tweet(parts[0]):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[THREAD] Teil 1/{total} nicht gepostet — Thread nicht begonnen",
                "TWEETS",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        root_url = str(getattr(self, "_last_posted_tweet_url", "") or "")
        root_id = self._record_thread_part(
            text=parts[0],
            position=1,
            total=total,
            status_url=root_url,
            root_id="",
            topic=topic,
        )
        if not root_url:
            self._log_thread_break(1, total, root_id, "keine Status-URL fuer Teil 1")
            return False

        previous_url = root_url
        known_urls = {root_url}
        for position, part in enumerate(parts[1:], start=2):
            # Menschliche Pause zwischen den Teilen, wie auf den anderen
            # Posting-Wegen ueblich.
            self.human_pause(6.0, 12.0)
            if not self._post_thread_reply(previous_url, part):
                self._log_thread_break(
                    position,
                    total,
                    root_id,
                    "Antwort nicht bestaetigt",
                )
                return False
            part_url = self._capture_new_own_status_url(known_urls, expected_text=part)
            self._record_thread_part(
                text=part,
                position=position,
                total=total,
                status_url=part_url,
                root_id=root_id,
                topic=topic,
            )
            if not part_url:
                self._log_thread_break(
                    position,
                    total,
                    root_id,
                    "keine Status-URL fuer diesen Teil",
                )
                return False
            known_urls.add(part_url)
            previous_url = part_url

        self.safe_log(
            f"[THREAD] Vollstaendig gepostet: {total} Teile, Wurzel-Record {root_id}",
            "TWEETS",
            "INFO",
        )
        return True

    def _resolve_published_content(
        self,
        payload: dict,
        final_text: str,
        *,
        thread_parts: Optional[List[str]],
        thread_posted: bool,
    ) -> tuple[dict, str]:
        """Return the payload/text that X actually received for learning."""
        published_payload = dict(payload) if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
        published_text = str(final_text or "")
        if not thread_posted or not thread_parts:
            return published_payload, published_text

        first_part = str(thread_parts[0])
        generation_metadata = getattr(
            self,
            "_last_thread_generation_metadata",
            {},
        )
        if isinstance(generation_metadata, dict):
            published_payload.update(generation_metadata)
        published_payload.update({
            "text": first_part,
            "source": "llm_thread",
            "format": "thread_first_part",
            "tone": "THREAD",
            "darwin_strategy": None,
        })
        if not (
            isinstance(generation_metadata, dict)
            and generation_metadata.get("prompt_version")
        ):
            published_payload["prompt_version"] = "unversioned:llm_thread"
        return published_payload, first_part

    def select_topic(self, pillar=None):
        """Waehlt ein Topic, bevorzugt Trends und historisch starke Themen.

        pillar=1 (F95 Hebel 2): zieht NUR aus config['pillar1_topics'] (Europa-KI-
        Wirtschaft, Saeule 1) — ignoriert search_keywords/best_topics/Trends, sonst
        kippt das Topic zurueck in Audit/Finance (search_keywords sind alle Saeule 2).
        So wird die positive Europa-Gegenstimme (die Differenzierung) ueberhaupt erst
        sichtbar. search_keywords bleibt fuer Follow/Reply reserviert (Finance-Ops).
        """
        if pillar == 1:
            p1 = self.config.get('pillar1_topics', []) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else []
            if p1:
                last_topic = getattr(self, "_last_used_keyword", None)
                # Persistenter Familien-Cooldown: Nach einem Neustart kennt der
                # In-Memory-Cooldown das letzte Thema nicht mehr. Vermeide deshalb
                # die Themenfamilien der letzten Standalone-Eigenposts, nicht nur
                # exakt gleiche Strings (z. B. ASML/Mistral/Chip-Infrastruktur).
                def _topic_family(value):
                    text = str(value or "").casefold()
                    families = (
                        ("infra", ("asml", "mistral", "aleph alpha", "chip", "euv", "hugging face", "infrastruktur", "open-source-llm")),
                        ("sovereignty", ("souverän", "souveraen", "us-cloud", "ki-souveränität", "ki-souveraenitaet")),
                        ("governance", ("ai act", "dsgvo", "audit", "compliance", "audit-trail")),
                        ("cost", ("kosten", "finops", "energie", "cfo")),
                        ("workforce", ("arbeitsplatz", "fachkräft", "fachkraeft", "produktivität", "produktivitaet")),
                        ("automation", ("automatisierung", "agenten")),
                    )
                    for family, terms in families:
                        if any(term in text for term in terms):
                            return family
                    return text.strip()

                recent_topics = []
                if last_topic:
                    recent_topics.append(last_topic)
                try:
                    learner_data = getattr(getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None), "_data", {})
                    for entry in reversed((learner_data or {}).get("tweets", [])):
                        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                            continue
                        source = str(entry.get("source") or "").casefold()
                        if source in {"reply", "quote_tweet", "quote", "thread", "thread_part"}:
                            continue
                        topic = str(entry.get("topic") or "").strip()
                        text = str(entry.get("text") or "").strip()
                        generic_topics = {"", "europa", "europe", "news", "trends", "trend", "tweet", "ai", "ki", "quote"}
                        if topic or text:
                            # Historisch generische Topic-Felder (z. B. nur
                            # "europa") werden mit dem Text aufgeloest. Bei
                            # konkreten Topics bleibt der Text aussen vor, damit
                            # beiläufige Wörter wie "Audit" keine False Positives
                            # im Familien-Cooldown erzeugen.
                            recent_topics.append(
                                f"{topic} {text}" if topic.casefold() in generic_topics else topic
                            )
                        if len(recent_topics) >= 5:
                            break
                except Exception:
                    recent_topics = [last_topic] if last_topic else []
                recent_families = {_topic_family(topic) for topic in recent_topics if topic}
                family_available = [
                    topic for topic in p1
                    if topic != last_topic and _topic_family(topic) not in recent_families
                ]
                available = family_available or [topic for topic in p1 if topic != last_topic] or list(p1)
                choice = random.choice(available)
                self._last_used_keyword = choice
                self.safe_log(f"[PILLAR1] Europa-Saeule-1-Topic gewaehlt: {choice}", "PILLAR1", "INFO")
                return choice
            # kein pillar1_topics konfiguriert -> normale Auswahl als Fallback
        fallback_pool = self.config.get('search_keywords', []) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else []
        best_topic_rates = {}
        legacy_best_topic_names = []
        try:
            # Der bestehende Trendbonus bleibt auf seiner bisherigen
            # Drei-Messungen-Schwelle; der kleine Reach-Impuls darunter darf
            # Trendranking und -gewichtung nicht rueckwirkend veraendern.
            _legacy_ranked_topics = (
                self.tweet_learner.get_best_topics(min_tweets=3)
                if self.tweet_learner else []
            )
            legacy_best_topic_names = [
                str(_topic).strip()
                for _topic, _rate in _legacy_ranked_topics
                if str(_topic or "").strip()
            ]
            # Zwei verifizierte Standalone-Messungen erlauben einen kleinen,
            # klar begrenzten Reach-Impuls (z. B. CFO-Tools DACH: 53/173 Views).
            # Nur positive Signale werden ueber den normalen Fallback-Pool
            # hinaus gewichtet; schwache/0%-Topics bleiben reine Exploration.
            _ranked_topics = (
                self.tweet_learner.get_best_topics(min_tweets=2)
                if self.tweet_learner else []
            )
            best_topics = []
            for _topic, _rate in _ranked_topics:
                try:
                    _rate = float(_rate)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    continue
                if not math.isfinite(_rate) or _rate <= 0:
                    continue
                _topic = str(_topic or "").strip()
                if not _topic or _topic in best_topic_rates:
                    continue
                best_topics.append(_topic)
                best_topic_rates[_topic] = min(1.0, _rate)
        except Exception:
            best_topics = []
            best_topic_rates = {}
            legacy_best_topic_names = []

        trending_topics = []
        trending_meta = {}
        try:
            if getattr(self, 'trend_analyzer', None):
                trends = self.trend_analyzer.get_current_trends() or []
                for t in trends:
                    if isinstance(t, dict) and t.get('topic'):
                        topic = t['topic']
                        # A broad trend is useful only when it can be translated
                        # into the account's CFO/automation/governance lens.
                        persona = getattr(self, 'persona_agent', None)
                        if persona and hasattr(persona, 'topic_fit_score'):
                            try:
                                if persona.topic_fit_score(topic) <= 0:
                                    self.safe_log(
                                        f"Trend wegen Profil-Fit verworfen: {topic}",
                                        "TRENDS", "DEBUG",
                                    )
                                    continue
                            except Exception:
                                continue
                        trending_topics.append(topic)
                        trending_meta[topic] = t
                    elif isinstance(t, str):
                        persona = getattr(self, 'persona_agent', None)
                        if persona and hasattr(persona, 'topic_fit_score') and persona.topic_fit_score(t) <= 0:
                            continue
                        trending_topics.append(t)
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Trend-Signal nicht verfuegbar: {e}", "TRENDS", "DEBUG")

        candidates = []
        used = set()
        for topic in trending_topics:
            if topic and topic not in used:
                meta = trending_meta.get(topic, {})
                try:
                    post_count = max(0, int(meta.get('post_count', 0)))
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    post_count = 0
                # Volume is a bounded tie-breaker, never a license to break
                # profile relevance or the existing quality gate.
                popularity_bonus = min(2.0, math.log10(post_count) if post_count > 0 else 0.0)
                weight = 2.0 + popularity_bonus + (
                    1.0 if topic in legacy_best_topic_names else 0
                )
                if topic == getattr(self, "_last_used_keyword", None) and len(candidates) > 0:
                    # Topic-Cooldown: eine vorhandene Alternative ist besser als
                    # zwei direkt aufeinanderfolgende Posts mit derselben These.
                    weight *= 0.25
                candidates.append((topic, weight))
                used.add(topic)
        for topic in best_topics:
            if topic and topic not in used:
                # Rate wird sanft in den Weight uebersetzt (max +0.6), damit
                # kleine Stichproben nicht zu einem harten Themen-Lock fuehren.
                weight = 1.0 + (0.6 * best_topic_rates.get(topic, 0.0))
                if topic == getattr(self, "_last_used_keyword", None) and len(candidates) > 0:
                    weight *= 0.25
                candidates.append((topic, weight))
                used.add(topic)
        for topic in fallback_pool or ["AI"]:
            if topic and topic not in used:
                weight = 1.0
                if topic == getattr(self, "_last_used_keyword", None) and len(candidates) > 0:
                    weight *= 0.25
                candidates.append((topic, weight))
                used.add(topic)

        if getattr(self, "session_plan", None):
            try:
                from intelligent_features.session_planner import SessionPlanner

                candidates = SessionPlanner.apply_topic_bias(candidates, self.session_plan)
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"[PLAN] Topic-Bias fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "PLANNER", "DEBUG")

        topics_only = [c[0] for c in candidates]
        weights = [max(0.1, c[1]) for c in candidates]
        if not topics_only:
            return "AI"
        try:
            choice = random.choices(topics_only, weights=weights, k=1)[0]
        except Exception:
            choice = topics_only[0]
        self._last_used_keyword = choice
        if choice in trending_topics:
            self.safe_log(f"Trend-basiertes Topic gewaehlt: {choice}", "TRENDS", "DEBUG")
        elif choice in best_topics:
            self.safe_log(f"Historisch starkes Topic gewaehlt: {choice}", "LEARNING", "DEBUG")
        return choice
    
    def _init_observation_bank(self):
        """Load ObservationBank and refill if needed."""
        try:
            from content.observation_bank import ObservationBank
            self._observation_bank = ObservationBank()
            stats = self._observation_bank.stats()
            self.safe_log(
                f"ObservationBank geladen: {stats['total']} total, {stats['unused']} unused",
                "OBSERVATIONS", "INFO",
            )

            # Recycle if all used but we have entries
            if stats['unused'] == 0 and stats['total'] > 0:
                self._observation_bank.reset_all()
                self.safe_log("ObservationBank recycelt (alle Eintraege zurueckgesetzt)", "OBSERVATIONS", "INFO")

            # Refill if < 10 unused
            if self._observation_bank.unused_count < 10:
                self._refill_observation_bank(count=5)

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"ObservationBank Init fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "OBSERVATIONS", "WARNING")
            self._observation_bank = None

    def _refill_observation_bank(self, count=3):
        """Refill observation bank from articles when running low."""
        bank = getattr(self, '_observation_bank', None)
        if not bank:
            return

        try:
            from content.linkedin_tweet_generator import LinkedInTweetGenerator
            gen = LinkedInTweetGenerator(config_path=self.config_file)
            if not gen.is_configured:
                self.safe_log("LinkedInTweetGenerator nicht konfiguriert - kein Refill", "OBSERVATIONS", "DEBUG")
                return

            observations = gen.extract_observations_batch(count=count)
            if observations:
                added = bank.add_many(observations, source="linkedin")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"ObservationBank aufgefuellt: {added} neue Observations aus {count} Artikeln",
                    "OBSERVATIONS", "INFO",
                )
            else:
                self.safe_log("Keine neuen Observations aus Artikeln extrahiert", "OBSERVATIONS", "DEBUG")

            # Recycle if still empty
            if bank.unused_count == 0 and bank.total > 0:
                bank.reset_all()
                self.safe_log("ObservationBank recycelt nach Refill-Versuch", "OBSERVATIONS", "INFO")

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"ObservationBank Refill fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "OBSERVATIONS", "WARNING")

    def _run_querdenker_director(self):
        """Querdenker als Themengeber: bei Session-Start zuerst ein unterbesetztes
        Thema waehlen und als Vorrang-Seeds in die ObservationBank legen.

        Vollstaendig defensiv: faellt etwas aus, laeuft die Session normal weiter
        (Posting dann aus der RSS-Reserve). Die Seeds durchlaufen beim Posten
        weiterhin TensionGenerator + Unified Quality Gate.
        """
        bank = getattr(self, '_observation_bank', None)
        if not bank:
