            temperature = min(0.8, temperature)

        if style_mode == "punchy":
            candidate_briefs = [
                "Angle A: eine klare, belegbare Entscheidungsspannung.",
                "Angle B: Starte mit einem konkreten Arbeitsvorgang aus dem Thema; zeige knapp, was leichter wird oder wo Nacharbeit bleibt. Keine erfundene Fallgeschichte.",
                "Angle C: ein konkreter Mechanismus ohne erfundene Geschichte.",
            ]
        elif style_mode == "detailed":
            candidate_briefs = [
                "Angle A: erklaere einen realen Mechanismus aus dem Input.",
                "Angle B: Starte mit einem konkreten Arbeitsvorgang aus dem Thema; erklaere den Nutzen oder die verbleibende Nacharbeit. Keine erfundene Fallgeschichte.",
                "Angle C: benenne einen ehrlichen Trade-off und seine Grenze.",
            ]
        else:
            candidate_briefs = [
                "Angle A: KI-Implementierung aus Operator-Sicht.",
                "Angle B: Starte mit einem konkreten Arbeitsvorgang aus dem Thema; zeige Nutzen oder Nacharbeit in Alltagssprache. Keine erfundene Fallgeschichte.",
                "Angle C: Sport-/Wingsurf-Linse nur bei belegtem, echtem Bezug.",
            ]

        return {
            "style_mode": style_mode,
            "model_preference": model_preference,
            "provider_hint": provider_hint,
            "preferred_tones": preferred_tones,
            "prompt_emphasis": prompt_emphasis,
            "content_format": content_format,
            "format_weight": format_weight,
            "format_explored": format_explored,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "candidate_briefs": candidate_briefs,
        }

    # H114 Pre-AY (2026-05-24): Sprach-Heuristik als Helper extrahiert.
    # Stage 1.5 ruft sie auf, Stage 6.5 (BRAIN-Pre-Improve) ruft sie nach jeder
    # LLM-Rewrite-Operation erneut auf — sonst kommen englische BRAIN-Versionen
    # am Sprach-Gate vorbei (Beweis: Live-Tweet 24.05 07:41 "Last month, you posted...").
    _LANG_EN_PAT = re.compile(r"\b(the|a|an|and|or|to|of|in|on|at|by|for|from|with|that|this|these|those|you|your|my|me|we|us|our|they|them|their|he|she|it|his|her|its|is|are|was|were|been|being|am|have|has|had|having|do|does|did|done|doing|can|could|will|would|should|shall|may|might|must|isn'?t|aren'?t|wasn'?t|weren'?t|don'?t|doesn'?t|didn'?t|won'?t|can'?t|i'?m|i'?ll|i'?ve|every|never|always|nothing|something|anything|here|there|when|where|which|while|because|about|after|before|between|through|each|all|any|more|most|less|least|much|many|some|than|then|so|too|very|just|only|even|still|also|like|than|but|if|how|why|what|who|whose|both|one|two|three|first|last|next|new|old|good|bad|big|small|long|short|right|wrong|same|other|own|few|each|into|over|under|out|off|up|down|back|away|again|much|more|need|needs|needed|make|makes|made|get|gets|got|go|goes|went|come|comes|came|see|sees|saw|seen|know|knows|knew|known|think|thinks|thought|say|says|said|use|uses|used|using|work|works|worked|working|cost|costs|tried|try|fail|fails|failed)\b", re.I)
    _LANG_DE_PAT = re.compile(r"\b(der|die|das|den|dem|des|ein|eine|einen|einem|einer|eines|mit|von|nach|aus|bei|im|in|am|an|auf|für|über|unter|gegen|durch|zur|zum|vom|ins|ans|beim|um|als|nicht|nur|sehr|mehr|wieder|schon|immer|kein|keine|seit|hier|dort|noch|auch|aber|oder|und|wenn|dann|weil|dass|ob|doch|sondern|also|sind|wird|werden|haben|hat|hatte|gibt|gab|ist|war|bin|bist|sein|ihre|sie|er|wir|uns|unser|mein|dein|meine|deine|seine|jeder|jede|jedes|alle|viele|wenig|nichts|etwas|alles|wo|wer|wie|was|warum|wann|sich|pro|je|nein|ja|wirklich|eigentlich|natürlich|tatsächlich|trotzdem|deshalb|darum|daher|dafür|dagegen|dazu|davon|davor|danach|jetzt|gerade|heute|gestern|morgen|wochen|tage|jahr|jahre|monat|monate|stunden|minuten)\b", re.I)
    _LANG_UMLAUT_PAT = re.compile(r"[äöüÄÖÜß]")

    # F73 (2026-05-28): non-Latin scripts → automatic DROP (Bot postete CJK)
    _LANG_NONLATIN_PAT = re.compile(
        r"[一-鿿"      # CJK Unified Ideographs (Chinese, Japanese Kanji)
        r"぀-ゟ"        # Hiragana
        r"゠-ヿ"        # Katakana
        r"가-힯"        # Hangul (Korean)
        r"Ѐ-ӿ"        # Cyrillic
        r"؀-ۿ"        # Arabic
        r"֐-׿"        # Hebrew
        r"฀-๿"        # Thai
        r"]"
    )

    # F95 (2026-06-09): EINDEUTIG englische Funktions-/Hilfswoerter (KEINE DE-Ueberlappung
    # wie in/an/am, KEINE Anglizismus-Nomen wie Reporting/Workflow, KEINE Programmier-/
    # Tool-Begriffe wie for/do). >=2 unterschiedliche Treffer = klar englischer/MIX-Tweet.
    # Validiert gegen 48 echte Tweets (09.06): faengt den komplett-EN llm_original,
    # 0 Fehlalarme auf deutschen Tweets mit Anglizismen.
    _LANG_EN_STRICT = re.compile(
        r"\b(the|of|and|your|you|what|which|who|whose|does|doesn|with|are|were|was|been|"
        r"has|had|would|should|could|within|without|ensure|ensures|accomplish|accomplishes|"
        r"review|manual|greater|whole|makes|every|never|always|nothing|something|anything|"
        r"here|there|where|when|while|about|after|before|between|through|only|even|than|"
        r"this|that|these|those|they|them|their)\b",
        re.I,
    )

    # F94/F95: Eigener 47€/unter-50€-Kosten-Anker + Buchtitel (NICHT externe 15k€/2M).
    # Spiegelt content_gatekeeper._COST_ANCHOR_PAT — der Reply-Pfad umgeht das Gate,
    # daher muss bot.py den Anker eigenstaendig erkennen.
    _COST_ANCHOR_PAT = re.compile(
        r"47\s*[-–]?\s*(?:€|eur|euro)"
        r"|€\s*[-–]?\s*47"
        r"|unter\s*50\s*[-–]?\s*(?:€|eur|euro)"
        r"|(?:€\s*50|50\s*€)\s*/?\s*monat"
        r"|f(?:ü|ue|u)nfzig\s*(?:euro|€|eur)"
        r"|kostet\s+(?:fast\s+)?nichts",
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    def _is_cost_anchor_drop(self, text):
        """True, wenn der Text den EIGENEN 47€/unter-50€-Kosten-Anker oder Buchtitel
        'Unter Fuenfzig Euro' enthaelt (F94, User-Direktive: komplett raus)."""
        return bool(text and isinstance(text, str) and self._COST_ANCHOR_PAT.search(text))

    def _is_english_drop(self, text):
        """F90 (2026-05-31): DE-Voice-Lock mit Non-Latin-Block (revertiert F71/F73 EN-Pivot).
        DROP wenn: (a) non-Latin script erkannt (CJK/Cyrillic/Arabic/Hebrew/Thai)
        ODER (b) viele EN-Marker UND keine Umlaute UND wenig DE-Marker (= klar Englisch)
        ODER (c) F94 (2026-06-08): ein EINZELNER eingebetteter EN-Satz in sonst
        deutschem Tweet (MIX-Drift). Der Gesamt-Check (b) winkt MIX-Tweets durch,
        weil der deutsche Teil genug Marker liefert — Beispiel: '...47 €/Monat.
        Yet, accountants still struggle with paper chaos...'."""
        if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
            return False
        # Non-Latin (CJK/Cyrillic/Arabic/...) → DROP (egal wie viel)
        if self._LANG_NONLATIN_PAT.search(text):
            return True
        en = len(self._LANG_EN_PAT.findall(text))
        de = len(self._LANG_DE_PAT.findall(text))
        has_umlaut = bool(self._LANG_UMLAUT_PAT.search(text))
        # (b) Gesamttext klar Englisch: viele EN-Marker, keine Umlaute, wenig DE
        if en >= 2 and not has_umlaut and de <= 1:
            return True
        # (d) F95 (2026-06-09): >=2 EINDEUTIG englische Funktionswoerter (strikte
        # Liste ohne in/an/am-Ueberlappung) — faengt komplett-englische Tweets, die
        # (b)/(c) verfehlen, weil "in" doppelt als DE-Marker zaehlt (Beispiel-Tweet
        # "In 3 hours, what 10 auditors accomplish in a week. Does manual review...").
        # Greift unabhaengig von Umlaut/DE-Markern, weil die Liste exklusiv EN ist.
        if len({m.lower() for m in self._LANG_EN_STRICT.findall(text)}) >= 2:
            return True
        # (c) F94: satzweise — fang einzelne eingebettete EN-Saetze.
        # Konservativ (>=3 EN-Funktionswoerter, 0 DE-Marker, kein Umlaut, >=5 Woerter),
        # damit DACH-Anglizismen (Reporting/Workflow/Compliance = keine Funktionswoerter)
        # und kurze CTAs nicht faelschlich gedroppt werden.
        # _de <= 1 (nicht == 0): einige Woerter (an/in/am) stehen in BEIDEN
        # Listen — ein engl. Satz wie "amid an affordable..." zaehlt "an"
        # faelschlich als DE-Marker. Echte deutsche Saetze haben dagegen _en≈0
        # (Anglizismen wie Reporting/Workflow sind Nomen, keine Funktionswoerter).
        for _sent in re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+|\n+", text):
            if len(_sent.split()) < 5:
                continue
            if self._LANG_UMLAUT_PAT.search(_sent):
                continue
            if (len(self._LANG_EN_PAT.findall(_sent)) >= 3
                    and len(self._LANG_DE_PAT.findall(_sent)) <= 1):
                return True
        return False

    @bounded_llm_task(label="quality_gate")
    def _apply_unified_quality_gate(self, text, source, topic=None, skip_gatekeeper=False):
        """Unified quality gate for ALL tweet paths.

        Verdict-only stages (cheapest first):
        1. Final-output guard (20-220 chars, no prompt/markdown leakage)
        2. Banned phrase check
        3. Kill gate (structural)
        3.5 Hook gate (staerkste Aussage muss in Zeile 1 stehen)
        4. Hashtag/Link/Emoji removal
        5. Duplicate check
        6. ContentAuditor (WEAK/TRASH = drop)
        7. Gatekeeper LLM verdict (no rewrite/regeneration)

        Returns: cleaned text or None.
        """
        if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
            return None
        if not text or not isinstance(text, str):
            return None
        text, guard_reason = prepare_final_tweet_text(
            text,
            max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS,
        )
        if not text:
            self.safe_log(
                f"Quality-Gate DROP: final_guard={guard_reason}, source={source}",
                "AUDIT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        # Stage 1: Length
        if len(text) < 20 or len(text) > MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS:
            self.safe_log(
                f"Quality-Gate DROP: Laenge ({len(text)} chars), source={source}",
                "AUDIT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        # Stage 1.5 — H112 Pre-AY (2026-05-23): Output-Sprach-Hard-Gate.
        # F37 (System-Prompt DE-Lock) reicht nicht — LLM imitiert die EN-Beispiele im
        # Prompt-Body. DROP wenn EN-Funktionswort-Dichte hoch UND keine Umlaute UND
        # keine DE-Funktionswörter. Heuristik in _is_english_drop() (H114).
        if self._is_english_drop(text):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[AUDIT] WARNING: LANGUAGE-GATE DROP, source={source}",
                "AUDIT", "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        # Stage 2: Banned phrases
        try:
            from intelligent_features.llm_responder import check_banned_phrases
            banned = check_banned_phrases(text)
            if banned:
                self.safe_log(
                    f'[AUDIT] WARNING: BANNED_PHRASE DROP: phrase="{banned}", source="{source}"',
                    "AUDIT", "WARNING",
                )
                return None
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quality-Gate DROP: required_check_unavailable=banned_phrases", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 2.5: Authenticity gate. Blocks invented first-person incidents
        # and unsupported outcome metrics before any LLM polish can make them
        # sound more believable than they are.
        try:
            from content.authenticity_gate import authenticity_gate
            ok_auth, auth_reason = authenticity_gate(text, source=source)
            if not ok_auth:
                preview = text[:100].replace("\n", " ")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-Gate DROP: authenticity={auth_reason}, source={source}, preview=[{preview}]",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return None
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quality-Gate DROP: required_check_unavailable=authenticity", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 3: Kill gate (structural)
        try:
            from content.kill_gate import kill_gate
            ok, reason = kill_gate(text)
            if not ok:
                preview = text[:100].replace("\n", " ")
                self.safe_log(f"Quality-Gate DROP: kill_gate={reason}, source={source}, preview=[{preview}]", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
                return None
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quality-Gate DROP: required_check_unavailable=kill_gate", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 3.5: Hook-Gate (Erste-Zeile-Struktur).
        # Laeuft bewusst VOR allen LLM-Stages: rein deterministisch, keine
        # API-Kosten fuer Texte, deren staerkste Aussage nicht in Zeile 1 steht.
        try:
            from content.hook_gate import (
                hook_advisories,
                hook_gate,
                repair_conditional_opener,
            )
            ok_hook, hook_reason = hook_gate(text)
            if not ok_hook and str(hook_reason).startswith("conditional_opener"):
                repaired_text, repaired = repair_conditional_opener(text)
                if repaired:
                    repaired_ok, repaired_reason = hook_gate(repaired_text)
                    if repaired_ok:
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[HOOK-REPAIR] Konditionalen Vorbau hinter die These verschoben",
                            "AUDIT",
                            "INFO",
                        )
                        text = repaired_text
                        ok_hook = True
                        hook_reason = "repaired_conditional_opener"
                    else:
                        hook_reason = repaired_reason
            if not ok_hook:
                preview = text[:100].replace("\n", " ")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-Gate DROP: hook_gate={hook_reason}, source={source}, preview=[{preview}]",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                try:
                    monitor = getattr(self, "health_monitor", None)
                    record_hook = getattr(monitor, "on_hook_rejected", None)
                    if callable(record_hook):
                        record_hook(source, hook_reason)
                except Exception:
                    # Hook telemetry must never affect the fail-closed gate.
                    pass
                return None
            for note in hook_advisories(text):
                self.safe_log(f"Hook-Advisory: {note}, source={source}", "AUDIT", "INFO")
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quality-Gate DROP: required_check_unavailable=hook_gate", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 4: Hashtag/Link/Emoji removal
        text = re.sub(r'#\S+', '', text).strip()
        text = re.sub(r'https?://\S+', '', text).strip()
        text = re.compile(
            "[\U0001F600-\U0001F64F\U0001F300-\U0001F5FF"
            "\U0001F680-\U0001F6FF\U0001F1E0-\U0001F1FF"
            "\U00002700-\U000027BF\U0001F900-\U0001F9FF"
            "\U00002600-\U000026FF]",
            flags=re.UNICODE,
        ).sub("", text).strip()
        if len(text) < 20:
            self.safe_log(f"Quality-Gate DROP: zu kurz nach Cleaning ({len(text)} chars), source={source}", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 5: Duplicate check
        if self._is_recent_duplicate_post(text):
            self.safe_log(f"Quality-Gate DROP: Duplikat, source={source}", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 5.5: TypeSafe veto for every standalone source. Identical
        # tournament checks are cached; changed text/history is re-evaluated.
        try:
            from content.typesafe_shadow import veto_reason
            typesafe_veto = veto_reason(text)
        except Exception as exc:
            typesafe_veto = None
            self.safe_log(f"TypeSafe unavailable: {type(exc).__name__}", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
        if typesafe_veto:
            self.safe_log(f"Quality-Gate DROP: {typesafe_veto}, source={source}", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 6: ContentAuditor
        try:
            from content.content_auditor import ContentAuditor
            llm = getattr(self, "llm_responder", None)
            auditor = ContentAuditor(llm_responder=llm, logger=self.safe_log)
            audit_result = auditor.audit(text, topic=topic)
            if self._quality_first_enabled() and audit_result.get("Verdict") != "STRONG":
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-first DROP: audit={audit_result.get('Verdict')}, source={source}",
                    "AUDIT", "INFO",
                )
                return None
            if not auditor.should_post(audit_result):
                verdict = audit_result.get("Verdict", "TRASH")
                failure_mode = audit_result.get("Primary Failure Mode", "Unknown")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-Gate DROP: audit={verdict}, mode={failure_mode}, source={source}",
                    "AUDIT", "WARNING",
                )
                return None
            self.safe_log(
                f"Quality-Gate PASS: audit={audit_result.get('Verdict')}, source={source}",
                "AUDIT", "INFO",
            )
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(
                f"Quality-Gate DROP: ContentAuditor error={e}, source={source}",
                "AUDIT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        # Stage 7: Gatekeeper verdict only. It may score the candidate, but the
        # text chosen by the measured format generator is immutable.
        gatekeeper = getattr(self, "content_gatekeeper", None)
        if self._required_content_gatekeeper() is None:
            return None
        if gatekeeper and not skip_gatekeeper:
            try:
                approved, _ignored_text = gatekeeper.check(
                    text,
                    content_type="tweet",
                )
                if not approved:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"Quality-Gate DROP: Gatekeeper score too low, source={source}",
                        "AUDIT", "WARNING",
                    )
                    return None
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-Gate DROP: Gatekeeper error={e}, source={source}",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return None

        # Final authenticity re-check before posting.
        try:
            from content.authenticity_gate import authenticity_gate
            ok_auth, auth_reason = authenticity_gate(text, source=source)
            if not ok_auth:
                preview = text[:100].replace("\n", " ")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"Quality-Gate DROP: final_authenticity={auth_reason}, source={source}, preview=[{preview}]",
                    "AUDIT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return None
        except ImportError:
            self.safe_log("Quality-Gate DROP: required_check_unavailable=final_authenticity", "AUDIT", "WARNING")
            return None

        # Stage 8.6 — H114 Pre-AY: FINAL Sprach-Check vor return.
        # Letzte Verteidigung: egal welche LLM-Stage rewriten hat, der finale Text
        # MUSS DE sein. Sonst DROP-statt-Post.
        if self._is_english_drop(text):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[AUDIT] WARNING: FINAL LANGUAGE-GATE DROP (post-rewrite EN), source={source}",
                "AUDIT", "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        # Final deterministic post guard after every rewrite stage.
        text, final_guard_reason = prepare_final_tweet_text(
            text,
            max_chars=MAX_FEED_SAFE_CHARS,
        )
        if not text:
            self.safe_log(
                f"Quality-Gate DROP: final_guard_post_rewrite={final_guard_reason}, source={source}",
                "AUDIT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        return text

    @bounded_llm_task(label="tweet_generation")
    def generate_emoji_free_tweet(self):
        """Generiert emoji-freien Tweet via unified pipeline.

        Pipeline priority:
        P1: measured mixed source
        P2: measured LLM original (3 attempts)
        P3: fail closed; fixed templates are retired
        """
        try:
            # P1: MIXED SOURCE - alternate Scrape vs Observation-Tension
            mixed_payload = self._generate_mixed_source_tweet()
            if mixed_payload and mixed_payload.get('text'):
                return mixed_payload

            # P2: LLM ORIGINAL (fresh content, 3 attempts)
            for _llm_attempt in range(3):
                llm_payload = self._generate_llm_original_tweet()
                if llm_payload and llm_payload.get('text'):
                    self.safe_log(f"[TWEETS] INFO: Source=LLM_ORIGINAL (attempt {_llm_attempt+1})", "TWEETS", "INFO")
                    return llm_payload

            # FAIL-CLOSED: If all sources failed, do not post
            self.safe_log(
                "Alle Tweet-Quellen erschoepft - ueberspringe Posting (fail-closed).",
                "TWEETS", "WARNING",
            )
            return None

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Tweet-Generierung: {e}", "TWEETS", "ERROR")
            return None

    def _idle_scroll(self, duration_seconds):
        """Scroll through feed like a real user — variable speed phases."""
        if getattr(self, "_typing_active", False):
            return
        self.safe_log(f"[SCROLL] idle_scroll start ({duration_seconds:.0f}s)", "SCROLL", "DEBUG")
        start = time.time()
        steps = 0

        # Phasen: "skim" (schnell ueberfliegen), "read" (langsam lesen), "pause" (stehen bleiben)
        phase = random.choice(["skim", "read", "read"])  # start meist lesend
        phase_remaining = random.randint(3, 7)  # Schritte bis Phasenwechsel

        while time.time() - start < duration_seconds:
            if getattr(self, "_typing_active", False):
                break

            # Phasenwechsel wenn aufgebraucht
            if phase_remaining <= 0:
                roll = random.random()
                if roll < 0.35:
                    phase = "skim"
                    phase_remaining = random.randint(2, 5)
                elif roll < 0.85:
                    phase = "read"
                    phase_remaining = random.randint(3, 8)
                else:
                    phase = "pause"
                    phase_remaining = 1  # nur 1 langer Stopp

            try:
                if phase == "skim":
                    # Schnelles Durchscrollen — groessere Scrolls, kurze Pausen
                    px = random.randint(200, 450)
                    self.driver.execute_script(
                        f"window.scrollBy({{top: {px}, behavior: 'smooth'}});"
                    )
                    delay = random.uniform(1.5, 4.0)

                elif phase == "read":
                    r = random.random()
                    if r < 0.55:
                        # Kleiner Scroll — einen Tweet weiterlesen
                        px = random.randint(80, 200)
                        self.driver.execute_script(
                            f"window.scrollBy({{top: {px}, behavior: 'smooth'}});"
                        )
                    elif r < 0.80:
                        # Lese-Pause — Position halten
                        pass
                    else:
                        # Zurueckscrollen — nochmal etwas lesen
                        px = -random.randint(30, 100)
                        self.driver.execute_script(
                            f"window.scrollBy({{top: {px}, behavior: 'smooth'}});"
                        )
                    delay = random.uniform(4.0, 12.0)

                else:  # phase == "pause"
                    # Langer Stopp — Tweet genau anschauen / nachdenken
                    delay = random.uniform(10.0, 25.0)

            except Exception:
                delay = random.uniform(3.0, 6.0)

            steps += 1
            phase_remaining -= 1

            # [BRAIN] Feed-Zustand alle 5 Schritte tracken (für suggest_navigation)
            if self.session_brain and steps % 5 == 0:
                try:
                    scroll_y = int(self.driver.execute_script("return window.scrollY || 0;") or 0)
                    self.session_brain.record_feed_state(
                        tweet_count=steps,
                        scroll_position=scroll_y,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass

            time.sleep(delay)

        self.safe_log(f"[SCROLL] idle_scroll end (steps={steps})", "SCROLL", "DEBUG")

        # [BRAIN] Navigation-Empfehlung nach Scroll prüfen
        if self.session_brain:
            try:
                nav_url = self.session_brain.suggest_navigation()
                if nav_url:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[BRAIN] Feed erschöpft → navigiere zu {nav_url}", "BRAIN", "DEBUG"
                    )
                    self.driver.get(nav_url)
                    self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Ausschließlich diese Quellen können heute hochwertige, eigenständige
    # Posts erzeugen. Die Liste ist absichtlich eine einzige Autorität für
    # Auswahl und Outcome-Policy; stillgelegte Arme dürfen keine Stichprobe
    # mehr blockieren oder unbemerkt wieder im Tournament auftauchen.
    _ACTIVE_DARWIN_STRATEGIES = ("scrape_rewrite", "llm_original")

    @classmethod
    def _active_darwin_strategies(cls) -> list[str]:
        return list(cls._ACTIVE_DARWIN_STRATEGIES)

    @staticmethod
    def _recent_published_darwin_strategies(
        instance,
        active: list[str],
    ) -> Optional[list[str]]:
        """Gibt echte Publish-Historie für eine kandidatensichere Lernphase zurück."""
        learner = getattr(instance, "tweet_learner", None)
        get_recent = getattr(learner, "get_recent_darwin_strategies", None)
        if not callable(get_recent):
            return None
        try:
            recent = get_recent(limit=max(1, len(active) - 1))
        except Exception:
            return None
        return [
            strategy
            for strategy in (recent or [])
            if str(strategy) in active
        ]

    def _get_darwin(self):
        """Lazy-Singleton für die idempotente Content-Outcome-Policy."""
        if not hasattr(self, '_darwin'):
            try:
                from learning.content_strategy_darwin import ContentStrategyDarwin
                self._darwin = ContentStrategyDarwin()
                # Source-Raten werden ausschließlich aus verifizierten 24h-Outcomes
                # innerhalb der Darwin-Policy abgeleitet. Der frühere Integer-Bias
                # vermischte 0..1-Gewichte mit 5..50 und wurde deshalb entfernt.
                self.safe_log("[DARWIN] ContentStrategyDarwin geladen", "DARWIN", "DEBUG")
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(f"[DARWIN] Import fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "DARWIN", "WARNING")
                self._darwin = None
        return self._darwin

    def _generate_mixed_source_tweet(self):
        """Darwinistisch gewichtete Auswahl zwischen aktiven, gemessenen Quellen.

        observation_tension deaktiviert (0 Wins, 26 Losses laut Darwin-State).
        Nutzt ContentStrategyDarwin für gewichteten Zufall.
        Fallback: Gleichverteilung wenn Darwin nicht verfügbar.
        """
        news_cfg = self.config.get("news_commentary", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
        if isinstance(news_cfg, dict) and bool(news_cfg.get("enabled", True)):
            force_first = bool(news_cfg.get("force_first_tweet", True))
            if force_first and int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0) <= 0:
                payload = self._generate_news_commentary_tweet()
                if payload and payload.get("text"):
                    payload["darwin_strategy"] = "news_commentary"
                    return payload
                self.safe_log("[NEWS] NEWS_COMMENTARY leer -> Fallback Mixed-Source", "NEWS", "DEBUG")

        darwin = self._get_darwin()
        # observation_tension entfernt: 0 Wins, 26 Losses — komplett ineffektiv
        available = ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced._active_darwin_strategies()

        forced_once = getattr(self, "_darwin_forced_strategy_once", None)
        if forced_once in available:
            chosen = forced_once
            self._darwin_forced_strategy_once = None
        elif darwin is not None:
            chosen = None
            try:
                pick_bootstrap = getattr(darwin, "pick_bootstrap_strategy", None)
                if callable(pick_bootstrap):
                    recent_published = (
                        ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced
                        ._recent_published_darwin_strategies(self, available)
                    )
                    try:
                        bootstrap_choice = pick_bootstrap(
                            available,
                            recent_published=recent_published,
                        )
                    except TypeError:
                        bootstrap_choice = pick_bootstrap(available)
                    if bootstrap_choice in available:
                        chosen = bootstrap_choice
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[DARWIN] Faire Lernphase: {chosen}",
                            "DARWIN",
                            "DEBUG",
                        )
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[DARWIN] Bootstrap-Auswahl fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                    "DARWIN",
                    "DEBUG",
                )
            if chosen is None:
                try:
                    weights = self._get_adaptive_source_weights()
                    chosen = random.choices(
                        list(weights),
                        weights=list(weights.values()),
                        k=1,
                    )[0]
                except Exception:
                    chosen = darwin.pick_strategy(available=available)
        else:
            import random as _random
            chosen = _random.choice(available)

        self.safe_log(f"[DARWIN] Mixed-Source gewählt: {chosen}", "DARWIN", "DEBUG")

        if chosen == "scrape_rewrite":
            payload = self._generate_scraped_tweet_payload()
            if payload and payload.get('text'):
                self.safe_log("[TWEETS] INFO: Source=SCRAPE_REWRITE (DARWIN)", "TWEETS", "INFO")
                payload["darwin_strategy"] = "scrape_rewrite"
                return payload
            # Fallback auf llm_original
            self.safe_log("[DARWIN] scrape_rewrite leer -> Fallback llm_original", "DARWIN", "DEBUG")
            payload = self._generate_llm_original_tweet()
            if payload and payload.get('text'):
                payload["darwin_strategy"] = "llm_original"
                return payload
            return None
        elif chosen == "llm_original":
            payload = self._generate_llm_original_tweet()
            if payload and payload.get('text'):
                self.safe_log("[TWEETS] INFO: Source=LLM_ORIGINAL (DARWIN)", "TWEETS", "INFO")
                payload["darwin_strategy"] = "llm_original"
                return payload
            # Fallback auf scrape_rewrite
            self.safe_log("[DARWIN] llm_original leer -> Fallback scrape_rewrite", "DARWIN", "DEBUG")
            payload = self._generate_scraped_tweet_payload()
            if payload and payload.get('text'):
                payload["darwin_strategy"] = "scrape_rewrite"
                return payload
            return None
        return None

    def _generate_strict_template_tweet(self):
        """Retired: fixed templates cannot satisfy measured format rotation."""
        self.safe_log(
            "STRICT_TEMPLATE dauerhaft deaktiviert; kein ungemessener Fallback.",
            "TWEETS",
            "INFO",
        )
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Engagement Feedback Loop: eigene Tweets pruefen + Source-Gewichte
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    _REACH_CAPPED_OUTCOME_SOURCES = {"reply", "quote", "quote_tweet"}
    _FOLLOWUP_OUTCOME_HORIZONS = {
        "48h": ("standalone_48h", 48.0),
        "7d": ("standalone_7d", 168.0),
    }
    _FOLLOWUP_BLOCKED_SOURCES = {
        "reply", "quote", "quote_tweet", "book_promo",
    }
    _FOLLOWUP_CANONICAL_STATUS_RE = re.compile(
        r"https://(?:x|twitter)\.com/"
        r"([A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15})/status/(\d+)",
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    @classmethod
    def _status_identity_from_url(cls, url: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """Extrahiert nur aus einer exakten, navigierbaren Status-URL."""
        match = cls._FOLLOWUP_CANONICAL_STATUS_RE.fullmatch(
            str(url or "").strip()
        )
        if match is None:
            return "", ""
        return match.group(1).lstrip("@"), match.group(2)

    @classmethod
    def _validated_owned_status_url(cls, tweet_info: dict, config: dict) -> str:
        """Validiert eine direkte X-Navigation vollstaendig und fail-closed."""
        candidate = str(
            tweet_info.get("canonical_url")
            or tweet_info.get("tweet_url")
            or tweet_info.get("url")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        expected_author = str(
            tweet_info.get("own_username")
            or (config or {}).get("own_username")
            or ""
        ).strip().lstrip("@")
        expected_status_id = str(tweet_info.get("status_id") or "").strip()
        if (
            not candidate
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15}", expected_author)
            or not expected_status_id.isdigit()
        ):
            return ""
        try:
            parsed = urlsplit(candidate)
            if (
                parsed.scheme.casefold() != "https"
                or parsed.hostname is None
                or parsed.hostname.casefold() not in {"x.com", "twitter.com"}
                or parsed.netloc.casefold() != parsed.hostname.casefold()
                or parsed.username is not None
                or parsed.password is not None
                or parsed.port is not None
                or parsed.query
                or parsed.fragment
            ):
                return ""
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return ""
        expected_path = f"/{expected_author}/status/{expected_status_id}"
        if parsed.path.casefold() != expected_path.casefold():
            return ""
        match = cls._FOLLOWUP_CANONICAL_STATUS_RE.fullmatch(candidate)
        if (
            match is None
            or match.group(1).casefold() != expected_author.casefold()
            or match.group(2) != expected_status_id
        ):
            return ""
        return candidate

    def _verify_owned_status_article(self, article, tweet_info: dict) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
        """Verifiziert Autor UND Status-ID im konkreten Tweet-Article.

        Text-Matching allein ist fuer Lern-Outcomes nicht ausreichend: aehnliche
        Posts oder ein Parent-Tweet koennten sonst fremde Views zuschreiben.
        """
        canonical_url = (
            tweet_info.get("canonical_url")
            or tweet_info.get("tweet_url")
            or ""
        )
        url_author, url_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(canonical_url)
        expected_author = str(
            tweet_info.get("own_username")
            or self.config.get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
            or ""
        ).lstrip("@")
        expected_status_id = str(tweet_info.get("status_id") or url_status_id or "")

        configured_author = str(self.config.get("own_username") or "").lstrip("@")
        if (
            not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15}", expected_author)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,20}", expected_status_id)
            or (configured_author and configured_author.casefold() != expected_author.casefold())
        ):
            return False, "", ""
        if canonical_url and (
            url_author.casefold() != expected_author.casefold()
            or url_status_id != expected_status_id
        ):
            return False, "", ""

        try:
            primary_urls = article.evaluate(
                """(root, author) => {
                    if (!root.matches('article[data-testid="tweet"]') ||
                        root.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') ||
                        root.parentElement?.closest('article')) return [];
                    const direct = node => node.closest('article') === root &&
                        !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]');
                    const ownAuthor = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(
                        '[data-testid="User-Name"] a[href]'
                    )).filter(direct).some(link => {
                        try {
                            const url = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), 'https://x.com');
                            return ['https://x.com', 'https://twitter.com'].includes(url.origin) &&
                                !url.username && !url.password && !url.search && !url.hash &&
                                url.pathname.toLowerCase() === '/' + author.toLowerCase();
                        } catch (_) { return false; }
                    });
                    if (!ownAuthor) return [];
                    return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('a[href]'))
                        .filter(direct)
                        .filter(link => Array.from(link.querySelectorAll('time')).some(direct))
                        .map(link => new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), 'https://x.com').href);
                }""",
                expected_author,
            )
            if not isinstance(primary_urls, list) or not primary_urls:
                return False, "", ""
            identities = {
                (author.casefold(), status_id)
                for url in primary_urls
                for author, status_id in [self._status_identity_from_url(url)]
            }
            if identities != {(expected_author.casefold(), expected_status_id)}:
                return False, "", ""
        except Exception:
            return False, "", ""
        return True, expected_author, expected_status_id

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_outcome_reward(metrics: dict) -> float:
        """Robuster View-dominanter Reward mit gedeckelten Neben-Signalen."""
        import math

        views = max(0, int(metrics.get("views", 0) or 0))
        likes = min(10, max(0, int(metrics.get("likes", 0) or 0)))
        retweets = min(5, max(0, int(metrics.get("retweets", 0) or 0)))
        replies = min(5, max(0, int(metrics.get("replies", 0) or 0)))
        return round(math.log1p(views) + likes * 0.15 + retweets * 0.20 + replies * 0.10, 6)

    def _record_provisional_outcome(self, tweet_info: dict, metrics: dict) -> bool:
        """Speichert einen 30m-Snapshot rein diagnostisch, ohne Policy-Update."""
        ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        tweet_id = str(tweet_info.get("tweet_id") or tweet_info.get("id") or "")
        canonical_url = str(
            tweet_info.get("canonical_url") or tweet_info.get("url")
            or tweet_info.get("tweet_url") or ""
        )
        _, status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(canonical_url)
        outcome_id = f"x:{tweet_info.get('status_id') or status_id}" if (tweet_info.get("status_id") or status_id) else ""
        if not ledger or not learner or not tweet_id or not outcome_id:
            return False
        try:
            ledger.record_provisional(
                outcome_id=outcome_id,
                tweet_id=tweet_id,
                canonical_url=canonical_url,
                observed_at=datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),
                metrics=metrics,
            )
            learner.record_provisional_observation(
                tweet_id,
                likes=metrics.get("likes", 0),
                views=metrics.get("views", 0),
                observed_at="30m",
            )
            return True
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(f"[OUTCOME] Provisional fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "LEARNING", "WARNING")
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def _validated_outcome_metrics(metrics: dict) -> Optional[dict]:
        """Unknown counters are not measured zeros; accept only complete counts."""
        if not isinstance(metrics, dict):
            return None
        result = {}
        for key in ("likes", "views", "retweets", "replies"):
            raw = metrics.get(key, metrics.get("comments") if key == "replies" else None)
            if isinstance(raw, bool) or raw is None:
                return None
            if not isinstance(raw, (int, float, str)):
                return None
            if isinstance(raw, str) and not raw.strip().isascii():
                return None
            if isinstance(raw, str) and not raw.strip().isdigit():
                return None
            try:
                value = int(raw)
                if isinstance(raw, float) and (not math.isfinite(raw) or raw != value):
                    return None
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                return None
            if value < 0:
                return None
            result[key] = value
        return result

    def _finalize_outcome_once(
        self,
        tweet_info: dict,
        metrics: dict,
        *,
        verified: bool,
        observed_author: str = "",
        observed_status_id: str = "",
        verification_reason: str = "",
        measurement_age_hours: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Einziger Consumer-Gateway fuer verifizierte 24h-Reichweiten-Outcomes."""
        ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        tweet_id = str(tweet_info.get("id") or tweet_info.get("tweet_id") or "")
        canonical_url = str(
            tweet_info.get("canonical_url") or tweet_info.get("tweet_url")
            or tweet_info.get("url") or ""
        )
        expected_author = str(
            tweet_info.get("own_username")
            or self.config.get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
            or ""
        ).lstrip("@")
        _, url_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(canonical_url)
        status_id = str(tweet_info.get("status_id") or url_status_id or "")
        outcome_id = f"x:{status_id}" if status_id else f"legacy:{tweet_id}"
        source = str(tweet_info.get("source") or "unknown")
        capped = source in self._REACH_CAPPED_OUTCOME_SOURCES or source.startswith("cmo_")
        cohort = "standalone_24h" if not capped else "capped_24h"
        identity_complete = bool(expected_author and status_id and canonical_url)
        verified = bool(verified and identity_complete and not capped)

        if not ledger or not learner or not tweet_id:
            return False

        if cohort == "standalone_24h":
            if measurement_age_hours is None:
                try:
                    measurement_age_hours = (
                        time.time() - float(tweet_info.get("timestamp"))
                    ) / 3600.0
                except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                    self.safe_log(
                        "[OUTCOME] 24h-Messalter fehlt; Messung bleibt offen",
                        "LEARNING",
                        "WARNING",
                    )
                    return False
            try:
                measurement_age_hours = float(measurement_age_hours)
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                return False
            if (
                not math.isfinite(measurement_age_hours)
                or measurement_age_hours < 24.0
                or measurement_age_hours > 48.0
            ):
                self.safe_log(
                    "[OUTCOME] 24h-Messung ausserhalb Fenster "
                    f"(age={measurement_age_hours!r}); kein Lernsignal",
                    "LEARNING",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

        normalized_metrics = self._validated_outcome_metrics(metrics)
        if normalized_metrics is None:
            self.safe_log(
                "[OUTCOME] Unvollstaendige oder ungueltige Metriken; Messung bleibt offen",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False
        parser_artifact = (
            normalized_metrics["views"] >= 10_000
            and normalized_metrics["likes"] == 0
        )
        if parser_artifact:
            normalized_metrics["views"] = 0
            verified = False
            verification_reason = verification_reason or "implausible_views_without_likes"
        reward = self._compute_outcome_reward(normalized_metrics)
        darwin = self._get_darwin()
        try:
            avg_views = learner.get_avg_views_standalone()
        except Exception:
            avg_views = None
        if darwin is not None:
            threshold = darwin.effective_reach_win_views(avg_views)
            is_win = darwin.is_win_for_source(
                normalized_metrics["likes"],
                normalized_metrics["views"],
                source,
                reach_win_views=threshold,
            )
        else:
            # Deterministischer Fail-safe, falls das Policy-Modul temporaer fehlt.
            is_win = bool(
                normalized_metrics["views"] >= 90
                or normalized_metrics["likes"] >= 2
                or (
                    normalized_metrics["likes"] >= 1
                    and normalized_metrics["views"] >= 60
                )
            )
        darwin_strategy = str(tweet_info.get("darwin_strategy") or "")
        topic = str(tweet_info.get("topic") or "")
        content_metadata = (
            dict(tweet_info.get("content_metadata") or {})
            if isinstance(tweet_info.get("content_metadata"), dict)
            else {}
        )
        try:
            final_event = ledger.finalize_once(
                outcome_id=outcome_id,
                verified=verified,
                source=source,
                cohort=cohort,
                observed_at=datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),
                metrics=normalized_metrics,
                tweet_id=tweet_id,
                canonical_url=canonical_url,
                expected_author=expected_author,
                observed_author=observed_author,
                observed_status_id=observed_status_id,
                strategy=darwin_strategy,
                topic=topic,
                reward=reward,
                success=is_win,
                content_metadata=content_metadata,
                measurement_target_hours=(
                    24.0 if cohort == "standalone_24h" else None
                ),
                measurement_age_hours=(
                    measurement_age_hours
                    if cohort == "standalone_24h"
                    else None
                ),
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(f"[OUTCOME] Finalisierung fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "LEARNING", "WARNING")
            return False

        final_event_tweet_id = str(final_event.get("tweet_id") or "")
        if final_event_tweet_id != tweet_id:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Final-Event/Tweet-ID-Mismatch: "
                f"event={final_event_tweet_id or '<missing>'} current={tweet_id}",
                "LEARNING",
                "ERROR",
            )
            return False

        canonical_metrics = self._validated_outcome_metrics(final_event.get("metrics"))
        if canonical_metrics is None:
            self.safe_log(
                "[OUTCOME] Kanonische Metriken ungueltig; Consumer bleiben offen",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        if not final_event.get("policy_eligible"):
            reason = verification_reason or "identity_or_cohort_unverified"
            try:
                learner.mark_unverified(tweet_id, reason=reason)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                ledger.mark_consumed(outcome_id)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Kein Lernsignal ({reason}) id={outcome_id}",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        try:
            ledger_health = ledger.health()
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Ledger-Health nicht lesbar, Consumer gesperrt: {exc}",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False
        if not ledger_health.get("policy_updates_allowed", False):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Ledger fehlerhaft, alle Consumer gesperrt "
                f"(errors={ledger_health.get('error_count', 'unknown')})",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        # Der erste Final-Event ist kanonisch. Spaetere Retries duerfen weder
        # Metriken noch Reward/Win-Klassifikation nachtraeglich veraendern.
        normalized_metrics = canonical_metrics
        try:
            stored_reward = final_event.get("reward")
            reward = float(stored_reward) if stored_reward is not None else self._compute_outcome_reward(normalized_metrics)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            reward = self._compute_outcome_reward(normalized_metrics)
        if isinstance(final_event.get("success"), bool):
            is_win = final_event["success"]
        darwin_strategy = str(final_event.get("strategy") or darwin_strategy)
        topic = str(final_event.get("topic") or topic)
        source = str(final_event.get("source") or source)
        newly_finalized = learner.record_engagement(
            tweet_id,
            likes=normalized_metrics["likes"],
            views=normalized_metrics["views"],
            retweets=normalized_metrics["retweets"],
            replies=normalized_metrics["replies"],
            verified=True,
            horizon="24h",
            final_reward=reward,
            final_success=is_win,
            measurement_target_hours=24.0,
            measurement_age_hours=final_event.get(
                "measurement_age_hours"
            ),
        )
        if not newly_finalized:
            already_persisted = getattr(learner, "contains_finalized_outcome", None)
            if not callable(already_persisted) or not already_persisted(
                tweet_id,
                metrics=normalized_metrics,
                reward=reward,
                success=is_win,
            ):
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[OUTCOME] TweetLearner-Consumer offen, Ledger bleibt unconsumed ({outcome_id})",
                    "LEARNING", "WARNING",
                )
                return False

        # Crash-Recovery: solange der Ledger-Event nicht consumed ist, werden die
        # idempotenten Consumer erneut angeboten. Darwin dedupliziert per outcome_id.
        if final_event.get("consumed_at") is None:
            policy_strategies = set(
                ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced._active_darwin_strategies()
            )
            if darwin_strategy in policy_strategies:
                try:
                    if darwin is None:
                        raise RuntimeError("Darwin-Policy nicht verfuegbar")
                    # Recovery must retain measurement age, not rejuvenate an
                    # old outcome merely because its consumer runs today.
                    evidence_at = 0.0
                    try:
                        measured_at = datetime.fromisoformat(str(final_event.get("observed_at") or ""))
                        if measured_at.tzinfo is not None:
                            evidence_at = measured_at.timestamp()
                    except (ValueError, OverflowError, OSError):
                        pass
                    recorded = darwin.record_outcome(
                        outcome_id=outcome_id,
                        strategy=darwin_strategy,
                        success=is_win,
                        reward=reward,
                        allow_policy_update=True,
                        available_strategies=sorted(policy_strategies),
                        evidence_at=evidence_at,
                    )
                    already_recorded = outcome_id in darwin._state.get("outcomes", {})
                    if not recorded and not already_recorded:
                        raise RuntimeError("Darwin-Outcome nicht persistent angenommen")
                except Exception as exc:
                    self.safe_log(f"[OUTCOME] Darwin-Consumer fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "DARWIN", "WARNING")
                    return False
            try:
                ledger.mark_consumed(outcome_id)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(f"[OUTCOME] Consume-Marker fehlgeschlagen: {exc}", "LEARNING", "WARNING")
                return False

        self.safe_log(
            f"[OUTCOME] 24h finalized id={outcome_id} source={source} "
            f"views={normalized_metrics['views']} reward={reward:.3f}",
            "LEARNING", "INFO",
        )
        return bool(newly_finalized)

    def _record_followup_outcome_once(
        self,
        tweet_info: dict,
        metrics: dict,
        horizon: str,
        *,
        verified: bool,
        observed_author: str = "",
        observed_status_id: str = "",
        observed_at: str = "",
        measurement_age_hours: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Persistiert eine verifizierte 48h-/7d-Messung ohne Policy-Update."""
        import math

        if horizon not in self._FOLLOWUP_OUTCOME_HORIZONS:
            raise ValueError("horizon muss 48h oder 7d sein")
        cohort, target_hours = self._FOLLOWUP_OUTCOME_HORIZONS[horizon]

        ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        if ledger is None or learner is None:
            return False

        tweet_id = str(
            tweet_info.get("id") or tweet_info.get("tweet_id") or ""
        ).strip()
        canonical_url = str(
            tweet_info.get("canonical_url")
            or tweet_info.get("tweet_url")
            or tweet_info.get("url")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        source = str(tweet_info.get("source") or "unknown").strip()
        source_key = source.casefold()
        expected_author = str(
            tweet_info.get("own_username")
            or self.config.get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")
            or ""
        ).strip().lstrip("@")
        status_id = str(tweet_info.get("status_id") or "").strip()
        observed_author = str(observed_author or "").strip().lstrip("@")
        observed_status_id = str(observed_status_id or "").strip()

        canonical_match = self._FOLLOWUP_CANONICAL_STATUS_RE.fullmatch(
            canonical_url
        )
        canonical_author = (
            canonical_match.group(1) if canonical_match else ""
        )
        canonical_status_id = (
            canonical_match.group(2) if canonical_match else ""
        )
        identity_verified = bool(
            verified
            and tweet_id
            and expected_author
            and status_id
            and canonical_match
            and canonical_author.casefold() == expected_author.casefold()
            and canonical_status_id == status_id
            and observed_author.casefold() == expected_author.casefold()
            and observed_status_id == status_id
        )
        source_blocked = (
            source_key in self._FOLLOWUP_BLOCKED_SOURCES
            or source_key.startswith("cmo_")
        )
        if not identity_verified or source_blocked:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Follow-up verworfen: "
                f"identity={identity_verified} source={source or '<missing>'}",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        normalized_metrics = self._validated_outcome_metrics(metrics)
        if normalized_metrics is None:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Follow-up unvollstaendig; Messung bleibt offen",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        parser_artifact = bool(
            normalized_metrics["views"] >= 10_000
            and normalized_metrics["likes"] == 0
        )
        if parser_artifact:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Follow-up Parser-Artefakt, "
                "Messung bleibt offen",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        if measurement_age_hours is None:
            try:
                published_at = float(tweet_info.get("timestamp"))
                measurement_age_hours = (
                    time.time() - published_at
                ) / 3600.0
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                return False
        try:
            measurement_age_hours = float(measurement_age_hours)
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            return False
        if (
            not math.isfinite(measurement_age_hours)
            or measurement_age_hours < target_hours
            or measurement_age_hours > target_hours + 24.0
        ):
            return False

        observed_at = str(
            observed_at or datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()
        )
        outcome_id = f"x:{status_id}:{horizon}"
        reward = self._compute_outcome_reward(normalized_metrics)
        success = bool(
            normalized_metrics["views"] >= 90
            or normalized_metrics["likes"] >= 2
            or (
                normalized_metrics["likes"] >= 1
                and normalized_metrics["views"] >= 60
            )
        )
        content_metadata = (
            dict(tweet_info.get("content_metadata") or {})
            if isinstance(tweet_info.get("content_metadata"), dict)
            else {}
        )

        try:
            final_event = ledger.finalize_once(
                outcome_id=outcome_id,
                verified=True,
                source=source,
                cohort=cohort,
                observed_at=observed_at,
                metrics=normalized_metrics,
                tweet_id=tweet_id,
                canonical_url=canonical_url,
                expected_author=expected_author,
                observed_author=observed_author,
                observed_status_id=observed_status_id,
                strategy=str(tweet_info.get("darwin_strategy") or ""),
                topic=str(tweet_info.get("topic") or ""),
                reward=reward,
                success=success,
                content_metadata=content_metadata,
                measurement_target_hours=target_hours,
                measurement_age_hours=measurement_age_hours,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Follow-up Finalisierung fehlgeschlagen: "
                f"{exc}",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        event_identity_ok = bool(
            str(final_event.get("outcome_id") or "") == outcome_id
            and str(final_event.get("tweet_id") or "") == tweet_id
            and str(final_event.get("canonical_url") or "") == canonical_url
            and str(final_event.get("cohort") or "") == cohort
            and str(final_event.get("expected_author") or "").casefold()
            == expected_author.casefold()
            and str(final_event.get("observed_author") or "").casefold()
            == observed_author.casefold()
            and str(final_event.get("observed_status_id") or "")
            == observed_status_id
        )
        if (
            not event_identity_ok
            or final_event.get("measurement_eligible") is not True
            or final_event.get("policy_eligible") is not False
        ):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Follow-up Event nicht messberechtigt "
                f"({outcome_id})",
                "LEARNING",
                "ERROR",
            )
            return False
        if final_event.get("consumed_at") is not None:
            return False

        try:
            event_target_hours = float(
                final_event.get("measurement_target_hours")
            )
            event_age_hours = float(final_event.get("measurement_age_hours"))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            return False
        if (
            not math.isfinite(event_target_hours)
            or event_target_hours != target_hours
            or not math.isfinite(event_age_hours)
            or event_age_hours < target_hours
            or event_age_hours > target_hours + 24.0
        ):
            return False

        event_metrics = self._validated_outcome_metrics(final_event.get("metrics"))
        if event_metrics is None:
            return False
        if (
            event_metrics["views"] >= 10_000
            and event_metrics["likes"] == 0
        ):
            return False

        try:
            event_reward = float(final_event.get("reward"))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            return False
        event_success = final_event.get("success")
        event_observed_at = str(final_event.get("observed_at") or "")
        if (
            not math.isfinite(event_reward)
            or not isinstance(event_success, bool)
            or not event_observed_at
        ):
            return False

        try:
            ledger_health = ledger.health()
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Ledger-Health nicht lesbar, "
                f"{horizon}-Consumer gesperrt: {exc}",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False
        if (
            not ledger_health.get("policy_updates_allowed", False)
            or ledger_health.get("error_count", 0) != 0
        ):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Ledger fehlerhaft, {horizon}-Consumer gesperrt",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        consumer_payload = {
            "horizon": horizon,
            "metrics": event_metrics,
            "observed_at": event_observed_at,
            "reward": event_reward,
            "success": event_success,
            "outcome_id": outcome_id,
            "measurement_age_seconds": event_age_hours * 3600.0,
        }
        try:
            newly_recorded = learner.record_followup_observation(
                tweet_id,
                **consumer_payload,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] TweetLearner-{horizon}-Consumer fehlgeschlagen: "
                f"{exc}",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False
        if not newly_recorded:
            contains = getattr(
                learner, "contains_followup_observation", None
            )
            if not callable(contains):
                contains = getattr(learner, "contains_followup_outcome", None)
            try:
                exact_retry = bool(
                    callable(contains)
                    and contains(tweet_id, **consumer_payload)
                )
            except Exception:
                exact_retry = False
            if not exact_retry:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[OUTCOME] TweetLearner-{horizon}-Consumer offen, "
                    f"Ledger bleibt unconsumed ({outcome_id})",
                    "LEARNING",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

        try:
            ledger.mark_consumed(outcome_id)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {horizon}-Consume-Marker fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "LEARNING",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

        self.safe_log(
            f"[OUTCOME] {horizon} measured id={outcome_id} source={source} "
            f"views={event_metrics['views']} reward={event_reward:.3f}",
            "LEARNING",
            "INFO",
        )
        return bool(newly_recorded)

    def _recover_unconsumed_outcomes(self) -> int:
        """Spielt offene Ledger-Consumer nach einem Crash idempotent erneut ab."""
        ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        if ledger is None or learner is None:
            return 0

        recovered = 0
        try:
            finalized_events = ledger.get_finalized()
            pending = [
                event for event in finalized_events
                if event.get("policy_eligible") and event.get("consumed_at") is None
            ]
            pending_followups = [
                event for event in finalized_events
                if event.get("measurement_eligible")
                and not event.get("policy_eligible")
                and event.get("cohort") in {"standalone_48h", "standalone_7d"}
                and event.get("consumed_at") is None
            ]
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Recovery-Lesezugriff fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "LEARNING", "WARNING",
            )
            return 0

        for event in pending:
            canonical_url = str(event.get("canonical_url") or "")
            url_author, url_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(canonical_url)
            tweet_info = {
                "id": str(event.get("tweet_id") or ""),
                "tweet_id": str(event.get("tweet_id") or ""),
                "canonical_url": canonical_url,
                "tweet_url": canonical_url,
                "status_id": str(event.get("observed_status_id") or url_status_id or ""),
                "own_username": str(
                    event.get("expected_author") or event.get("observed_author")
                    or url_author or ""
                ),
                "source": str(event.get("source") or "unknown"),
                "topic": str(event.get("topic") or ""),
                "darwin_strategy": str(event.get("strategy") or ""),
                "content_metadata": dict(
                    event.get("content_metadata") or {}
                ),
            }
            if not tweet_info["tweet_id"]:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[OUTCOME] Recovery uebersprungen: tweet_id fehlt ({event.get('outcome_id')})",
                    "LEARNING", "WARNING",
                )
                continue
            before = event.get("consumed_at")
            self._finalize_outcome_once(
                tweet_info,
                event.get("metrics") or {},
                verified=True,
                observed_author=str(event.get("observed_author") or url_author or ""),
                observed_status_id=str(event.get("observed_status_id") or url_status_id or ""),
                verification_reason="ledger_recovery",
                measurement_age_hours=event.get("measurement_age_hours"),
            )
            try:
                refreshed = next(
                    row for row in ledger.get_finalized()
                    if row.get("outcome_id") == event.get("outcome_id")
                )
                if before is None and refreshed.get("consumed_at") is not None:
                    recovered += 1
            except Exception:
                continue

        for event in pending_followups:
            canonical_url = str(event.get("canonical_url") or "")
            url_author, url_status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(
                canonical_url
            )
            horizon = (
                "48h"
                if event.get("cohort") == "standalone_48h"
                else "7d"
            )
            tweet_info = {
                "id": str(event.get("tweet_id") or ""),
                "tweet_id": str(event.get("tweet_id") or ""),
                "canonical_url": canonical_url,
                "tweet_url": canonical_url,
                "status_id": str(
                    event.get("observed_status_id") or url_status_id or ""
                ),
                "own_username": str(
                    event.get("expected_author")
                    or event.get("observed_author")
                    or url_author
                    or ""
                ),
                "source": str(event.get("source") or "unknown"),
                "topic": str(event.get("topic") or ""),
                "darwin_strategy": str(event.get("strategy") or ""),
                "content_metadata": dict(
                    event.get("content_metadata") or {}
                ),
            }
            if not tweet_info["tweet_id"]:
                continue
            before = event.get("consumed_at")
            self._record_followup_outcome_once(
                tweet_info,
                event.get("metrics") or {},
                horizon,
                verified=True,
                observed_author=str(
                    event.get("observed_author") or url_author or ""
                ),
                observed_status_id=str(
                    event.get("observed_status_id") or url_status_id or ""
                ),
                observed_at=str(event.get("observed_at") or ""),
                measurement_age_hours=event.get("measurement_age_hours"),
            )
            try:
                refreshed = next(
                    row for row in ledger.get_finalized()
                    if row.get("outcome_id") == event.get("outcome_id")
                )
                if before is None and refreshed.get("consumed_at") is not None:
                    recovered += 1
            except Exception:
                continue

        if recovered:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] {recovered} offene Consumer nach Crash nachgespielt",
                "LEARNING", "INFO",
            )
        return recovered

    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_for_match(text: str) -> str:
        """Normalisiert Text fuer fuzzy Matching: Whitespace/Newlines entfernen, lowercase."""
        import re as _re
        return _re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text).strip().lower()

    def _reconcile_reply_identities_from_page(self, unchecked_list):
        """Recover exact own reply identities, never metrics, from visible articles."""
        reconcile = getattr(
            getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None), "reconcile_reply_identity", None
        )
        if not callable(reconcile):
            return 0
        own = str((self.config or {}).get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz"))
        recovered = 0
        for record in unchecked_list[:40]:
            if record.get("source") != "reply" or record.get("status_id"):
                continue
            if not record.get("id") or not record.get("text"):
                continue
            try:
                canonical = self._confirm_reply_status_article_dom(
                    record["text"], return_url=True
                )
                if not isinstance(canonical, str) or not canonical:
                    continue
                if reconcile(
                    record["id"], expected_text=record["text"],
                    own_username=own, canonical_url=canonical,
                ):
                    recovered += 1
            except Exception as error:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[REPLY-ID] Profilabgleich offen: {type(error).__name__}",
                    "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG",
                )
        if recovered:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[REPLY-ID] {recovered} eigene IDs nachgetragen; Messung bleibt offen",
                "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
            )
        return recovered

    def _scrape_engagement_from_page(self, unchecked_list):
        """Scrapt Engagement-Metriken fuer unchecked Tweets von der aktuellen Seite.

        Returns: (checked_count, success_count, weak_count)
        """
        self.safe_log(f"[ENG-DIAG] scrape_enter n={len(unchecked_list)}", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")

        recover = getattr(self, "_reconcile_reply_identities_from_page", None)
        if callable(recover):
            recover(unchecked_list)

        try:
            tweet_elements = self.page.query_selector_all('article[data-testid="tweet"]')
        except Exception:
            tweet_elements = []

        self.safe_log(
            f"[ENG-DIAG] DOM: {len(tweet_elements)} tweet-articles gefunden",
            "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
        )

        if not tweet_elements:
            self.safe_log("[ENG-DIAG] abort: no_dom_articles", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")
            return 0, 0, 0

        # DOM-Texte vorab extrahieren fuer schnellen Vergleich
        # Bis zu 40 Elemente (with_replies zeigt Original + Reply im Thread)
        dom_data = []
        for el in tweet_elements[:40]:
            try:
                text_el = el.query_selector('[data-testid="tweetText"]')
                text = text_el.inner_text() if text_el else ""
                dom_data.append((text, el))
            except Exception:
                dom_data.append(("", el))

        # Debug: erste 5 DOM-Texte loggen
        for i, (dt, _) in enumerate(dom_data[:5]):
            preview = self._normalize_for_match(dt)[:80]
            self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] DOM[{i}]: {preview!r}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")

        checked_count = 0
        success_count = 0
        weak_count = 0
        matched_count = 0
        no_match_count = 0
        no_match_logged = 0

        for tweet_info in unchecked_list:
            raw_text = tweet_info.get("text", "")
            if not raw_text:
                continue

            # Normalisierter Prefix fuer Matching (80 chars statt 50)
            norm_stored = self._normalize_for_match(raw_text)
            stored_prefix = norm_stored[:80]

            matched_el = None
            is_reply = tweet_info.get("source") in {"reply", "quote_tweet"}
            # Replies sind kuerzer → weichere Overlap-Schwelle
            overlap_threshold = 0.40 if is_reply else 0.60
            for dom_text, el in dom_data:
                norm_dom = self._normalize_for_match(dom_text)
                # Strategie 1: Prefix-Match (erste 30 chars reichen fuer Replies)
                prefix_len = 30 if is_reply else 40
                if stored_prefix and stored_prefix[:prefix_len] in norm_dom[:150]:
                    matched_el = el
                    break
                # Strategie 2: Erstes Wort mit $ (Dollar-Hooks sind eindeutig)
                if raw_text.startswith("$") and norm_dom.startswith(norm_stored[:20]):
                    matched_el = el
                    break
                # Strategie 3: Woerter-Overlap (fuer Replies lockerer)
                if len(norm_stored) > 15 and len(norm_dom) > 15:
                    stored_words = set(norm_stored[:100].split())
                    dom_words = set(norm_dom[:100].split())
                    if stored_words and dom_words:
                        overlap = len(stored_words & dom_words) / len(stored_words)
                        if overlap >= overlap_threshold:
                            matched_el = el
                            break

            if not matched_el:
                no_match_count += 1
                if no_match_logged < 5:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[ENG-DIAG] no_match: {stored_prefix[:50]!r}",
                        "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
                    )
                    no_match_logged += 1
                continue

            try:
                metrics = self._extract_engagement_playwright(matched_el) or {}
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] Engagement-Extraktion fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")
                continue

            # Wenn Views-Messung fehlgeschlagen (element fehlt oder Parse-Fehler):
            # Nicht als "checked" markieren + Zaehler hochfahren
            if not metrics.get("evidence_complete", False):
                reason = metrics.get("evidence_reason", "unknown")
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[VIEWS-DIAG] Messung unvollstaendig fuer {tweet_info.get('id')} "
                    f"(Grund: {reason}); checked=False",
                    "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
                )
                # Zaehler fuer fehlgeschlagene Messversuche
                if "views_probe_failed" not in tweet_info:
                    tweet_info["views_probe_failed"] = 0
                tweet_info["views_probe_failed"] += 1
                continue  # Nicht als geprueft speichern

            likes = metrics.get("likes", 0)
            views = metrics.get("views", 0)
            retweets = metrics.get("retweets", 0)

            # DOM-Fallback hat weder unveraenderliche Status-ID noch explizite
