            # Parser-Evidenz. Er bleibt rein diagnostisch; der Outcome bleibt
            # bis zum Ende des 48h-Fensters offen und wird danach neutral als
            # verpasste Messung geschlossen.
            self.safe_log(
                "[FEEDBACK] DOM-Fallback nur diagnostisch; "
                "24h-Messung bleibt offen",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "DEBUG",
            )

            # Auch der Reply-Learner darf aus unscharfen DOM-Treffern nicht
            # lernen. Verifizierte Outcomes werden im direkten Pfad konsumiert.

            matched_count += 1
            checked_count += 1
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] MATCH: L={likes} V={views} R={retweets} | {stored_prefix[:40]}",
                "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG",
            )

            # Telemetrie-Zaehler (nur success/weak-Statistik, keine harte Logik):
            # views-Schwelle 100 -> 60 fuer Konsistenz mit der Ist-Schnitt-Kalibrierung
            # (Sven 2026-07-20, ~30 Views Schnitt -> 2x).
            if likes >= 1 or views >= 60:
                success_count += 1
            else:
                weak_count += 1

        self.safe_log(
            f"[ENG-DIAG] summary matched={matched_count} no_match={no_match_count} "
            f"checked={checked_count} weak={weak_count}",
            "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
        )
        return checked_count, success_count, weak_count

    def _extract_engagement_playwright(self, article) -> dict:
        """Liest Engagement-Metriken direkt via Playwright-native API aus einem article-Element.

        Nullen sind nur dann echte Messwerte, wenn alle erwarteten
        Engagement-Felder strukturell erkannt und fehlerfrei geparst wurden.
        """
        metrics = {
            "likes": 0,
            "retweets": 0,
            "comments": 0,
            "views": 0,
            "evidence_complete": False,
            "evidence_reason": "not_inspected",
        }
        observed_fields: set[str] = set()
        evidence_errors: list[str] = []

        # F88: Plausibilitaets-Obergrenzen je Metrik. Eigene Tweets sind klein;
        # alles darueber stammt fast sicher aus eingebetteten Quote-/Parent-Tweets
        # oder Fehl-Parsing und wuerde die Lern-Daten vergiften (z.B. 22 Mio. Views).
        _caps = {"likes": 5_000_000, "retweets": 2_000_000,
                 "comments": 1_000_000, "views": 100_000_000}

        def _parse(text: str) -> Optional[int]:
            """Parst eine Zahl inkl. K/M/Mrd-Suffix, deutsch & englisch."""
            if not text:
                return None
            try:
                low = text.lower()
                m = re.search(r'(\d[\d.,]*)', low)
                if not m:
                    return None
                raw = m.group(1)
                tail = low[m.end():m.end() + 12]
                if re.match(r'\s*(mrd|milliard|billion|b\b)', tail):
                    mult = 1_000_000_000
                elif re.match(r'\s*(mio|million|m\b)', tail):
                    mult = 1_000_000
                elif re.match(r'\s*(tsd|thousand|k\b)', tail):
                    mult = 1_000
                else:
                    mult = 1
                if mult > 1:
                    # Bei abgekuerzten Werten ist ein einzelner Punkt oder ein
                    # einzelnes Komma das Dezimalzeichen ("1.2K", "1,2 Mio").
                    # Gemischte Locale-Werte nutzen den letzten Trenner als
                    # Dezimalzeichen und den anderen als Gruppierung.
                    if "." in raw and "," in raw:
                        decimal_separator = (
                            "." if raw.rfind(".") > raw.rfind(",") else ","
                        )
                        grouping_separator = (
                            "," if decimal_separator == "." else "."
                        )
                        normalized = raw.replace(grouping_separator, "")
                        if normalized.count(decimal_separator) != 1:
                            return None
                        normalized = normalized.replace(
                            decimal_separator,
                            ".",
                        )
                    elif "." in raw or "," in raw:
                        decimal_separator = "." if "." in raw else ","
                        if raw.count(decimal_separator) != 1:
                            return None
                        normalized = raw.replace(decimal_separator, ".")
                    else:
                        normalized = raw
                    num = float(normalized)
                else:
                    # reine Ganzzahl mit Tausender-Trennern
                    num = float(re.sub(r'[.,]', '', raw))
                return int(num * mult)
            except Exception:
                return None

        # aria-label Schluesselwoerter → Metrik-Key (deutsch + englisch)
        _patterns = {
            "repost": "retweets", "retweet": "retweets",
            "antwort": "comments", "repl": "comments", "kommentar": "comments",
            "gefäll": "likes", "gefall": "likes", "like": "likes",
            "aufruf": "views", "angezeigt": "views", "anzeige": "views", "view": "views",
        }

        def _assign(label: str) -> None:
            for pattern, key in _patterns.items():
                if pattern in label:
                    count = _parse(label)
                    if count is None:
                        evidence_errors.append(f"parse_error:{key}")
                        return
                    observed_fields.add(key)
                    if count < 0 or count > _caps[key]:
                        evidence_errors.append(f"out_of_range:{key}")
                        return
                    metrics[key] = max(int(metrics[key]), count)
                    break

        def _direct_elements(selector: str):
            return [
                element for element in article.query_selector_all(selector)
                if element.evaluate(
                    """(node, root) =>
                        root.matches('article[data-testid="tweet"]') &&
                        !root.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') &&
                        !root.parentElement?.closest('article') &&
                        node.closest('article') === root &&
                        !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]')""",
                    article,
                ) is True
            ]

        try:
            # 1) Engagement-Leiste (role="group"): EINE kombinierte aria-label direkt
            #    am Tweet — ohne eingebettete Quote-/Parent-Metriken aus dem Subtree.
            group = None
            for g in _direct_elements('[role="group"][aria-label]'):
                glabel = (g.get_attribute("aria-label") or "").lower()
                if any(p in glabel for p in _patterns):
                    group = g
                    break
            if group is not None:
                glabel = (group.get_attribute("aria-label") or "").lower()
                # z.B. "2 Antworten, 5 Reposts, 18 Gefällt mir, 1.234 Aufrufe"
                for seg in re.split(r'[,;.] ', glabel):
                    seg = seg.strip()
                    _assign(seg)

            # 2) Fallback: einzelne Engagement-Buttons fuer fehlende Felder.
            for tid, key in (
                ("reply", "comments"),
                ("retweet", "retweets"),
                ("like", "likes"),
            ):
                if key not in observed_fields:
                    for btn in _direct_elements(f'[data-testid="{tid}"]'):
                        _assign(
                            (btn.get_attribute("aria-label") or "").lower()
                        )

            # 3) Views via analytics-Link (oft separat, nicht in der group)
            if "views" not in observed_fields:
                for analytics_el in _direct_elements("a[href*='/analytics']"):
                    count = _parse(
                        analytics_el.get_attribute("aria-label") or ""
                    )
                    if count is None:
                        evidence_errors.append("parse_error:views")
                    else:
                        observed_fields.add("views")
                        if count > _caps["views"]:
                            evidence_errors.append("out_of_range:views")
                        else:
                            metrics["views"] = max(
                                int(metrics["views"]),
                                count,
                            )

        except Exception as e:
            evidence_errors.append(f"selector_error:{type(e).__name__}")
            self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] _extract_engagement_playwright: {e}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")

        missing_fields = sorted(
            {"likes", "retweets", "comments", "views"} - observed_fields
        )

        # Trenne soft_errors (harmlose Duplikat-Segmente) von hard_errors (echte Fehler)
        soft_errors = []
        hard_errors = []
        for error in evidence_errors:
            if error.startswith("parse_error:"):
                key = error.split(":", 1)[1] if ":" in error else ""
                if key in observed_fields:
                    # Harmlos: Schlüssel wurde bereits korrekt aus anderem Segment gelesen
                    soft_errors.append(error)
                else:
                    # Echt: Schlüssel wurde nie beobachtet
                    hard_errors.append(error)
            else:
                # out_of_range:* und selector_error:* sind immer hart
                hard_errors.append(error)

        # [PARSE-DIAG] Logzeile für Diagnostik: Fehler + beobachtete/fehlende Felder + views_raw
        if evidence_errors:
            try:
                _diag_observed = ",".join(sorted(observed_fields)) or "-"
                _diag_missing = ",".join(missing_fields) or "-"
                _diag_views_raw = str(int(metrics.get("views", 0))) if isinstance(metrics.get("views"), (int, float)) else "-"
                _diag_errors = ",".join(sorted(set(evidence_errors)))[:60]
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[PARSE-DIAG] error={_diag_errors} observed={_diag_observed} missing={_diag_missing} views_raw={_diag_views_raw}",
                    "FEEDBACK",
                    "INFO"
                )
            except Exception:
                # Diagnostik-Fehler stillschweigend ignorieren
                pass

        # Entscheidungslogik basiert NUR auf hard_errors
        if hard_errors:
            metrics["evidence_reason"] = ",".join(
                sorted(set(hard_errors))
            )[:240]
        elif missing_fields:
            metrics["evidence_reason"] = (
                "missing_fields:" + ",".join(missing_fields)
            )
        else:
            metrics["evidence_complete"] = True
            metrics["evidence_reason"] = "complete_engagement_structure"
            # Wenn soft_errors vorhanden waren, log sie als Diagnostik
            if soft_errors:
                _soft_list = ",".join(sorted(set(soft_errors)))
                _observed_list = ",".join(sorted(observed_fields))
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[EVIDENCE-DOWNGRADE] soft={_soft_list} observed={_observed_list}",
                    "FEEDBACK",
                    "INFO"
                )

        # Diagnose-Zeile (reine Beobachtung, kein Kontrollfluss):
        # evidence_reason allein verschweigt, ob die View-Zahl vorher ueberhaupt
        # gelesen wurde — bei gesetzten evidence_errors wird "missing_fields"
        # unterdrueckt. observed/missing sind deterministisch sortiert, damit die
        # Zeilen im Log spaeter zaehlbar sind. "-" bedeutet: leere Liste.
        if not metrics["evidence_complete"]:
            _observed = ",".join(sorted(observed_fields)) or "-"
            _missing = ",".join(missing_fields) or "-"
            self.safe_log(
                "[FEEDBACK] Engagement-Evidenz unvollstaendig "
                f"({metrics['evidence_reason']}) "
                f"observed={_observed} missing={_missing}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "INFO",
            )
        return metrics

    def _check_followup_engagement_direct(
        self,
        tweet_info: dict,
        horizon: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """Liest genau einen eigenen Status fuer eine read-only Folgemessung."""
        if horizon not in self._FOLLOWUP_OUTCOME_HORIZONS:
            raise ValueError("horizon muss 48h oder 7d sein")

        canonical_url = self._validated_owned_status_url(
            tweet_info,
            self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {},
        )
        source = str(tweet_info.get("source") or "unknown").strip()
        source_key = source.casefold()
        if (
            not canonical_url
            or source_key in self._FOLLOWUP_BLOCKED_SOURCES
            or source_key.startswith("cmo_")
        ):
            return False

        try:
            self.driver.get(canonical_url)
            self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
            try:
                self.page.wait_for_selector(
                    'article[data-testid="tweet"]',
                    timeout=5000,
                )
            except Exception:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}: Timeout fuer {canonical_url}",
                    "FEEDBACK",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return False

            articles = self.page.query_selector_all(
                'article[data-testid="tweet"]'
            )
            for article in articles or []:
                identity_verified, observed_author, observed_status_id = (
                    self._verify_owned_status_article(article, tweet_info)
                )
                if not identity_verified:
                    continue
                engagement = self._extract_engagement_playwright(article)
                if engagement.get("evidence_complete") is False:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}: Engagement-Evidenz "
                        "unvollstaendig "
                        f"({engagement.get('evidence_reason', 'unknown')}); "
                        "Messung bleibt offen",
                        "FEEDBACK",
                        "WARNING",
                    )
                    return False
                try:
                    measurement_age_hours = (
                        time.time() - float(tweet_info.get("timestamp"))
                    ) / 3600.0
                except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                    return False
                return self._record_followup_outcome_once(
                    tweet_info,
                    engagement,
                    horizon,
                    verified=True,
                    observed_author=observed_author,
                    observed_status_id=observed_status_id,
                    observed_at=datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),
                    measurement_age_hours=measurement_age_hours,
                )

            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}: exakte eigene Status-Identity "
                f"nicht gefunden ({canonical_url})",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}: Direct-Check fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return False

    def _check_due_followup_outcomes(self) -> int:
        """Arbeitet je Stundenlauf maximal zwei 48h- und zwei 7d-Posts ab."""
        checked = 0
        learner = getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None)
        try:
            get_due = getattr(learner, "get_due_followup_outcomes", None)
            if not callable(get_due):
                return 0
            for horizon in ("48h", "7d"):
                try:
                    due = get_due(horizon, limit=2)
                except Exception as exc:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}-Queue nicht lesbar: {exc}",
                        "LEARNING",
                        "WARNING",
                    )
                    continue
                for tweet_info in list(due or [])[:2]:
                    try:
                        if self._check_followup_engagement_direct(
                            tweet_info,
                            horizon,
                        ):
                            checked += 1
                    except Exception as exc:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[FOLLOWUP] {horizon}-Messung fehlgeschlagen: "
                            f"{exc}",
                            "LEARNING",
                            "WARNING",
                        )
        finally:
            try:
                self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
                self.human_pause(1.0, 2.0)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return checked

    def _goto_status_robust(self, url: str) -> bool:
        """Navigiert robust zu Status-URL mit Retry-Logik und URL-Verifikation.

        Ersetzt self.driver.get() für Direct-Engagement-Checks. Behandelt:
        - Timeouts: Prüft ob Seite trotzdem geladen ist (page.url prüfung)
        - Unterbrochene Navigation: EIN Retry bei "interrupted by another navigation"
        - Keine Exception nach außen: Gibt False zurück bei Fehlschlag

        Returns: True wenn Navigation erfolgreich (Seite ist da), False sonst
        """
        # Defensiv: Check ob page noch existiert
        if self.page is None:
            return False
        try:
            if self.page.is_closed():
                return False
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Ziel-Status-ID aus URL extrahieren
        status_id_match = re.search(r'/status/(\d+)', url)
        target_status_id = status_id_match.group(1) if status_id_match else ""

        max_attempts = 2
        last_error = None

        for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
            try:
                # Navigiere mit commit wait_until (nicht domcontentloaded)
                self.page.goto(url, wait_until="commit", timeout=20000)
                # Erfolg: Navigation hat committed
                current_url = self.page.url or ""
                if target_status_id and target_status_id in current_url:
                    # URL passt — Navigation erfolgreich trotz möglicherweise unvollständigem Load
                    try:
                        self.page.wait_for_selector("article", timeout=8000)
                    except Exception:
                        # Inhalt wartet noch, aber Navigation gilt als erfolgreich
                        pass
                    return True
                return True  # Falls Status-ID nicht extrahiert wurde, trotzdem erfolgreich
            except Exception as exc:
                exc_str = str(exc)
                last_error = exc

                # Prüfe ob URL trotz Exception geladen ist
                try:
                    current_url = self.page.url or ""
                except Exception:
                    current_url = ""

                if target_status_id and target_status_id in current_url:
                    # Seite ist trotz Exception geladen → erfolg
                    try:
                        self.page.wait_for_selector("article", timeout=8000)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    return True

                # Nur bei "interrupted by another navigation" einen Retry erlauben
                if "interrupted by another navigation" in exc_str and attempt < max_attempts:
                    continue
                else:
                    # Kein Retry: Timeout oder andere Exception → break
                    break

        # Versuch fehlgeschlagen: Log und False
        self.safe_log(
            f"[FEEDBACK] Direct-Check fehlgeschlagen: {type(last_error).__name__} {url}",
            "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
        )
        return False

    def _check_engagement_direct(self, tweet_info: dict) -> tuple:
        """Navigiert direkt zur tweet_url und liest Engagement aus einem eindeutig eigenen Artikel.

        Viel zuverlaessiger als DOM-Text-Matching auf der Profilseite.
        Returns: (checked, success, weak)  — je 0 oder 1.
        """
        tweet_url = self._validated_owned_status_url(
            tweet_info,
            self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {},
        )
        if not tweet_url:
            # Fallback fuer Reply/Quote-Tweets: canonical_url zeigt auf Parent,
            # aber status_id ist unsere eigene Reply-ID. Versuche, die korrekte
            # URL zu konstruieren und nochmal zu validieren.
            # WICHTIG: Nur fuer Reply/Quote-Tweets verwenden, nie fuer andere Sources.
            is_reply_source = tweet_info.get("source") in {"reply", "quote_tweet"}

            fallback_ok = False
            status_id = str(tweet_info.get("status_id") or "").strip()
            if is_reply_source:
                own_user = str(
                    tweet_info.get("own_username")
                    or (self.config or {}).get("own_username", "")
                ).strip().lstrip("@")
                if (
                    re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15}", own_user)
                    and status_id.isdigit()
                ):
                    # Baue konstruierte URL und validiere nochmal (mit Kopie)
                    constructed_url = f"https://x.com/{own_user}/status/{status_id}"
                    fallback_info = dict(tweet_info)
                    fallback_info["canonical_url"] = constructed_url
                    tweet_url = self._validated_owned_status_url(
                        fallback_info,
                        self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {},
                    )
                    if tweet_url:
                        fallback_ok = True

            self.safe_log(
                f"[DIRECT-DIAG] url_rejected src={tweet_info.get('source')} "
                f"has_status_id={bool(status_id)} fallback={'ok' if fallback_ok else 'nein'}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "INFO",
            )

            if not tweet_url:
                return 0, 0, 0

        try:
            # Prüfe, ob die Seite noch lebt, bevor wir navigieren
            if self.page is None:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[FEEDBACK] Direct-Check abgebrochen: Page geschlossen",
                    "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                )
                return 0, 0, 0
            try:
                page_closed = self.page.is_closed()
            except Exception:
                # Fehler beim is_closed()-Aufruf, weitermachen wie normal
                page_closed = False
            if page_closed:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[FEEDBACK] Direct-Check abgebrochen: Page geschlossen",
                    "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                )
                return 0, 0, 0

            if not self._goto_status_robust(tweet_url):
                return 0, 0, 0
            self.human_pause(1.5, 2.5)
            try:
                self.page.wait_for_selector('article[data-testid="tweet"]', timeout=5000)
            except Exception:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[FEEDBACK] Direct: Timeout fuer {tweet_url}",
                    "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                )
                # Seite hat nicht geladen → transient, Tweet NICHT als checked markieren
                return 0, 0, 0

            articles = self.page.query_selector_all('article[data-testid="tweet"]')
            if not articles:
                # Kein belastbarer Article: transient offen lassen, nie als Null
                # oder Loss finalisieren.
                return 0, 0, 0

            # Verify every article with the strict ownership helper.  ``tweet_url``
            # is already the effective validated URL, including the reply/quote
            # fallback constructed at method entry.  Preserve the input record;
            # only the verifier/finalizer copy receives that effective identity.
            measurement_info = dict(tweet_info)
            is_reply_source = tweet_info.get("source") in {"reply"}
            measurement_info["canonical_url"] = tweet_url
            measurement_info["tweet_url"] = tweet_url
            matches = []
            for article in articles or []:
                valid, author, status_id = self._verify_owned_status_article(
                    article, measurement_info
                )
                if valid:
                    matches.append((article, author, status_id))
            if len(matches) != 1:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[FEEDBACK] Direct: genau ein eigener Artikel erforderlich "
                    f"(matches={len(matches)}, articles={len(articles)}, quelle={tweet_info.get('source')})",
                    "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                )
                return 0, 0, 0
            first_article, observed_author, observed_status_id = matches[0]
            identity_verified = True

            # Playwright-native extraction (avoids Selenium adapter layer that silently returns 0)
            metrics = self._extract_engagement_playwright(first_article)
            if metrics.get("evidence_complete") is False:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[FEEDBACK] Direct: Engagement-Evidenz unvollstaendig "
                    f"({metrics.get('evidence_reason', 'unknown')}); "
                    "Messung bleibt offen",
                    "FEEDBACK",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return 0, 0, 0

            likes    = metrics.get("likes", 0)
            views    = metrics.get("views", 0)
            retweets = metrics.get("retweets", 0)
            replies  = metrics.get("comments", 0)

            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Direct: likes={likes} views={views} rt={retweets} rep={replies}",
                "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG",
            )

            # Diagnostisches Feld: Speichere Messwerte erst nach der strikten
            # Artikel-/Identitaetspruefung.
            # Persistiere DIREKT in tweet_learner, nicht in tweet_info.
            if measurement_info.get("source") in {"reply", "quote_tweet"}:
                try:
                    if hasattr(self, "tweet_learner") and self.tweet_learner is not None:
                        # Berechne expected_status_id aus URL
                        expected_sid = ""
                        try:
                            canonical_url = measurement_info.get("canonical_url") or measurement_info.get("tweet_url") or ""
                            if canonical_url:
                                _, expected_sid = self._status_identity_from_url(canonical_url)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        # Fallback: wenn expected_sid leer, aus tweet_info
                        if not expected_sid:
                            for field in ("status_id", "tweet_id", "id"):
                                candidate = tweet_info.get(field)
                                if candidate:
                                    candidate_str = str(candidate).strip()
                                    # Nur Ziffernfolge >= 15
                                    if candidate_str.isdigit() and len(candidate_str) >= 15:
                                        expected_sid = candidate_str
                                        break
                        # WARN-Log wenn expected_sid oder observed_status_id leer
                        if not expected_sid or not observed_status_id:
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[DIAG] own_status_match unbestimmbar: expected={expected_sid} observed={observed_status_id} source={tweet_info.get('source')}",
                                "ENGAGEMENT", "WARN",
                            )
                        # Abstinenz statt Falschmessung: wenn observed != expected, views/likes auf None
                        diag_views = views
                        diag_likes = likes
                        if expected_sid and observed_status_id and expected_sid != observed_status_id:
                            diag_views = None
                            diag_likes = None
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[DIAG] own_status_match=False: Messung verworfen (expected={expected_sid}, observed={observed_status_id})",
                                "ENGAGEMENT", "INFO",
                            )
                        self.tweet_learner.record_diag_measurement(
                            tweet_id=tweet_info.get("id") or tweet_info.get("tweet_id"),
                            views=diag_views,
                            likes=diag_likes,
                            retweets=retweets,
                            comments=replies,
                            observed_status_id=observed_status_id,
                            expected_status_id=expected_sid,
                        )
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[DIAG] Fehler beim Speichern diag_measured: {e}",
                        "ENGAGEMENT", "WARN",
                    )

            # Das Messalter muss den Zeitpunkt des erfolgreichen Abrufs abbilden.
            # Ein beim Rundenstart noch gueltiger Post darf nicht mit einem alten
            # Zeitstempel in ein inzwischen abgelaufenes 24h-Fenster gelangen.
            try:
                measurement_age_hours = (
                    time.time() - float(tweet_info.get("timestamp"))
                ) / 3600.0
            except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                measurement_age_hours = None

            finalized_ok = self._finalize_outcome_once(
                measurement_info,
                {
                    "likes": likes,
                    "views": views,
                    "retweets": retweets,
                    "replies": replies,
                },
                verified=identity_verified,
                observed_author=observed_author,
                observed_status_id=observed_status_id,
                verification_reason="owned_status_identity_mismatch",
                measurement_age_hours=measurement_age_hours,
            )

            # F34 Reach-Pivot Exit-Trigger: erster echter Like.
            if likes >= 1 and identity_verified and not is_reply_source:
                try:
                    _pivot_path = os.path.join(
                        os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
                        "state", "reach_pivot.json",
                    )
                    if os.path.exists(_pivot_path):
                        with open(_pivot_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as _pf:
                            _pivot = json.load(_pf)
                        if _pivot.get("active") and not _pivot.get("exit_triggered"):
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[F34] REACH-PIVOT EXIT-KRITERIUM ERREICHT: "
                                f"first_real_like detected (likes={likes}) on "
                                f"{tweet_info.get('tweet_url','?')}. User soll F34 reverten.",
                                "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                            )
                            _pivot["exit_triggered"] = True
                            _pivot["exit_triggered_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                            _pivot["exit_first_like_url"] = tweet_info.get("tweet_url", "")
                            with open(_pivot_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as _pf:
                                json.dump(_pivot, _pf, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # Reply-Learner-Feedback
            reply_lid = tweet_info.get("reply_learner_id")
            if (reply_lid and identity_verified and finalized_ok
                    and hasattr(self, 'reply_learner') and self.reply_learner):
                try:
                    self.reply_learner.record_engagement(reply_lid, likes=likes, retweets=retweets)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            norm_preview = self._normalize_for_match(tweet_info.get("text", ""))[:50]
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Direct-Match: L={likes} V={views} R={retweets} | {norm_preview}",
                "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG",
            )

            if not identity_verified or is_reply_source or not finalized_ok:
                return 1, 0, 1
            if likes >= 1 or views >= 100:
                return 1, 1, 0
            return 1, 0, 1

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Direct-Check fehlgeschlagen: {type(e).__name__}: {e} url={tweet_url}",
                "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
            )
            return 0, 0, 0

    def _get_macro_event_hint(self) -> dict:
        """Gibt Makro-Event-Signal fuer heute zurueck.

        Prueft ob heute ein bekanntes Makro-Event stattfindet (CPI, Fed, ECB, NFP).
        Basiert auf typischen US/EU Kalendermustern — nicht 100% exakt, aber nahe genug
        um den Mode-Switch zu triggern wenn es passt.

        Returns dict mit keys: topic, mode, reason — oder leeres dict.
        """
        try:
            from datetime import date
            today = date.today()
            weekday = today.weekday()  # 0=Mo, 4=Fr
            day = today.day

            # NFP: erster Freitag des Monats
            if weekday == 4 and day <= 7:
                return {"topic": "jobs report", "mode": "austrian",
                        "reason": "NFP day — U.S. jobs data, macro-relevant"}

            # CPI: typisch 2. Dienstag-Mittwoch des Monats (8.-14.)
            if weekday in (1, 2) and 8 <= day <= 14:
                return {"topic": "CPI", "mode": "austrian",
                        "reason": "CPI week — inflation data, macro-relevant"}

            # PCE: typisch letzter Freitag des Monats
            if weekday == 4 and day >= 25:
                return {"topic": "PCE", "mode": "austrian",
                        "reason": "PCE week — core inflation, Fed watches this"}

            # Fed FOMC: typisch 2. Mittwoch alle ~6 Wochen — approximiert durch Monate
            if weekday == 2 and 15 <= day <= 21:
                return {"topic": "Fed", "mode": "austrian",
                        "reason": "Potential FOMC week — rate decision window"}

            # ECB: typisch 2. Donnerstag alle 6 Wochen — ähnliches Muster
            if weekday == 3 and 8 <= day <= 14:
                return {"topic": "ECB", "mode": "austrian",
                        "reason": "ECB meeting window — European rate policy"}

            return {}
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def _run_quick_tweet_check(self, entry: dict) -> None:
        """30min Post-Feedback: Navigiert zu geposteter Tweet-URL, scrapt Engagement,
        und speichert ausschliesslich einen provisional Snapshot. Darwin, Brain und
        Topic-Lernen werden erst vom verifizierten 24h-Gateway aktualisiert.
        """
        url = self._validated_owned_status_url(
            entry,
            self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {},
        )
        topic = entry.get("topic", "unknown")
        source = entry.get("source", "unknown")

        if not url:
            return

        try:
            self.page.goto(url, timeout=15000)
            self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)

            likes, views, _replies = 0, 0, 0
            retweets = 0
            identity_verified = False
            try:
                _art = self.page.query_selector('article[data-testid="tweet"]')
                if _art:
                    identity_verified, _, _ = self._verify_owned_status_article(_art, entry)
                    extracted = self._extract_engagement_playwright(_art)
                    if extracted.get("evidence_complete") is False:
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[QUICK-CHECK] Engagement-Evidenz "
                            "unvollstaendig; Snapshot bleibt offen",
                            "FEEDBACK",
                            "WARNING",
                        )
                        return
                    likes = int(extracted.get("likes", 0) or 0)
                    views = int(extracted.get("views", 0) or 0)
                    retweets = int(extracted.get("retweets", 0) or 0)
                    _replies = int(extracted.get("comments", 0) or 0)
            except Exception:
                return

            self.safe_log(
                f"[QUICK-CHECK] {topic}/{source}: L={likes} V={views} | {url.split('/')[-1]}",
                "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
            )
            if identity_verified:
                self._record_provisional_outcome(
                    entry,
                    {
                        "likes": likes,
                        "views": views,
                        "retweets": retweets,
                        "replies": _replies,
                    },
                )
            else:
                self.safe_log(
                    "[QUICK-CHECK] Identity nicht verifiziert -> kein Lernsignal",
                    "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
                )

        except Exception as _e:
            self.safe_log(f"[QUICK-CHECK] Navigation fehlgeschlagen: {_e}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")
        finally:
            # Zurück zur Home-Timeline
            try:
                self.page.goto("https://x.com/home", timeout=10000)
                self.human_pause(1.0, 2.0)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _apply_pressure_session_boost(self) -> None:
        """Erfasst Follower-Druck ohne die Sichtungsmission umzulenken.

        Wird alle 30min aufgerufen. Passt `self.session_targets` (in-memory) an —
        nie die Config-Datei. Boost wird einmalig pro Session angewendet.

        Schwellwert: follower_pressure > 0.80 → +25% Targets (innerhalb Safety-Caps).
        """
        if self._pressure_boost_applied:
            return

        mission = ((getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {})
        if is_visibility_objective(mission.get("objective", QUALIFIED_VISIBILITY_OBJECTIVE)):
            self._pressure_boost_applied = True
            self.safe_log(
                "[PRESSURE-BOOST] Follower-Druck bleibt Diagnose; Sichtungen steuern die Session.",
                "BRAIN",
                "INFO",
            )
            return

        goal_tracker = getattr(self, "goal_tracker", None)
        if goal_tracker is None:
            return

        try:
            pressure = float(goal_tracker.get_follower_pressure())
        except Exception:
            return

        if pressure <= 0.80:
            return

        # Hole aktuelle Targets (live oder config)
        targets = getattr(self, "session_targets", None)
        if not isinstance(targets, dict) or not targets:
            cfg = self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            targets = dict(cfg.get("session_limits", {}))

        safety_caps = {}
        try:
            safety_caps = (self.config or {}).get("safety_limits", {}).get("session_caps", {})
        except Exception:
            pass

        boost_factor = 1.25
        new_targets = dict(targets)
        for key, cap_key in [
            ("tweets_per_session", "tweets"),
            ("likes_per_session", "likes"),
            ("comments_per_session", "comments"),
        ]:
            old_val = int(targets.get(key, 0) or 0)
            cap_val = int(safety_caps.get(cap_key, 9999) or 9999)
            new_val = min(cap_val, int(old_val * boost_factor))
            new_targets[key] = new_val

        self.session_targets = new_targets
        self._pressure_boost_applied = True
        self.safe_log(
            f"[PRESSURE-BOOST] Follower-Druck={pressure:.2f} → Targets angepasst: "
            f"tweets={new_targets.get('tweets_per_session')} "
            f"likes={new_targets.get('likes_per_session')} "
            f"comments={new_targets.get('comments_per_session')}",
            "BRAIN", "INFO",
        )

    def _check_own_engagement(self):
        """Prueft Engagement fuer unchecked Tweets.

        Strategie:
        - Tweets MIT gespeicherter tweet_url → direkte Navigation (zuverlaessig)
        - Tweets OHNE URL → DOM-Scan auf Profil-/Replies-Seite (Fallback)
        """
        if not self.tweet_learner:
            self.safe_log("[ENG-DIAG] abort: no_tweet_learner", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")
            return

        try:
            # Outbox zuerst: ein Crash zwischen TweetLearner/Darwin und dem
            # Consume-Marker darf kein dauerhaft verlorenes Lernsignal erzeugen.
            self._recover_unconsumed_outcomes()
            own_user = self.config.get("own_username", "ai_studioxyz")

            # Follower-Zahl tracken (immer, unabhaengig von unchecked tweets)
            try:
                _profile_url = f"https://x.com/{own_user}"
                self.driver.get(_profile_url)
                self.human_pause(2.0, 3.0)
                own_followers = self._get_profile_follower_count()
                if own_followers > 0:
                    self._track_own_follower_count(own_followers)
                    self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] Follower getrackt: {own_followers}", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")
                else:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[FEEDBACK] Follower-Zahl nicht lesbar (Wert={own_followers}) "
                        f"auf {_profile_url} - Profil-Selektor pruefen",
                        "FEEDBACK", "WARN",
                    )
            except Exception as _fe:
                self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] Follower-Tracking fehlgeschlagen: {_fe}", "FEEDBACK", "WARN")

            # Zurueck zur Timeline vor unchecked-Check
            try:
                self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
                self.human_pause(1.0, 2.0)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Unabhaengige read-only Messreihen: auch ohne offenen 24h-Backlog
            # pro Stundenlauf die kleinen 48h-/7d-Batches abarbeiten.
            self._check_due_followup_outcomes()

            # Fester 24h-Horizont: nur vergleichbare, reife Outcomes finalisieren.
            # Dieser Startzeitpunkt waehlt den Batch atomar aus; das endgueltige
            # Messalter wird nach dem einzelnen erfolgreichen Seitenabruf ermittelt.
            measurement_now = time.time()
            expire_missed = getattr(
                self.tweet_learner,
                "expire_missed_outcomes",
                None,
            )
            if callable(expire_missed):
                missed_count = expire_missed(now=measurement_now)
                if missed_count:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[OUTCOME] {missed_count} verpasste 24h-Messfenster "
                        "neutral geschlossen",
                        "LEARNING",
                        "INFO",
                    )
            if hasattr(self.tweet_learner, "get_due_outcomes"):
                unchecked = self.tweet_learner.get_due_outcomes(
                    limit=None,
                    min_age_hours=24.0,
                    max_age_hours=48.0,
                    now=measurement_now,
                )
            else:  # defensiver Rollback-Fallback
                unchecked = self.tweet_learner.get_unchecked_tweets(limit=16)

            # [OUTCOME-RUN] Logging: Fälligkeitsbatch erfassen
            due_count = len(unchecked) if unchecked else 0
            batch_count = due_count
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME-RUN] faellig={due_count} batch={batch_count}",
                "LEARNING",
                "INFO",
            )

            # Diagnostik: Anzahl unchecked Tweets und tweet_id-Verteilung
            with_id = sum(1 for t in unchecked if t.get("id") or t.get("tweet_id") or t.get("status_id"))
            self.safe_log(f"[ENG-DIAG] unchecked_count={len(unchecked)} with_id={with_id}", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")

            if not unchecked:
                self.safe_log("[ENG-DIAG] abort: empty_unchecked", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")
                self.safe_log("[FEEDBACK] Keine unchecked Tweets vorhanden", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")
                return

            # Aufteilen: direkte URL vorhanden vs. kein URL
            def _outcome_url(t):
                return str(t.get("canonical_url") or t.get("tweet_url") or "")

            with_url = [t for t in unchecked if "/status/" in _outcome_url(t)]
            without_url = [t for t in unchecked if "/status/" not in _outcome_url(t)]

            total_checked = 0
            total_success = 0
            total_weak    = 0

            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Starte Engagement-Check: {len(with_url)} direkt, "
                f"{len(without_url)} via DOM-Scan",
                "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
            )

            # --- Pfad A: Direkte URL-Navigation (alle faelligen Records) ---
            for tweet_info in with_url:
                c, s, w = self._check_engagement_direct(tweet_info)
                total_checked += c
                total_success += s
                total_weak    += w

            # --- Pfad B: DOM-Scan fuer Tweets ohne URL (Fallback) ---
            if without_url:
                reply_sources   = {"reply", "quote_tweet"}
                dom_main    = [t for t in without_url if t.get("source") not in reply_sources]
                dom_replies = [t for t in without_url if t.get("source") in reply_sources]

                # Profil-Seite fuer normale Tweets
                if dom_main:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[ENG-DIAG] filter: in={len(without_url)} out={len(dom_main)} "
                        f"reason=exclude_reply_quote_tweet",
                        "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
                    )
                    profile_url = f"https://x.com/{own_user}"
                    self.driver.get(profile_url)
                    self.human_pause(3.0, 4.5)
                    try:
                        self.page.wait_for_selector('article[data-testid="tweet"]', timeout=8000)
                    except Exception:
                        self.safe_log("[FEEDBACK] Timeout: Profilseite", "FEEDBACK", "WARNING")

                    # Follower-Stand mitnehmen, solange das eigene Profil offen ist.
                    # Kein zusaetzlicher Seitenaufruf, kein Einfluss auf den
                    # Engagement-Abruf darunter.
                    try:
                        self._record_follower_snapshot(own_user)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                    c, s, w = self._scrape_engagement_from_page(dom_main)
                    total_checked += c
                    total_success += s
                    total_weak    += w

                # Replies-Tab fuer Replies/Quotes
                if dom_replies:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[ENG-DIAG] filter: in={len(without_url)} out={len(dom_replies)} "
                        f"reason=include_reply_quote_tweet",
                        "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
                    )
                    replies_url = f"https://x.com/{own_user}/with_replies"
                    self.driver.get(replies_url)
                    self.human_pause(3.0, 4.5)
                    try:
                        self.page.wait_for_selector('article[data-testid="tweet"]', timeout=8000)
                    except Exception:
                        self.safe_log("[FEEDBACK] Timeout: Replies-Tab", "FEEDBACK", "WARNING")

                    c, s, w = self._scrape_engagement_from_page(dom_replies)
                    total_checked += c
                    total_success += s
                    total_weak    += w

            # Waehrend eines langen Abrufs kann ein anfangs faelliger Post das
            # 48h-Fenster verlassen. Solche offenen Records werden bewusst neutral
            # abgeschlossen, statt beim naechsten Lauf als scheinbare Null-Performance
            # in die Lernbasis zu geraten.
            if callable(expire_missed):
                late_missed_count = expire_missed(now=time.time())
                if late_missed_count:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[OUTCOME] {late_missed_count} nach der Messrunde "
                        "neutral geschlossen",
                        "LEARNING",
                        "INFO",
                    )

            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] {total_checked}/{len(unchecked)} gecheckt "
                f"({len(with_url)} direkt, {len(without_url)} DOM): "
                f"{total_success} erfolgreich, {total_weak} schwach",
                "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
            )

            ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
            if ledger is not None:
                health = ledger.health()
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[OUTCOME-HEALTH] final={health.get('finalized_count', 0)} "
                    f"eligible={health.get('policy_eligible_count', 0)} "
                    f"unverified={health.get('unverified_count', 0)} "
                    f"errors={health.get('error_count', 0)}",
                    "LEARNING",
                    "INFO" if health.get("policy_updates_allowed") else "WARNING",
                )

            # Cross-Session Feedback-Loop: Engagement in GoalTracker persistieren
            if self.goal_tracker and total_checked > 0:
                try:
                    self.goal_tracker.record_received_engagement(
                        success=total_success,
                        weak=total_weak,
                        checked=total_checked,
                    )
                except Exception as _ge:
                    self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] GoalTracker-Update fehlgeschlagen: {_ge}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")

            # Zurueck zur Timeline
            self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
            self.human_pause(2.0, 3.0)

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log("[ENG-DIAG] abort: exception", "FEEDBACK", "INFO")
            self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] Engagement-Check fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "FEEDBACK", "WARNING")
            try:
                self.driver.get("https://x.com/home")
                self.human_pause(2.0, 3.0)
            except Exception:
                pass

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_follower_number(raw: str) -> int | None:
        """Parse follower count from X's display format.

        Returns None if parsing fails.
        Handles: "1.234"→1234, "1,234"→1234, "1.2K"→1200, "12.345"→12345, ""→None
        Rule: . or , is decimal separator ONLY with K/M/B suffix; else thousands separator.
        """
        if not raw:
            return None

        import re
        # Extract the numeric part (number + optional suffix + optional trailing word)
        text = raw.strip()
        if not text:
            return None

        # Match numeric string with optional suffix; stop at whitespace or non-alphanumeric
        match = re.match(r'^([0-9,.]+[KMBkmb]?)\b', text)
        if not match:
            return None

        text = match.group(1)
        if not text:
            return None

        # Determine if suffix exists
        has_suffix = text.upper()[-1] in ('K', 'M', 'B')

        try:
            if has_suffix:
                # Decimal separator
                num_str = text[:-1].replace(',', '.')
                num = float(num_str)
                multipliers = {'K': 1_000, 'M': 1_000_000, 'B': 1_000_000_000}
                return int(num * multipliers[text[-1].upper()])
            else:
                # Thousands separator - validate format
                if '.' in text and ',' in text:
                    return None  # Invalid: both separators mixed
                # Validate thousands separator pattern: 1-3 digits, then groups of 3
                sep = '.' if '.' in text else ',' if ',' in text else None
                if sep:
                    pattern = rf'^[0-9]{{1,3}}(?:\{sep}[0-9]{{3}})*$'
                    if not re.match(pattern, text):
                        return None
                # Remove all . and ,
                num_str = text.replace('.', '').replace(',', '')
                return int(num_str)
        except (ValueError, KeyError, IndexError):
            return None

    # Obergrenze fuer die Follower-Zeitreihe, damit growth_history.json nicht waechst.
    _FOLLOWER_SNAPSHOT_LIMIT = 500

    def _record_follower_snapshot(self, own_user: str) -> int:
        """Liest die eigene Follower-Zahl von der GELADENEN Profilseite und schreibt sie fort.

        Warum das noetig war: `_get_profile_follower_count` wurde am 2026-08-11 repariert
        (Tausendertrenner), aber ausschliesslich aus den Follow-Pfaden aufgerufen
        (bot_modules/follow.py, growth_runner.py) — und follow_strategy ist seit F86 AUS.
        Der Parser lief also nie, und `growth_history.json` enthielt keine Eigenmessung.
        Der Digest meldete dauerhaft "Stand=nicht messbar".

        Aufrufpunkt ist bewusst `_check_own_engagement`, direkt nach dem Laden des
        eigenen Profils: dort ist die Seite schon offen, es entsteht KEIN zusaetzlicher
        Seitenaufruf und damit keine weitere Spur.

        Format nach dem Vertrag von scripts/daily_digest.py: Liste unter "snapshots"
        mit `timestamp`, `username` und `followers`. Rueckgabe: die gemessene Zahl,
        oder 0 wenn nicht lesbar.
        """
        try:
            anzahl = self._get_profile_follower_count()
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWER-SNAP] Messung fehlgeschlagen: {type(e).__name__}",
                "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
            )
            return 0

        if not isinstance(anzahl, int) or anzahl <= 0:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWER-SNAP] kein plausibler Wert gelesen (roh={anzahl!r})",
                "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
            )
            return 0

        pfad = os.path.join(
            os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "state", "growth_history.json"
        )
        try:
            daten = {}
            if os.path.exists(pfad):
                with open(pfad, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                    geladen = json.load(fh)
                if isinstance(geladen, dict):
                    daten = geladen

            # Neue Liste bilden statt die bestehende zu mutieren; alle uebrigen
            # Schluessel der Datei bleiben unangetastet.
            bisher = daten.get("snapshots")
            bisher = list(bisher) if isinstance(bisher, list) else []
            eintrag = {
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "username": own_user,
                "followers": anzahl,
                "source": "profile_scrape",
            }
            neu = (bisher + [eintrag])[-self._FOLLOWER_SNAPSHOT_LIMIT:]
            daten = {**daten, "snapshots": neu}

            # Atomar schreiben: die Datei wird auch von den Follow-Pfaden benutzt,
            # ein Teilschreiben wuerde sie unlesbar machen.
            temp = pfad + ".tmp"
            with open(temp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                json.dump(daten, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            os.replace(temp, pfad)

            vorher = bisher[-1].get("followers") if bisher and isinstance(bisher[-1], dict) else None
            delta = f" delta={anzahl - vorher:+d}" if isinstance(vorher, int) else ""
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWER-SNAP] followers={anzahl}{delta} eintraege={len(neu)}",
                "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
            )
            return anzahl
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FOLLOWER-SNAP] Fortschreiben fehlgeschlagen: {type(e).__name__}: {e}",
                "FEEDBACK", "WARNING",
            )
            return anzahl

    def _get_profile_follower_count(self) -> int:
        """Scrapt die eigene Follower-Zahl von der aktuell geladenen Profilseite via Playwright."""
        try:
            raw = self.page.evaluate("""() => {
                // Versuche mehrere Fundwege, erster Treffer gewinnt

                // Fundweg 1: a[href*="/followers"] (bestehend)
                let links = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/followers"]'));
                for (const link of links) {
                    const spans = Array.from(link.querySelectorAll('span'));
                    for (const span of spans) {
                        const txt = (span.textContent || span.innerText || '').trim();
                        if (/^[0-9,.]+[KMBkmb]?$/.test(txt) && txt.length > 0) {
                            return txt;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Fundweg 2: a[href$="/verified_followers"]
                links = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('a[href$="/verified_followers"]'));
                for (const link of links) {
                    const spans = Array.from(link.querySelectorAll('span'));
                    for (const span of spans) {
                        const txt = (span.textContent || span.innerText || '').trim();
                        if (/^[0-9,.]+[KMBkmb]?$/.test(txt) && txt.length > 0) {
                            return txt;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Fundweg 3: Suche nach Element, dessen Textinhalt auf "Follower" oder "Followers" endet
                const allElements = document.querySelectorAll('*');
                for (const el of allElements) {
                    const text = (el.textContent || '').trim();
                    // Nur reine Label-Knoten, keine Container: sonst liefert der
                    // Elternknoten die "Folge ich"-Zahl statt der Follower-Zahl.
                    if (text.length <= 12 && (text.endsWith('Follower') || text.endsWith('Followers'))) {
                        // Extrahiere Zahl aus demselben Elternknoten
                        const parent = el.parentElement;
                        if (parent) {
                            const siblings = parent.querySelectorAll('span, div');
                            for (const sibling of siblings) {
                                const sibText = (sibling.textContent || sibling.innerText || '').trim();
                                if (/^[0-9,.]+[KMBkmb]?$/.test(sibText) && sibText.length > 0) {
                                    return sibText;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                return null;
            }""")
            if not raw:
                return 0
            parsed = self._parse_follower_number(raw)
            return parsed if parsed is not None else 0
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"[GROWTH] follower-scraping fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "GROWTH", "DEBUG")
            return 0

    # Suffix -> Multiplikator. Reihenfolge egal, die Marker sind eindeutig.
    _FOLLOWER_COUNT_SUFFIXES = (
        ("K", 1_000),
        ("M", 1_000_000),
        ("B", 1_000_000_000),
    )

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_follower_count_text(raw: str) -> int:
        """Wandelt die X-Follower-Anzeige in eine ganze Zahl um.

        Regel — bitte NICHT "vereinfachen":
        1. MIT Suffix K/M/B ist die Zahl eine Dezimalzahl. Punkt UND Komma sind
           dort Dezimaltrennzeichen: "1.2K" und "1,2K" bedeuten beide 1200.
        2. OHNE Suffix sind Punkt UND Komma Tausendertrenner und werden
           ENTFERNT, nicht ersetzt: "1.234" -> 1234, "12,345" -> 12345.
           Frueher wurde "," durch "." ersetzt und float() genommen; dadurch
           wurde "1.234" zu 1 und die Funktion konnte prinzipiell keinen Wert
           ueber 1000 liefern.
        3. Nicht lesbar -> ValueError. Der Aufrufer faengt das ab und liefert 0.
        """
        text = (raw or "").strip()
        if not text:
            raise ValueError("leere Follower-Anzeige")

        for marker, mul in ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced._FOLLOWER_COUNT_SUFFIXES:
            if text.upper().endswith(marker):
                decimal = text[:-1].strip().replace(",", ".")
                return int(float(decimal) * mul)

        return int(text.replace(".", "").replace(",", ""))

    def _track_own_follower_count(self, count: int):
        """Speichert die eigene Follower-Zahl mit Timestamp in growth_history.json."""
        # S#56-Pre-U F34 Reach-Pivot Exit-Check. Wenn pivot aktiv und Follower-Target
        # erreicht -> WARNING im Log. Kein Auto-Revert; User entscheidet.
        try:
            _pivot_path = os.path.join(
                os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
                "state", "reach_pivot.json",
            )
            if os.path.exists(_pivot_path):
                with open(_pivot_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as _pf:
                    _pivot = json.load(_pf)
                if _pivot.get("active") and not _pivot.get("exit_triggered"):
                    _target = int(_pivot.get("target_followers", 50))
                    if count >= _target:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[F34] REACH-PIVOT EXIT-KRITERIUM ERREICHT: "
                            f"followers={count} >= target={_target}. "
                            f"User soll F34 reverten (Caps zurueck auf 7 Tweets / "
                            f"0.18 reply / 0.16 tweet share, follow caps 10/10).",
                            "GROWTH", "WARNING",
                        )
                        _pivot["exit_triggered"] = True
                        _pivot["exit_triggered_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                        _pivot["exit_followers"] = count
                        with open(_pivot_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as _pf:
                            json.dump(_pivot, _pf, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            gh_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "state", "growth_history.json")
            data = {}
            if os.path.exists(gh_path):
                with open(gh_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)

            timeline = data.setdefault("own_followers", [])
            today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

            # 1x pro Tag speichern — aber Schaetzeintraege (mit "note") mit echten Werten ueberschreiben
            changed = False
            if not timeline:
                timeline.append({"date": today, "count": count})
                changed = True
            elif timeline[-1].get("date") != today:
                timeline.append({"date": today, "count": count})
                changed = True
            elif timeline[-1].get("note"):
                # Replace estimated entry with real scraped value
                timeline[-1] = {"date": today, "count": count}
                changed = True

            if changed:
                # Max 90 Tage behalten
                data["own_followers"] = timeline[-90:]
                from bot_modules.safety_helpers import atomic_json_write
                atomic_json_write(gh_path, data, logger_func=lambda msg: self.safe_log(msg, "GROWTH", "ERROR"))
                self.safe_log(f"[GROWTH] Follower-Count gespeichert: {count}", "GROWTH", "DEBUG")
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"[GROWTH] Follower-Tracking fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "GROWTH", "DEBUG")

        # [GOALS] Auch in GoalTracker speichern für Follower-Ziel-Tracking
        if getattr(self, 'goal_tracker', None):
            try:
                self.goal_tracker.record_follower_count(count)
                # Auto-Deadline-Anpassung wenn Velocity das Ziel unrealistisch macht
                new_dl = self.goal_tracker.maybe_adjust_deadline()
                if new_dl:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GOALS] Deadline auto-angepasst → {new_dl} (Velocity realistisch)",
                        "GOALS", "WARNING",
                    )
                # Follower-Loss-Detection: Verlust >=3 in 3 Tagen → attack-Modus
                loss = self.goal_tracker.detect_follower_loss()
                if loss:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GOALS] FOLLOWER-VERLUST erkannt: -{loss['lost']} in {loss['days']} Tagen "
                        f"({loss['previous']}→{loss['current']})",
                        "GOALS", "WARNING",
                    )
                    if self.session_brain:
                        self.session_brain._strategy["mode"] = "attack"
                        self.session_brain._strategy["reply_penalty"] = max(
                            0.0, self.session_brain._strategy.get("reply_penalty", 0.0) - 0.15
                        )
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[GOALS] Reaktion: attack-Modus + Reply-Bonus aktiviert",
                            "GOALS", "INFO",
                        )
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _handle_stuck(self, stuck_detector) -> None:
        """Reagiert auf erkannte Stuck-Situation mit aktivem Taktikwechsel.

        Aufgerufen wenn stuck_detector.is_stuck() True zurückgibt.
        Wechselt Darwin-Strategie, erzwingt Pause oder setzt Brain-Penalties.
        """
        try:
            suggestion = stuck_detector.suggest_switch()
            stuck_actions = stuck_detector.get_stuck_actions()
            self.safe_log(
                f"[STUCK] Taktikwechsel ausgelöst | actions={stuck_actions} suggestion={suggestion}",
                "STUCK", "WARNING",
            )

            if suggestion == "change_darwin":
                # Darwin-Strategie wechseln: aktuell dominante Gewichte reduzieren
                try:
                    darwin = self._get_darwin()
                    if darwin:
                        # Einmaliger Session-Wechsel ohne persistente Policy-Mutation.
                        # Dauerhafte Gewichte duerfen nur verifizierte 24h-Outcomes aendern.
                        weights_raw = darwin._state.get("weights", {})
                        if weights_raw:
                            top = max(weights_raw, key=lambda k: float(weights_raw.get(k, 0)))
                            alternatives = [
                                strategy for strategy in (
                                    "scrape_rewrite", "llm_original"
                                )
                                if strategy != top
                            ]
                        else:
                            alternatives = ["scrape_rewrite", "llm_original"]
                        new_strategy = darwin.pick_strategy(available=alternatives)
                        self._darwin_forced_strategy_once = new_strategy
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[STUCK] Einmaliger Darwin-Wechsel: {new_strategy}",
                            "STUCK", "INFO",
                        )
                except Exception as _de:
                    self.safe_log(f"[STUCK] Darwin-Wechsel fehlgeschlagen: {_de}", "STUCK", "WARNING")
                # Brain-Penalty für stuck-Actions setzen
                if self.session_brain:
                    for _act in stuck_actions:
                        _pk = f"{_act}_penalty"
                        if _pk in self.session_brain._strategy:
                            self.session_brain._strategy[_pk] = min(
                                0.6, self.session_brain._strategy.get(_pk, 0.0) + 0.3
                            )

            elif suggestion == "force_pause":
                _pause_sec = random.uniform(120, 240)
                self.safe_log(f"[STUCK] Zwangspause {_pause_sec:.0f}s", "STUCK", "INFO")
                self._idle_scroll(_pause_sec)

            else:  # force_action_type
                # Brain-Penalty für stuck-Actions erhöhen → Brain vermeidet diese Actions
                if self.session_brain:
                    for _act in stuck_actions:
                        _pk = f"{_act}_penalty"
                        if _pk in self.session_brain._strategy:
                            self.session_brain._strategy[_pk] = min(
                                0.6, self.session_brain._strategy.get(_pk, 0.0) + 0.3
                            )

        except Exception as _exc:
            self.safe_log(f"[STUCK] _handle_stuck fehlgeschlagen: {_exc}", "STUCK", "WARNING")
        finally:
            stuck_detector.reset()

    def _run_post_session_agents(self):
        """Fuehrt Post-Session agentic Module aus: StrategyAdvisor, ABTester."""
        sessions_completed = 0
        if self.session_brain and hasattr(self.session_brain, '_memory'):
            sessions_completed = int(self.session_brain._memory.get("sessions_completed", 0))

        # Meta-Policies duerfen nicht aus dem kontaminierten Legacy-Mix lernen.
        # Saubere Outcomes werden sofort gesammelt; Gewichtsaenderungen beginnen
        # konservativ erst ab der Council-Schwelle von 60 verified 24h-Samples.
        ledger = getattr(self, "outcome_ledger", None)
        if ledger is None:
            self.safe_log("[OUTCOME] Meta-Policy pausiert: Ledger fehlt", "LEARNING", "WARNING")
            return
        health = ledger.health()
        if (
            not health.get("policy_updates_allowed", False)
            or int(health.get("policy_eligible_count", 0) or 0) < 60
        ):
            self.safe_log(
                f"[OUTCOME] Meta-Policy bleibt auf Baseline "
                f"({health.get('policy_eligible_count', 0)}/60 verified Outcomes)",
                "LEARNING", "INFO",
            )
            return

        # 1. StrategyAdvisor — LLM-Meta-Analyse alle 5 Sessions
        try:
            from intelligent_features.strategy_advisor import StrategyAdvisor
            advisor = StrategyAdvisor(self.config)
            if advisor.should_run():
                self.safe_log("[ADVISOR] Starte Meta-Analyse...", "ADVISOR", "INFO")
                advice = advisor.run()
                if advice and advice.get("mode_hint"):
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[ADVISOR] Empfehlung: mode={advice.get('mode_hint')} "
                        f"boost={advice.get('boost_topics', [])} "
                        f"preferred={advice.get('preferred_mode', '')}",
                        "ADVISOR", "INFO",
                    )
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"[ADVISOR] Fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "ADVISOR", "WARNING")

        # 2. ABTester — Content-Pipeline-Evaluation alle 10 Sessions
        try:
            from learning.ab_tester import ABTester
            ab = ABTester()
            if ab.should_run(sessions_completed):
                self.safe_log("[AB] Starte Content-Pipeline-Evaluation...", "AB", "INFO")
                results = ab.run(sessions_completed)
                if results and results.get("winner"):
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[AB] Winner={results['winner']} | weights={results.get('recommended_weights', {})}",
                        "AB", "INFO",
                    )
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"[AB] Fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "AB", "WARNING")

    def _get_follower_growth_rate(self) -> float:
        """Berechnet Follower-Wachstumsrate der letzten 7 Tage. Returns 0.0 wenn keine Daten."""
        try:
            gh_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "state", "growth_history.json")
            if not os.path.exists(gh_path):
                return 0.0
            with open(gh_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
            timeline = data.get("own_followers", [])
            if len(timeline) < 2:
                return 0.0
            recent = timeline[-1]["count"]
            week_ago = timeline[-min(7, len(timeline))]["count"]
            if week_ago <= 0:
                return 0.0
            return (recent - week_ago) / week_ago  # z.B. 0.05 = 5% Wachstum
        except Exception:
            return 0.0

    def _pick_keyword(self) -> str:
        """Waehlt das naechste Such-Keyword intelligent aus.

        Drei Schichten:
        1. topic_switch_needed (Brain) → anderes Keyword als zuletzt
        2. Topic-Performance (TweetLearner) → erfolgreiches Thema mit 40% Chance
        3. Fallback: random.choice wie bisher
        """
        keywords = self.config.get("search_keywords", []) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else []
        if not keywords:
            return "AI"

        pool = list(keywords)

        # Layer -1: Persona-Relevanz (Persona-Topics erhalten Gewichts-Boost im Pool)
        if getattr(self, "persona_agent", None):
            try:
                pool = self.persona_agent.score_topic_relevance(pool)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Schicht 0: Brain-Topic-History (cross-session, 25% Chance)
        if self.session_brain and random.random() < 0.25:
            try:
                brain_topic = self.session_brain.get_best_topic(pool)
                if brain_topic:
                    self._last_used_keyword = brain_topic
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[BRAIN] Topic-History: '{brain_topic}' bevorzugt",
                        "BRAIN", "DEBUG",
                    )
                    return brain_topic
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Schicht 1: Brain sagt Topic-Switch → aktuelles Keyword ausschliessen
        brain_says_switch = False
        if self.session_brain:
            try:
                brain_says_switch = bool(
                    self.session_brain.get_agentic_state().get("topic_switch_needed", False)
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

        last_kw = self._last_used_keyword
        if brain_says_switch and last_kw and len(pool) > 1:
            alt_pool = [k for k in pool if k != last_kw]
            if alt_pool:
                pool = alt_pool
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[BRAIN] TOPIC-SWITCH: weg von '{last_kw}' ({len(pool)} Alternativen)",
                    "BRAIN", "INFO",
                )

        # Schicht 2: Best-Topic aus TweetLearner (40% Chance, min 3 Tweets Daten)
        if self.tweet_learner and random.random() < 0.40:
            try:
                best_topics = self.tweet_learner.get_best_topics(min_tweets=3)
                if best_topics:
                    top_topic = best_topics[0][0].lower()
                    top_rate = best_topics[0][1]
                    # Nur wenn messbar besser (>55% Erfolgsrate)
                    if top_rate > 0.55:
                        matched = [k for k in pool if top_topic in k.lower() or k.lower() in top_topic]
                        if matched:
                            chosen = random.choice(matched)
                            self._last_used_keyword = chosen
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[FEEDBACK] Topic-Routing: '{chosen}' (best={top_topic}, rate={top_rate:.0%})",
                                "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG",
                            )
                            return chosen
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Schicht 3: Fallback random
        chosen = random.choice(pool)
        self._last_used_keyword = chosen
        return chosen

    _GRAPH_ALLOWED_TONES = frozenset({
        "DIRECT",
        "QUESTIONING",
        "IRONIC",
        "VULNERABLE",
        "PROVOCATIVE",
        "MINIMAL",
        "AGGRESSIVE",
        "FUNNY",
        "DATA_DRIVEN",
        "STORY",
    })
    _GRAPH_FORMAT_GUIDANCE = {
        "forensic_observation": (
            "forensische Beobachtung: konkretes Detail -> Bedeutung"
        ),
        "thesis_reason": (
            "These mit Begruendung: klare Position -> ein belastbarer Grund"
        ),
        "decision_rule": (
            "Entscheidungsregel: Wenn-dann -> Grenze oder Ausnahme"
        ),
        "external_fact_implication": (
            "externer Fakt mit Implikation: belegbarer Input -> CFO-/Audit-Folge"
        ),
        "verified_field_note": (
            "verifizierte Feldnotiz: reales Artefakt/Experiment -> Erkenntnis"
        ),
        "tradeoff": (
            "Trade-off: zwei attraktive Ziele -> ehrliche Entscheidungskosten"
        ),
    }
    _GRAPH_FORMAT_ALIASES = {
        "four_step_contrarian": "thesis_reason",
        "signal_paradox_operator": "forensic_observation",
    }
    _GRAPH_SAFE_TOPIC_FALLBACKS = (
        "Agenten-Governance",
        "KI-Verifikationsschulden",
        "Automatisierungs-Kontrollsysteme",
    )
    _GRAPH_INSTRUCTION_MARKERS = re.compile(
        r"(?:"
        r"\b(?:ignore|disregard|forget)\b.{0,48}"
        r"\b(?:previous|prior|all|instructions?|rules?|prompts?)\b"
        r"|\bignorier(?:e)?\b.{0,48}"
        r"\b(?:alle|vorherige[nrms]?|bisherige[nrms]?)?\s*"
        r"(?:anweisungen?|regeln?|prompts?)\b"
        r"|\b(?:act\s+as|you\s+are|du\s+bist|override|jailbreak|"
        r"befolge|write\s+only|output\s+only|schreibe\s+nur)\b"
        r"|\b(?:gib|verrate|reveal)\b.{0,32}"
        r"\b(?:secrets?|geheimnisse?|system[-\s]?prompt)\b"
        r"|\b(?:system|developer)\s+(?:prompt|message|role)\b\s*:?"
        r"|<\|(?:system|assistant|developer|user)\|>"
        r"|```|###"
        r")",
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )

    @classmethod
