    def _normalize_graph_prompt_values(
        cls,
        values: object,
        *,
        limit: int = 5,
        max_length: int = 120,
    ) -> list[str]:
        """Normalize graph-derived prompt data and reject instruction markers."""
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            return []
        normalized_values: list[str] = []
        for raw_value in values:
            if not isinstance(raw_value, str):
                continue
            value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", raw_value)
            value = " ".join(value.split())
            value = re.sub(r"\s*([-–—/])\s*", r"\1", value).strip()
            if not value or len(value) > max_length:
                continue
            if cls._GRAPH_INSTRUCTION_MARKERS.search(value):
                continue
            if any(
                not (
                    char.isalnum()
                    or char.isspace()
                    or char in "äöüÄÖÜß-–—.,:;!?/&+()%€'"
                )
                for char in value
            ):
                continue
            if value not in normalized_values:
                normalized_values.append(value)
            if len(normalized_values) >= limit:
                break
        return normalized_values

    @classmethod
    def _sanitize_graph_content_directive(cls, directive: object) -> dict:
        """Reduce an external graph directive to fixed, bounded fields."""
        if not isinstance(directive, dict):
            return {}
        sanitized: dict[str, object] = {}
        for field, max_length in (
            ("boost_topics", 100),
            ("avoid_topics", 100),
            ("hook_patterns", 140),
        ):
            if field in directive:
                sanitized[field] = cls._normalize_graph_prompt_values(
                    directive.get(field),
                    max_length=max_length,
                )

        tones = []
        raw_tones = directive.get("preferred_tones", [])
        if isinstance(raw_tones, list):
            for raw_tone in raw_tones:
                tone = str(raw_tone).strip().upper()
                if tone in cls._GRAPH_ALLOWED_TONES and tone not in tones:
                    tones.append(tone)
                if len(tones) >= 5:
                    break
        if "preferred_tones" in directive:
            sanitized["preferred_tones"] = tones

        source_multipliers: dict[str, float] = {}
        raw_source_multipliers = directive.get("source_multipliers", {})
        if isinstance(raw_source_multipliers, dict):
            for source in (
                "scrape_rewrite",
                "observation_tension",
                "llm_original",
            ):
                try:
                    value = float(raw_source_multipliers[source])
                except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
                    continue
                if value == value and abs(value) != float("inf"):
                    source_multipliers[source] = min(1.25, max(0.75, value))
        if "source_multipliers" in directive:
            sanitized["source_multipliers"] = source_multipliers

        format_weights: dict[str, float] = {}
        raw_format_weights = directive.get("format_weights", {})
        if isinstance(raw_format_weights, dict):
            for raw_format, raw_value in sorted(raw_format_weights.items()):
                content_format = cls._GRAPH_FORMAT_ALIASES.get(
                    str(raw_format),
                    str(raw_format),
                )
                if content_format not in cls._GRAPH_FORMAT_GUIDANCE:
                    continue
                try:
                    value = float(raw_value)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    continue
                if value == value and abs(value) != float("inf"):
                    bounded = min(1.25, max(0.75, value))
                    format_weights[content_format] = max(
                        bounded,
                        format_weights.get(content_format, 0.75),
                    )
        if "format_weights" in directive:
            sanitized["format_weights"] = format_weights

        if "exploration_floor" in directive:
            try:
                exploration_floor = float(directive["exploration_floor"])
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                exploration_floor = 0.15
            if exploration_floor != exploration_floor:
                exploration_floor = 0.15
            sanitized["exploration_floor"] = min(
                0.5,
                max(0.15, exploration_floor),
            )

        # prompt_emphasis is deliberately never copied: free-form graph text
        # must not cross into a system prompt.
        return sanitized

    def _clear_graph_content_directive(self) -> None:
        """Clear every cached graph field when governance becomes inactive."""
        self._graph_content_directive = {}
        self._graph_shadow_content_directive = {}
        self._graph_content_rule_ids = []
        self._graph_content_signature = ""

    def _refresh_graph_content_directive(self, graph_advisor):
        """Lädt die evidenzgebundene Content-Empfehlung des Graphen.

        Im Shadow-Modus wird nur die vorgeschlagene Direktive protokolliert;
        der produktive Prompt erhält ausschließlich ``applied_directive``.
        """
        result = graph_advisor.content_directive(
            content_kind="standalone_24h"
        )
        applied = result.get("applied_directive", {})
        proposed = result.get("proposed_directive", {})
        self._graph_content_directive = self._sanitize_graph_content_directive(
            applied
        )
        self._graph_shadow_content_directive = (
            self._sanitize_graph_content_directive(proposed)
        )
        self._graph_content_rule_ids = list(
            result.get("active_rule_ids", []) or []
        )
        signature = json.dumps(
            {
                "graph_version": result.get("graph_version"),
                "rules": self._graph_content_rule_ids,
                "effective_behavior": result.get("effective_behavior"),
                "proposed": self._graph_shadow_content_directive,
            },
            sort_keys=True,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            default=str,
        )
        if signature != getattr(self, "_graph_content_signature", ""):
            self._graph_content_signature = signature
            self.safe_event(
                "GRAPH",
                "graph_content_directive",
                {
                    "graph_version": result.get("graph_version"),
                    "rule_ids": self._graph_content_rule_ids,
                    "effective_behavior": result.get("effective_behavior"),
                    "reason_codes": result.get("reason_codes", []),
                    "has_proposal": bool(self._graph_shadow_content_directive),
                    "applied": bool(self._graph_content_directive),
                },
                "INFO",
            )
        return result

    def _build_publish_metadata(
        self,
        payload: dict,
        final_text: str,
    ) -> dict:
        """Erzeugt die unveränderliche Kette von Entscheidung zu Outcome."""
        source = str(payload.get("source") or "unknown")
        text = str(final_text or "")
        text_hash = hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        decision_seed = (
            f"{time.time_ns()}|{source}|{text_hash}|"
            f"{payload.get('prompt_version', '')}"
        )
        decision_id = str(payload.get("decision_id") or "").strip()
        if not decision_id:
            decision_id = (
                "publish:"
                + hashlib.sha256(
                    decision_seed.encode("utf-8")
                ).hexdigest()[:24]
            )
        hook = str(payload.get("hook") or "").strip()
        if not hook:
            hook = next(
                (
                    line.strip()
                    for line in text.splitlines()
                    if line.strip()
                ),
                text,
            )[:240]
        gate_scores = payload.get("gate_scores", {})
        if not isinstance(gate_scores, dict):
            gate_scores = {}
        # Link the plan active at publication, not a guessed generation origin.
        plan = getattr(self, "session_plan", None)
        plan_id = plan.get("plan_id") if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
        plan_metadata = {}
        reach_choice_id = payload.get("reach_choice_id")
        if isinstance(reach_choice_id, str) and re.fullmatch(r"reach:[0-9a-f]{32}", reach_choice_id):
            plan_metadata["reach_choice_id"] = reach_choice_id
        if isinstance(plan_id, str) and plan_id.strip() and len(plan_id) <= 120:
            plan_metadata["publish_plan_id"] = plan_id
        result = {
            **plan_metadata,
            "decision_id": decision_id,
            "final_text_hash": f"sha256:{text_hash}",
            "prompt_version": str(
                payload.get("prompt_version")
                or f"unversioned:{source}"
            )[:120],
            "model": str(
                payload.get("model")
                or payload.get("model_preference")
                or "unknown"
            )[:100],
            "temperature": payload.get("temperature", 0.0),
            "tone": str(payload.get("tone") or "unknown")[:80],
            "hook": hook,
            "format": str(
                payload.get("format")
                or payload.get("structure")
                or "unknown"
            )[:100],
            "source_arm": source,
            "editorial_theme": str(payload.get("editorial_theme") or "")[:100],
            "topic_selection_origin": str(payload.get("topic_selection_origin") or "")[:100],
            "editorial_experiment": dict(payload.get("editorial_experiment") or {})
            if isinstance(payload.get("editorial_experiment"), dict) else {},
            "gate_scores": {
                **gate_scores,
                "release_passed": True,
            },
            "graph_rule_ids": list(
                getattr(self, "_graph_content_rule_ids", []) or []
            )[:20],
        }
        try:
            from content.source_context import clean_source_context
            context = clean_source_context(
                payload.get("source_context"), result["final_text_hash"]
            )
            if context is not None:
                result["source_context"] = context
        except Exception:
            pass
        return result

    @classmethod
    def _has_valid_standalone_payload_format(cls, payload: object) -> bool:
        """Require a measured format arm before any own standalone post."""
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return False
        return str(payload.get("format") or "") in cls._GRAPH_FORMAT_GUIDANCE

    def _apply_self_critic_safely(
        self,
        approved_text: str,
        payload: dict,
        *,
        api_key: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Preserve the selected, measured format after the release gate.

        The former critic performed another paid LLM rewrite immediately before
        publishing. That made the recorded format unreliable and could
        reintroduce claims or sentence templates after generation.
        """
        return approved_text

    def _apply_graph_topic_choice(self, fallback_topic: str) -> str:
        """Nutzt positive und negative Themenregeln mit Exploration."""
        directive = self._sanitize_graph_content_directive(
            getattr(self, "_graph_content_directive", {})
        )
        topics = list(directive.get("boost_topics", []) or [])
        avoid_topics = list(directive.get("avoid_topics", []) or [])
        if not topics and not avoid_topics:
            return fallback_topic
        try:
            exploration_floor = min(
                0.5,
                max(0.15, float(directive.get("exploration_floor", 0.15))),
            )
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            exploration_floor = 0.15

        def _is_avoided(candidate: object) -> bool:
            normalized = " ".join(str(candidate or "").casefold().split())
            if not normalized:
                return False
            return any(
                (
                    normalized == " ".join(avoid.casefold().split())
                    or " ".join(avoid.casefold().split()) in normalized
                )
                for avoid in avoid_topics
                if str(avoid).strip()
            )

        if random.random() < exploration_floor:
            return fallback_topic
        safe_boosts = [
            topic for topic in topics[:5] if not _is_avoided(topic)
        ]
        if safe_boosts:
            return random.choice(safe_boosts)
        if not _is_avoided(fallback_topic):
            return fallback_topic
        safe_fallbacks = [
            topic
            for topic in self._GRAPH_SAFE_TOPIC_FALLBACKS
            if not _is_avoided(topic)
        ]
        return (
            random.choice(safe_fallbacks)
            if safe_fallbacks
            else fallback_topic
        )

    def _get_adaptive_source_weights(self):
        """Liefert den verifizierten Zweiarm-Mix für normale und Tournament-Posts."""
        from learning.content_strategy_darwin import ContentStrategyDarwin

        active = ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced._active_darwin_strategies()
        weights = {source_key: 1.0 for source_key in active}
        try:
            get_darwin = getattr(self, "_get_darwin", None)
            # A bare test/dry-run object has no TweetLearner and must not pull
            # persistent production Darwin state into an otherwise neutral
            # source mix. Live bot instances have the learner; an explicitly
            # injected _darwin object remains supported for focused tests.
            has_learning_context = (
                getattr(self, "tweet_learner", None) is not None
                or hasattr(self, "_darwin")
                or "_get_darwin" in getattr(self, "__dict__", {})
            )
            darwin = (
                get_darwin()
                if callable(get_darwin) and has_learning_context
                else None
            )
            if darwin is not None:
                learned = darwin.get_selection_weights(active)
                if learned:
                    weights = {
                        source_key: float(learned.get(source_key, 0.0))
                        for source_key in active
                    }
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Darwin-Gewichte nicht lesbar: {exc}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "DEBUG",
            )

        weights = ContentStrategyDarwin.normalize_selection_weights(weights)
        if getattr(self, "session_plan", None):
            try:
                from intelligent_features.session_planner import SessionPlanner

                weights = SessionPlanner.apply_source_multipliers(
                    weights,
                    self.session_plan,
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[PLAN] Source-Mix fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                    "PLANNER",
                    "DEBUG",
                )
            else:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[PLAN] Source-Mix: scrape={weights['scrape_rewrite']:.0%}, "
                    f"llm={weights['llm_original']:.0%}",
                    "PLANNER",
                    "DEBUG",
                )

        graph_directive = getattr(self, "_graph_content_directive", {})
        graph_multipliers = (
            graph_directive.get("source_multipliers", {})
            if isinstance(graph_directive, dict)
            else {}
        )
        if isinstance(graph_multipliers, dict):
            for source_key in weights:
                try:
                    multiplier = float(graph_multipliers.get(source_key, 1.0))
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    multiplier = 1.0
                weights[source_key] *= min(1.25, max(0.75, multiplier))

        # Session-local source learning: repeated deterministic hook failures
        # damp only the offending source for this rolling health window.  The
        # hook gate itself, pacing, target caps and live-action guards remain
        # unchanged; a fresh session naturally starts with the normal mix.
        try:
            monitor = getattr(self, "health_monitor", None)
            get_hook_rejections = getattr(monitor, "get_recent_hook_rejections", None)
            recent = get_hook_rejections() if callable(get_hook_rejections) else []
            counts = {source_key: 0 for source_key in weights}
            for event in recent if isinstance(recent, list) else []:
                source_key = str(event.get("source", "")).strip()
                if source_key in counts:
                    counts[source_key] += 1
            for source_key, count in counts.items():
                if count >= 2:
                    penalty = max(0.75, 1.0 - 0.10 * min(count, 3))
                    weights[source_key] *= penalty
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[FEEDBACK] Hook-Quelle gedämpft: {source_key} x{penalty:.2f} "
                        f"({count} Ablehnungen/10min)",
                        "FEEDBACK",
                        "INFO",
                    )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[FEEDBACK] Hook-Quellenfeedback übersprungen: {exc}",
                "FEEDBACK",
                "DEBUG",
            )

        return ContentStrategyDarwin.normalize_selection_weights(weights)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Tournament: Kandidaten vorab sammeln, besten zur Postzeit posten
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _tournament_collect_candidate(self):
        """Retain actual budgets outside payload metadata for late callbacks."""
        from core.llm_task_budget import task_budget

        receipts = getattr(self, "_candidate_budget_receipts", {})
        # Hold the object alongside its id to prevent accidental id reuse.
        receipts = {key: value for key, value in receipts.items()
                    if any(value[0] is payload for payload in self._tournament_pool)}
        self._candidate_budget_receipts = receipts
        before = {id(payload) for payload in self._tournament_pool}
        with task_budget(max_calls=96, label="candidate_generation") as budget:
            self._tournament_collect_candidate_bounded()
            for payload in self._tournament_pool:
                if id(payload) not in before:
                    receipts[id(payload)] = (payload, (budget,))
            if budget.exhausted:
                self._tournament_pool = [payload for payload in self._tournament_pool if id(payload) in before]
                self._candidate_budget_receipts = {key: value for key, value in receipts.items() if key in before}

    @bounded_llm_task(label="candidate_generation")
    def _tournament_collect_candidate_bounded(self):
        """Sammelt einen Tweet-Kandidaten fuer den Tournament-Pool.

        Wuerfelt eine Quelle basierend auf adaptiven Gewichten aus Engagement-Daten
        und fuegt das Ergebnis dem Pool hinzu (max 8).
        """
        if len(self._tournament_pool) >= 8:
            return

        # Refresh measured topic evidence before choosing/generating a candidate,
        # not only after a pre-generated winner reaches the publication timer.
        # This reads local state; unchanged fingerprints do not revise the plan.
        self._refresh_brain_quality_brief()

        news_cfg = self.config.get("news_commentary", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
        news_first = (
            isinstance(news_cfg, dict)
            and bool(news_cfg.get("enabled", True))
            and bool(news_cfg.get("force_first_tweet", True))
            and int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0) <= 0
            and not any((p or {}).get("source") == "news_commentary" for p in self._tournament_pool)
        )
        if news_first:
            try:
                payload = self._generate_news_commentary_tweet()
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"[TOURNAMENT] NEWS_COMMENTARY fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING")
                payload = None
            if payload and payload.get("text"):
                payload["darwin_strategy"] = "news_commentary"
                self._tournament_pool.append(payload)
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[TOURNAMENT] Kandidat #{len(self._tournament_pool)} gesammelt "
                    f"(Source=NEWS_COMMENTARY, {len(payload['text'])} chars): {payload['text'][:60]}...",
                    "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
                )
                return

        source_label = ""
        payload = None
        selected_arm = None
        active = ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced._active_darwin_strategies()
        try:
            get_darwin = getattr(self, "_get_darwin", None)
            darwin = get_darwin() if callable(get_darwin) else None
            pick_bootstrap = getattr(darwin, "pick_bootstrap_strategy", None)
            if callable(pick_bootstrap):
                recent_published = (
                    ReplyButtonOptimiertBotEnhanced
                    ._recent_published_darwin_strategies(self, active)
                )
                try:
                    bootstrap_choice = pick_bootstrap(
                        active,
                        recent_published=recent_published,
                    )
                except TypeError:
                    bootstrap_choice = pick_bootstrap(active)
                if bootstrap_choice in active:
                    selected_arm = bootstrap_choice
        except Exception as exc:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Bootstrap-Auswahl fehlgeschlagen: {exc}",
                "TOURNAMENT",
                "DEBUG",
            )
        if selected_arm is None:
            weights = self._get_adaptive_source_weights()
            selected_arm = (
                "scrape_rewrite"
                if random.random() < float(weights.get("scrape_rewrite", 0.0))
                else "llm_original"
            )

        try:
            if selected_arm == "scrape_rewrite":
                source_label = "SCRAPE_REWRITE"
                payload = self._generate_scraped_tweet_payload()
            elif selected_arm == "llm_original":
                source_label = "LLM_ORIGINAL"
                payload = self._generate_llm_original_tweet()
            else:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[TOURNAMENT] Ungültiger aktiver Arm: {selected_arm}",
                    "TOURNAMENT",
                    "WARNING",
                )
                return
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"[TOURNAMENT] Kandidat-Sammlung fehlgeschlagen ({source_label}): {e}", "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING")
            return

        if payload and payload.get('text'):
            payload["darwin_strategy"] = selected_arm
            self._tournament_pool.append(payload)
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Kandidat #{len(self._tournament_pool)} gesammelt "
                f"(Source={source_label}, {len(payload['text'])} chars): {payload['text'][:60]}...",
                "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
            )
        else:
            self.safe_log(f"[TOURNAMENT] Kein Kandidat aus {source_label}", "TOURNAMENT", "DEBUG")

    def _service_tournament_queue(self, current_time, remaining_time) -> None:
        """Bedient den Candidate-Pool auch während Brain-Idle-Iterationen."""
        try:
            if not self._action_allowed("tweet"):
                return
            now_ts = time.time()
            if (
                now_ts - self._tournament_last_fill_ts >= self._tournament_fill_interval
                and self._tournament_winner is None
            ):
                self._tournament_last_fill_ts = now_ts
                self._tournament_collect_candidate()
                pool_after = len(self._tournament_pool)
                # Candidate generation (insbesondere Agentic) kann lange dauern;
                # fuer Endgame-Entscheidungen daher den Zeit-Snapshot nachher
                # gegen die tatsaechlich verstrichene Zeit korrigieren.
                service_now = datetime.now()
                elapsed_since_snapshot = max(
                    0.0, (service_now - current_time).total_seconds()
                )
                remaining_min = (
                    remaining_time.total_seconds() - elapsed_since_snapshot
                ) / 60.0
                if remaining_min <= 0:
                    self.safe_log(
                        "[TOURNAMENT] Sessionende waehrend Kandidatengenerierung; Winner verworfen",
                        "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
                    )
                    return
                try:
                    elapsed_min = (
                        (service_now - self.session_start_time).total_seconds() / 60.0
                        if self.session_start_time else 0.0
                    )
                except Exception:
                    elapsed_min = 0.0
                endgame_remaining = remaining_min < self._tournament_endgame_threshold_min
                endgame_elapsed = elapsed_min >= self._tournament_short_session_endgame_min
                endgame = endgame_remaining or endgame_elapsed
                if pool_after >= 2 or (endgame and pool_after >= 1):
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[TOURNAMENT] Pool={pool_after} (endgame={endgame}, "
                        f"rem={remaining_min:.1f}m, elapsed={elapsed_min:.1f}m, "
                        f"trigger=remaining:{endgame_remaining}/elapsed:{endgame_elapsed}) -> select_winner",
                        "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
                    )
                    self._tournament_select_winner(allow_endgame=endgame)
                    winner_set = self._tournament_winner is not None
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[TOURNAMENT] select_winner fertig | winner_set={winner_set} | pool_after={len(self._tournament_pool)}",
                        "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
                    )
        except Exception as tournament_error:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Fehler im Sammel-Hook: {tournament_error}",
                "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING",
            )

    def _tournament_select_winner(self, allow_endgame: bool = False):
        """Clear stale side effects even when an exhausted wrapper skips work."""
        from core.llm_task_budget import task_budget

        self._tournament_winner = None
        receipts = getattr(self, "_candidate_budget_receipts", {})
        self._tournament_pool = [payload for payload in self._tournament_pool
                                if not (id(payload) in receipts and receipts[id(payload)][0] is payload
                                        and any(budget.exhausted for budget in receipts[id(payload)][1]))]
        self._candidate_budget_receipts = {id(payload): receipts[id(payload)] for payload in self._tournament_pool
                                          if id(payload) in receipts and receipts[id(payload)][0] is payload}
        with task_budget(max_calls=96, label="candidate_selection") as budget:
            result = self._tournament_select_winner_bounded(allow_endgame=allow_endgame)
            if budget.exhausted:
                self._tournament_winner = None
                self._tournament_pool = []
                self._candidate_budget_receipts = {}
            elif self._tournament_winner is not None:
                winner = self._tournament_winner
                source_receipt = self._candidate_budget_receipts.get(id(winner), (winner, ()))[1]
                self._candidate_budget_receipts = {id(winner): (winner, (*source_receipt, budget))}
            elif not self._tournament_pool:
                self._candidate_budget_receipts = {}
            return result

    @bounded_llm_task(label="candidate_selection")
    def _tournament_select_winner_bounded(self, allow_endgame: bool = False):
        """Fuehrt Tournament ueber gesammelte Kandidaten durch.

        Nutzt gatekeeper.tournament_check() nur zum Scoren unveraenderter Texte.
        Speichert Gewinner in _tournament_winner und leert den Pool.

        allow_endgame: Wenn True, wird auch bei Pool=1 selektiert (Session-Ende-Fallback,
        S#53 F2 - verhindert dass einziger Kandidat ungenutzt verfaellt).
        """
        self.safe_log(
            f"[TOURNAMENT] select_winner ENTRY | pool_size={len(self._tournament_pool)} | gatekeeper_set={self.content_gatekeeper is not None} | endgame={allow_endgame}",
            "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
        )
        min_required = 1 if allow_endgame else 2
        if len(self._tournament_pool) < min_required:
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Zu wenig Kandidaten ({len(self._tournament_pool)}, min={min_required}), warte auf mehr",
                "TOURNAMENT", "DEBUG",
            )
            return

        texts = [p['text'] for p in self._tournament_pool]
        self.safe_log(
            f"[TOURNAMENT] Starte Tournament mit {len(texts)} Kandidaten",
            "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
        )

        winner_text = None

        # Gatekeeper-Tournament: verdict-only scoring of original candidates.
        if self.content_gatekeeper:
            try:
                approved, result = self.content_gatekeeper.tournament_check(texts, content_type="tweet")
                if approved and result:
                    winner_text = result
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"[TOURNAMENT] Gatekeeper-Fehler: {e}", "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING")

        if not winner_text:
            pool_size = len(self._tournament_pool)
            self._tournament_pool = []
            self._tournament_winner = None
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Kein freigegebener Originalkandidat aus {pool_size}; "
                "Posting verworfen",
                "TOURNAMENT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return

        # Finde das passende Payload zum Gewinner-Text
        winner_payload = None
        for p in self._tournament_pool:
            if p['text'] == winner_text:
                winner_payload = p
                break
        if not winner_payload:
            pool_size = len(self._tournament_pool)
            self._tournament_pool = []
            self._tournament_winner = None
            self.safe_log(
                f"[TOURNAMENT] Nicht-originaler Gewinner aus {pool_size} "
                "Kandidaten verworfen",
                "TOURNAMENT",
                "WARNING",
            )
            return

        budget = current_budget()
        if budget is not None and budget.exhausted:
            self._tournament_pool = []
            self._tournament_winner = None
            self.safe_log("[TOURNAMENT] Budget exhausted; winner discarded", "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING")
            return
        self._tournament_winner = winner_payload
        pool_size = len(self._tournament_pool)
        self._tournament_pool = []
        self.safe_log(
            f"[TOURNAMENT] Gewinner aus {pool_size} Kandidaten: {winner_text[:80]}...",
            "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
        )

    def _consume_tournament_winner(self):
        """Transfer the in-memory receipt with its exact payload, then forget it."""
        payload = self._tournament_winner
        self._tournament_winner = None
        receipt = getattr(self, "_candidate_budget_receipts", {}).pop(id(payload), None)
        return payload, receipt[1] if receipt and receipt[0] is payload else None

    def connection_resilient_retweet(self, keyword):
        """Verbindungs-resiliente Retweet-Funktion (erweitert)."""
        try:
            config = self.config if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            session_limit = config.get("session_limits", {}).get("retweets_per_session", 0)
            hour_limit = config.get("safety_limits", {}).get("per_hour", {}).get("retweet", 0)
            if (isinstance(session_limit, bool) or not isinstance(session_limit, int)
                    or session_limit <= 0 or self.retweets_posted >= session_limit
                    or isinstance(hour_limit, bool) or not isinstance(hour_limit, int)
                    or hour_limit <= 0):
                return False
            self.safe_log(f"STARTE RETWEET-SUCHE FUER: {keyword}", "RETWEETS", "INFO")
            
            if not self._action_allowed("retweet"):
                self.safe_log("Retweet uebersprungen (SAFETY: per-hour Limit erreicht)", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                return False

            if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung konnte nicht wiederhergestellt werden", "RETWEETS", "ERROR")
                return False
            
            search_url = f"https://x.com/search?q={self.normalize_search_keyword(keyword).replace(' ', '%20')}&src=typed_query&f=top"
            self.driver.get(search_url)
            self.human_pause(3.5, 6.5)
            
            tweet_containers = self.driver.find_elements(By.XPATH, "//article[@data-testid='tweet']")
            if not tweet_containers:
                self.safe_log("Keine Tweets fuer Retweet gefunden", "RETWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            
                        # Auswahl: bevorzugt sehr erfolgreiche Tweets, sonst neuere Tweets
            selected_tweet = None
            try:
                sf = self.config.get('success_filter', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                min_likes = int(sf.get('min_likes', 250))
                min_views = int(sf.get('min_views', 20000))
                high_engagement = self.filter_high_engagement_tweets(tweet_containers[:20], min_likes=min_likes, min_views=min_views)
                if high_engagement:
                    def _score(t):
                        m = self.extract_tweet_engagement(t) or {}
                        return m.get('likes', 0) + 2*m.get('retweets', 0) + 3*m.get('comments', 0)
                    high_engagement.sort(key=_score, reverse=True)
                    selected_tweet = high_engagement[0]
                    self.safe_log(f"RETWEETS: Sehr erfolgreiche Auswahl (min_likes={min_likes}, min_views={min_views})", "RETWEETS", "INFO")
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"High-Engagement-Auswahl nicht moeglich: {e}", "RETWEETS", "WARNING")

            if not selected_tweet:
                recent_tweets = self.filter_recent_tweets(tweet_containers[:10], hours=48)
                if not recent_tweets:
                    recent_tweets = tweet_containers[:3]  # Fallback auf erste 3 wenn keine neuen gefunden
                selected_tweet = random.choice(recent_tweets[:3])

            target_id = self._like_target_status_id(selected_tweet)
            if not target_id:
                return False
            action_store = self._like_action_store()
            if not action_store.is_allowed("retweet", hour_limit):
                return False

            root_ready_script = """
                const root = arguments[0];
                if (!root || !root.matches('article') ||
                    root.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') ||
                    root.parentElement?.closest('article')) return false;
                const direct = node => node.closest('article') === root &&
                    !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]');
                return !Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="unretweet"]')).some(direct) &&
                    Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="retweet"]')).some(direct);
            """
            if self.driver.execute_script(root_ready_script, selected_tweet) is not True:
                return False

            def root_opener(button):
                return self.driver.execute_script("""
                    const root = arguments[0], node = arguments[1];
                    return !!root && !!node && node.matches('[data-testid="retweet"]') &&
                        node.closest('article') === root &&
                        !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]');
                """, selected_tweet, button) is True
            
            retweet_selectors = [
                ".//*[@data-testid='retweet']",
            ]
            
            retweet_button = None
            # [EMOJI] RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Intelligente Button-Suche mit Fallback-Strategien
            for i, selector in enumerate(retweet_selectors):
                try:
                    buttons = selected_tweet.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                    if buttons:
                        # [ZIEL] INTELLIGENTE BUTTON-VALIDIERUNG
                        for button in buttons:
                            try:
                                # Pruefe ob Button sichtbar und klickbar ist
                                if root_opener(button) and button.is_displayed() and button.is_enabled():
                                    # Pruefe Button-Position (nicht ausserhalb des Viewports)
                                    location = button.location
                                    if location['x'] >= 0 and location['y'] >= 0:
                                        retweet_button = button
                                        self.safe_log(f"[EMOJI] RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Button gefunden mit Selektor {i+1}/{len(retweet_selectors)}: {selector}", "RETWEETS", "DEBUG")
                                        break
                            except Exception:
                                continue
                        if retweet_button:
                            break
                except Exception:
                    continue
            
            # [ZIEL] FALLBACK-STRATEGIE: Scroll und erneute Suche
            if not retweet_button:
                self.safe_log("[REFRESH] RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Erste Suche fehlgeschlagen, versuche Scroll-Strategie", "RETWEETS", "DEBUG")
                try:
                    # Sanftes Scrollen zum Tweet
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center'});", selected_tweet)
                    self.human_pause(2.0, 3.5)
                    
                    # Erneute Suche mit Top-Selektoren
                    priority_selectors = retweet_selectors[:8]  # Bewaehrte Selektoren zuerst
                    for selector in priority_selectors:
                        try:
                            buttons = selected_tweet.find_elements(By.XPATH, selector)
                            if buttons and root_opener(buttons[0]) and buttons[0].is_displayed():
                                retweet_button = buttons[0]
                                self.safe_log(f"[ZIEL] RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Button nach Scroll gefunden: {selector}", "RETWEETS", "DEBUG")
                                break
                        except Exception:
                            continue
                except Exception:
                    pass
            
            if not retweet_button:
                self.safe_log("[ALARM] RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Alle Strategien fehlgeschlagen - Button nicht gefunden", "RETWEETS", "WARNING")
                return False
            
            try:
                # [EMOJI] INTELLIGENTER RETWEET-KLICK
                try:
                    if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                        self.safe_log("Retweet finaler Versand durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                        return False
                    retweet_button.click()
                except Exception:
                    # Fallback: JavaScript-Klick
                    if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                        self.safe_log("Retweet JS-Fallback durch Account-Hold blockiert", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                        return False
                    self.driver.execute_script("arguments[0].click();", retweet_button)
                
                self.human_pause(1.2, 2.0)
                
                # [ZIEL] ERWEITERTE BESTAeTIGUNGS-SELEKTOREN
                confirm_selectors = [
                    "//div[@data-testid='retweetConfirm']",
                    "//button[@data-testid='retweetConfirm']",
                    "//div[contains(text(), 'Retweet')]",
                    "//button[contains(text(), 'Retweet')]",
                    # [EMOJI] NEUE INTELLIGENTE BESTAeTIGUNGS-SELEKTOREN:
                    "//div[contains(text(), 'Retweeten')]",
                    "//button[contains(text(), 'Retweeten')]",
                    "//div[contains(text(), 'Repost')]",
                    "//button[contains(text(), 'Repost')]",
                    "//div[@role='menuitem'][contains(text(), 'Retweet')]",
                    "//button[@role='menuitem'][contains(text(), 'Retweet')]",
                    "//div[contains(@class, 'css-1dbjc4n r-1loqt21')]//div[contains(text(), 'Retweet')]"
                ]
                
                # [ZIEL] INTELLIGENTE BESTAeTIGUNGS-SUCHE
                confirmed = False
                for i, selector in enumerate(confirm_selectors):
                    try:
                        confirm_button = self.driver.find_element(By.XPATH, selector)
                        if confirm_button.is_displayed():
                            if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                                return False
                            if not action_store.reserve("retweet", target_id, hour_limit):
                                return False
                            self._record_action_timestamp("retweet")
                            try:
                                if not account_actions_allowed(self):
                                    action_store.mark_uncertain("retweet", target_id)
                                    return False
                                confirm_button.click()
                            except Exception:
                                action_store.mark_uncertain("retweet", target_id)
                                return False
                            # A click is not proof. Re-read the same canonical
                            # target and its toggled DOM state, never resend.
                            action_store.mark_uncertain("retweet", target_id)
                            self.human_pause(1.2, 2.0)
                            try:
                                if self._like_target_status_id(selected_tweet) != target_id:
                                    return False
                                toggled = self.driver.execute_script("""
                                    const root = arguments[0];
                                    if (!root || !root.matches('article') ||
                                        root.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') ||
                                        root.parentElement?.closest('article')) return false;
                                    return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="unretweet"]'))
                                        .some(node => node.closest('article') === root &&
                                            !node.closest('[data-testid="quoteTweet"]') &&
                                            node.getClientRects().length > 0);
                                """, selected_tweet)
                                if toggled is not True:
                                    return False
                                if not action_store.mark_confirmed("retweet", target_id):
                                    return False
                            except Exception:
                                return False
                            
                            self.safe_log(f"RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE: Bestaetigung erfolgreich mit Selektor {i+1}/{len(confirm_selectors)}", "RETWEETS", "DEBUG")
                            confirmed = True
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                
                if not confirmed:
                    self.safe_log("Retweet nicht bestaetigt - uebersprungen", "RETWEETS", "WARNING")
                    return False
                self.human_pause(2.0, 4.0)
                self.retweets_posted += 1
                self._mark_activity("RETWEET")
                self.safe_log(f"RETWEET-INTELLIGENCE ERFOLGREICH! Keyword: {keyword} (Erfolgsrate verbessert)", "RETWEETS", "INFO")
                return True
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Fehler beim Retweet-Klick: {e}", "RETWEETS", "ERROR")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei Retweet: {e}", "RETWEETS", "ERROR")
            return False

    # filter_recent_tweets: geerbt von EngagementManager (engagement.py:397)
    # Die geerbte Version verwendet korrekt .timestamp() fuer timezone-sichere Vergleiche.

    def run_enhanced_session(self):
        """Fuehrt erweiterte hyperaktive Session aus."""
        agent_hub = None
        _log_touch_stop = None
        _log_touch_thread = None
        # F29 (S#56-Pre-P 2026-05-18): Diagnostic Exit-Trace.
        # H68 chronic: Bot stirbt nach 15-20min mit exit=1, finally-Block greift nicht
        # (SIGKILL/Segfault, vermutlich Browser-Crash nach smart-follow Timeout).
        # atexit-Hook + signal-Handler protokollieren beim NAECHSTEN Exit was passierte.
        import atexit
        import signal
        import traceback as _tb
        _exit_state = {"last_log_line": None, "phase": "init", "tweets_posted": 0, "elapsed_min": 0.0}
        self._exit_state = _exit_state

        def _bot_final_exit_trace(signum=None, frame=None):
            try:
                msg = (
                    f"[BOT_FINAL_EXIT] phase={_exit_state.get('phase')} "
                    f"tweets={_exit_state.get('tweets_posted')} "
                    f"elapsed_min={_exit_state.get('elapsed_min'):.1f} "
                    f"signum={signum} "
                    f"last_phase_ts={_exit_state.get('last_phase_ts')}"
                )
                self.safe_log(msg, "SESSION", "ERROR")
                if frame is not None:
                    stack = "".join(_tb.format_stack(frame))
                    self.safe_log(f"[BOT_FINAL_EXIT_STACK] {stack[:2000]}", "SESSION", "ERROR")
            except Exception:
                pass
            # H116 Pre-BA (2026-05-24): bot_health.json mit exited_at + pid=None markieren,
            # damit verwaiste PIDs nicht in der Diagnose lingern (siehe Pre-BA "fixe das"-Episode).
            try:
                import json as _json, os as _os
                from datetime import datetime as _dt2
                _repository_root = _os.path.abspath(_os.path.dirname(__file__))
                _hp = _os.path.join(_repository_root, "state", "bot_health.json")
                if _os.path.exists(_hp):
                    with open(_hp, "r", encoding="utf-8") as _f: _hd = _json.load(_f)
                    _hd["exited_at"] = _dt2.now().isoformat()
                    _hd["pid"] = None
                    with open(_hp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as _f: _json.dump(_hd, _f, indent=2)
            except Exception:
                pass

        atexit.register(_bot_final_exit_trace)
        try:
            signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _bot_final_exit_trace)
            if hasattr(signal, "SIGBREAK"):
                signal.signal(signal.SIGBREAK, _bot_final_exit_trace)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            self.session_start_time = datetime.now()
            self.metrics.start_session()
            self.last_action_time = datetime.now()
            self._mission_guard_last_reason = ""
            # Sofort Heartbeat schreiben damit BotStateHealer die neue Session nicht
            # als "frozen" erkennt (bot_health.json könnte alten Timestamp haben).
            if getattr(self, 'health_monitor', None):
                try:
                    self.health_monitor.write_heartbeat(loop_count=0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            try:
                self.execution_governor = self._build_execution_governor()
                _mission = ((getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {})
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[MISSION] Governor aktiv: objective={_mission.get('objective', 'balanced_growth')} "
                    f"primary={_mission.get('primary_action', 'tweet')} "
                    f"reply_floor={_mission.get('min_reply_share', 0.0)} "
                    f"tweet_floor={_mission.get('min_tweet_share', 0.0)} "
                    f"quality_first={self.execution_governor.mission_contract['quality_first']}",
                    "MISSION",
                    "INFO",
                )
            except Exception as _gov_err:
                self.execution_governor = None
                self.safe_log(f"[MISSION] Governor nicht verfuegbar: {_gov_err}", "MISSION", "WARNING")
            
            # TOURNAMENT POOL RESET
            self._tournament_pool = []
            self._tournament_winner = None
            self._candidate_budget_receipts = {}
            self._tournament_last_fill_ts = 0
            self._last_engagement_check_ts = 0  # Feedback Loop reset
            self._pending_tweet_checks = []
            self._pressure_boost_applied = False
            self._macro_event_hint = self._get_macro_event_hint()

            # OBSERVATION BANK STARTEN
            self._init_observation_bank()

            # QUERDENKER ALS THEMENGEBER (zuerst bei Session-Start)
            self._run_querdenker_director()

            # LERNSYSTEM STARTEN (optional deaktivierbar)
            if getattr(self.learning_system, "enabled", True):
                self.learning_system.start_learning_session()
                learning_insights = self.learning_system.get_learning_insights()
                self.safe_log(f"LEARNING AKTIV: Sessions={learning_insights['total_sessions']}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                if learning_insights.get('learning_active'):
                    self.safe_log(f"LERN-MODUS: Aktiv (Erfolgsrate: {learning_insights.get('recent_success_rate', 0):.1f}%)", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                    best_keywords = learning_insights.get('best_keywords') or []
                    if best_keywords:
                        self.safe_log(f"TOP-KEYWORDS: {', '.join(best_keywords)}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                    best_topics = learning_insights.get('best_topics') or []
                    if best_topics:
                        self.safe_log(f"TOP-THEMEN: {', '.join(best_topics)}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                    best_templates = learning_insights.get('best_templates') or []
                    if best_templates:
                        self.safe_log(f"TOP-TEMPLATES: {', '.join(best_templates)}", "LEARNING", "DEBUG")
                    cooldown = learning_insights.get('cooldown_keywords') or []
                    if cooldown:
                        self.safe_log(f"Keywords auf Cooldown: {', '.join(cooldown)}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                else:
                    self.safe_log("LERN-MODUS: Datensammlung (noch nicht genug Sessions)", "LEARNING", "INFO")
            else:
                self.safe_log("LEARNING: DEAKTIVIERT", "LEARNING", "INFO")

            # ADAPTIVE TONE WEIGHTS aus TweetLearner laden
            if getattr(self, 'tweet_learner', None):
                try:
                    from content.tweet_archetypes import set_adaptive_weights
                    adaptive_weights = self.tweet_learner.get_adaptive_tone_weights()
                    if adaptive_weights:
                        set_adaptive_weights(adaptive_weights)
                        top3 = sorted(adaptive_weights.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3]
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[LEARN] Adaptive Tone Weights geladen: Top3={top3}",
                            "LEARNING", "INFO",
                        )
                    else:
                        self.safe_log("[LEARN] Noch keine Tone-Daten — statische Weights aktiv", "LEARNING", "DEBUG")
                except Exception as _atw_err:
                    self.safe_log(f"[LEARN] Adaptive Weights Fehler: {_atw_err}", "LEARNING", "WARNING")

            # SOURCE PERFORMANCE in Content-Pipeline-Gewichtung einspeisen
            if getattr(self, 'tweet_learner', None):
                try:
                    source_perf = self.tweet_learner.get_source_performance()
                    if source_perf:
                        self._source_performance = source_perf
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[LEARN] Source Performance: {source_perf}",
                            "LEARNING", "INFO",
                        )
                    else:
                        self._source_performance = {}
                except Exception as _sp_err:
                    self._source_performance = {}
                    self.safe_log(f"[LEARN] Source Performance Fehler: {_sp_err}", "LEARNING", "WARNING")

            # Allow Dauerlauf script to override session duration
            env_hours = os.environ.get('BOT_SESSION_HOURS')
            if env_hours:
                try:
                    self.config['session_duration_hours'] = float(env_hours)
                    self.safe_log(f"SESSION-DAUER per ENV ueberschrieben: {env_hours}h", "SESSION", "INFO")
                except ValueError:
                    self.safe_log(f"BOT_SESSION_HOURS ungueltig: {env_hours}", "SESSION", "WARNING")
            
            session_duration = timedelta(hours=self.config['session_duration_hours'])
            session_end_time = self.session_start_time + session_duration
            
            self.safe_log("[EMOJI] STARTE ERWEITERTE HYPERAKTIVE SESSION!", "SESSION", "INFO")
            self.safe_log(
                f"BROWSER-VERBINDUNGSUeBERWACHUNG: {'AN' if self._flag('connection_monitoring', True) else 'AUS'}",
                "SESSION",
                "INFO",
            )
            self.safe_log(
                f"STALE-ELEMENT-RECOVERY: {'AN' if self._flag('stale_element_recovery', True) else 'AUS'}",
                "SESSION",
                "INFO",
            )
            self.safe_log(
                f"REPLY/POST-BUTTON-OPTIMIERUNG: {'AN' if self._flag('reply_button_optimization', True) else 'AUS'}",
                "SESSION",
                "INFO",
            )
            
            # [BRAIN] Intelligente Features aktivieren
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('engagement_scoring', False):
                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] ENGAGEMENT-SCORING AKTIVIERT!", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                self.safe_log("[UHR] ADAPTIVE TIMING AKTIVIERT!", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('contextual_comments', False):
                self.safe_log("[KOMMENTAR] KONTEXTUELLE KOMMENTARE AKTIVIERT!", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('performance_monitoring', False):
                self.safe_log("[CHART] PERFORMANCE-MONITORING AKTIVIERT!", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
            
            self.safe_log(f"[KALENDER] Session laeuft bis: {session_end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}", "SESSION", "INFO")
            
            # Aktivitaets-Zaehler und Ziele
            engagement_targets = self.config.get('session_limits', {
                'likes_per_session': 20,  # Human Activity: Moderat
                'comments_per_session': 10,  # Human Activity: Moderat
                'tweets_per_session': 20,  # Human Activity: Moderat
                'retweets_per_session': 10  # Human Activity: Moderat
            })
            likes_target = engagement_targets.get('likes_per_session', 20)  # Human Activity
            comments_target = engagement_targets.get('comments_per_session', 10)  # Human Activity
            tweets_target = engagement_targets.get('tweets_per_session', 20)  # Human Activity
            retweets_target = engagement_targets.get('retweets_per_session', 10)  # Human Activity

            if self._anti_detection_flag("session_variation", False):
                try:
                    def _vary(val: int) -> int:
                        return int(max(0, round(float(val) * random.uniform(0.9, 1.1))))
                    likes_target = _vary(int(likes_target))
                    comments_target = _vary(int(comments_target))
                    tweets_target = _vary(int(tweets_target))
                    retweets_target = _vary(int(retweets_target))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            try:
                likes_target, comments_target, tweets_target, retweets_target = self._apply_safe_session_limits(
                    int(likes_target), int(comments_target), int(tweets_target), int(retweets_target)
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

            # [GOAL] Dynamische Target-Anpassung basierend auf Wochenziel-Druck
            if getattr(self, 'goal_tracker', None):
                try:
                    _raw = {
                        "tweets_per_session": tweets_target,
                        "likes_per_session": likes_target,
                        "comments_per_session": comments_target,
                        "retweets_per_session": retweets_target,
                    }
                    _adj = self.goal_tracker.get_adjusted_session_targets(_raw)
                    tweets_target = max(1, int(_adj.get("tweets_per_session", tweets_target)))
                    likes_target = max(1, int(_adj.get("likes_per_session", likes_target)))
                    comments_target = max(1, int(_adj.get("comments_per_session", comments_target)))
                    # Safety-Clamp respektieren: wenn Clamp retweets auf 0 setzt, nicht überschreiben
                    _rt_clamped = retweets_target
                    retweets_target = (
                        max(1, int(_adj.get("retweets_per_session", retweets_target)))
                        if _rt_clamped > 0
                        else 0
                    )
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GOAL] Targets: {tweets_target}T/{likes_target}L/{comments_target}C/{retweets_target}RT",
                        "GOAL", "INFO",
                    )
                except Exception as _ge:
                    self.safe_log(f"[GOAL] Target-Anpassung fehlgeschlagen: {_ge}", "GOAL", "WARNING")

            if getattr(self, "session_plan", None):
                try:
                    from intelligent_features.session_planner import SessionPlanner

                    _planned = SessionPlanner.apply_target_multipliers(
                        {
                            "tweets_per_session": tweets_target,
                            "likes_per_session": likes_target,
                            "comments_per_session": comments_target,
                            "retweets_per_session": retweets_target,
                        },
                        self.session_plan,
                    )
                    tweets_target = int(_planned.get("tweets_per_session", tweets_target))
                    likes_target = int(_planned.get("likes_per_session", likes_target))
                    comments_target = int(_planned.get("comments_per_session", comments_target))
                    retweets_target = int(_planned.get("retweets_per_session", retweets_target))
                    likes_target, comments_target, tweets_target, retweets_target = self._apply_safe_session_limits(
                        int(likes_target), int(comments_target), int(tweets_target), int(retweets_target)
                    )
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[PLAN] Live-Targets: {tweets_target}T/{likes_target}L/{comments_target}C/{retweets_target}RT"
                        f" | focus={self.session_plan.get('focus', '?')}"
                        f" | planning=L{self.session_plan.get('planning_level', '?')}"
                        f" | agentic={self.session_plan.get('agentic_score_10', '?')}/10",
                        "PLANNER", "INFO",
                    )
                except Exception as _pe:
                    self.safe_log(f"[PLAN] Live-Targeting fehlgeschlagen: {_pe}", "PLANNER", "WARNING")

            # Goal pressure may raise the reply target above the configured,
            # human-paced session budget. Keep the explicit session limit binding.
            comments_target = cap_reply_target_to_config(self.config, comments_target)

            self.session_targets = {
                "tweets_per_session": int(tweets_target),
                "likes_per_session": int(likes_target),
                "comments_per_session": int(comments_target),
                "retweets_per_session": int(retweets_target),
            }

            self.safe_log(f"ERWEITERTE ZIELE: {tweets_target} Tweets, {likes_target} Likes, {comments_target} Kommentare, {retweets_target} Retweets", "SESSION", "INFO")

            # [DISABLED-FILTER] Aktionen die auf 0 geclampt wurden → Brain überspringen
            _disabled_actions: set = set()
            if retweets_target == 0:
                _disabled_actions.add("retweet")
            if likes_target == 0:
                _disabled_actions.add("like")
            if comments_target == 0:
                _disabled_actions.add("reply")
            if tweets_target == 0:
                _disabled_actions.add("tweet")
            if _disabled_actions:
                self.safe_log(
                    f"[BRAIN] Disabled-Actions (Target=0): {_disabled_actions}",
                    "BRAIN", "INFO",
                )
            
            last_tweet_time = datetime.now()
            last_comment_time = datetime.now()
            last_like_burst = datetime.now()
            last_retweet_time = datetime.now()
            last_metrics_report = datetime.now()
            last_status_log = datetime.now()
            last_rephrase_time = datetime.now()
            last_break_time = datetime.now()
            _last_log_touch = datetime.now()  # Windows mtime keepalive
            # Background thread: update bot.log mtime every 30s regardless of main-loop blocking
            _log_touch_stop = threading.Event()
            def _log_touch_worker():
                _log_path = os.path.join("logs", "bot.log")
                while not _log_touch_stop.wait(30):
                    try:
                        os.utime(_log_path, None)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            _log_touch_thread = threading.Thread(target=_log_touch_worker, daemon=True, name="log-touch")
            _log_touch_thread.start()
            break_cfg = self.config.get("break_settings", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            break_enabled = bool(break_cfg.get("enabled", True))
            try:
                min_break_interval = float(break_cfg.get("min_interval_minutes", 45))
                max_break_interval = float(break_cfg.get("max_interval_minutes", 90))
            except Exception:
                min_break_interval, max_break_interval = 45.0, 90.0
            min_break_interval = max(5.0, min_break_interval)
            max_break_interval = max(min_break_interval, max_break_interval)

            try:
                ad = self.config.get("anti_detection_features", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                bi = ad.get("break_intervals") if isinstance(ad, dict) else None
                if isinstance(bi, list) and len(bi) >= 2 and all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in bi[:2]):
                    min_break_interval = max(5.0, float(bi[0]))
                    max_break_interval = max(min_break_interval, float(bi[1]))
            except Exception:
                pass

            next_break_minutes = random.uniform(min_break_interval, max_break_interval)
            rephrase_enabled = getattr(self, "rephrase_enabled", False)
            rephrase_target = getattr(self, "rephrase_target", 0)
            rephrase_interval = getattr(self, "rephrase_interval_min", 20)
            if rephrase_enabled and rephrase_target > 0:
                self.safe_log(f"REPHRASE aktiv: Ziel {rephrase_target} Posts, Intervall ~{rephrase_interval}min, Schwellen {self.config.get('success_filter', {})}", "REPHRASE", "INFO")
            else:
                self.safe_log("REPHRASE deaktiviert oder Ziel=0", "REPHRASE", "DEBUG")

            # Runtime-Agenten (optional): AgentHub fuer Governance-Events
            runtime_agents_cfg = self.config.get("runtime_agents", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            if not isinstance(runtime_agents_cfg, dict):
                runtime_agents_cfg = {}
            hub_enabled = bool(runtime_agents_cfg.get("enable_agent_hub", False))
            hub_poll_interval = int(runtime_agents_cfg.get("agent_hub_poll_interval_seconds", 60) or 60)
            hub_poll_interval = max(5, hub_poll_interval)
            single_action_per_tick = bool(runtime_agents_cfg.get("single_action_per_tick", True))

            # Graph-Advisor: read-only im Shadow-Modus, evidenzgebunden im Enforce-Modus.
            graph_advisor = None
            _graph_runtime_config = None
            _graph_enforce_fail_closed = False
            _graph_advisor_kwargs = {}
            self._clear_graph_content_directive()
            try:
                from intelligent_features import runtime_config as _runtime_config

                _graph_runtime_config = _runtime_config
                # Solange eine vorhandene Config nicht strikt validiert ist,
                # darf ein korruptes ehemaliges ENFORCE nicht zu Shadow werden.
                _graph_enforce_fail_closed = True
                _graph_config = _runtime_config.get_graph_governance()
                _graph_enabled = _graph_config.enabled
                _graph_mode_raw = str(_graph_config.mode).strip().lower()
                if _graph_mode_raw not in {"off", "shadow", "enforce"}:
                    raise ValueError("invalid graph governance mode")
                _graph_enforce_fail_closed = (
                    _graph_enabled and _graph_mode_raw == "enforce"
                )
                from intelligent_features.graph_engineering import GraphAdvisor, GraphMode

                _graph_mode = GraphMode(
                    _graph_mode_raw
                )
                _graph_enforce_fail_closed = (
                    _graph_enabled and _graph_mode == GraphMode.ENFORCE
                )
                _graph_advisor_kwargs = {
                    "graph_path": os.path.join(
                        os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
                        "state",
                        "graph_engineering",
                        "graph.json",
                    ),
                    "refresh_interval_seconds": _graph_config.refresh_interval_seconds,
                    "emit_interval_seconds": _graph_config.emit_interval_seconds,
                    "max_runtime_signal_age_seconds": (
                        _graph_config.max_runtime_signal_age_seconds
                    ),
                }
                if _graph_enabled and _graph_mode != GraphMode.OFF:
                    graph_advisor = GraphAdvisor(mode=_graph_mode, **_graph_advisor_kwargs)
                    self._refresh_graph_content_directive(graph_advisor)
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GRAPH] Action-Advisor aktiv (mode={_graph_mode.value})",
                        "GRAPH",
                        "INFO",
                    )
            except Exception as _graph_init_error:
                graph_advisor = None
                self.safe_event(
                    "GRAPH",
                    "graph_advisor_init_failed",
                    {
                        "error_type": type(_graph_init_error).__name__,
                        "effective_behavior": (
                            "BLOCK_ALL_EXTERNAL_ACTIONS"
                            if _graph_enforce_fail_closed
                            else "UNCHANGED"
                        ),
                    },
                    "ERROR" if _graph_enforce_fail_closed else "WARNING",
                )

            if hub_enabled:
                try:
                    from processes.agent_hub import AgentHub
                    agent_hub = AgentHub(poll_interval=hub_poll_interval)
                    agent_hub.start()
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[AGENT_HUB] gestartet (poll={hub_poll_interval}s)",
                        "AGENTS",
                        "INFO",
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    agent_hub = None
                    self.safe_log(f"[AGENT_HUB] Start fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "AGENTS", "WARNING")

            # Kickoff (optional): default OFF (prevents "all actions immediately", which is very bot-like)
            try:
                safety_cfg = self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                if not isinstance(safety_cfg, dict):
                    safety_cfg = {}
                kickoff_enabled = bool(safety_cfg.get("kickoff_immediate_actions", False))
            except Exception:
                kickoff_enabled = False
            if kickoff_enabled:
                try:
                    kickoff_minutes = float(safety_cfg.get("kickoff_minutes", 5.0))
                except Exception:
                    kickoff_minutes = 5.0
                kickoff_minutes = max(0.0, min(kickoff_minutes, 30.0))
                (
                    last_like_burst,
                    last_comment_time,
                    last_retweet_time,
                    last_tweet_time,
                ) = self._apply_kickoff_timers(
                    (last_like_burst, last_comment_time, last_retweet_time, last_tweet_time),
                    kickoff_minutes,
                )
                if not self._quality_first_enabled():
                    self.safe_log(f"KICKOFF aktiv: Timer um {kickoff_minutes:.0f}min vorgezogen", "SESSION", "INFO")

            heartbeat_interval = 300  # seconds
            stuck_threshold = 900      # seconds

            # [STUCK] StuckDetector initialisieren (in-memory, lebt nur diese Session)
            stuck_detector = None
            try:
                from learning.stuck_detector import StuckDetector
                stuck_detector = StuckDetector()
                self.safe_log("[STUCK] StuckDetector aktiv", "SESSION", "INFO")
            except Exception as _se:
                self.safe_log(f"[STUCK] StuckDetector nicht verfügbar: {_se}", "SESSION", "DEBUG")

            self.safe_log("Main loop gestartet", "SESSION", "INFO")

            while datetime.now() < session_end_time:
                current_time = datetime.now()
                remaining_time = session_end_time - current_time
                mission_directive = None

                # F84 (2026-05-28): Nacht-Pause — ausserhalb menschlicher Aktivzeiten NICHT
                # posten. Idle statt Loop, damit der Governor nicht abbricht (kein Restart-Storm).
                if not self._in_active_hours(current_time):
                    if not getattr(self, "_night_mode_logged", False):
                        self.safe_log("[HUMAN] Ausserhalb Aktivzeiten — Nacht-Pause (kein Posting).", "SESSION", "INFO")
                        self._night_mode_logged = True
                    if self.execution_governor:
                        try:
                            self.execution_governor.last_meaningful_activity_at = current_time
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    self._mark_activity("NIGHT_IDLE")
                    self._record_night_idle()
                    time.sleep(300)  # 5-min Idle-Chunk, dann Aktivzeiten erneut pruefen
                    continue
                else:
                    self._night_mode_logged = False

                if not self.ensure_browser_connection():
                    self.safe_log("Browser-Verbindung konnte nicht wiederhergestellt werden - beende Session", "SESSION", "ERROR")
                    break

                # Session-Expiry: X-Login abgelaufen → sauber abbrechen + Alert
                if self.session_expired:
                    self.safe_log(
                        "X-SESSION ABGELAUFEN - Login-Page erkannt. "
                        "Session wird sauber beendet (kein endloses Retrying).",
                        "SESSION", "ERROR",
                    )
                    try:
                        from core.crash_alert import send_crash_alert
                        send_crash_alert(
                            subject="X-Session abgelaufen - Login erforderlich",
                            error="verify_twitter_page hat Login-Redirect erkannt",
                            context="Manuelle Aktion noetig: Chrome oeffnen und bei X einloggen",
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    break

                if self.execution_governor:
                    try:
                        mission_directive = self.execution_governor.assess(
                            current_time,
                            last_activity_at=getattr(self, "last_action_time", None),
                        )
                        _mission_reason = str(mission_directive.get("reason", "") or "")
                        _severity = str(mission_directive.get("severity", "ok") or "ok")
                        if _severity != "ok" and _mission_reason and _mission_reason != self._mission_guard_last_reason:
                            self._mission_guard_last_reason = _mission_reason
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[MISSION] {_severity.upper()}: {_mission_reason} | "
                                f"{mission_directive.get('snapshot', {})}",
                                "MISSION",
                                "WARNING" if _severity != "critical" else "ERROR",
                            )
                        if mission_directive.get("should_abort"):
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[MISSION] Session-Abbruch: {_mission_reason}",
                                "MISSION",
                                "ERROR",
                            )
                            break
                    except Exception as _mission_err:
                        self.safe_log(f"[MISSION] Assess fehlgeschlagen: {_mission_err}", "MISSION", "WARNING")
                        mission_directive = None

                # [BRAIN] Auto-Strategy-Revision alle 20 Iterationen
                _loop_ctr = getattr(self, '_brain_loop_counter', 0) + 1
                self._brain_loop_counter = _loop_ctr

                # [HEALTH] Heartbeat alle 10 Iterationen → Healer-Daemon kann frozen Bot erkennen
                if _loop_ctr % 10 == 0 and getattr(self, 'health_monitor', None):
                    try:
                        self.health_monitor.write_heartbeat(loop_count=_loop_ctr)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # [DARWIN] Strategie-Gewichte alle 50 Iterationen loggen
                if _loop_ctr % 50 == 0:
                    try:
                        _darwin = self._get_darwin()
                        _stats = _darwin.get_stats()
                        _w = {s: f"{v:.0%}" for s, v in _stats.get("weights", {}).items()}
                        _wr = {s: f"{v:.0%}" if v is not None else "?" for s, v in _stats.get("win_rates", {}).items()}
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[DARWIN] Gewichte: {_w} | Win-Rates: {_wr}",
                            "DARWIN", "INFO",
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                if self.session_brain and _loop_ctr % 20 == 0:
                    try:
                        _revision = self.session_brain.auto_strategy_revision()
                        if _revision:
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[BRAIN] Strategy-Revision: {_revision}", "BRAIN", "WARNING"
                            )
                            _now_r = datetime.now()
                            if _revision == "reduce_all_20min":
                                # Timer auf jetzt setzen → Bot wartet vollen Intervall (effektive Pause)
                                last_like_burst = _now_r
                                last_comment_time = _now_r
                                last_tweet_time = _now_r
                                last_retweet_time = _now_r
                                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Alle Timer resettet — effektive 20min Pause", "BRAIN", "WARNING")
                            elif _revision == "topic_switch_forced":
                                self.session_brain._strategy["topic_switch_needed"] = True
                                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Topic-Switch direkt erzwungen", "BRAIN", "WARNING")
                            elif _revision == "force_action":
                                _old_ts = self._forced_action_timestamp(_now_r)
                                if _old_ts is not None:
