                                    last_like_burst = _old_ts
                                    last_comment_time = _old_ts
                                    last_tweet_time = _old_ts
                                    last_retweet_time = _old_ts
                                    self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Alle Timer zurückgesetzt — sofortige Aktion", "BRAIN", "WARNING")
                                else:
                                    self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Force-Action unter Quality-First übersprungen", "BRAIN", "INFO")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # [REBALANCE] Mid-Session Goal-Rebalancing alle 10 Iterationen
                if self.session_brain and _loop_ctr % 10 == 0:
                    try:
                        def _safe_ratio(posted, target):
                            return float(posted) / float(target) if target else 1.0
                        _rb = self.session_brain.get_session_rebalance_hint(
                            tweets_progress=_safe_ratio(self.tweets_posted, tweets_target),
                            likes_progress=_safe_ratio(self.likes_given, likes_target),
                            replies_progress=_safe_ratio(self.comments_posted, comments_target),
                            retweets_progress=_safe_ratio(self.retweets_posted, retweets_target) if retweets_target else 1.0,
                        )
                        if _rb:
                            _boost = _rb.get("boost")
                            _reduce = _rb.get("reduce")
                            self.safe_log(
                                (
                                    f"[BRAIN] Rebalance: {_boost} suggestion suppressed by Quality-First, {_reduce} reduced"
                                    if self._quality_first_enabled()
                                    else f"[BRAIN] Rebalance: {_boost} boosted, {_reduce} reduced"
                                ),
                                "BRAIN", "INFO",
                            )
                            # Boost: Timer für gebooste Aktion freischalten
                            (
                                last_like_burst,
                                last_comment_time,
                                last_retweet_time,
                                last_tweet_time,
                            ) = self._apply_rebalance_boost(
                                _boost,
                                datetime.now(),
                                (last_like_burst, last_comment_time, last_retweet_time, last_tweet_time),
                            )
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # [STUCK] Stuck-Diagnose: Taktikwechsel wenn Bot feststeckt
                if stuck_detector and stuck_detector.is_stuck():
                    self._handle_stuck(stuck_detector)

                # [BRAIN] SessionBrain konsultieren (Phase 2: steuert Aktionsreihenfolge)
                brain_suggestion = None
                brain_binding = False  # True = Brain-Entscheidung ist bindend (kein Fallback)
                brain_binding_requested = False
                if self.session_brain:
                    try:
                        brain_suggestion = self.session_brain.suggest_next_action(disabled_actions=_disabled_actions)
                        strategy_info = self.session_brain.get_strategy_summary()
                        agentic = self.session_brain.get_agentic_state()
                        confidence = float(agentic.get("confidence", 0.0))
                        brain_binding_requested = (
                            bool(agentic.get("enabled", False)) and confidence > 0.6
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        brain_suggestion = None
                        brain_binding = False

                # [DISABLED-FILTER] Brain-Ergebnis überschreiben wenn Aktion deaktiviert (Target=0)
                if brain_suggestion and brain_suggestion in _disabled_actions:
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[BRAIN] Disabled-Action-Filter: '{brain_suggestion}' hat Target=0 → überspringe",
                        "BRAIN",
                        "DEBUG",
                    )
                    brain_suggestion = None  # None → normale action_order, kein idle-skip
                    brain_binding = False

                # Scheduled break to reduce bot-like patterns (konfigurierbar + progress-aware)
                if break_enabled and not (mission_directive or {}).get("suppress_breaks", False) and (current_time - last_break_time).total_seconds() >= next_break_minutes * 60:
                    try:
                        min_break_sec = float(break_cfg.get("min_seconds", 300))
                        max_break_sec = float(break_cfg.get("max_seconds", 900))
                    except Exception:
                        min_break_sec, max_break_sec = 300.0, 900.0
                    min_break_sec = max(30.0, min_break_sec)
                    max_break_sec = max(min_break_sec, max_break_sec)
                    cool = random.uniform(min_break_sec, max_break_sec)

                    # Wenn der Bot hinter den Zielen liegt, Pause kuerzen
                    try:
                        elapsed = (current_time - self.session_start_time).total_seconds()
                        total = session_duration.total_seconds()
                        expected_fraction = min(1.0, max(0.0, elapsed / total)) if total > 0 else 0.0
                        def _safe_frac(x, y):
                            return float(x) / float(y) if y else 0.0
                        progress_fraction = (
                            _safe_frac(self.tweets_posted, tweets_target)
                            + _safe_frac(self.likes_given, likes_target)
                            + _safe_frac(self.comments_posted, comments_target)
                            + _safe_frac(self.retweets_posted, retweets_target)
                        ) / 4.0
                        if progress_fraction + 0.15 < expected_fraction:
                            cool = min(cool, min_break_sec)
                            self.safe_log("Pause verkuerzt (Ziele im Rueckstand)", "SESSION", "INFO")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                    self.safe_log(f"Geplante Pause fuer {cool:.0f}s (Anti-Pattern) - idle-scrolling", "SESSION", "INFO")
                    self._idle_scroll(cool)
                    last_break_time = datetime.now()
                    next_break_minutes = random.uniform(min_break_interval, max_break_interval)

                # Force bot.log mtime every 60s — Windows mtime caching workaround for stall detector
                if (current_time - _last_log_touch).total_seconds() >= 60:
                    try:
                        os.utime(os.path.join("logs", "bot.log"), None)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    _last_log_touch = current_time

                if (current_time - last_status_log).total_seconds() >= heartbeat_interval:

                    self.safe_log(f"??x8f? Verbleibende Zeit: {str(remaining_time).split('.')[0]}", "SESSION", "DEBUG")
                    self.safe_log(f"????  Fortschritt: {self.tweets_posted}/{tweets_target} Tweets, {self.likes_given}/{likes_target} Likes, {self.comments_posted}/{comments_target} Kommentare, {self.retweets_posted}/{retweets_target} Retweets", "SESSION", "INFO")
                    t_pool = len(self._tournament_pool)
                    t_winner = "JA" if self._tournament_winner else "NEIN"
                    self.safe_log(f"[TOURNAMENT] Pool={t_pool}, Gewinner bereit={t_winner}", "TOURNAMENT", "INFO")
                    inactivity = (current_time - getattr(self, "last_action_time", self.session_start_time)).total_seconds()
                    if inactivity >= stuck_threshold:
                        _recovery_times = self._recover_stuck_session()
                        if _recovery_times:
                            (
                                last_like_burst,
                                last_comment_time,
                                last_retweet_time,
                                last_tweet_time,
                            ) = _recovery_times
                    last_status_log = current_time

                # [BRAIN] Berechne intelligenten Engagement-Score
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('engagement_scoring', False):
                    engagement_score = self.intelligence.calculate_engagement_score(
                        self.likes_given, self.comments_posted, self.retweets_posted, self.tweets_posted
                    )
                    self.engagement_score = engagement_score
                    self.safe_log(f"[BRAIN] Engagement-Score: {engagement_score}", "INTELLIGENCE", "DEBUG")

                # [CHART] Performance-Metriken alle 30 Minuten
                if (current_time - last_metrics_report).total_seconds() >= 1800:  # 30 Minuten
                    if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('performance_monitoring', False):
                        metrics_summary = self.metrics.get_summary()
                        self.safe_log(f"[CHART] Performance-Metriken: {metrics_summary}", "METRICS", "INFO")
                    # [PRESSURE-BOOST] Follower-Druck → Session-Targets auto-anpassen
                    try:
                        self._apply_pressure_session_boost()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    last_metrics_report = current_time
                
                # Fortschritt wird nur im Heartbeat geloggt, um Log-Overhead zu reduzieren.

                # [FEEDBACK] Engagement-Check alle ~2h
                _now_ts = time.time()
                if _now_ts - self._last_engagement_check_ts >= 3600:  # 1 Stunde
                    try:
                        self._check_own_engagement()
                        self._last_engagement_check_ts = _now_ts
                        # Aktuelle Gewichte loggen
                        _w = self._get_adaptive_source_weights()
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[FEEDBACK] Weights: scrape={_w.get('scrape_rewrite', 0.0):.0%}, "
                            f"obs={_w.get('observation_tension', 0.0):.0%}, "
                            f"llm={_w.get('llm_original', 0.0):.0%}",
                            "FEEDBACK", "INFO",
                        )
                    except Exception as _fb_err:
                        self.safe_log(f"[FEEDBACK] Check fehlgeschlagen: {_fb_err}", "FEEDBACK", "WARNING")

                # [FEEDBACK] Per-Tweet 30min Quick-Check (Closed-Loop innerhalb Session)
                _due_checks = [
                    e for e in self._pending_tweet_checks
                    if not e.get("checked") and 1500 <= _now_ts - e.get("ts", 0) <= 2400
                ]
                for _qc_entry in _due_checks[:2]:  # max 2 pro Loop-Durchlauf
                    try:
                        self._run_quick_tweet_check(_qc_entry)
                    except Exception as _qc_err:
                        self.safe_log(f"[QUICK-CHECK] Fehler: {_qc_err}", "FEEDBACK", "DEBUG")
                    finally:
                        _qc_entry["checked"] = True
                # Alte Einträge aufräumen (älter als 3h)
                self._pending_tweet_checks = [
                    e for e in self._pending_tweet_checks if _now_ts - e.get("ts", 0) < 10800
                ]

                # ============================================================
                # [BRAIN] Phase 2: Brain-gesteuerte Aktionsreihenfolge
                # Brain empfiehlt welche Aktion Prioritaet hat. Timer-Guards
                # bleiben als Sicherheitsnetz aktiv. Wenn Brain "idle"/"skip"
                # empfiehlt, werden Aktionen uebersprungen.
                # ============================================================
                if should_honor_idle_suggestion(brain_suggestion, mission_directive):
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[BRAIN] Aktion ausgesetzt ({brain_suggestion}) - nur idle-scroll",
                        "BRAIN",
                        "DEBUG",
                    )
                    self._idle_scroll(random.uniform(10, 30))
                    # Idle pausiert nur Engagement-Aktionen. Tournament und
                    # Force-Post bleiben bedienbar, damit freigegebene
                    # Eigenpost-Kandidaten nicht im Pool verhungern.
                    try:
                        # Nach dem Scroll sind die alten Timerwerte nicht mehr
                        # belastbar (Agentic-Generierung kann bis zu 60s dauern).
                        _idle_now = datetime.now()
                        _idle_remaining = session_end_time - _idle_now
                        self._service_tournament_queue(_idle_now, _idle_remaining)
                        if datetime.now() < session_end_time:
                            self._force_post_if_overdue()
                    except Exception as _idle_tournament_error:
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[TOURNAMENT] Idle-Service fehlgeschlagen: {_idle_tournament_error}",
                            "TOURNAMENT", "WARNING",
                        )
                    continue
                elif brain_suggestion in ("idle", "skip"):
                    self.safe_log(
                        "[MISSION] Idle-Vorschlag uebersprungen: Governor erzwingt eine Recovery-Aktion",
                        "MISSION",
                        "INFO",
                    )

                # Berechne Intervalle fuer alle Aktionen (wird von allen Pfaden benoetigt)
                base_like_interval = self._base_interval_minutes('like_burst_minutes', 15)
                if self.session_brain:
                    try:
                        base_like_interval = self.session_brain.get_adjusted_interval("like", base_like_interval)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                like_interval = self.get_variable_interval(base_like_interval, 40)
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                    like_interval = self.intelligence.get_adaptive_timing('like_burst', like_interval)

                base_comment_interval = self._base_interval_minutes('comment_interval_minutes', 20)
                if self.session_brain:
                    try:
                        base_comment_interval = self.session_brain.get_adjusted_interval("reply", base_comment_interval)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                comment_interval = self.get_variable_interval(base_comment_interval, 50)
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                    comment_interval = self.intelligence.get_adaptive_timing('comment', comment_interval)

                base_tweet_interval = self._base_interval_minutes('tweet_interval_minutes', 30)
                if self.session_brain:
                    try:
                        base_tweet_interval = self.session_brain.get_adjusted_interval("tweet", base_tweet_interval)
                        timing_mult = self.session_brain.get_timing_multiplier()
                        if timing_mult != 1.0:
                            base_tweet_interval = round(base_tweet_interval * timing_mult, 1)
                            self.safe_log(
                                f"[BRAIN] Tweet-Timing ×{timing_mult:.2f} → {base_tweet_interval:.1f}min",
                                "BRAIN", "DEBUG",
                            )
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                tweet_interval = self.get_variable_interval(base_tweet_interval, 60)
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                    tweet_interval = self.intelligence.get_adaptive_timing('tweet', tweet_interval)

                base_retweet_interval = self._base_interval_minutes('retweet_interval_minutes', 36)
                if self.session_brain:
                    try:
                        base_retweet_interval = self.session_brain.get_adjusted_interval("retweet", base_retweet_interval)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                retweet_interval = self.get_variable_interval(base_retweet_interval, 45)
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('adaptive_timing', False):
                    retweet_interval = self.intelligence.get_adaptive_timing('retweet', retweet_interval)

                # Timer-Bereitschaft pruefen
                like_timer_ready = (current_time - last_like_burst).total_seconds() >= like_interval * 60
                comment_timer_ready = (current_time - last_comment_time).total_seconds() >= comment_interval * 60
                tweet_timer_ready = (current_time - last_tweet_time).total_seconds() >= tweet_interval * 60
                retweet_timer_ready = (current_time - last_retweet_time).total_seconds() >= retweet_interval * 60

                # Only currently sendable recommendations are binding evidence.
                # Compute this after the real timers, without forcing readiness.
                if self.session_brain and brain_suggestion:
                    _brain_timer_ready = {
                        "like": like_timer_ready, "reply": comment_timer_ready,
                        "quote": comment_timer_ready, "tweet": tweet_timer_ready,
                        "retweet": retweet_timer_ready,
                    }.get(brain_suggestion, False)
                    _brain_guard_action = "comment" if brain_suggestion in ("reply", "quote") else brain_suggestion
                    try:
                        brain_binding = bool(
                            brain_binding_requested and _brain_timer_ready
                            and self._action_allowed(_brain_guard_action)
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        brain_binding = False
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[BRAIN] {'BINDEND' if brain_binding else 'Empfehlung'}: {brain_suggestion}"
                        f" | conf={confidence:.2f} | Strategy: {strategy_info}",
                        "BRAIN", "INFO",
                    )
                    if brain_binding and stuck_detector:
                        try:
                            if stuck_detector.record_bindend(brain_suggestion):
                                brain_binding = False
                                self.safe_log(
                                    f"[BRAIN] BINDEND-Loop erkannt ({brain_suggestion}) → "
                                    "deaktiviere BINDEND für diese Iteration",
                                    "BRAIN", "WARNING",
                                )
                                _bp = self.session_brain._strategy.get("bindend_penalty", 0.0)
                                self.session_brain._strategy["bindend_penalty"] = min(1.0, _bp + 0.2)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

                try:
                    news_cfg = self.config.get("news_commentary", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                    if (
                        isinstance(news_cfg, dict)
                        and bool(news_cfg.get("enabled", True))
                        and bool(news_cfg.get("kickoff_first_tweet", True))
                        and int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0) <= 0
                        and self._forced_readiness_allowed()
                    ):
                        tweet_timer_ready = True
                except Exception:
                    pass

                # [GARANTIE] Minimum-Tweet-Sicherung: wenn Session fast vorbei und zu wenig Tweets
                # → Tweet-Timer als bereit markieren, egal wie lange er noch laufen würde
                try:
                    _safety_limits = (self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) or {})
                    _min_tweet_guarantee = self._configured_guarantee(
                        _safety_limits, "min_tweets_guarantee", 5
                    )
                    _elapsed_fraction = (
                        (current_time - self.session_start_time).total_seconds()
                        / max(1.0, session_duration.total_seconds())
                    )
                    _tweets_behind = self.tweets_posted < _min_tweet_guarantee
                    # Greift ab 40% der Session wenn noch hinter Minimum
                    if (
                        self._forced_readiness_allowed()
                        and _tweets_behind
                        and _elapsed_fraction >= 0.40
                        and not tweet_timer_ready
                    ):
                        tweet_timer_ready = True
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[GARANTIE] Tweet-Timer erzwungen: {self.tweets_posted}/{_min_tweet_guarantee} "
                            f"Mindest-Tweets, {_elapsed_fraction:.0%} der Session vergangen",
                            "SESSION", "WARNING",
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

                # [GARANTIE] Output-Pace: Likes und Replies duerfen nicht bis zum
                # Session-Ende verhungern, wenn Brain/Timer zu passiv werden.
                # F88: Wenn GARANTIE eine Aktion erzwingt, MUSS sie die Stunden-
                # Suppression uebersteuern. Sonst hebelt should_suppress() den
                # Output-Floor aus (0/71 Likes trotz GARANTIE — F87-Falle).
                _like_guaranteed = False
                _reply_guaranteed = False
                try:
                    _safety_limits = (self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) or {})
                    _min_like_guarantee = min(
                        likes_target,
                        self._configured_guarantee(_safety_limits, "min_likes_guarantee", 12),
                    )
                    _min_reply_guarantee = min(
                        comments_target,
                        self._configured_guarantee(_safety_limits, "min_replies_guarantee", 4),
                    )
                    _expected_fraction = max(0.0, _elapsed_fraction - 0.20)
                    _like_floor = int(_min_like_guarantee * _expected_fraction)
                    _reply_floor = int(_min_reply_guarantee * _expected_fraction)

                    if (
                        _min_like_guarantee > 0
                        and self.likes_given < _min_like_guarantee
                        and (_elapsed_fraction >= 0.55 or self.likes_given < _like_floor)
                        and not like_timer_ready
                    ):
                        like_timer_ready = True
                        _like_guaranteed = True
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[GARANTIE] Like-Timer erzwungen: {self.likes_given}/{_min_like_guarantee} "
                            f"Mindest-Likes, {_elapsed_fraction:.0%} der Session vergangen",
                            "SESSION", "WARNING",
                        )

                    if (
                        self._forced_readiness_allowed()
                        and _min_reply_guarantee > 0
                        and self.comments_posted < _min_reply_guarantee
                        and (_elapsed_fraction >= 0.55 or self.comments_posted < _reply_floor)
                        and not comment_timer_ready
                    ):
                        comment_timer_ready = True
                        _reply_guaranteed = True
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[GARANTIE] Reply-Timer erzwungen: {self.comments_posted}/{_min_reply_guarantee} "
                            f"Mindest-Replies, {_elapsed_fraction:.0%} der Session vergangen",
                            "SESSION", "WARNING",
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

                _reply_first_lock = False
                try:
                    _mission_snapshot = (mission_directive or {}).get("snapshot", {}) or {}
                    _mission_contract = (getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {}
                    _min_reply_share = float(_mission_contract.get("min_reply_share", 0.0) or 0.0)
                    _reply_share_now = float(_mission_snapshot.get("reply_share", 0.0) or 0.0)
                    _min_reply_guarantee_cfg = self._configured_guarantee(
                        (self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) or {}),
                        "min_replies_guarantee",
                        0,
                    )
                    _reply_first_lock = (
                        self._forced_readiness_allowed()
                        and
                        str(_mission_contract.get("objective", "")) == "follower_growth"
                        and self.comments_posted < comments_target
                        and comment_timer_ready
                        and (
                            _reply_share_now + 0.08 < _min_reply_share
                            or self.comments_posted < min(comments_target, _min_reply_guarantee_cfg)
                        )
                    )
                    if _reply_first_lock and brain_suggestion == "like":
                        brain_binding = False
                        brain_suggestion = "reply"
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[REPLY] Reply-First-Lock: Like-BINDEND uebersteuert, Reply hat Rueckstand",
                            "SESSION",
                            "INFO",
                        )
                except Exception:
                    _reply_first_lock = False

                # Brain-gesteuerte Aktionsreihenfolge:
                # - Brain empfiehlt die wichtigste Aktion (groesster Rueckstand)
                # - Diese wird ZUERST versucht
                # - Danach werden die restlichen Aktionen in normaler Reihenfolge geprueft
                # - "idle"/"skip" â†’ nur idle-scroll, keine Aktionen
                _mission_contract_for_order = (
                    (getattr(self, "session_plan", {}) or {}).get("mission_contract", {}) or {}
                )
                action_order = action_order_for_objective(
                    _mission_contract_for_order.get("objective", QUALIFIED_VISIBILITY_OBJECTIVE)
                )

                if brain_suggestion in ("like", "reply", "quote", "tweet", "retweet"):
                    mapped = "reply" if brain_suggestion == "quote" else brain_suggestion
                    if brain_binding and mapped in action_order:
                        # BINDEND: Nur die Brain-Aktion ausführen — aber nur wenn ihr Timer bereit ist.
                        # Wenn der Timer NICHT bereit ist, Advisory-Modus: Aktion an Anfang, kein Ausschluss
                        # (verhindert Deadlock: alle anderen Aktionen bleiben erreichbar)
                        _bound_timer_ready = {
                            "like": like_timer_ready,
                            "reply": comment_timer_ready,
                            "tweet": tweet_timer_ready,
                            "retweet": retweet_timer_ready,
                        }.get(mapped, True)
                        # A ready timer must not bind the whole tick to an
                        # exhausted tweet budget; keep normal alternatives reachable.
                        if _bound_timer_ready and (
                            mapped != "tweet" or self._action_allowed("tweet")
                        ):
                            action_order = [mapped]
                        else:
                            # Timer nicht bereit → Advisory-Modus statt Deadlock
                            action_order.remove(mapped)
                            action_order.insert(0, mapped)
                        # S#54 F13: Tournament-Winner-Override am Session-Ende
                        # H33: Brain bindet oft auf reply am Session-Ende, blockiert dann die
                        # Tournament-Konsumtion. Wenn Pool-Winner bereit + Session-Rest <5min:
                        # 'tweet' ergaenzen damit der Winner nicht ungenutzt verfaellt.
                        try:
                            _rem_min = remaining_time.total_seconds() / 60.0
                            if (mapped != "tweet"
                                    and self._tournament_winner is not None
                                    and _rem_min < 5.0
                                    and tweet_timer_ready
                                    and "tweet" not in action_order):
                                action_order.append("tweet")
                                self.safe_log(
                                    f"[BRAIN] BINDEND={mapped} aber Tournament-Winner ready "
                                    f"+ rem={_rem_min:.1f}m -> tweet ergaenzt (F13 Endgame-Override)",
                                    "BRAIN", "INFO",
                                )
                        except Exception as _f13e:
                            self.safe_log(f"F13 Endgame-Override Fehler: {_f13e}", "BRAIN", "DEBUG")
                    elif mapped in action_order:
                        # Advisory: Brain-Empfehlung an Anfang, rest bleibt
                        action_order.remove(mapped)
                        action_order.insert(0, mapped)

                forced_action_order = list((mission_directive or {}).get("forced_action_order", []) or [])
                if forced_action_order:
                    disable_binding = bool((mission_directive or {}).get("disable_binding"))
                    if disable_binding:
                        brain_binding = False
                    action_order = merge_forced_action_order(
                        action_order,
                        forced_action_order,
                        objective=_mission_contract_for_order.get(
                            "objective", QUALIFIED_VISIBILITY_OBJECTIVE
                        ),
                        disable_binding=disable_binding,
                    )

                if _reply_first_lock and "reply" in action_order:
                    if action_order == ["like"]:
                        action_order = ["reply", "like"]
                    else:
                        action_order = ["reply"] + [action for action in action_order if action != "reply"]

                try:
                    news_cfg = self.config.get("news_commentary", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                    if (
                        isinstance(news_cfg, dict)
                        and bool(news_cfg.get("enabled", True))
                        and bool(news_cfg.get("kickoff_first_tweet", True))
                        and self._forced_readiness_allowed()
                        and tweet_timer_ready
                        and int(getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0) or 0) <= 0
                        and int(tweets_target or 0) > 0
                    ):
                        if "tweet" in action_order:
                            action_order.remove("tweet")
                        action_order.insert(0, "tweet")
                        self.safe_log(
                            "[NEWS] Kickoff-News-Tweet priorisiert (uebersteuert BINDEND einmalig vor Reply)",
                            "NEWS", "INFO",
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

                # [REACH] Standalone-Budget darf nicht verfallen: ist die Session
                # schon weit fortgeschritten und noch Tweet-Budget offen, wird
                # "tweet" vor "reply" gezogen. Sonst gewinnt Reply-First jeden Tick
                # (single_action_per_tick) und der Cap 3 bleibt bei ~1 Tweet stehen
                # (gemessener tweet_share 0.07-0.17, brain_memory reach 2026-06/07).
                # Timer-gated (tweet_interval unveraendert) -> feuert selten, Replies
                # bleiben durch min_replies_guarantee=28 geschuetzt.
                try:
                    _reach_prio_fraction = float(
                        (self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) or {}).get(
                            "reach_goal_priority_fraction", 0.60
                        )
                    )
                    _reach_elapsed_frac = (
                        (current_time - self.session_start_time).total_seconds()
                        / max(1.0, session_duration.total_seconds())
                    )
                    if self._reach_goal_missed(
                        _reach_elapsed_frac, tweets_target, tweet_timer_ready, _reach_prio_fraction
                    ):
                        if "tweet" in action_order:
                            action_order.remove("tweet")
                        action_order.insert(0, "tweet")
                        self.safe_log(
                            f"[REACH] Tweet vorgezogen: {int(getattr(self, 'tweets_posted', 0) or 0)}/"
                            f"{int(tweets_target or 0)} Standalone bei {_reach_elapsed_frac:.0%} der "
                            f"Session — Budget darf nicht verfallen",
                            "REACH", "INFO",
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

                # Der Advisor sieht die finale Reihenfolge. Der Modus wird an
                # jeder Action-Boundary neu gelesen, damit ein Notfallwechsel
                # shadow -> enforce ohne Session-Neustart greift.
                if action_order:
                    try:
                        if _graph_runtime_config is not None:
                            # Ein Parse-/Validierungsfehler blockiert diese
                            # Action-Boundary unabhängig vom zuletzt gelesenen
                            # Modus. So kann ENFORCE nie still degradieren.
                            _graph_enforce_fail_closed = True
                            _live_graph_config = (
                                _graph_runtime_config.get_graph_governance()
                            )
                            _live_enabled = _live_graph_config.enabled
                            _live_mode_raw = str(
                                _live_graph_config.mode
                            ).strip().lower()
                            if _live_mode_raw not in {"off", "shadow", "enforce"}:
                                raise ValueError("invalid graph governance mode")
                            _live_mode = GraphMode(_live_mode_raw)
                            _graph_enforce_fail_closed = (
                                _live_enabled and _live_mode == GraphMode.ENFORCE
                            )
                            if not _live_enabled or _live_mode == GraphMode.OFF:
                                graph_advisor = None
                                self._clear_graph_content_directive()
                            elif (
                                graph_advisor is None
                                or graph_advisor.mode != _live_mode
                            ):
                                graph_advisor = GraphAdvisor(
                                    mode=_live_mode,
                                    **_graph_advisor_kwargs,
                                )

                        if graph_advisor is not None:
                            self._refresh_graph_content_directive(graph_advisor)
                            _graph_filter = graph_advisor.filter_actions(action_order)
                            for _graph_assessment in _graph_filter["assessments"]:
                                if not graph_advisor.should_emit(_graph_assessment):
                                    continue
                                _graph_hold = (
                                    _graph_assessment.get("proposed_decision") == "HOLD"
                                )
                                self.safe_event(
                                    "GRAPH",
                                    "graph_action_hold"
                                    if _graph_hold
                                    else "graph_action_observed",
                                    {
                                        "mode": _graph_assessment.get("mode"),
                                        "graph_version": _graph_assessment.get(
                                            "graph_version"
                                        ),
                                        "health": _graph_assessment.get("health"),
                                        "proposed_decision": _graph_assessment.get(
                                            "proposed_decision"
                                        ),
                                        "effective_behavior": (
                                            "BLOCK_CANDIDATE"
                                            if graph_advisor.mode == GraphMode.ENFORCE
                                            and _graph_hold
                                            else "UNCHANGED"
                                        ),
                                        "candidate_action": _graph_assessment.get(
                                            "candidate_action"
                                        ),
                                        "reason_codes": _graph_assessment.get(
                                            "reason_codes", []
                                        ),
                                    },
                                    "WARNING" if _graph_hold else "INFO",
                                )
                            action_order = _graph_filter["allowed_actions"]
                        elif _graph_enforce_fail_closed:
                            self.safe_event(
                                "GRAPH",
                                "graph_action_fail_closed",
                                {
                                    "candidate_action": action_order[0],
                                    "effective_behavior": "BLOCK_ALL_EXTERNAL_ACTIONS",
                                    "reason_codes": ["advisor_unavailable"],
                                },
                                "ERROR",
                            )
                            action_order = []
                    except Exception as _graph_action_error:
                        _fail_closed = bool(
                            _graph_enforce_fail_closed
                            or (
                                graph_advisor is not None
                                and getattr(graph_advisor, "mode", None)
                                == GraphMode.ENFORCE
                            )
                        )
                        self.safe_event(
                            "GRAPH",
                            "graph_action_fail_closed"
                            if _fail_closed
                            else "graph_action_fail_open",
                            {
                                "error_type": type(_graph_action_error).__name__,
                                "candidate_action": action_order[0] if action_order else None,
                                "effective_behavior": (
                                    "BLOCK_ALL_EXTERNAL_ACTIONS"
                                    if _fail_closed
                                    else "UNCHANGED"
                                ),
                            },
                            "ERROR" if _fail_closed else "WARNING",
                        )
                        if _fail_closed:
                            action_order = []
                action_done_this_iteration = False

                for action_name in action_order:
                    # ---- LIKE BURST ----
                    if action_name == "like" and like_timer_ready:
                        if self.likes_given < likes_target:
                            if _reply_first_lock and not _like_guaranteed:
                                self.safe_log(
                                    "[REPLY] Like uebersprungen: Reply-First-Lock aktiv",
                                    "SESSION",
                                    "INFO",
                                )
                                last_like_burst = current_time
                                action_done_this_iteration = True
                                if single_action_per_tick:
                                    break
                                continue
                            # [BRAIN] Stunden-Suppression: aktiv überspringen bei nachweislich schlechter Stunde.
                            # F88: NICHT unterdrücken, wenn GARANTIE den Like erzwungen hat (Floor schlägt Suppression).
                            if (
                                self.session_brain
                                and not _like_guaranteed
                                and self.session_brain.should_suppress("like")
                            ):
                                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Like unterdrückt (schlechte Stunde)", "BRAIN", "DEBUG")
                                last_like_burst = current_time
                                action_done_this_iteration = True
                                if single_action_per_tick:
                                    break
                                continue
                            keyword = self._pick_keyword()
                            safety_cfg = self.config.get("safety_limits", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                            if not isinstance(safety_cfg, dict):
                                safety_cfg = {}
                            try:
                                max_per_burst = int(safety_cfg.get("like_burst_max_likes", 8))
                            except Exception:
                                max_per_burst = 8
                            max_per_burst = max(1, min(max_per_burst, 15))
                            likes_needed = min(max_per_burst, likes_target - self.likes_given)
                            before_likes = self.likes_given
                            self._last_like_attempt_uncertain = False
                            self._last_like_attempt_stopped = False
                            self.connection_resilient_like_burst(keyword, likes_needed)
                            like_success = self.likes_given > before_likes
                            self._record_scheduler_action_result(
                                "like", like_success, reason="" if like_success else "no_likes_found",
                                confirmed_delta=max(0, self.likes_given - before_likes),
                                stuck_detector=stuck_detector,
                            )
                            last_like_burst = current_time
                            action_done_this_iteration = True
                            if single_action_per_tick:
                                break

                    # ---- KOMMENTAR / REPLY ----
                    elif action_name == "reply" and comment_timer_ready:
                        if self.comments_posted < comments_target:
                            # [BRAIN] Stunden-Suppression
                            # F88: NICHT unterdrücken, wenn GARANTIE den Reply erzwungen hat (Floor schlägt Suppression).
                            if (
                                self.session_brain
                                and not _reply_guaranteed
                                and self.session_brain.should_suppress("reply")
                            ):
                                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Reply unterdrückt (schlechte Stunde)", "BRAIN", "DEBUG")
                                last_comment_time = current_time
                                action_done_this_iteration = True
                                if single_action_per_tick:
                                    break
                                continue
                            if not self._action_allowed("comment"):
                                self.safe_log("Kommentar uebersprungen (SAFETY: per-hour Limit erreicht)", "SAFETY", "WARNING")
                                last_comment_time = current_time
                            else:
                                success_comment = False
                                _reply_producer = "reply"
                                self._last_reply_outcome_status = "skipped"
                                try:
                                    reply_strategy = self.config.get("reply_strategy", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                                    if not isinstance(reply_strategy, dict):
                                        reply_strategy = {}
                                    try:
                                        direct_reply_ratio = float(reply_strategy.get("direct_reply_ratio", 0.90))
                                    except Exception:
                                        direct_reply_ratio = 0.90
                                    direct_reply_ratio = max(0.0, min(1.0, direct_reply_ratio))

                                    if random.random() < direct_reply_ratio:
                                        _reply_producer = "reply"
                                        self._last_reply_outcome_status = "skipped"
                                        success_comment = bool(self.reply_to_tweet_intelligent())
                                    else:
                                        _reply_producer = "quote"
                                        self._last_quote_outcome_status = "skipped"
                                        success_comment = bool(self.quote_tweet_optimized())
                                finally:
                                    self._record_scheduler_action_result(
                                        "reply", bool(success_comment), producer=_reply_producer,
                                        reason="" if success_comment else "reply_failed",
                                        learning_action="comment", duration=comment_interval,
                                        stuck_detector=stuck_detector,
                                    )
                                last_comment_time = current_time
                                action_done_this_iteration = True
                                if single_action_per_tick:
                                    break

                    # ---- TWEET ----
                    elif action_name == "tweet" and tweet_timer_ready:
                        if not self._action_allowed("tweet"):
                            continue
                        if self.tweets_posted < tweets_target:
                            self._refresh_brain_quality_brief()
                            # Tournament-Winner zuerst pruefen
                            tournament_budget_receipt = None
                            if self._tournament_winner:
                                tweet_data, tournament_budget_receipt = self._consume_tournament_winner()
                                self.safe_log(
                                    f"[TOURNAMENT] Gewinner wird gepostet: {tweet_data['text'][:60]}...",
                                    "TOURNAMENT", "INFO",
                                )
                            else:
                                tweet_data = self.generate_emoji_free_tweet()
                            if not tweet_data:
                                self.safe_log("Kein Tweet generiert (Scrape leer) - Posting uebersprungen", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                                if self.session_brain:
                                    try:
                                        self.session_brain.record_action("tweet", False, reason="generation_failed")
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
                                self._record_execution_signal("tweet", False, reason="generation_failed")
                                last_tweet_time = current_time
                                continue
                            tweet_payload = tweet_data if isinstance(tweet_data, dict) else {'text': tweet_data, 'topic': None, 'template_id': None, 'video': None}
                            if not tweet_payload.get('text'):
                                self.safe_log("Tweet-Text leer - Posting uebersprungen", "TWEETS", "WARNING")
                                if self.session_brain:
                                    try:
                                        self.session_brain.record_action("tweet", False, reason="generation_failed")
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
                                self._record_execution_signal("tweet", False, reason="generation_failed")
                                last_tweet_time = current_time
                                continue
                            if not self._has_valid_standalone_payload_format(
                                tweet_payload
                            ):
                                self.safe_log(
                                    "Tweet ohne gemessenes Einzelpost-Format "
                                    "verworfen (fail-closed)",
                                    "TWEETS",
                                    "WARNING",
                                )
                                if self.session_brain:
                                    try:
                                        self.session_brain.record_action(
                                            "tweet",
                                            False,
                                            reason="invalid_format",
                                        )
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
                                self._record_execution_signal(
                                    "tweet",
                                    False,
                                    reason="invalid_format",
                                )
                                last_tweet_time = current_time
                                continue

                            success = False
                            tweet_text = tweet_payload['text']
                            # [BRAIN] Pre-post Selbstkritik via LLM
                            _api_key = self.config.get("openai_api_key", "") if isinstance(self.config, dict) else ""
                            tweet_text = self._apply_self_critic_safely(
                                tweet_text,
                                tweet_payload,
                                api_key=_api_key,
                            )

                            success = bool(self.connection_resilient_tweet(tweet_text)
                                           if tournament_budget_receipt is None
                                           else self.connection_resilient_tweet(
                                               tweet_text, _budget_receipt=tournament_budget_receipt))
                            self._record_health_action("tweet", success)
                            _published_payload, _published_text = (
                                self._resolve_published_content(
                                    tweet_payload,
                                    tweet_text,
                                    thread_parts=None,
                                    thread_posted=False,
                                )
                            )
                            # UI-Delivery ist ein Betriebs-, kein Reichweiten-Erfolg.
                            # Topic/Template-Lernen erfolgt erst im verifizierten
                            # 24h-Outcome-Gateway; andernfalls lernt der Bot nur, dass
                            # ein Button-Klick funktionierte.
                            if self.session_brain:
                                try:
                                    self.session_brain.record_action("tweet", success, reason="" if success else "ui_timeout")
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                            self._record_execution_signal("tweet", success, reason="" if success else "ui_timeout")
                            if stuck_detector:
                                try:
                                    if success:
                                        stuck_detector.record_success("tweet")
                                    else:
                                        stuck_detector.record_failure("tweet")
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                            if success and self.tweet_learner:
                                try:
                                    _posted_url = getattr(self, '_last_posted_tweet_url', '') or ''
                                    _url_author, _status_id = self._status_identity_from_url(_posted_url)
                                    _own_username = str(
                                        self.config.get('own_username', 'ai_studioxyz') or ''
                                    ).lstrip('@')
                                    _canonical_url = _posted_url if (
                                        _status_id and _url_author.lower() == _own_username.lower()
                                    ) else ''
                                    _content_metadata = self._build_publish_metadata(
                                        _published_payload,
                                        _published_text,
                                    )
                                    _tweet_id = self.tweet_learner.record_tweet(
                                        text=_published_text,
                                        topic=_published_payload.get('topic', 'unknown') or 'unknown',
                                        tone=_published_payload.get('tone', 'unknown') or 'unknown',
                                        source=_published_payload.get('source', 'unknown') or 'unknown',
                                        darwin_strategy=_published_payload.get('darwin_strategy'),
                                        tweet_url=_posted_url,
                                        status_id=_status_id if _canonical_url else '',
                                        own_username=_own_username,
                                        canonical_url=_canonical_url,
                                        content_metadata=_content_metadata,
                                    )
                                    self._remember_posted_text(_published_text)
                                    # [QUICK-CHECK] Tweet in 30min-Feedback-Queue eintragen
                                    if _canonical_url:
                                        self._pending_tweet_checks.append({
                                            "id": _tweet_id,
                                            "tweet_id": _tweet_id,
                                            "url": _canonical_url,
                                            "tweet_url": _canonical_url,
                                            "canonical_url": _canonical_url,
                                            "status_id": _status_id,
                                            "own_username": _own_username,
                                            "ts": time.time(),
                                            "topic": _published_payload.get('topic', 'unknown') or 'unknown',
                                            "source": _published_payload.get('source', 'unknown') or 'unknown',
                                            "darwin_strategy": _published_payload.get('darwin_strategy'),
                                            "content_metadata": _content_metadata,
                                            "checked": False,
                                        })
                                except Exception as _track_err:
                                    self.safe_log(
                                        f"[OUTCOME] Publish-Tracking fehlgeschlagen: {_track_err}",
                                        "LEARNING", "WARNING",
                                    )
                            # Sofortreflexion ist eine Hypothese, keine Reichweitenmessung.
                            if success:
                                try:
                                    from intelligent_features.brain import reflect as brain_reflect
                                    _r = brain_reflect(
                                        action_taken=f"posted tweet (source={_published_payload.get('source','?')}): {_published_text[:120]}",
                                        result="tweet successfully posted on X",
                                        expected="tweet live and visible on X",
                                    )
                                    if _r.get("improve_next_time"):
                                        self.safe_log(
                                            f"[BRAIN] Reflexionshypothese (keine Wirkungsmessung): {_r.get('insight','')} | "
                                            f"next: {_r.get('improve_next_time','')}",
                                            "BRAIN", "INFO",
                                        )
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                            last_tweet_time = current_time
                            action_done_this_iteration = True
                            if single_action_per_tick:
                                break

                    # ---- RETWEET ----
                    elif action_name == "retweet" and retweet_timer_ready:
                        if self.retweets_posted < retweets_target:
                            # [BRAIN] Stunden-Suppression
                            if self.session_brain and self.session_brain.should_suppress("retweet"):
                                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Retweet unterdrückt (schlechte Stunde)", "BRAIN", "DEBUG")
                                last_retweet_time = current_time
                                action_done_this_iteration = True
                                if single_action_per_tick:
                                    break
                                continue
                            keyword = self._pick_keyword()
                            before_retweets = self.retweets_posted
                            if self.connection_resilient_retweet(keyword):
                                pass
                            retweet_success = self.retweets_posted > before_retweets
                            self._record_health_action("retweet", retweet_success)
                            if self.session_brain:
                                try:
                                    self.session_brain.record_action("retweet", retweet_success, reason="" if retweet_success else "retweet_failed")
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                            self._record_execution_signal("retweet", retweet_success, reason="" if retweet_success else "retweet_failed")
                            if stuck_detector:
                                try:
                                    if retweet_success:
                                        stuck_detector.record_success("retweet")
                                    else:
                                        stuck_detector.record_failure("retweet")
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                            last_retweet_time = current_time
                            action_done_this_iteration = True
                            if single_action_per_tick:
                                break

                # Rephrase (unabhaengig vom Brain â€" laeuft parallel)
                if rephrase_enabled and self.rephrased_posts < rephrase_target:
                    if (current_time - last_rephrase_time).total_seconds() >= rephrase_interval * 60:
                        success_rephrase = bool(self.rephrase_and_post_trending())
                        last_rephrase_time = current_time
                        try:
                            self.learning_system.record_action_result('tweet', 'rephrase', bool(success_rephrase), rephrase_interval, {})
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        if success_rephrase:
                            self.rephrased_posts += 1

                # INTELLIGENTES FOLGEN: Verschoben ans Session-Ende (run_growth_cycle)
                # um die Haupt-Loop nicht durch Seitenwechsel zu stoeren.
                
                # --- TOURNAMENT: Kandidaten sammeln / Gewinner waehlen ---
                self._service_tournament_queue(current_time, remaining_time)

                # F30 Force-Post: garantiert 1 eigener Tweet alle 90min, umgeht Tournament
                try:
                    self._exit_state["phase"] = "force_post_check"
                    self._exit_state["last_phase_ts"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                    self._exit_state["tweets_posted"] = self.tweets_posted
                    self._exit_state["elapsed_min"] = (
                        (datetime.now() - self.session_start_time).total_seconds() / 60.0
                        if self.session_start_time else 0.0
                    )
                    if datetime.now() < session_end_time:
                        self._force_post_if_overdue()
                except Exception as _fp_err:
                    self.safe_log(f"[FORCE_POST] Loop-Fehler: {_fp_err}", "TWEETS", "WARNING")

                # Pruefe ob alle Ziele erreicht
                if (self.tweets_posted >= tweets_target and 
                    self.likes_given >= likes_target and 
                    self.comments_posted >= comments_target and
                    self.retweets_posted >= retweets_target):
                    self.safe_log("[FEIER] ALLE ERWEITERTEN ZIELE ERREICHT!", "SESSION", "INFO")
                    break
                
                # Idle-Scrolling + Phantom-Aktionen: menschliches Verhalten simulieren
                # 30% Chance auf eine Phantom-Aktion (Notifications, Profil, Thread, Explore, Fake-Draft)
                # Rest: normaler Idle-Scroll
                _phantom_done = False
                if action_done_this_iteration:
                    # Nach echter Aktion: etwas höhere Chance für menschliche Pause-Aktivität
                    _phantom_chance = float(
                        (self.config.get("human_behavior_settings", {}) or {}).get(
                            "phantom_chance_after_action", 0.35
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    _phantom_chance = float(
                        (self.config.get("human_behavior_settings", {}) or {}).get(
                            "phantom_chance_idle", 0.20
                        )
                    )
                try:
                    _phantom_done = self.maybe_do_phantom_action(probability=_phantom_chance)
                except Exception as _ph_err:
                    self.safe_log(f"[HUMAN] Phantom-Fehler: {_ph_err}", "HUMAN", "DEBUG")

                if not _phantom_done:
                    self._idle_scroll(random.uniform(12, 40))
            
            # Session-Zusammenfassung
            session_duration_actual = datetime.now() - self.session_start_time
            
            # [BRAIN] SessionBrain Phase 2 Zusammenfassung
            if self.session_brain:
                try:
                    insight = self.session_brain.get_session_insight()
                    self.safe_log(f"[BRAIN] PHASE 2 Insight: {insight}", "BRAIN", "INFO")
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # [BRAIN] Finaler Engagement-Score
            if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('engagement_scoring', False):
                final_engagement_score = self.intelligence.calculate_engagement_score(
                    self.likes_given, self.comments_posted, self.retweets_posted, self.tweets_posted
                )
                self.engagement_score = final_engagement_score

            # [BRAIN] SessionBrain Zusammenfassung
            if self.session_brain:
                try:
                    insight = self.session_brain.get_session_insight()
                    self.safe_log(f"[BRAIN] Session-Insight: {insight}", "BRAIN", "INFO")
                except Exception:
                    pass

            self.print_enhanced_statistics(session_duration_actual, tweets_target, likes_target, comments_target, retweets_target)

            # [GROWTH] Follow/Unfollow am Session-Ende (stoert keine laufenden Aktionen)
            try:
                strat = self.config.get('follow_strategy', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                if strat and strat.get('enabled', False):
                    self.safe_log("=== Growth Cycle am Session-Ende ===", "GROWTH", "INFO")
                    self.run_growth_cycle()
            except Exception as _ge:
                self.safe_log(f"Growth Cycle Fehler: {_ge}", "GROWTH", "ERROR")

        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            self.safe_log("[TIMER] Session vom Benutzer beendet.", "SESSION", "WARNING")
            self.print_enhanced_statistics_on_exit()
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler waehrend erweiteter Session: {e}", "SESSION", "ERROR")
            self.print_enhanced_statistics_on_exit()
        finally:
            if self.session_brain:
                try:
                    self.session_brain.save_memory()
                    self.safe_log("[BRAIN] Memory gespeichert", "BRAIN", "INFO")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"[BRAIN] Memory-Save fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "BRAIN", "WARNING")
            if getattr(self, "content_gatekeeper", None):
                try:
                    self.content_gatekeeper.save_session_stats()
                    self.safe_log("[GATE] Gatekeeper-Learning gespeichert", "GATEKEEPER", "INFO")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"[GATE] Gatekeeper-Save fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "GATEKEEPER", "WARNING")
            if self.goal_tracker:
                try:
                    self.goal_tracker.record_session(
                        tweets=getattr(self, "tweets_posted", 0),
                        likes=getattr(self, "likes_given", 0),
                        replies=getattr(self, "comments_posted", 0),
                        retweets=getattr(self, "retweets_posted", 0),
                    )
                    self.safe_log(
                        f"[GOALS] Session gespeichert | {self.goal_tracker.get_weekly_summary()}",
                        "GOALS", "INFO",
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"[GOALS] Record-Session fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "GOALS", "WARNING")
            # --- Agentic Post-Session: StrategyAdvisor, ABTester ---
            self._run_post_session_agents()

            if agent_hub:
                try:
                    agent_hub.stop()
                    self.safe_log("[AGENT_HUB] gestoppt", "AGENTS", "INFO")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.safe_log(f"[AGENT_HUB] Stop fehlgeschlagen: {e}", "AGENTS", "WARNING")
            if _log_touch_stop is not None:
                try:
                    _log_touch_stop.set()
                    if _log_touch_thread is not None:
                        _log_touch_thread.join(timeout=2)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if self.driver:
                try:
                    self.driver.quit()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def print_enhanced_statistics(self, duration, tweets_target, likes_target, comments_target, retweets_target):
        """Druckt erweiterte finale Statistiken."""
        self.safe_log("[FEIER] ERWEITERTE SESSION ABGESCHLOSSEN!", "SESSION", "INFO")
        self.safe_log(f"[TIMER] Dauer: {str(duration).split('.')[0]}", "SESSION", "INFO")
        
        self.safe_log("[CHART] FINALE STATISTIKEN:", "STATS", "INFO")
        self.safe_log(f"   [BIRD] Tweets gepostet: {self.tweets_posted} (Ziel: {tweets_target})", "STATS", "INFO")
        self.safe_log(f"   [HERZ] Likes vergeben: {self.likes_given} (Ziel: {likes_target})", "STATS", "INFO")
        self.safe_log(f"   [KOMMENTAR] Kommentare gepostet: {self.comments_posted} (Ziel: {comments_target})", "STATS", "INFO")
        self.safe_log(f"   [REFRESH] Retweets gepostet: {self.retweets_posted} (Ziel: {retweets_target})", "STATS", "INFO")
        
        # [BRAIN] Intelligente Metriken
        if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('engagement_scoring', False):
            self.safe_log(f"   [BRAIN] Engagement-Score: {self.engagement_score}", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
        
        self.safe_log(f"   [REFRESH] Wiederverbindungen: {self.reconnection_attempts}", "STATS", "INFO")
        
        # [CHART] Performance-Metriken
        if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('performance_monitoring', False):
            metrics_summary = self.metrics.get_summary()
            self.safe_log("[CHART] PERFORMANCE-ANALYSE:", "METRICS", "INFO")
            for key, value in metrics_summary.items():
                self.safe_log(f"   [TREND] {key}: {value}", "METRICS", "INFO")

            # Export metrics snapshot for later tuning
            if self._enhanced_flag("metrics_tracking", True):
                try:
                    export_dir = "backups"
                    os.makedirs(export_dir, exist_ok=True)
                    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
                    path = os.path.join(export_dir, f"performance_{ts}.json")
                    ok = self.metrics.export_summary(
                        path,
                        extra={
                            "tweets_posted": self.tweets_posted,
                            "likes_given": self.likes_given,
                            "comments_posted": self.comments_posted,
                            "retweets_posted": self.retweets_posted,
                        },
                    )
                    if ok:
                        self.safe_log(f"Performance-Export gespeichert: {path}", "METRICS", "INFO")
                except Exception:
                    pass
        
        # Performance-Analyse
        if duration.total_seconds() > 0:
            likes_per_hour = (self.likes_given / duration.total_seconds()) * 3600
            comments_per_hour = (self.comments_posted / duration.total_seconds()) * 3600
            self.safe_log("[TREND] ERWEITERTE PERFORMANCE-ANALYSE:", "PERFORMANCE", "INFO")
            self.safe_log(f"   [BLITZ] Likes/Stunde: {likes_per_hour:.1f}", "PERFORMANCE", "INFO")
            self.safe_log(f"   [BLITZ] Kommentare/Stunde: {comments_per_hour:.1f}", "PERFORMANCE", "INFO")
            
            # Effizienz-Rating
            efficiency = self.metrics.calculate_efficiency()
            self.safe_log(f"   [ZIEL] Effizienz-Rating: {efficiency:.1f}%", "PERFORMANCE", "INFO")
        
        # [BRAIN] LERNSYSTEM ABSCHLIESSEN (nur wenn aktiv)
        if getattr(self.learning_system, "enabled", True):
            try:
                final_metrics = {
                    'tweets': self.tweets_posted,
                    'likes': self.likes_given,
                    'comments': self.comments_posted,
                    'retweets': self.retweets_posted,
                    'efficiency': efficiency if 'efficiency' in locals() else 0
                }
                self.learning_system.end_learning_session(final_metrics)
                learning_insights = self.learning_system.get_learning_insights()
                
                self.safe_log("[BRAIN] LERNSYSTEM ABGESCHLOSSEN:", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [BUCH] Gesamt-Sessions: {learning_insights['total_sessions']}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                if learning_insights['learning_active']:
                    self.safe_log(f"   [ZIEL] Erfolgsrate: {learning_insights.get('recent_success_rate', 0):.1f}%", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                    best_keywords = learning_insights['best_keywords']
                    if best_keywords:
                        self.safe_log(f"   [POKAL] Beste Keywords: {', '.join(best_keywords)}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [SCHILD] Sicherheitsstatus: {learning_insights['safety_status']}", "LEARNING", "INFO")
                
                # [SCHILD] FALLBACK-SYSTEM STATISTIKEN
                best_strategy = self.fallback_system.get_best_strategy('like_burst')
                self.safe_log(f"   [SCHILD] Beste Fallback-Strategie: {best_strategy}", "FALLBACK", "INFO")
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.safe_log(f"Fehler beim Lernsystem-Abschluss: {e}", "LEARNING", "ERROR")
        else:
            self.safe_log("[BRAIN] LERNSYSTEM: DEAKTIVIERT (keine Speicherung)", "LEARNING", "DEBUG")
    
    def print_enhanced_statistics_on_exit(self):
        """Druckt erweiterte finale Statistiken bei vorzeitigem Exit."""
        try:
            if hasattr(self, 'session_start_time') and self.session_start_time:
                duration = datetime.now() - self.session_start_time
                self.safe_log("[CHART] FINALE STATISTIKEN (VORZEITIGER EXIT):", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [TIMER] Laufzeit: {str(duration).split('.')[0]}", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [BIRD] Tweets gepostet: {self.tweets_posted}", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [HERZ] Likes vergeben: {self.likes_given}", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [KOMMENTAR] Kommentare gepostet: {self.comments_posted}", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [REFRESH] Retweets gepostet: {self.retweets_posted}", "STATS", "INFO")
                self.safe_log(f"   [REFRESH] Wiederverbindungen: {self.reconnection_attempts}", "STATS", "INFO")
                
                # [BRAIN] Intelligente Metriken
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('engagement_scoring', False):
                    self.safe_log(f"   [BRAIN] Engagement-Score: {self.engagement_score}", "INTELLIGENCE", "INFO")
                
                # [CHART] Performance-Metriken
                if self.config.get('intelligence_settings', {}).get('performance_monitoring', False):
                    metrics_summary = self.metrics.get_summary()
                    self.safe_log("[CHART] PERFORMANCE-METRIKEN:", "METRICS", "INFO")
                    for key, value in metrics_summary.items():
                        self.safe_log(f"   [TREND] {key}: {value}", "METRICS", "INFO")
                        
        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler bei finaler Statistik: {e}", "STATS", "ERROR")
    
    def start(self):
        """Startet den erweiterten hyperaktiven reply-button-optimierten Bot."""
        try:
            print("[ZIEL] ERWEITERTER HYPERAKTIVER TWITTER BOT")
            print("=" * 60)
            print("ALLE BEWAEHRTEN FUNKTIONEN + ERWEITERTE INTELLIGENCE!")
            print()
            print("[TOOL] ERWEITERTE FUNKTIONEN:")
            print("[OK]  18+ moderne Reply-Button-Selektoren fuer X.com")
            print("[OK]  Dynamische Reply-Button-Erkennung nach Texteingabe")
            session_limits = self.config.get('session_limits', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            likes_limit = int(session_limits.get('likes_per_session', 20) or 20)
            comments_limit = int(session_limits.get('comments_per_session', 10) or 10)
            tweets_limit = int(session_limits.get('tweets_per_session', 20) or 20)
            retweets_limit = int(session_limits.get('retweets_per_session', 10) or 10)
            print(f"[OK]  {likes_limit} Likes pro Session (Config: session_limits)")
            print(f"[OK]  {comments_limit} Kommentare pro Session (Config: session_limits)")
            print(f"[OK]  {tweets_limit} Tweets pro Session (Config: session_limits)")
            print(f"[OK]  {retweets_limit} Retweets pro Session (Config: session_limits)")
            print("[OK]  [BRAIN] Intelligentes Engagement-Scoring")
            print("[OK]  [UHR] Adaptive Timing-Optimierung")
            print("[OK]  [KOMMENTAR] Kontextuelle Kommentar-Generierung")
            print("[OK]  [CHART] Erweiterte Performance-Metriken")
            print("[OK] [GLOCKE] Verbindungsueberwachung und Stale-Element-Recovery")
            print("[OK]  [NOTIZ] Kategorisierte Logging-Funktionen")
            print("[OK]  [ZIEL] Robuste Fehlerbehandlung")
            try:
                ad = self.config.get("anti_detection_features", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
                if isinstance(ad, dict):
                    print(f"[OK]  Anti-Detection: random_delays={bool(ad.get('random_delays', True))}, varied_patterns={bool(ad.get('varied_interaction_patterns', True))}")
            except Exception:
                pass
            print()
            print("[BLITZ] INTELLIGENTE TIMING:")
            ai = self.config.get('activity_intervals', {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}
            print(f"   [FEUER] Like-Bursts alle ~{ai.get('like_burst_minutes', 15)} Minuten (config + adaptiv)")
            print(f"   [KOMMENTAR] Kommentare alle ~{ai.get('comment_interval_minutes', 20)} Minuten (config + adaptiv)")
