            print(f"   [BIRD] Tweets alle ~{ai.get('tweet_interval_minutes', 30)} Minuten (config + adaptiv)")
            print(f"   [REFRESH] Retweets alle ~{ai.get('retweet_interval_minutes', 36)} Minuten (config + adaptiv)")
            print()
            print("[EMOJI] STARTE ERWEITERTE HYPERAKTIVE SESSION...")
            print("=" * 60)
            
            # Verbinde mit Browser
            if not self.connect_to_existing_browser():
                print("[X]  Fehler: Konnte nicht mit Chrome verbinden!")
                return
            
            # Finde und wechsle zu Twitter-Tab
            if not self.find_twitter_tab():
                print("[X]  Fehler: Konnte Twitter-Tab nicht finden!")
                return
            
            # Verifiziere Twitter-Seite
            if not self.verify_twitter_page():
                print("[X]  Fehler: Nicht auf der richtigen Twitter-Seite!")
                return
            
            print("[OK]  ERWEITERTER REPLY-BUTTON-OPTIMIERTER BOT BEREIT!")
            print()
            print("[ZIEL] Der erweiterte Bot wird nun:")
            engagement_targets = self.config.get('engagement_targets', {
                'tweets_per_session': 35,  # Follower Growth: Mehr Sichtbarkeit
                'likes_per_session': 40,  # Follower Growth: Mehr Engagement
                'comments_per_session': 25,  # Follower Growth: Mehr Interaktionen
                'retweets_per_session': 20  # Follower Growth: Mehr Reichweite
            })
            print(f"[BIRD] {engagement_targets.get('tweets_per_session', 35)} Follower Growth Tweets posten")
            print(f"[HERZ] {engagement_targets.get('likes_per_session', 40)} Follower Growth Likes vergeben")
            print(f"[KOMMENTAR] {engagement_targets.get('comments_per_session', 25)} Follower Growth Kommentare posten") 
            print(f"[REFRESH] {engagement_targets.get('retweets_per_session', 20)} Follower Growth Retweets durchfuehren")
            print(f"[UHR] {self.config['session_duration_hours']} Stunden stabil arbeiten")
            print(f"[GLOCKE] Browser-Verbindung alle {self.connection_check_interval}s ueberwachen")
            print(f"[BRAIN] Intelligente Features nutzen fuer optimale Performance")
            print()
            
            confirm = input("[EMOJI] Moechten Sie die erweiterte hyperaktive Session starten? (j/n): ").lower().strip()
            if confirm in ['j', 'ja', 'y', 'yes']:
                self.run_enhanced_session()
            else:
                print("[X] Session abgebrochen.")

        except Exception as e:
            self.safe_log(f"Fehler beim Starten: {e}", "STARTUP", "ERROR")
        finally:
            if self.driver:
                try:
                    self.driver.quit()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    """Kompatibilitaets-Wrapper fuer `cli.backup.create_backup`."""
    from cli.backup import create_backup as cli_create_backup

    return cli_create_backup(retention=retention)

    """Erstellt ein separates Backup nur fuer den Hauptbot-Code."""
    from cli.backup import create_hauptbot_backup as cli_create_hauptbot_backup

    return cli_create_hauptbot_backup(package_dir=package_dir, retention=retention)

def setup_logging_runner(debug: bool) -> None:
    """Kompatibilitaets-Wrapper fuer `cli.runner.setup_logging_runner`."""
    from cli.runner import setup_logging_runner as cli_setup_logging_runner
    cli_setup_logging_runner(debug=debug)

def check_python_version(min_major=3, min_minor=8) -> None:
    """Kompatibilitaets-Wrapper fuer `cli.preflight.check_python_version`."""
    from cli.preflight import check_python_version as cli_check_python_version
    cli_check_python_version(min_major=min_major, min_minor=min_minor)

def ensure_bot_class_loaded(log: Optional[logging.Logger] = None) -> None:
    """Kompatibilitaets-Wrapper fuer `cli.preflight.ensure_bot_class_loaded`."""
    from cli.preflight import ensure_bot_class_loaded as cli_ensure_bot_class_loaded
    cli_ensure_bot_class_loaded(log=log)

def preflight(config_path: str, debug: bool) -> None:
    """Kompatibilitaets-Wrapper fuer `cli.preflight.preflight`."""
    from cli.preflight import preflight as cli_preflight
    cli_preflight(config_path, debug=debug)

def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
    """CLI entry point; delegates to `cli.runner.main`."""
    from cli.runner import main as runner_main

    return runner_main(argv)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Doppelstart-Schutz: Lock pruefen bevor irgendetwas passiert
    from cli.instance_lock import acquire_lock
    _repository_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.dirname(__file__))
    _lock_path = os.path.join(_repository_root, "state", "bot.lock")
    _heartbeat_path = os.path.join(_repository_root, "state", "bot_health.json")
    if not acquire_lock(_lock_path, heartbeat_path=_heartbeat_path):
        print("ABBRUCH: Eine andere Bot-Instanz laeuft bereits!", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)
    sys.exit(main())
